# Ezekiel

יחזקאל

Link: [https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel)

נבואות תוכחה ונחמה, חזון בית המקדש העתידי.

Dramatic symbolism conveying rebuke or hope, and visions of a future Temple.

Sections: Chapter-Verse

Location: Tanakh/Prophets

Originally from [Sefaria](https://www.sefaria.org) via [TorahLM.org](https://torahlm.org) — see [licensing](https://torahlm.org/sefaria_license).

Version (He): Miqra according to the Masorah (Segments: 1273, License: CC-BY-SA)

Version (En): The Koren Jerusalem Bible (Segments: 1273, License: CC-BY-NC)


## Chapter 1

###### Ezekiel 1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/1:1)


וַיְהִ֣י**׀** בִּשְׁלֹשִׁ֣ים שָׁנָ֗ה בָּֽרְבִיעִי֙ בַּחֲמִשָּׁ֣ה לַחֹ֔דֶשׁ וַאֲנִ֥י בְתֽוֹךְ־הַגּוֹלָ֖ה עַל־נְהַר־כְּבָ֑ר נִפְתְּחוּ֙ הַשָּׁמַ֔יִם וָאֶרְאֶ֖ה מַרְא֥וֹת אֱלֹהִֽים׃


Now it came to pass in the thirtieth year, in the fourth month, on the fifth day of the month, as I was among the exiles by the river Kevar, that the heavens were opened, and I saw visions of God.


###### Ezekiel 1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/1:2)


בַּחֲמִשָּׁ֖ה לַחֹ֑דֶשׁ הִ֚יא הַשָּׁנָ֣ה הַחֲמִישִׁ֔ית לְגָל֖וּת הַמֶּ֥לֶךְ יוֹיָכִֽין׃


In the fifth day of the month, which was the fifth year of the exile of king Yoyakhin,


###### Ezekiel 1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/1:3)


הָיֹ֣ה הָיָ֣ה דְבַר־יְ֠הֹוָ֠ה אֶל־יְחֶזְקֵ֨אל בֶּן־בּוּזִ֧י הַכֹּהֵ֛ן בְּאֶ֥רֶץ כַּשְׂדִּ֖ים עַל־נְהַר־כְּבָ֑ר וַתְּהִ֥י עָלָ֛יו שָׁ֖ם יַד־יְהֹוָֽה׃


the word of the Lord came to Yeĥezqel the priest, the son of Buzi, in the land of Kasdim by the river Kevar; and the hand of the Lord was there upon him.


###### Ezekiel 1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/1:4)


וָאֵ֡רֶא וְהִנֵּה֩ ר֨וּחַ סְעָרָ֜ה בָּאָ֣ה מִן־הַצָּפ֗וֹן עָנָ֤ן גָּדוֹל֙ וְאֵ֣שׁ מִתְלַקַּ֔חַת וְנֹ֥גַֽהּ־ל֖וֹ סָבִ֑יב וּמִ֨תּוֹכָ֔הּ כְּעֵ֥ין הַחַשְׁמַ֖ל מִתּ֥וֹךְ הָאֵֽשׁ׃


And I looked, and, behold, a storm wind came out of the north, a great cloud, and a fire flaring up, and a brightness was about it, and out of the midst of it, as it were the colour of electrum, out of the midst of the fire.


###### Ezekiel 1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/1:5)


וּמִ֨תּוֹכָ֔הּ דְּמ֖וּת אַרְבַּ֣ע חַיּ֑וֹת וְזֶה֙ מַרְאֵיהֶ֔ן דְּמ֥וּת אָדָ֖ם לָהֵֽנָּה׃


Also out of the midst of it came the likeness of four living creatures. And this was their appearance; they had the likeness of a man.


###### Ezekiel 1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/1:6)


וְאַרְבָּעָ֥ה פָנִ֖ים לְאֶחָ֑ת וְאַרְבַּ֥ע כְּנָפַ֖יִם לְאַחַ֥ת לָהֶֽם׃


And every one had four faces, and every one had four wings.


###### Ezekiel 1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/1:7)


וְרַגְלֵיהֶ֖ם רֶ֣גֶל יְשָׁרָ֑ה וְכַ֣ף רַגְלֵיהֶ֗ם כְּכַף֙ רֶ֣גֶל עֵ֔גֶל וְנֹ֣צְצִ֔ים כְּעֵ֖ין נְחֹ֥שֶׁת קָלָֽל׃


And their feet were straight feet; and the sole of their feet was like the sole of a calf’s foot: and they sparkled like the colour of burnished brass.


###### Ezekiel 1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/1:8)


(וידו) [וִידֵ֣י] אָדָ֗ם מִתַּ֙חַת֙ כַּנְפֵיהֶ֔ם עַ֖ל אַרְבַּ֣עַת רִבְעֵיהֶ֑ם וּפְנֵיהֶ֥ם וְכַנְפֵיהֶ֖ם לְאַרְבַּעְתָּֽם׃


And they had the hands of a man under their wings on their four sides; and as for their faces and their wings (of those four)


###### Ezekiel 1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/1:9)


חֹ֥בְרֹ֛ת אִשָּׁ֥ה אֶל־אֲחוֹתָ֖הּ כַּנְפֵיהֶ֑ם לֹא־יִסַּ֣בּוּ בְלֶכְתָּ֔ן אִ֛ישׁ אֶל־עֵ֥בֶר פָּנָ֖יו יֵלֵֽכוּ׃


their wings were joined one to another; they turned not when they went; they went everyone straight forward.


###### Ezekiel 1:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/1:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/1:10)


וּדְמ֣וּת פְּנֵיהֶם֮ פְּנֵ֣י אָדָם֒ וּפְנֵ֨י אַרְיֵ֤ה אֶל־הַיָּמִין֙ לְאַרְבַּעְתָּ֔ם וּפְנֵי־שׁ֥וֹר מֵהַשְּׂמֹ֖אול לְאַרְבַּעְתָּ֑ן וּפְנֵי־נֶ֖שֶׁר לְאַרְבַּעְתָּֽן׃


As for the likeness of their faces, they had the face of a man, and they four had the face of a lion, on the right side: and they four had the face of an ox on the left side; they four also had the face of an eagle.


###### Ezekiel 1:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/1:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/1:11)


וּפְנֵיהֶ֕ם וְכַנְפֵיהֶ֥ם פְּרֻד֖וֹת מִלְמָ֑עְלָה לְאִ֗ישׁ שְׁ֚תַּיִם חֹבְר֣וֹת אִ֔ישׁ וּשְׁתַּ֣יִם מְכַסּ֔וֹת אֵ֖ת גְּוִיֹּתֵיהֶֽנָה׃


Thus were their faces: and their wings were divided upwards; two wings of everyone were joined one to another, and two covered their bodies.


###### Ezekiel 1:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/1:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/1:12)


וְאִ֛ישׁ אֶל־עֵ֥בֶר פָּנָ֖יו יֵלֵ֑כוּ אֶ֣ל אֲשֶׁר֩ יִֽהְיֶה־שָּׁ֨מָּה הָר֤וּחַ לָלֶ֙כֶת֙ יֵלֵ֔כוּ לֹ֥א יִסַּ֖בּוּ בְּלֶכְתָּֽן׃


And they went everyone straight forward: wherever the spirit was minded to go, they went; they turned not when they went.


###### Ezekiel 1:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/1:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/1:13)


וּדְמ֨וּת הַחַיּ֜וֹת מַרְאֵיהֶ֣ם כְּגַחֲלֵי־אֵ֗שׁ בֹּֽעֲרוֹת֙ כְּמַרְאֵ֣ה הַלַּפִּדִ֔ים הִ֕יא מִתְהַלֶּ֖כֶת בֵּ֣ין הַחַיּ֑וֹת וְנֹ֣גַהּ לָאֵ֔שׁ וּמִן־הָאֵ֖שׁ יוֹצֵ֥א בָרָֽק׃


As for the likeness of the living creatures, their appearance was like coals of fire, burning like the appearance of torches: it flashed up and down among the living creatures; and the fire was bright, and out of the fire went forth lightning.


###### Ezekiel 1:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/1:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/1:14)


וְהַחַיּ֖וֹת רָצ֣וֹא וָשׁ֑וֹב כְּמַרְאֵ֖ה הַבָּזָֽק׃


And the living creatures ran and returned like the appearance of a flash of lightning.


###### Ezekiel 1:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/1:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/1:15)


וָאֵ֖רֶא הַחַיּ֑וֹת וְהִנֵּה֩ אוֹפַ֨ן אֶחָ֥ד בָּאָ֛רֶץ אֵ֥צֶל הַחַיּ֖וֹת לְאַרְבַּ֥עַת פָּנָֽיו׃


Now as I beheld the living creatures, behold one wheel upon the earth by the living creatures, with his four faces.


###### Ezekiel 1:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/1:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/1:16)


מַרְאֵ֨ה הָאוֹפַנִּ֤ים וּמַֽעֲשֵׂיהֶם֙ כְּעֵ֣ין תַּרְשִׁ֔ישׁ וּדְמ֥וּת אֶחָ֖ד לְאַרְבַּעְתָּ֑ן וּמַרְאֵיהֶם֙ וּמַ֣עֲשֵׂיהֶ֔ם כַּאֲשֶׁ֛ר יִהְיֶ֥ה הָאוֹפַ֖ן בְּת֥וֹךְ הָאוֹפָֽן׃


The appearance of the wheels and their work was like the colour of an emerald: and they four had one likeness: and their appearance and their work was as it were a wheel in the middle of a wheel.


###### Ezekiel 1:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/1:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/1:17)


עַל־אַרְבַּ֥עַת רִבְעֵיהֶ֖ן בְּלֶכְתָּ֣ם יֵלֵ֑כוּ לֹ֥א יִסַּ֖בּוּ בְּלֶכְתָּֽן׃


When they went, they went toward their four sides: and they turned not when they went.


###### Ezekiel 1:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/1:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/1:18)


וְגַ֨בֵּיהֶ֔ן וְגֹ֥בַהּ לָהֶ֖ם וְיִרְאָ֣ה לָהֶ֑ם וְגַבֹּתָ֗ם מְלֵאֹ֥ת עֵינַ֛יִם סָבִ֖יב לְאַרְבַּעְתָּֽן׃


As for their rims, they were so high that they were dreadful, and their rims were full of eyes round about them four.


###### Ezekiel 1:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/1:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/1:19)


וּבְלֶ֙כֶת֙ הַחַיּ֔וֹת יֵלְכ֥וּ הָאוֹפַנִּ֖ים אֶצְלָ֑ם וּבְהִנָּשֵׂ֤א הַחַיּוֹת֙ מֵעַ֣ל הָאָ֔רֶץ יִנָּשְׂא֖וּ הָאוֹפַנִּֽים׃


And when the living creatures moved, the wheels went by them: and when the living creatures were lifted up from the earth, the wheels were lifted up.


###### Ezekiel 1:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/1:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/1:20)


עַ֣ל אֲשֶׁר֩ יִֽהְיֶה־שָּׁ֨ם הָר֤וּחַ לָלֶ֙כֶת֙ יֵלֵ֔כוּ שָׁ֥מָּה הָר֖וּחַ לָלֶ֑כֶת וְהָאוֹפַנִּ֗ים יִנָּֽשְׂאוּ֙ לְעֻמָּתָ֔ם כִּ֛י ר֥וּחַ הַחַיָּ֖ה בָּאוֹפַנִּֽים׃


Wherever the spirit was minded to go, they moved, for the spirit was minded to go there; and the wheels were lifted up along with them: for the spirit of the living creature was in the wheels.


###### Ezekiel 1:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/1:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/1:21)


בְּלֶכְתָּ֣ם יֵלֵ֔כוּ וּבְעׇמְדָ֖ם יַעֲמֹ֑דוּ וּֽבְהִנָּשְׂאָ֞ם מֵעַ֣ל הָאָ֗רֶץ יִנָּשְׂא֤וּ הָאֽוֹפַנִּים֙ לְעֻמָּתָ֔ם כִּ֛י ר֥וּחַ הַחַיָּ֖ה בָּאוֹפַנִּֽים׃


When those moved, these moved; and when those stood still, these stood still; and when those were lifted up from the earth, the wheels were lifted up along with them: for the spirit of the living creature was in the wheels.


###### Ezekiel 1:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/1:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/1:22)


וּדְמ֞וּת עַל־רָאשֵׁ֤י הַחַיָּה֙ רָקִ֔יעַ כְּעֵ֖ין הַקֶּ֣רַח הַנּוֹרָ֑א נָט֥וּי עַל־רָאשֵׁיהֶ֖ם מִלְמָֽעְלָה׃


And over the heads of the living creatures there was the likeness of a firmament, like the colour of the terrible ice, stretched out over their heads above.


###### Ezekiel 1:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/1:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/1:23)


וְתַ֙חַת֙ הָרָקִ֔יעַ כַּנְפֵיהֶ֣ם יְשָׁר֔וֹת אִשָּׁ֖ה אֶל־אֲחוֹתָ֑הּ לְאִ֗ישׁ שְׁתַּ֤יִם מְכַסּוֹת֙ לָהֵ֔נָּה וּלְאִ֗ישׁ שְׁתַּ֤יִם מְכַסּוֹת֙ לָהֵ֔נָּה אֵ֖ת גְּוִיֹּתֵיהֶֽם׃


And under the firmament their wings were held straight, the one toward the other: everyone had two, which covered them, namely, everyone two, which covered their bodies.


###### Ezekiel 1:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/1:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/1:24)


וָאֶשְׁמַ֣ע אֶת־ק֣וֹל כַּנְפֵיהֶ֡ם כְּקוֹל֩ מַ֨יִם רַבִּ֤ים כְּקוֹל־שַׁדַּי֙ בְּלֶכְתָּ֔ם ק֥וֹל הֲמֻלָּ֖ה כְּק֣וֹל מַחֲנֶ֑ה בְּעׇמְדָ֖ם תְּרַפֶּ֥ינָה כַנְפֵיהֶֽן׃


And when they moved, I heard the noise of their wings, like the noise of great waters, like the voice of the Almighty, the noise of a tumult, like the noise of a host: when they stood still, they let down their wings.


###### Ezekiel 1:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/1:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/1:25)


וַיְהִי־ק֕וֹל מֵעַ֕ל לָרָקִ֖יעַ אֲשֶׁ֣ר עַל־רֹאשָׁ֑ם בְּעׇמְדָ֖ם תְּרַפֶּ֥ינָה כַנְפֵיהֶֽן׃


And there was a voice from above the firmament that was over their heads; when they stood still, they let down their wings.


###### Ezekiel 1:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/1:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/1:26)


וּמִמַּ֗עַל לָרָקִ֙יעַ֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר עַל־רֹאשָׁ֔ם כְּמַרְאֵ֥ה אֶבֶן־סַפִּ֖יר דְּמ֣וּת כִּסֵּ֑א וְעַל֙ דְּמ֣וּת הַכִּסֵּ֔א דְּמ֞וּת כְּמַרְאֵ֥ה אָדָ֛ם עָלָ֖יו מִלְמָֽעְלָה׃


And above the firmament that was over their heads was the likeness of a throne, in appearance like a sapphire stone: and upon the likeness of the throne was the likeness as the appearance of a man above upon it.


###### Ezekiel 1:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/1:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/1:27)


וָאֵ֣רֶא**׀** כְּעֵ֣ין חַשְׁמַ֗ל כְּמַרְאֵה־אֵ֤שׁ בֵּֽית־לָהּ֙ סָבִ֔יב מִמַּרְאֵ֥ה מׇתְנָ֖יו וּלְמָ֑עְלָה וּמִמַּרְאֵ֤ה מׇתְנָיו֙ וּלְמַ֔טָּה רָאִ֙יתִי֙ כְּמַרְאֵה־אֵ֔שׁ וְנֹ֥גַֽהּ ל֖וֹ סָבִֽיב׃


And I saw something like the colour of electrum, like the appearance of fire round about enclosing it; from what appeared to be his loins upward, and from what appeared to be his loins downward, I saw what appeared to be fire, and there was a brightness round about him.


###### Ezekiel 1:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/1:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/1:28)


כְּמַרְאֵ֣ה הַקֶּ֡שֶׁת אֲשֶׁר֩ יִהְיֶ֨ה בֶעָנָ֜ן בְּי֣וֹם הַגֶּ֗שֶׁם כֵּ֣ן מַרְאֵ֤ה הַנֹּ֙גַהּ֙ סָבִ֔יב ה֕וּא מַרְאֵ֖ה דְּמ֣וּת כְּבוֹד־יְהֹוָ֑ה וָֽאֶרְאֶה֙ וָאֶפֹּ֣ל עַל־פָּנַ֔י וָאֶשְׁמַ֖ע ק֥וֹל מְדַבֵּֽר׃{פ}


As the appearance of the bow that is in the cloud in the day of rain, so was the appearance of the brightness round about. This was the appearance of the likeness of the glory of the Lord. And when I saw it, I fell upon my face, and I heard the voice of one that spoke.



## Chapter 2

###### Ezekiel 2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/2:1)


וַיֹּ֖אמֶר אֵלָ֑י בֶּן־אָדָם֙ עֲמֹ֣ד עַל־רַגְלֶ֔יךָ וַאֲדַבֵּ֖ר אֹתָֽךְ׃


And he said to me, Son of man, stand upon thy feet, and I will speak to thee.


###### Ezekiel 2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/2:2)


וַתָּ֧בֹא בִ֣י ר֗וּחַ כַּֽאֲשֶׁר֙ דִּבֶּ֣ר אֵלַ֔י וַתַּעֲמִדֵ֖נִי עַל־רַגְלָ֑י וָאֶשְׁמַ֕ע אֵ֖ת מִדַּבֵּ֥ר אֵלָֽי׃{פ}


And a spirit entered into me when he spoke to me, and set me upon my feet, and I heard him that spoke to me.


###### Ezekiel 2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/2:3)


וַיֹּ֣אמֶר אֵלַ֗י בֶּן־אָדָם֙ שׁוֹלֵ֨חַ אֲנִ֤י אֽוֹתְךָ֙ אֶל־בְּנֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל אֶל־גּוֹיִ֥ם הַמּוֹרְדִ֖ים אֲשֶׁ֣ר מָרְדוּ־בִ֑י הֵ֤מָּה וַֽאֲבוֹתָם֙ פָּ֣שְׁעוּ בִ֔י עַד־עֶ֖צֶם הַיּ֥וֹם הַזֶּֽה׃


And he said to me, Son of man, I sent thee to the children of Yisra᾽el, to rebellious tribes that have rebelled against me: they and their fathers have transgressed against me, to this very day.


###### Ezekiel 2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/2:4)


וְהַבָּנִ֗ים קְשֵׁ֤י פָנִים֙ וְחִזְקֵי־לֵ֔ב אֲנִ֛י שׁוֹלֵ֥חַ אוֹתְךָ֖ אֲלֵיהֶ֑ם וְאָמַרְתָּ֣ אֲלֵיהֶ֔ם כֹּ֥ה אָמַ֖ר אֲדֹנָ֥י יֱהֹוִֽה׃


For the children are defiant and stiffhearted. I do send thee to them; and thou shalt say to them, Thus says the Lord God.


###### Ezekiel 2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/2:5)


וְהֵ֙מָּה֙ אִם־יִשְׁמְע֣וּ וְאִם־יֶחְדָּ֔לוּ כִּ֛י בֵּ֥ית מְרִ֖י הֵ֑מָּה וְיָ֣דְע֔וּ כִּ֥י נָבִ֖יא הָיָ֥ה בְתוֹכָֽם׃{פ}


And they, whether they will hear, or whether they will refuse to hear, (for they are a rebellious house,) yet they shall know that there has been a prophet among them.


###### Ezekiel 2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/2:6)


וְאַתָּ֣ה בֶן־אָ֠דָ֠ם אַל־תִּירָ֨א מֵהֶ֜ם וּמִדִּבְרֵיהֶ֣ם אַל־תִּירָ֗א כִּ֣י סָרָבִ֤ים וְסַלּוֹנִים֙ אוֹתָ֔ךְ וְאֶל־עַקְרַבִּ֖ים אַתָּ֣ה יוֹשֵׁ֑ב מִדִּבְרֵיהֶ֤ם אַל־תִּירָא֙ וּמִפְּנֵיהֶ֣ם אַל־תֵּחָ֔ת כִּ֛י בֵּ֥ית מְרִ֖י הֵֽמָּה׃


And thou, son of man, be not afraid of them, neither be afraid of their words, though briers and thorns be with thee, and thou dost sit upon scorpions: be not afraid of their words, nor be dismayed at their looks, for they are a rebellious house.


###### Ezekiel 2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/2:7)


וְדִבַּרְתָּ֤ אֶת־דְּבָרַי֙ אֲלֵיהֶ֔ם אִֽם־יִשְׁמְע֖וּ וְאִם־יֶחְדָּ֑לוּ כִּ֥י מְרִ֖י הֵֽמָּה׃{פ}


And thou shalt speak my words to them, whether they will hear, or whether they will refuse to hear: for they are most rebellious.


###### Ezekiel 2:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/2:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/2:8)


וְאַתָּ֣ה בֶן־אָדָ֗ם שְׁמַע֙ אֵ֤ת אֲשֶׁר־אֲנִי֙ מְדַבֵּ֣ר אֵלֶ֔יךָ אַל־תְּהִי־מֶ֖רִי כְּבֵ֣ית הַמֶּ֑רִי פְּצֵ֣ה פִ֔יךָ וֶאֱכֹ֕ל אֵ֥ת אֲשֶׁר־אֲנִ֖י נֹתֵ֥ן אֵלֶֽיךָ׃


But thou, son of man, hear what I say to thee; Be not thou rebellious like that rebellious house: open thy mouth, and eat that which I give thee.


###### Ezekiel 2:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/2:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/2:9)


וָאֶרְאֶ֕ה וְהִנֵּה־יָ֖ד שְׁלוּחָ֣ה אֵלָ֑י וְהִנֵּה־ב֖וֹ מְגִלַּת־סֵֽפֶר׃


And when I looked, behold, a hand was sent to me; and, lo, a scroll of a book was in it;


###### Ezekiel 2:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/2:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/2:10)


וַיִּפְרֹ֤שׂ אוֹתָהּ֙ לְפָנַ֔י וְהִ֥יא כְתוּבָ֖ה פָּנִ֣ים וְאָח֑וֹר וְכָת֣וּב אֵלֶ֔יהָ קִנִ֥ים וָהֶ֖גֶה וָהִֽי׃{ס}


and he spread it before me; and it was written inside and outside: and in it was written lamentations, and mourning, and woe.



## Chapter 3

###### Ezekiel 3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/3:1)


וַיֹּ֣אמֶר אֵלַ֔י בֶּן־אָדָ֕ם אֵ֥ת אֲשֶׁר־תִּמְצָ֖א אֱכ֑וֹל אֱכוֹל֙ אֶת־הַמְּגִלָּ֣ה הַזֹּ֔את וְלֵ֥ךְ דַּבֵּ֖ר אֶל־בֵּ֥ית יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


And he said to me, Son of man, eat what thou findest; eat this scroll, and go and speak to the house of Yisra᾽el.


###### Ezekiel 3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/3:2)


וָאֶפְתַּ֖ח אֶת־פִּ֑י וַיַּ֣אֲכִילֵ֔נִי אֵ֖ת הַמְּגִלָּ֥ה הַזֹּֽאת׃


So I opened my mouth, and he caused me to eat that scroll.


###### Ezekiel 3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/3:3)


וַיֹּ֣אמֶר אֵלַ֗י בֶּן־אָדָם֙ בִּטְנְךָ֤ תַֽאֲכֵל֙ וּמֵעֶ֣יךָ תְמַלֵּ֔א אֵ֚ת הַמְּגִלָּ֣ה הַזֹּ֔את אֲשֶׁ֥ר אֲנִ֖י נֹתֵ֣ן אֵלֶ֑יךָ וָאֹ֣כְלָ֔ה וַתְּהִ֥י בְּפִ֖י כִּדְבַ֥שׁ לְמָתֽוֹק׃{פ}


And he said to me, Son of man, feed thy belly, and fill thy bowels with this scroll that I give thee. Then I did eat it; and it became as sweet as honey in my mouth.


###### Ezekiel 3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/3:4)


וַיֹּ֖אמֶר אֵלָ֑י בֶּן־אָדָ֗ם לֶךְ־בֹּא֙ אֶל־בֵּ֣ית יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל וְדִבַּרְתָּ֥ בִדְבָרַ֖י אֲלֵיהֶֽם׃


And he said to me, Son of man, go, get thee to the house of Yisra᾽el, and speak with my words to them.


###### Ezekiel 3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/3:5)


כִּ֡י לֹא֩ אֶל־עַ֨ם עִמְקֵ֥י שָׂפָ֛ה וְכִבְדֵ֥י לָשׁ֖וֹן אַתָּ֣ה שָׁל֑וּחַ אֶל־בֵּ֖ית יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


For thou art not sent to a people of a strange speech and of a hard language, but to the house of Yisra᾽el;


###### Ezekiel 3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/3:6)


לֹ֣א**׀** אֶל־עַמִּ֣ים רַבִּ֗ים עִמְקֵ֤י שָׂפָה֙ וְכִבְדֵ֣י לָשׁ֔וֹן אֲשֶׁ֥ר לֹא־תִשְׁמַ֖ע דִּבְרֵיהֶ֑ם אִם־לֹ֤א אֲלֵיהֶם֙ שְׁלַחְתִּ֔יךָ הֵ֖מָּה יִשְׁמְע֥וּ אֵלֶֽיךָ׃


not to many peoples of a strange speech and of a hard language, whose words thou canst not understand. Surely, had I sent thee to them, they would have hearkened to thee.


###### Ezekiel 3:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/3:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/3:7)


וּבֵ֣ית יִשְׂרָאֵ֗ל לֹ֤א יֹאבוּ֙ לִשְׁמֹ֣עַ אֵלֶ֔יךָ כִּי־אֵינָ֥ם אֹבִ֖ים לִשְׁמֹ֣עַ אֵלָ֑י כִּ֚י כׇּל־בֵּ֣ית יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל חִזְקֵי־מֵ֥צַח וּקְשֵׁי־לֵ֖ב הֵֽמָּה׃


But the house of Yisra᾽el will not hearken to thee; for they will not hearken to me: for all the house of Yisra᾽el are defiant and hardhearted.


###### Ezekiel 3:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/3:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/3:8)


הִנֵּ֨ה נָתַ֧תִּי אֶת־פָּנֶ֛יךָ חֲזָקִ֖ים לְעֻמַּ֣ת פְּנֵיהֶ֑ם וְאֶֽת־מִצְחֲךָ֥ חָזָ֖ק לְעֻמַּ֥ת מִצְחָֽם׃


Behold, I have made thy face hard against their faces, and forehead strong against their foreheads.


###### Ezekiel 3:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/3:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/3:9)


כְּשָׁמִ֛יר חָזָ֥ק מִצֹּ֖ר נָתַ֣תִּי מִצְחֶ֑ךָ לֹֽא־תִירָ֤א אוֹתָם֙ וְלֹא־תֵחַ֣ת מִפְּנֵיהֶ֔ם כִּ֛י בֵּ֥ית מְרִ֖י הֵֽמָּה׃{פ}


Like adamant harder than flint have I made thy forehead: fear them not, neither be downcast at their looks, though they are a rebellious house.


###### Ezekiel 3:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/3:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/3:10)


וַיֹּ֖אמֶר אֵלָ֑י בֶּן־אָדָ֕ם אֶת־כׇּל־דְּבָרַי֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר אֲדַבֵּ֣ר אֵלֶ֔יךָ קַ֥ח בִּֽלְבָבְךָ֖ וּבְאׇזְנֶ֥יךָ שְּׁמָֽע׃


Moreover he said to me, Son of man, all my words that I shall speak to thee take to thy heart, and hear with thy ears.


###### Ezekiel 3:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/3:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/3:11)


וְלֵ֨ךְ בֹּ֤א אֶל־הַגּוֹלָה֙ אֶל־בְּנֵ֣י עַמֶּ֔ךָ וְדִבַּרְתָּ֤ אֲלֵיהֶם֙ וְאָמַרְתָּ֣ אֲלֵיהֶ֔ם כֹּ֥ה אָמַ֖ר אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֑ה אִֽם־יִשְׁמְע֖וּ וְאִם־יֶחְדָּֽלוּ׃


And go, get thee to the exiles, to the children of thy people, and speak to them, and tell them. Thus says the Lord God; whether they will hear, or whether they will refuse to hear.


###### Ezekiel 3:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/3:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/3:12)


וַתִּשָּׂאֵ֣נִי ר֔וּחַ וָאֶשְׁמַ֣ע אַחֲרַ֔י ק֖וֹל רַ֣עַשׁ גָּד֑וֹל בָּר֥וּךְ כְּבוֹד־יְהֹוָ֖ה מִמְּקוֹמֽוֹ׃


Then a spirit took me up, and I heard behind me a voice of a great rushing, saying, Blessed be the glory of the Lord from his place;


###### Ezekiel 3:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/3:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/3:13)


וְק֣וֹל**׀** כַּנְפֵ֣י הַחַיּ֗וֹת מַשִּׁיקוֹת֙ אִשָּׁ֣ה אֶל־אֲחוֹתָ֔הּ וְק֥וֹל הָאוֹפַנִּ֖ים לְעֻמָּתָ֑ם וְק֖וֹל רַ֥עַשׁ גָּדֽוֹל׃


also the noise of the wings of the living creatures as they touched one another, and the noise of the wheels along with them, and a noise of a great rushing,


###### Ezekiel 3:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/3:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/3:14)


וְר֥וּחַ נְשָׂאַ֖תְנִי וַתִּקָּחֵ֑נִי וָאֵלֵ֥ךְ מַר֙ בַּחֲמַ֣ת רוּחִ֔י וְיַד־יְהֹוָ֥ה עָלַ֖י חָזָֽקָה׃


so a spirit lifted me up, and took me away, and I went in bitterness, in the heat of my spirit; and the hand of the Lord was strong upon me.


###### Ezekiel 3:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/3:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/3:15)


וָאָב֨וֹא אֶל־הַגּוֹלָ֜ה תֵּ֣ל אָ֠בִ֠יב הַיֹּשְׁבִ֤ים אֶֽל־נְהַר־כְּבָר֙ (ואשר) [וָאֵשֵׁ֔ב] הֵ֖מָּה יוֹשְׁבִ֣ים שָׁ֑ם וָאֵשֵׁ֥ב שָׁ֛ם שִׁבְעַ֥ת יָמִ֖ים מַשְׁמִ֥ים בְּתוֹכָֽם׃


Then I came to the exiles at Tel-aviv, that dwelt by the river Kevar, and I sat where they sat, and remained there overwhelmed among them for seven days.


###### Ezekiel 3:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/3:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/3:16)


וַיְהִ֕י מִקְצֵ֖ה שִׁבְעַ֣ת יָמִ֑ים{פ}
וַיְהִ֥י דְבַר־יְהֹוָ֖ה אֵלַ֥י לֵאמֹֽר׃


And it came to pass at the end of seven days that the word of the Lord came to me, saying,


###### Ezekiel 3:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/3:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/3:17)


בֶּן־אָדָ֕ם צֹפֶ֥ה נְתַתִּ֖יךָ לְבֵ֣ית יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל וְשָׁמַעְתָּ֤ מִפִּי֙ דָּבָ֔ר וְהִזְהַרְתָּ֥ אוֹתָ֖ם מִמֶּֽנִּי׃


Son of man, I have made thee a watchman to the house of Yisra᾽el: and when thou shalt hear a word at my mouth, thou shalt give them warning from me.


###### Ezekiel 3:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/3:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/3:18)


בְּאׇמְרִ֤י לָרָשָׁע֙ מ֣וֹת תָּמ֔וּת וְלֹ֣א הִזְהַרְתּ֗וֹ וְלֹ֥א דִבַּ֛רְתָּ לְהַזְהִ֥יר רָשָׁ֛ע מִדַּרְכּ֥וֹ הָרְשָׁעָ֖ה לְחַיֹּת֑וֹ ה֤וּא רָשָׁע֙ בַּעֲוֺנ֣וֹ יָמ֔וּת וְדָמ֖וֹ מִיָּדְךָ֥ אֲבַקֵּֽשׁ׃


When I say to the wicked man, Thou shalt surely die; and thou givest him not warning, nor speakest to warn the wicked from his wicked way, to save his life; the same wicked man shall die in his iniquity; but his blood will I require at thy hand.


###### Ezekiel 3:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/3:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/3:19)


וְאַתָּה֙ כִּי־הִזְהַ֣רְתָּ רָשָׁ֔ע וְלֹא־שָׁב֙ מֵֽרִשְׁע֔וֹ וּמִדַּרְכּ֖וֹ הָרְשָׁעָ֑ה ה֚וּא בַּעֲוֺנ֣וֹ יָמ֔וּת וְאַתָּ֖ה אֶֽת־נַפְשְׁךָ֥ הִצַּֽלְתָּ׃


Yet if thou warn the wicked man, and he turn not from his wickedness, nor from his wicked way, he shall die in his iniquity; but thou hast saved thy soul.


###### Ezekiel 3:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/3:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/3:20)


וּבְשׁ֨וּב צַדִּ֤יק מִצִּדְקוֹ֙ וְעָ֣שָׂה עָ֔וֶל וְנָתַתִּ֥י מִכְשׁ֛וֹל לְפָנָ֖יו ה֣וּא יָמ֑וּת כִּ֣י לֹ֤א הִזְהַרְתּוֹ֙ בְּחַטָּאת֣וֹ יָמ֔וּת וְלֹ֣א תִזָּכַ֗רְןָ צִדְקֹתָו֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר עָשָׂ֔ה וְדָמ֖וֹ מִיָּדְךָ֥ אֲבַקֵּֽשׁ׃


Again, when a righteous man turns from his righteousness, and commits iniquity, and I lay a stumblingblock before him he shall die: because thou hast not given him warning, he shall die in his sin, and his righteousness which he has done shall not be remembered; but his blood will I require at thy hand.


###### Ezekiel 3:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/3:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/3:21)


וְאַתָּ֞ה כִּ֧י הִזְהַרְתּ֣וֹ צַדִּ֗יק לְבִלְתִּ֥י חֲטֹ֛א צַדִּ֖יק וְה֣וּא לֹֽא־חָטָ֑א חָי֤וֹ יִֽחְיֶה֙ כִּ֣י נִזְהָ֔ר וְאַתָּ֖ה אֶֽת־נַפְשְׁךָ֥ הִצַּֽלְתָּ׃{פ}


Nevertheless if thou warn the righteous man, that the righteous sin not, and he does not sin, then he shall surely live, because he is warned; and thou hast saved thy soul.


###### Ezekiel 3:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/3:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/3:22)


וַתְּהִ֥י עָלַ֛י שָׁ֖ם יַד־יְהֹוָ֑ה וַיֹּ֣אמֶר אֵלַ֗י ק֥וּם צֵא֙ אֶל־הַבִּקְעָ֔ה וְשָׁ֖ם אֲדַבֵּ֥ר אוֹתָֽךְ׃


And the hand of the Lord was there upon me; and he said to me, Arise, go out into the plain, and I will talk with thee there.


###### Ezekiel 3:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/3:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/3:23)


וָאָקוּם֮ וָאֵצֵ֣א אֶל־הַבִּקְעָה֒ וְהִנֵּה־שָׁ֤ם כְּבוֹד־יְהֹוָה֙ עֹמֵ֔ד כַּכָּב֕וֹד אֲשֶׁ֥ר רָאִ֖יתִי עַל־נְהַר־כְּבָ֑ר וָאֶפֹּ֖ל עַל־פָּנָֽי׃


Then I arose, and went out into the plain: and, behold, the glory of the Lord stood there, as the glory which I saw by the river Kevar: and I fell on my face.


###### Ezekiel 3:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/3:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/3:24)


וַתָּבֹא־בִ֣י ר֔וּחַ וַתַּעֲמִדֵ֖נִי עַל־רַגְלָ֑י וַיְדַבֵּ֤ר אֹתִי֙ וַיֹּ֣אמֶר אֵלַ֔י בֹּ֥א הִסָּגֵ֖ר בְּת֥וֹךְ בֵּיתֶֽךָ׃


Then a spirit entered into me, and set me upon my feet; and he spoke with me, and said to me, Go, shut thyself within thy house.


###### Ezekiel 3:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/3:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/3:25)


וְאַתָּ֣ה בֶן־אָדָ֗ם הִנֵּ֨ה נָתְנ֤וּ עָלֶ֙יךָ֙ עֲבוֹתִ֔ים וַאֲסָר֖וּךָ בָּהֶ֑ם וְלֹ֥א תֵצֵ֖א בְּתוֹכָֽם׃


But thou, O son of man, behold, they shall put cords upon thee, and shall bind thee with them, and thou shalt not go out among them:


###### Ezekiel 3:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/3:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/3:26)


וּלְשֽׁוֹנְךָ֙ אַדְבִּ֣יק אֶל־חִכֶּ֔ךָ וְנֶֽאֱלַ֔מְתָּ וְלֹא־תִהְיֶ֥ה לָהֶ֖ם לְאִ֣ישׁ מוֹכִ֑יחַ כִּ֛י בֵּ֥ית מְרִ֖י הֵֽמָּה׃


and I will make thy tongue cleave to the roof of thy mouth, that thou shalt be dumb, and shalt not be to them a reprover: for they are a rebellious house.


###### Ezekiel 3:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/3:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/3:27)


וּֽבְדַבְּרִ֤י אֽוֹתְךָ֙ אֶפְתַּ֣ח אֶת־פִּ֔יךָ וְאָמַרְתָּ֣ אֲלֵיהֶ֔ם כֹּ֥ה אָמַ֖ר אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֑ה הַשֹּׁמֵ֤עַ׀יִשְׁמָע֙ וְהֶחָדֵ֣ל׀יֶחְדָּ֔ל כִּ֛י בֵּ֥ית מְרִ֖י הֵֽמָּה׃{פ}


But when I speak with thee, I will open thy mouth, and thou shalt say to them, Thus says the Lord God; He that hears, let him hear; and he that refuses to hear, let him refuse: for they are a rebellious house.



## Chapter 4

###### Ezekiel 4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/4:1)


וְאַתָּ֤ה בֶן־אָדָם֙ קַח־לְךָ֣ לְבֵנָ֔ה וְנָתַתָּ֥ה אוֹתָ֖הּ לְפָנֶ֑יךָ וְחַקּוֹתָ֥ עָלֶ֛יהָ עִ֖יר אֶת־יְרוּשָׁלָֽ͏ִם׃


Thou also, son of man, take thee a brick, and lay it before thee, and trace upon it a city, namely, Yerushalayim:


###### Ezekiel 4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/4:2)


וְנָתַתָּ֨ה עָלֶ֜יהָ מָצ֗וֹר וּבָנִ֤יתָ עָלֶ֙יהָ֙ דָּיֵ֔ק וְשָׁפַכְתָּ֥ עָלֶ֖יהָ סֹלְלָ֑ה וְנָתַתָּ֨ה עָלֶ֧יהָ מַחֲנ֛וֹת וְשִׂים־עָלֶ֥יהָ כָּרִ֖ים סָבִֽיב׃


and lay siege against it, and build a siege wall against it, and cast a mound against it; set camps also against it, and set battering rams against it round about.


###### Ezekiel 4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/4:3)


וְאַתָּ֤ה קַח־לְךָ֙ מַחֲבַ֣ת בַּרְזֶ֔ל וְנָתַתָּ֤ה אוֹתָהּ֙ קִ֣יר בַּרְזֶ֔ל בֵּינְךָ֖ וּבֵ֣ין הָעִ֑יר וַהֲכִינֹתָה֩ אֶת־פָּנֶ֨יךָ אֵלֶ֜יהָ וְהָיְתָ֤ה בַמָּצוֹר֙ וְצַרְתָּ֣ עָלֶ֔יהָ א֥וֹת הִ֖יא לְבֵ֥ית יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃{פ}


And take thou an iron pan, and set it for a wall of iron between thee and the city: and set thy face towards it, and it shall be besieged, and thou shalt lay siege against it. This shall be a sign to the house of Yisra᾽el.


###### Ezekiel 4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/4:4)


וְאַתָּ֤ה שְׁכַב֙ עַל־צִדְּךָ֣ הַשְּׂמָאלִ֔י וְשַׂמְתָּ֛ אֶת־עֲוֺ֥ן בֵּֽית־יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל עָלָ֑יו מִסְפַּ֤ר הַיָּמִים֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר תִּשְׁכַּ֣ב עָלָ֔יו תִּשָּׂ֖א אֶת־עֲוֺנָֽם׃


Moreover lie thou upon thy left side, and lay the iniquity of the house of Yisra᾽el upon it: according to the number of the days that thou shalt lie upon it thou shalt bear their iniquity.


###### Ezekiel 4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/4:5)


וַאֲנִ֗י נָתַ֤תִּֽי לְךָ֙ אֶת־שְׁנֵ֣י עֲוֺנָ֔ם לְמִסְפַּ֣ר יָמִ֔ים שְׁלֹשׁ־מֵא֥וֹת וְתִשְׁעִ֖ים י֑וֹם וְנָשָׂ֖אתָ עֲוֺ֥ן בֵּֽית־יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


For I have laid upon thee the years of their iniquity, by an equivalent number of days, three hundred and ninety days: so shalt thou bear the iniquity of the house of Yisra᾽el.


###### Ezekiel 4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/4:6)


וְכִלִּיתָ֣ אֶת־אֵ֗לֶּה וְשָׁ֨כַבְתָּ֜ עַֽל־צִדְּךָ֤ (הימיני) [הַיְמָנִי֙] שֵׁנִ֔ית וְנָשָׂ֖אתָ אֶת־עֲוֺ֣ן בֵּית־יְהוּדָ֑ה אַרְבָּעִ֣ים י֔וֹם י֧וֹם לַשָּׁנָ֛ה י֥וֹם לַשָּׁנָ֖ה נְתַתִּ֥יו לָֽךְ׃


And when thou hast accomplished them, lie again on thy right side, and thou shalt bear the iniquity of the house of Yehuda: I have appointed thee forty days, each day for a year.


###### Ezekiel 4:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/4:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/4:7)


וְאֶל־מְצ֤וֹר יְרוּשָׁלַ֙͏ִם֙ תָּכִ֣ין פָּנֶ֔יךָ וּֽזְרֹעֲךָ֖ חֲשׂוּפָ֑ה וְנִבֵּאתָ֖ עָלֶֽיהָ׃


Therefore thou shalt set thy face towards the siege of Yerushalayim, and thy arm shall be uncovered, and thou shalt prophesy against it.


###### Ezekiel 4:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/4:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/4:8)


וְהִנֵּ֛ה נָתַ֥תִּי עָלֶ֖יךָ עֲבוֹתִ֑ים וְלֹֽא־תֵהָפֵ֤ךְ מִֽצִּדְּךָ֙ אֶל־צִדֶּ֔ךָ עַד־כַּלּוֹתְךָ֖ יְמֵ֥י מְצוּרֶֽךָ׃


And, behold, I will lay cords upon thee, so that thou shalt not be able to turn thyself from one side to another, till thou hast completed the days of thy siege.


###### Ezekiel 4:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/4:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/4:9)


וְאַתָּ֣ה קַח־לְךָ֡ חִטִּ֡ין וּ֠שְׂעֹרִ֠ים וּפ֨וֹל וַעֲדָשִׁ֜ים וְדֹ֣חַן וְכֻסְּמִ֗ים וְנָתַתָּ֤ה אוֹתָם֙ בִּכְלִ֣י אֶחָ֔ד וְעָשִׂ֧יתָ אוֹתָ֛ם לְךָ֖ לְלָ֑חֶם מִסְפַּ֨ר הַיָּמִ֜ים אֲשֶׁר־אַתָּ֣ה**׀** שׁוֹכֵ֣ב עַֽל־צִדְּךָ֗ שְׁלֹשׁ־מֵא֧וֹת וְתִשְׁעִ֛ים י֖וֹם תֹּאכְלֶֽנּוּ׃


Take thou also wheat, and barley, and beans, and lentils, and millet, and spelt, and put them in one vessel, and make thee bread from them; for the number of the days that thou shalt lie upon thy side, three hundred and ninety days shalt thou eat it.


###### Ezekiel 4:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/4:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/4:10)


וּמַאֲכָֽלְךָ֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר תֹּאכְלֶ֔נּוּ בְּמִשְׁק֕וֹל עֶשְׂרִ֥ים שֶׁ֖קֶל לַיּ֑וֹם מֵעֵ֥ת עַד־עֵ֖ת תֹּאכְלֶֽנּוּ׃


And thy food which thou shalt eat shall be by weight, twenty shekels a day: once a day shalt thou eat it.


###### Ezekiel 4:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/4:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/4:11)


וּמַ֛יִם בִּמְשׂוּרָ֥ה תִשְׁתֶּ֖ה שִׁשִּׁ֣ית הַהִ֑ין מֵעֵ֥ת עַד־עֵ֖ת תִּשְׁתֶּֽה׃


Thou shalt drink also water by measure, the sixth part of a hin: once a day shalt thou drink.


###### Ezekiel 4:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/4:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/4:12)


וְעֻגַ֥ת שְׂעֹרִ֖ים תֹּאכְלֶ֑נָּה וְהִ֗יא בְּגֶֽלְלֵי֙ צֵאַ֣ת הָֽאָדָ֔ם תְּעֻגֶ֖נָה לְעֵינֵיהֶֽם׃{ס}


And thou shalt eat it like barley cakes, and thou shalt bake it with human excrement, in their sight.


###### Ezekiel 4:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/4:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/4:13)


וַיֹּ֣אמֶר יְהֹוָ֔ה כָּ֣כָה יֹאכְל֧וּ בְנֵי־יִשְׂרָאֵ֛ל אֶת־לַחְמָ֖ם טָמֵ֑א בַּגּוֹיִ֕ם אֲשֶׁ֥ר אַדִּיחֵ֖ם שָֽׁם׃


And the Lord said, Thus shall the children of Yisra᾽el eat their bread, unclean, among the nations, whither I will drive them.


###### Ezekiel 4:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/4:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/4:14)


וָאֹמַ֗ר אֲהָהּ֙ אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֔ה הִנֵּ֥ה נַפְשִׁ֖י לֹ֣א מְטֻמָּאָ֑ה וּנְבֵלָ֨ה וּטְרֵפָ֤ה לֹא־אָכַ֙לְתִּי֙ מִנְּעוּרַ֣י וְעַד־עַ֔תָּה וְלֹא־בָ֥א בְּפִ֖י בְּשַׂ֥ר פִּגּֽוּל׃{ס}


Then I said, Ah Lord God, behold my soul has never been polluted: for from my youth up even till now I have not eaten that which dies of itself, or is torn by beasts, nor did loathsome meat ever come into my mouth.


###### Ezekiel 4:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/4:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/4:15)


וַיֹּ֣אמֶר אֵלַ֔י רְאֵ֗ה נָתַ֤תִּֽי לְךָ֙ אֶת־[צְפִיעֵ֣י] (צפועי) הַבָּקָ֔ר תַּ֖חַת גֶּלְלֵ֣י הָאָדָ֑ם וְעָשִׂ֥יתָ אֶֽת־לַחְמְךָ֖ עֲלֵיהֶֽם׃{ס}


Then he said to me, Lo, I have given thee cow’s dung instead of human dung, and thou shalt pre­pare thy bread upon that.


###### Ezekiel 4:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/4:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/4:16)


וַיֹּ֣אמֶר אֵלַ֗י בֶּן־אָדָם֙ הִנְנִ֨י שֹׁבֵ֤ר מַטֵּֽה־לֶ֙חֶם֙ בִּיר֣וּשָׁלַ֔͏ִם וְאָכְלוּ־לֶ֥חֶם בְּמִשְׁקָ֖ל וּבִדְאָגָ֑ה וּמַ֕יִם בִּמְשׂוּרָ֥ה וּבְשִׁמָּמ֖וֹן יִשְׁתּֽוּ׃


Moreover he said to me, Son of man, behold, I will break the staff of bread in Yerushalayim: and they shall eat bread by weight, and with anxiety; and they shall drink water by measure, and in appalment:


###### Ezekiel 4:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/4:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/4:17)


לְמַ֥עַן יַחְסְר֖וּ לֶ֣חֶם וָמָ֑יִם וְנָשַׁ֙מּוּ֙ אִ֣ישׁ וְאָחִ֔יו וְנָמַ֖קּוּ בַּעֲוֺנָֽם׃{פ}


that they may lack bread and water, and be appalled with one another, and waste away for their iniquity.



## Chapter 5

###### Ezekiel 5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/5:1)


וְאַתָּ֨ה בֶן־אָדָ֜ם קַח־לְךָ֣**׀** חֶ֣רֶב חַדָּ֗ה תַּ֤עַר הַגַּלָּבִים֙ תִּקָּחֶ֣נָּה לָּ֔ךְ וְהַעֲבַרְתָּ֥ עַל־רֹאשְׁךָ֖ וְעַל־זְקָנֶ֑ךָ וְלָקַחְתָּ֥ לְךָ֛ מֹאזְנֵ֥י מִשְׁקָ֖ל וְחִלַּקְתָּֽם׃


And thou, Son of man, take a sharp knife; a barber’s razor take for thyself; and pass it over thy head, and over thy beard: then take balances to weigh, and divide the hair.


###### Ezekiel 5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/5:2)


שְׁלִשִׁ֗ית בָּא֤וּר תַּבְעִיר֙ בְּת֣וֹךְ הָעִ֔יר כִּמְלֹ֖את יְמֵ֣י הַמָּצ֑וֹר וְלָקַחְתָּ֣ אֶת־הַשְּׁלִשִׁ֗ית תַּכֶּ֤ה בַחֶ֙רֶב֙ סְבִ֣יבוֹתֶ֔יהָ וְהַשְּׁלִשִׁית֙ תִּזְרֶ֣ה לָר֔וּחַ וְחֶ֖רֶב אָרִ֥יק אַחֲרֵיהֶֽם׃


Thou shalt burn with fire a third part in the midst of the city, when the days of the siege are fulfilled: and thou shalt take a third part, and smite about it with the sword: and a third part thou shalt scatter to the wind; and I will draw out a sword after them.


###### Ezekiel 5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/5:3)


וְלָקַחְתָּ֥ מִשָּׁ֖ם מְעַ֣ט בְּמִסְפָּ֑ר וְצַרְתָּ֥ אוֹתָ֖ם בִּכְנָפֶֽיךָ׃


Thou shalt also take from these a small number, and bind them in thy skirts.


###### Ezekiel 5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/5:4)


וּמֵהֶם֙ ע֣וֹד תִּקָּ֔ח וְהִשְׁלַכְתָּ֤ אוֹתָם֙ אֶל־תּ֣וֹךְ הָאֵ֔שׁ וְשָׂרַפְתָּ֥ אֹתָ֖ם בָּאֵ֑שׁ מִמֶּ֥נּוּ תֵֽצֵא־אֵ֖שׁ אֶל־כׇּל־בֵּ֥ית יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃{פ}


Then take more of them, and cast them into the midst of the fire, and burn them in the fire; from there a fire shall come forth into all the house of Yisra᾽el.


###### Ezekiel 5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/5:5)


כֹּ֤ה אָמַר֙ אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֔ה זֹ֚את יְר֣וּשָׁלַ֔͏ִם בְּת֥וֹךְ הַגּוֹיִ֖ם שַׂמְתִּ֑יהָ וּסְבִיבוֹתֶ֖יהָ אֲרָצֽוֹת׃


Thus says the Lord God; This is Yerushalayim: I have set it in the midst of the nations; and countries are round about her.


###### Ezekiel 5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/5:6)


וַתֶּ֨מֶר אֶת־מִשְׁפָּטַ֤י לְרִשְׁעָה֙ מִן־הַגּוֹיִ֔ם וְאֶ֨ת־חֻקּוֹתַ֔י מִן־הָאֲרָצ֖וֹת אֲשֶׁ֣ר סְבִיבוֹתֶ֑יהָ כִּ֤י בְמִשְׁפָּטַי֙ מָאָ֔סוּ וְחֻקּוֹתַ֖י לֹא־הָלְכ֥וּ בָהֶֽם׃{ס}


And she has rebelled against my judgments more wickedly than the nations, and against my statutes more than the countries that are round her: for they have refused my judgments, and as for my statutes, they have not followed them.


###### Ezekiel 5:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/5:7)


לָכֵ֞ן כֹּה־אָמַ֣ר**׀** אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֗ה יַ֤עַן הֲמׇנְכֶם֙ מִן־הַגּוֹיִם֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר סְבִיבוֹתֵיכֶ֔ם בְּחֻקּוֹתַי֙ לֹ֣א הֲלַכְתֶּ֔ם וְאֶת־מִשְׁפָּטַ֖י לֹ֣א עֲשִׂיתֶ֑ם וּֽכְמִשְׁפְּטֵ֧י הַגּוֹיִ֛ם אֲשֶׁ֥ר סְבִיבוֹתֵיכֶ֖ם לֹ֥א עֲשִׂיתֶֽם׃


Therefore thus says the Lord God; Because you have been more turbulent than the nations that are round about you, and have not walked in my statutes, nor have you kept my judgments, nor have you done according to the practices of the nations that are round about you;


###### Ezekiel 5:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/5:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/5:8)


לָכֵ֗ן כֹּ֤ה אָמַר֙ אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֔ה הִנְנִ֥י עָלַ֖יִךְ גַּם־אָ֑נִי וְעָשִׂ֧יתִי בְתוֹכֵ֛ךְ מִשְׁפָּטִ֖ים לְעֵינֵ֥י הַגּוֹיִֽם׃


therefore thus says the Lord God; Behold, I, even I, am against thee, and will execute judgments in the midst of thee in the sight of the nations.


###### Ezekiel 5:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/5:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/5:9)


וְעָשִׂ֣יתִי בָ֗ךְ אֵ֚ת אֲשֶׁ֣ר לֹֽא־עָשִׂ֔יתִי וְאֵ֛ת אֲשֶֽׁר־לֹא־אֶעֱשֶׂ֥ה כָמֹ֖הוּ ע֑וֹד יַ֖עַן כׇּל־תּוֹעֲבֹתָֽיִךְ׃{פ}


And I will do in thee that which I have not done, and like which I will not do again, because of all thy abominations.


###### Ezekiel 5:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/5:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/5:10)


לָכֵ֗ן אָב֞וֹת יֹאכְל֤וּ בָנִים֙ בְּתוֹכֵ֔ךְ וּבָנִ֖ים יֹאכְל֣וּ אֲבוֹתָ֑ם וְעָשִׂ֤יתִי בָךְ֙ שְׁפָטִ֔ים וְזֵרִיתִ֥י אֶת־כׇּל־שְׁאֵרִיתֵ֖ךְ לְכׇל־רֽוּחַ׃{ס}


Therefore the fathers shall eat the sons in the midst of thee, and the sons shall eat their fathers; and I will execute judgments in thee, and the whole remnant of thee will I scatter to all the winds.


###### Ezekiel 5:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/5:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/5:11)


לָכֵ֣ן חַי־אָ֗נִי נְאֻם֮ אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִה֒ אִם־לֹ֗א יַ֚עַן אֶת־מִקְדָּשִׁ֣י טִמֵּ֔את בְּכׇל־שִׁקּוּצַ֖יִךְ וּבְכׇל־תּוֹעֲבֹתָ֑יִךְ וְגַם־אֲנִ֤י אֶגְרַע֙ וְלֹא־תָח֣וֹס עֵינִ֔י וְגַם־אֲנִ֖י לֹ֥א אֶחְמֽוֹל׃


Therefore, as I live, says the Lord God; Surely, because thou hast defiled my sanctuary with all thy detestable things, and with all thy abominations, therefore will I also diminish thee; neither shall my eye spare, neither will I have any pity.


###### Ezekiel 5:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/5:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/5:12)


שְׁלִשִׁתֵ֞יךְ בַּדֶּ֣בֶר יָמ֗וּתוּ וּבָֽרָעָב֙ יִכְל֣וּ בְתוֹכֵ֔ךְ וְהַ֨שְּׁלִשִׁ֔ית בַּחֶ֖רֶב יִפְּל֣וּ סְבִיבוֹתָ֑יִךְ וְהַשְּׁלִישִׁית֙ לְכׇל־ר֣וּחַ אֱזָרֶ֔ה וְחֶ֖רֶב אָרִ֥יק אַחֲרֵיהֶֽם׃


A third part of thee shall die with the pestilence, and with famine shall they be consumed in the midst of thee: and a third part shall fall by the sword round about thee; and I will scatter a third part to all the winds, and I will draw out a sword after them.


###### Ezekiel 5:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/5:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/5:13)


וְכָלָ֣ה אַפִּ֗י וַהֲנִחוֹתִ֧י חֲמָתִ֛י בָּ֖ם וְהִנֶּחָ֑מְתִּי וְֽיָדְע֞וּ כִּֽי־אֲנִ֣י יְהֹוָ֗ה דִּבַּ֙רְתִּי֙ בְּקִנְאָתִ֔י בְּכַלּוֹתִ֥י חֲמָתִ֖י בָּֽם׃


Thus shall my anger spend itself, and I will relieve my fury upon them, and I will be comforted: and they shall know that I the Lord have spoken it in my zeal, when I have spent my fury in them.


###### Ezekiel 5:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/5:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/5:14)


וְאֶתְּנֵךְ֙ לְחׇרְבָּ֣ה וּלְחֶרְפָּ֔ה בַּגּוֹיִ֖ם אֲשֶׁ֣ר סְבִיבוֹתָ֑יִךְ לְעֵינֵ֖י כׇּל־עוֹבֵֽר׃


Moreover I will make thee a desolation, and a reproach among the nations that are round about thee, in the sight of all that pass by.


###### Ezekiel 5:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/5:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/5:15)


וְֽהָ֨יְתָ֜ה חֶרְפָּ֤ה וּגְדוּפָה֙ מוּסָ֣ר וּמְשַׁמָּ֔ה לַגּוֹיִ֖ם אֲשֶׁ֣ר סְבִיבוֹתָ֑יִךְ בַּעֲשׂ֩וֹתִי֩ בָ֨ךְ שְׁפָטִ֜ים בְּאַ֤ף וּבְחֵמָה֙ וּבְתֹכְח֣וֹת חֵמָ֔ה אֲנִ֥י יְהֹוָ֖ה דִּבַּֽרְתִּי׃


So it shall be a reproach and a taunt, an instruction and an astonishment to the nations that are round about thee, when I shall execute judgments in thee in anger and in fury and in furious rebukes. I the Lord have spoken it.


###### Ezekiel 5:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/5:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/5:16)


בְּֽשַׁלְּחִ֡י אֶת־חִצֵּי֩ הָרָעָ֨ב הָרָעִ֤ים בָּהֶם֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר הָי֣וּ לְמַשְׁחִ֔ית אֲשֶׁר־אֲשַׁלַּ֥ח אוֹתָ֖ם לְשַׁחֶתְכֶ֑ם וְרָעָב֙ אֹסֵ֣ף עֲלֵיכֶ֔ם וְשָׁבַרְתִּ֥י לָכֶ֖ם מַטֵּה־לָֽחֶם׃


When I shall send upon them the evil arrows of famine, which shall be for destruction, which I will send to destroy you: and I will increase the famine upon you, and will break your staff of bread:


###### Ezekiel 5:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/5:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/5:17)


וְשִׁלַּחְתִּ֣י עֲ֠לֵיכֶ֠ם רָעָ֞ב וְחַיָּ֤ה רָעָה֙ וְשִׁכְּלֻ֔ךְ וְדֶ֥בֶר וָדָ֖ם יַעֲבׇר־בָּ֑ךְ וְחֶ֙רֶב֙ אָבִ֣יא עָלַ֔יִךְ אֲנִ֥י יְהֹוָ֖ה דִּבַּֽרְתִּי׃{פ}


so will I send upon you famine and evil beasts, and they shall bereave thee; and pestilence and blood shall pass through thee; and I will bring the sword upon thee. I the Lord have spoken.



## Chapter 6

###### Ezekiel 6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/6:1)


וַיְהִ֥י דְבַר־יְהֹוָ֖ה אֵלַ֥י לֵאמֹֽר׃


And the word of the Lord came to me, saying,


###### Ezekiel 6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/6:2)


בֶּן־אָדָ֕ם שִׂ֥ים פָּנֶ֖יךָ אֶל־הָרֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל וְהִנָּבֵ֖א אֲלֵיהֶֽם׃


Son of man, set thy face towards the mountains of Yisra᾽el, and prophesy against them,


###### Ezekiel 6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/6:3)


וְאָ֣מַרְתָּ֔ הָרֵי֙ יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל שִׁמְע֖וּ דְּבַר־אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֑ה כֹּה־אָמַ֣ר אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱ֠הֹוִ֠ה לֶהָרִ֨ים וְלַגְּבָע֜וֹת לָאֲפִיקִ֣ים (ולגיאות) [וְלַגֵּיאָי֗וֹת] הִנְנִ֨י אֲנִ֜י מֵבִ֤יא עֲלֵיכֶם֙ חֶ֔רֶב וְאִבַּדְתִּ֖י בָּמוֹתֵיכֶֽם׃


and say, You mountains of Yisra᾽el, hear the word of the Lord God; Thus says the Lord God to the mountains, and to the hills, to the ravines, and to the valleys; Behold, I, even I, will bring a sword upon you, and I will destroy your high places.


###### Ezekiel 6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/6:4)


וְנָשַׁ֙מּוּ֙ מִזְבְּח֣וֹתֵיכֶ֔ם וְנִשְׁבְּר֖וּ חַמָּנֵיכֶ֑ם וְהִפַּלְתִּי֙ חַלְלֵיכֶ֔ם לִפְנֵ֖י גִּלּוּלֵיכֶֽם׃


And your altars shall be desolate, and your sun images shall be broken: and I will cast down your slain men before your idols.


###### Ezekiel 6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/6:5)


וְנָתַתִּ֗י אֶת־פִּגְרֵי֙ בְּנֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל לִפְנֵ֖י גִּלּוּלֵיהֶ֑ם וְזֵרִיתִי֙ אֶת־עַצְמ֣וֹתֵיכֶ֔ם סְבִיב֖וֹת מִזְבְּחוֹתֵיכֶֽם׃


And I will lay the dead carcasses of the children of Yisra᾽el before their idols; and I will scatter your bones round about your altars.


###### Ezekiel 6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/6:6)


בְּכֹל֙ מוֹשְׁב֣וֹתֵיכֶ֔ם הֶעָרִ֣ים תֶּחֱרַ֔בְנָה וְהַבָּמ֖וֹת תִּישָׁ֑מְנָה לְמַ֩עַן֩ יֶחֶרְב֨וּ וְיֶאְשְׁמ֜וּ מִזְבְּחֽוֹתֵיכֶ֗ם וְנִשְׁבְּר֤וּ וְנִשְׁבְּתוּ֙ גִּלּ֣וּלֵיכֶ֔ם וְנִגְדְּעוּ֙ חַמָּ֣נֵיכֶ֔ם וְנִמְח֖וּ מַעֲשֵׂיכֶֽם׃


In all your dwelling places the cities shall be laid waste, and the high places shall be desolate; that your altars may be laid waste and made desolate, and your idols may be broken and cease, and your sun images may be cut down, and your works may be wiped out.


###### Ezekiel 6:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/6:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/6:7)


וְנָפַ֥ל חָלָ֖ל בְּתוֹכְכֶ֑ם וִידַעְתֶּ֖ם כִּֽי־אֲנִ֥י יְהֹוָֽה׃


And the slain shall fall in the midst of you, and you shall know that I am the Lord.


###### Ezekiel 6:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/6:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/6:8)


וְהוֹתַרְתִּ֗י בִּהְי֥וֹת לָכֶ֛ם פְּלִ֥יטֵי חֶ֖רֶב בַּגּוֹיִ֑ם בְּהִזָּרוֹתֵיכֶ֖ם בָּאֲרָצֽוֹת׃


Yet will I leave a remnant, that you may have some that shall escape the sword among the nations, when you shall be scattered through the countries.


###### Ezekiel 6:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/6:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/6:9)


וְזָכְר֨וּ פְלִיטֵיכֶ֜ם אוֹתִ֗י בַּגּוֹיִם֮ אֲשֶׁ֣ר נִשְׁבּוּ־שָׁם֒ אֲשֶׁ֨ר נִשְׁבַּ֜רְתִּי אֶת־לִבָּ֣ם הַזּוֹנֶ֗ה אֲשֶׁר־סָר֙ מֵֽעָלַ֔י וְאֵת֙ עֵֽינֵיהֶ֔ם הַזֹּנ֕וֹת אַחֲרֵ֖י גִּלּוּלֵיהֶ֑ם וְנָקֹ֙טּוּ֙ בִּפְנֵיהֶ֔ם אֶל־הָֽרָעוֹת֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר עָשׂ֔וּ לְכֹ֖ל תּוֹעֲבֹתֵיהֶֽם׃


And your fugitives shall remember me among the nations whither they shall be carried captives, when I have broken their wanton heart, which has departed from me, and their eyes, which go astray after their idols: and they shall loathe themselves for the evils which they have committed in all their abominations.


###### Ezekiel 6:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/6:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/6:10)


וְיָדְע֖וּ כִּֽי־אֲנִ֣י יְהֹוָ֑ה לֹ֤א אֶל־חִנָּם֙ דִּבַּ֔רְתִּי לַעֲשׂ֥וֹת לָהֶ֖ם הָרָעָ֥ה הַזֹּֽאת׃{פ}


And they shall know that I am the Lord, and that not in vain did I say that I would do this evil to them.


###### Ezekiel 6:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/6:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/6:11)


כֹּֽה־אָמַ֞ר אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֗ה הַכֵּ֨ה בְכַפְּךָ֜ וּרְקַ֤ע בְּרַגְלְךָ֙ וֶאֱמׇר־אָ֔ח אֶ֛ל כׇּל־תּוֹעֲב֥וֹת רָע֖וֹת בֵּ֣ית יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל אֲשֶׁ֗ר בַּחֶ֛רֶב בָּרָעָ֥ב וּבַדֶּ֖בֶר יִפֹּֽלוּ׃


Thus says the Lord God; Smite with thy hand, and stamp with thy foot, and say, Alas for all the evil abominations of the house of Yisra᾽el! for they shall fall by the sword, by the famine, and by the pestilence.


###### Ezekiel 6:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/6:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/6:12)


הָרָח֞וֹק בַּדֶּ֣בֶר יָמ֗וּת וְהַקָּרוֹב֙ בַּחֶ֣רֶב יִפּ֔וֹל וְהַנִּשְׁאָר֙ וְהַנָּצ֔וּר בָּרָעָ֖ב יָמ֑וּת וְכִלֵּיתִ֥י חֲמָתִ֖י בָּֽם׃


He that is far off shall die of the pestilence; and he that is near shall fall by the sword; and he that remains and is besieged shall die by the famine: thus will I spend my fury upon them.


###### Ezekiel 6:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/6:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/6:13)


וִֽידַעְתֶּם֙ כִּֽי־אֲנִ֣י יְהֹוָ֔ה בִּֽהְי֣וֹת חַלְלֵיהֶ֗ם בְּתוֹךְ֙ גִּלּ֣וּלֵיהֶ֔ם סְבִיב֖וֹת מִזְבְּחוֹתֵיהֶ֑ם אֶל֩ כׇּל־גִּבְעָ֨ה רָמָ֜ה בְּכֹ֣ל**׀** רָאשֵׁ֣י הֶהָרִ֗ים וְתַ֨חַת כׇּל־עֵ֤ץ רַֽעֲנָן֙ וְתַ֙חַת֙ כׇּל־אֵלָ֣ה עֲבֻתָּ֔ה מְק֗וֹם אֲשֶׁ֤ר נָֽתְנוּ־שָׁם֙ רֵ֣יחַ נִיחֹ֔חַ לְכֹ֖ל גִּלּוּלֵיהֶֽם׃


Then shall you know that I am the Lord, when their slain men shall be among their idols round about their altars, upon every high hill, in all the tops of the mountains, and under every green tree, and under every thick oak, the place where they did offer sweet savour to all their idols.


###### Ezekiel 6:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/6:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/6:14)


וְנָטִ֤יתִי אֶת־יָדִי֙ עֲלֵיהֶ֔ם וְנָתַתִּ֨י אֶת־הָאָ֜רֶץ שְׁמָמָ֤ה וּמְשַׁמָּה֙ מִמִּדְבַּ֣ר דִּבְלָ֔תָה בְּכֹ֖ל מוֹשְׁבֽוֹתֵיהֶ֑ם וְיָדְע֖וּ כִּֽי־אֲנִ֥י יְהֹוָֽה׃{פ}


So will I stretch out my hand upon them, and make the land desolate and waste, from the wilderness to Divla, in all their habitations: and they shall know that I am the Lord.



## Chapter 7

###### Ezekiel 7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/7:1)


וַיְהִ֥י דְבַר־יְהֹוָ֖ה אֵלַ֥י לֵאמֹֽר׃


Moreover the word of the Lord came to me, saying,


###### Ezekiel 7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/7:2)


וְאַתָּ֣ה בֶן־אָדָ֗ם כֹּֽה־אָמַ֞ר אֲדֹנָ֧י יֱהֹוִ֛ה לְאַדְמַ֥ת יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל קֵ֑ץ בָּ֣א הַקֵּ֔ץ עַל־[אַרְבַּ֖ע] (ארבעת) כַּנְפ֥וֹת הָאָֽרֶץ׃


Also, thou son of man, thus says the Lord God to the land of Yisra᾽el; An end, the end is come upon the four corners of the land.


###### Ezekiel 7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/7:3)


עַתָּה֙ הַקֵּ֣ץ עָלַ֔יִךְ וְשִׁלַּחְתִּ֤י אַפִּי֙ בָּ֔ךְ וּשְׁפַטְתִּ֖יךְ כִּדְרָכָ֑יִךְ וְנָתַתִּ֣י עָלַ֔יִךְ אֵ֖ת כׇּל־תּוֹעֲבוֹתָֽיִךְ׃


Now is the end come upon thee, and I will send my anger upon thee, and I will judge thee according to thy ways, and I will recompense upon thee all thy abominations.


###### Ezekiel 7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/7:4)


וְלֹא־תָח֥וֹס עֵינִ֛י עָלַ֖יִךְ וְלֹ֣א אֶחְמ֑וֹל כִּ֣י דְרָכַ֜יִךְ עָלַ֣יִךְ אֶתֵּ֗ן וְתוֹעֲבוֹתַ֙יִךְ֙ בְּתוֹכֵ֣ךְ תִּֽהְיֶ֔יןָ וִידַעְתֶּ֖ם כִּי־אֲנִ֥י יְהֹוָֽה׃{פ}


And my eye shall not spare thee, neither will I have pity: but I will recompense thy ways upon thee, and thy abominations shall be in the midst of thee: and you shall know that I am the Lord.


###### Ezekiel 7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/7:5)


כֹּ֥ה אָמַ֖ר אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֑ה רָעָ֛ה אַחַ֥ת רָעָ֖ה הִנֵּ֥ה בָאָֽה׃


Thus says the Lord God! An evil, a singular evil, behold, is come.


###### Ezekiel 7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/7:6)


קֵ֣ץ בָּ֔א בָּ֥א הַקֵּ֖ץ הֵקִ֣יץ אֵלָ֑יִךְ הִנֵּ֖ה בָּאָֽה׃


An end is come; the end is come, which he has stirred up against thee; behold, it is come.


###### Ezekiel 7:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/7:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/7:7)


בָּ֧אָה הַצְּפִירָ֛ה אֵלֶ֖יךָ יוֹשֵׁ֣ב הָאָ֑רֶץ בָּ֣א הָעֵ֗ת קָר֛וֹב הַיּ֥וֹם מְהוּמָ֖ה וְלֹא־הֵ֥ד הָרִֽים׃


The dawn is come to thee, O thou that dwellest in the land: the time comes, it is near; the day is a day of confusion, and not of joyful shouting on the mountains.


###### Ezekiel 7:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/7:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/7:8)


עַתָּ֣ה מִקָּר֗וֹב אֶשְׁפּ֤וֹךְ חֲמָתִי֙ עָלַ֔יִךְ וְכִלֵּיתִ֤י אַפִּי֙ בָּ֔ךְ וּשְׁפַטְתִּ֖יךְ כִּדְרָכָ֑יִךְ וְנָתַתִּ֣י עָלַ֔יִךְ אֵ֖ת כׇּל־תּוֹעֲבוֹתָֽיִךְ׃


Now will I shortly pour out my fury upon thee, and accomplish my anger upon thee: and I will judge thee according to thy ways, and I will recompense thee for all thy abominations.


###### Ezekiel 7:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/7:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/7:9)


וְלֹא־תָח֥וֹס עֵינִ֖י וְלֹ֣א אֶחְמ֑וֹל כִּדְרָכַ֜יִךְ עָלַ֣יִךְ אֶתֵּ֗ן וְתוֹעֲבוֹתַ֙יִךְ֙ בְּתוֹכֵ֣ךְ תִּהְיֶ֔יןָ וִידַעְתֶּ֕ם כִּ֛י אֲנִ֥י יְהֹוָ֖ה מַכֶּֽה׃


And my eye shall not spare, neither will I have pity: I will recompense thee according to thy ways and thy abominations that are in the midst of thee; and you shall know that I the Lord do smite.


###### Ezekiel 7:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/7:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/7:10)


הִנֵּ֥ה הַיּ֖וֹם הִנֵּ֣ה בָאָ֑ה יָֽצְאָה֙ הַצְּפִרָ֔ה צָ֚ץ הַמַּטֶּ֔ה פָּרַ֖ח הַזָּדֽוֹן׃


Behold the day, behold, it is coming: the dawn has gone forth; the rod has blossomed, arrogance has budded.


###### Ezekiel 7:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/7:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/7:11)


הֶחָמָ֥ס׀קָ֖ם לְמַטֵּה־רֶ֑שַׁע לֹא־מֵהֶ֞ם וְלֹ֧א מֵהֲמוֹנָ֛ם וְלֹ֥א מֶהֱמֵהֶ֖ם וְלֹא־נֹ֥הַּ בָּהֶֽם׃


Violence is risen up into a rod of wickedness: none of them shall remain, nothing of their multitude, nor of their splendour: neither shall there be wailing for them.


###### Ezekiel 7:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/7:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/7:12)


בָּ֤א הָעֵת֙ הִגִּ֣יעַ הַיּ֔וֹם הַקּוֹנֶה֙ אַל־יִשְׂמָ֔ח וְהַמֹּכֵ֖ר אַל־יִתְאַבָּ֑ל כִּ֥י חָר֖וֹן אֶל־כׇּל־הֲמוֹנָֽהּ׃


The time is come, the day draws near: let not the buyer rejoice, nor the seller mourn: for wrath is upon all its multitude.


###### Ezekiel 7:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/7:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/7:13)


כִּ֣י הַמֹּכֵ֗ר אֶל־הַמִּמְכָּר֙ לֹ֣א יָשׁ֔וּב וְע֥וֹד בַּחַיִּ֖ים חַיָּתָ֑ם כִּֽי־חָז֤וֹן אֶל־כׇּל־הֲמוֹנָהּ֙ לֹ֣א יָשׁ֔וּב וְאִ֧ישׁ בַּעֲוֺנ֛וֹ חַיָּת֖וֹ לֹ֥א יִתְחַזָּֽקוּ׃


For the seller shall not return to that which he has sold, although they were yet alive: for the vision concerns their whole multitude; none returns; for every man lives in his sin: they do not hold firm.


###### Ezekiel 7:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/7:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/7:14)


תָּקְע֤וּ בַתָּק֙וֹעַ֙ וְהָכִ֣ין הַכֹּ֔ל וְאֵ֥ין הֹלֵ֖ךְ לַמִּלְחָמָ֑ה כִּ֥י חֲרוֹנִ֖י אֶל־כׇּל־הֲמוֹנָֽהּ׃


They have blown the trumpet, and made all ready; but none goes to the battle: for my wrath is upon all their multitude.


###### Ezekiel 7:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/7:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/7:15)


הַחֶ֣רֶב בַּח֔וּץ וְהַדֶּ֥בֶר וְהָרָעָ֖ב מִבָּ֑יִת אֲשֶׁ֤ר בַּשָּׂדֶה֙ בַּחֶ֣רֶב יָמ֔וּת וַאֲשֶׁ֣ר בָּעִ֔יר רָעָ֥ב וָדֶ֖בֶר יֹאכְלֶֽנּוּ׃


The sword is outside, and the pestilence and the famine within: he that is in the field shall die with the sword; and he that is in the city, famine and pestilence shall devour him.


###### Ezekiel 7:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/7:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/7:16)


וּפָֽלְטוּ֙ פְּלִ֣יטֵיהֶ֔ם וְהָי֣וּ אֶל־הֶהָרִ֗ים כְּיוֹנֵ֧י הַגֵּאָי֛וֹת כֻּלָּ֖ם הֹמ֑וֹת אִ֖ישׁ בַּעֲוֺנֽוֹ׃


And those fugitives that escape, shall be on the mountains like doves of the valleys, all of them moaning, every one for his iniquity.


###### Ezekiel 7:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/7:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/7:17)


כׇּל־הַיָּדַ֖יִם תִּרְפֶּ֑ינָה וְכׇל־בִּרְכַּ֖יִם תֵּלַ֥כְנָה מָּֽיִם׃


All hands shall be feeble, and all knees shall drip with water.


###### Ezekiel 7:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/7:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/7:18)


וְחָגְר֣וּ שַׂקִּ֔ים וְכִסְּתָ֥ה אוֹתָ֖ם פַּלָּצ֑וּת וְאֶ֤ל כׇּל־פָּנִים֙ בּוּשָׁ֔ה וּבְכׇל־רָאשֵׁיהֶ֖ם קׇרְחָֽה׃


They shall also gird themselves with sackcloth, and horror shall cover them; and shame shall be upon all faces, and baldness upon all their heads.


###### Ezekiel 7:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/7:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/7:19)


כַּסְפָּ֞ם בַּחוּצ֣וֹת יַשְׁלִ֗יכוּ וּזְהָבָם֮ לְנִדָּ֣ה יִֽהְיֶה֒ כַּסְפָּ֨ם וּזְהָבָ֜ם לֹא־יוּכַ֣ל לְהַצִּילָ֗ם בְּיוֹם֙ עֶבְרַ֣ת יְהֹוָ֔ה נַפְשָׁם֙ לֹ֣א יְשַׂבֵּ֔עוּ וּמֵעֵיהֶ֖ם לֹ֣א יְמַלֵּ֑אוּ כִּֽי־מִכְשׁ֥וֹל עֲוֺנָ֖ם הָיָֽה׃


They shall cast their silver in the streets, and their gold shall be like an unclean thing: their silver and their gold shall not be able to deliver them in the day of the wrath of the Lord: they shall not satisfy their souls, neither fill their bowels: because it is the stumblingblock of their iniquity.


###### Ezekiel 7:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/7:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/7:20)


וּצְבִ֤י עֶדְיוֹ֙ לְגָא֣וֹן שָׂמָ֔הוּ וְצַלְמֵ֧י תוֹעֲבֹתָ֛ם שִׁקּוּצֵיהֶ֖ם עָ֣שׂוּ ב֑וֹ עַל־כֵּ֛ן נְתַתִּ֥יו לָהֶ֖ם לְנִדָּֽה׃


As for the beauty of his ornament which he set in majesty: they have set up in it the images of their abominations and of their detestable things: therefore I have set it far from them.


###### Ezekiel 7:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/7:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/7:21)


וּנְתַתִּ֤יו בְּיַֽד־הַזָּרִים֙ לָבַ֔ז וּלְרִשְׁעֵ֥י הָאָ֖רֶץ לְשָׁלָ֑ל (וחללוה) [וְחִלְּלֽוּהוּ]׃


And I will give it into the hands of the strangers for a prey, and to the wicked of the earth for a spoil; and they shall profane it.


###### Ezekiel 7:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/7:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/7:22)


וַהֲסִבּוֹתִ֤י פָנַי֙ מֵהֶ֔ם וְחִלְּל֖וּ אֶת־צְפוּנִ֑י וּבָאוּ־בָ֥הּ פָּרִיצִ֖ים וְחִלְּלֽוּהָ׃{פ}


My face will I turn also from them, and they shall profane my secret place: for the robbers shall enter into it, and profane it.


###### Ezekiel 7:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/7:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/7:23)


עֲשֵׂ֖ה הָרַתּ֑וֹק כִּ֣י הָאָ֗רֶץ מָֽלְאָה֙ מִשְׁפַּ֣ט דָּמִ֔ים וְהָעִ֖יר מָלְאָ֥ה חָמָֽס׃


Make the chain: for the land is full of bloody crimes, and the city is full of violence.


###### Ezekiel 7:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/7:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/7:24)


וְהֵֽבֵאתִי֙ רָעֵ֣י גוֹיִ֔ם וְיָרְשׁ֖וּ אֶת־בָּתֵּיהֶ֑ם וְהִשְׁבַּתִּי֙ גְּא֣וֹן עַזִּ֔ים וְנִחֲל֖וּ מְקַֽדְשֵׁיהֶֽם׃


And I will bring the worst of the nations, and they shall possess their houses: I will also make the pride of the strong to cease; and their holy places shall be profaned.


###### Ezekiel 7:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/7:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/7:25)


קְפָ֖דָה־בָ֑א וּבִקְשׁ֥וּ שָׁל֖וֹם וָאָֽיִן׃


Anguish comes; and they shall seek peace, but there shall be none.


###### Ezekiel 7:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/7:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/7:26)


הֹוָ֤ה עַל־הֹוָה֙ תָּב֔וֹא וּשְׁמֻעָ֥ה אֶל־שְׁמוּעָ֖ה תִּֽהְיֶ֑ה וּבִקְשׁ֤וּ חָזוֹן֙ מִנָּבִ֔יא וְתוֹרָה֙ תֹּאבַ֣ד מִכֹּהֵ֔ן וְעֵצָ֖ה מִזְּקֵנִֽים׃


Calamity shall follow upon calamity, and rumour shall come upon rumour; then shall they seek a vision from the prophet; but Tora shall perish from the priest, and counsel from the elders.


###### Ezekiel 7:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/7:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/7:27)


הַמֶּ֣לֶךְ יִתְאַבָּ֗ל וְנָשִׂיא֙ יִלְבַּ֣שׁ שְׁמָמָ֔ה וִידֵ֥י עַם־הָאָ֖רֶץ תִּבָּהַ֑לְנָה מִדַּרְכָּ֞ם אֶעֱשֶׂ֤ה אֹתָם֙ וּבְמִשְׁפְּטֵיהֶ֣ם אֶשְׁפְּטֵ֔ם וְיָדְע֖וּ כִּֽי־אֲנִ֥י יְהֹוָֽה׃{פ}


The king shall mourn, and the prince shall be wrapped in despair, and the hands of the people of the land shall be enfeebled: I will do to them after their way, and according to their deserts will I judge them; and they shall know that I am the Lord.



## Chapter 8

###### Ezekiel 8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/8:1)


וַיְהִ֣י**׀** בַּשָּׁנָ֣ה הַשִּׁשִּׁ֗ית בַּשִּׁשִּׁי֙ בַּחֲמִשָּׁ֣ה לַחֹ֔דֶשׁ אֲנִי֙ יוֹשֵׁ֣ב בְּבֵיתִ֔י וְזִקְנֵ֥י יְהוּדָ֖ה יוֹשְׁבִ֣ים לְפָנָ֑י וַתִּפֹּ֤ל עָלַי֙ שָׁ֔ם יַ֖ד אֲדֹנָ֥י יֱהֹוִֽה׃


And it came to pass in the sixth year, in the sixth month, on the fifth day of the month, as I sat in my house, and the elders of Yehuda sat before me, that the hand of the Lord God fell there upon me.


###### Ezekiel 8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/8:2)


וָאֶרְאֶ֗ה וְהִנֵּ֤ה דְמוּת֙ כְּמַרְאֵה־אֵ֔שׁ מִמַּרְאֵ֥ה מׇתְנָ֛יו וּלְמַ֖טָּה אֵ֑שׁ וּמִמׇּתְנָ֣יו וּלְמַ֔עְלָה כְּמַרְאֵה־זֹ֖הַר כְּעֵ֥ין הַחַשְׁמַֽלָה׃


Then I beheld, and lo a likeness as the appearance of fire: from what appeared to be his loins downward, fire; and from his loins upward, as it were the appearance of brightness, something like the colour of electrum.


###### Ezekiel 8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/8:3)


וַיִּשְׁלַח֙ תַּבְנִ֣ית יָ֔ד וַיִּקָּחֵ֖נִי בְּצִיצִ֣ת רֹאשִׁ֑י וַתִּשָּׂ֣א אֹתִ֣י ר֣וּחַ׀בֵּֽין־הָאָ֣רֶץ וּבֵ֣ין הַשָּׁמַ֡יִם וַתָּבֵא֩ אֹתִ֨י יְרוּשָׁלַ֜͏ְמָה בְּמַרְא֣וֹת אֱלֹהִ֗ים אֶל־פֶּ֜תַח שַׁ֤עַר הַפְּנִימִית֙ הַפּוֹנֶ֣ה צָפ֔וֹנָה אֲשֶׁר־שָׁ֣ם מוֹשַׁ֔ב סֵ֖מֶל הַקִּנְאָ֥ה הַמַּקְנֶֽה׃


And he put out the shape of a hand, and took me by a lock of my head; and a wind lifted me up between the earth and the heaven, and brought me in the visions of God to Yerushalayim, to the door of the inner gate that looks towards the north; where was the seat of the image of jealousy, which provokes to jealousy.


###### Ezekiel 8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/8:4)


וְהִ֨נֵּה־שָׁ֔ם כְּב֖וֹד אֱלֹהֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל כַּמַּרְאֶ֕ה אֲשֶׁ֥ר רָאִ֖יתִי בַּבִּקְעָֽה׃


And, behold, the glory of the God of Yisra᾽el was there, according to the vision that I saw in the plain.


###### Ezekiel 8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/8:5)


וַיֹּ֣אמֶר אֵלַ֔י בֶּן־אָדָ֕ם שָׂא־נָ֥א עֵינֶ֖יךָ דֶּ֣רֶךְ צָפ֑וֹנָה וָאֶשָּׂ֤א עֵינַי֙ דֶּ֣רֶךְ צָפ֔וֹנָה וְהִנֵּ֤ה מִצָּפוֹן֙ לְשַׁ֣עַר הַמִּזְבֵּ֔חַ סֵ֛מֶל הַקִּנְאָ֥ה הַזֶּ֖ה בַּבִּאָֽה׃


Then he said to me, Son of man, lift up thy eyes now the way towards the north. So I lifted up my eyes the way towards the north, and behold northward at the gate of the altar this image of jealousy in the entry.


###### Ezekiel 8:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/8:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/8:6)


וַיֹּ֣אמֶר אֵלַ֔י בֶּן־אָדָ֕ם הֲרֹאֶ֥ה אַתָּ֖ה (מהם) [מָ֣ה הֵ֣ם] עֹשִׂ֑ים תּוֹעֵב֨וֹת גְּדֹל֜וֹת אֲשֶׁ֥ר בֵּ֥ית יִשְׂרָאֵ֣ל**׀** עֹשִׂ֣ים פֹּ֗ה לְרׇֽחֳקָה֙ מֵעַ֣ל מִקְדָּשִׁ֔י וְעוֹד֙ תָּשׁ֣וּב תִּרְאֶ֔ה תּוֹעֵב֖וֹת גְּדֹלֽוֹת׃{פ}


And he said to me, Son of man, seest thou what they do? the great abominations that the house of Yisra᾽el commits here, to drive me far off from my sanctuary? but thou shalt see yet again greater abominations.


###### Ezekiel 8:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/8:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/8:7)


וַיָּבֵ֥א אֹתִ֖י אֶל־פֶּ֣תַח הֶחָצֵ֑ר וָאֶרְאֶ֕ה וְהִנֵּ֥ה חֹר־אֶחָ֖ד בַּקִּֽיר׃


And he brought me to the door of the court; and when I looked, behold a hole in the wall.


###### Ezekiel 8:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/8:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/8:8)


וַיֹּ֣אמֶר אֵלַ֔י בֶּן־אָדָ֖ם חֲתׇר־נָ֣א בַקִּ֑יר וָאֶחְתֹּ֣ר בַּקִּ֔יר וְהִנֵּ֖ה פֶּ֥תַח אֶחָֽד׃{ס}


Then said he to me, Son of man, dig now in the wall: and when I had dug in the wall, behold a door.


###### Ezekiel 8:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/8:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/8:9)


וַיֹּ֖אמֶר אֵלָ֑י בֹּ֤א וּרְאֵה֙ אֶת־הַתּוֹעֵב֣וֹת הָרָע֔וֹת אֲשֶׁ֛ר הֵ֥ם עֹשִׂ֖ים פֹּֽה׃


And he said to me, Go in, and behold the wicked abominations that they are doing here.


###### Ezekiel 8:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/8:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/8:10)


וָאָבוֹא֮ וָאֶרְאֶה֒ וְהִנֵּ֨ה כׇל־תַּבְנִ֜ית רֶ֤מֶשׂ וּבְהֵמָה֙ שֶׁ֔קֶץ וְכׇל־גִּלּוּלֵ֖י בֵּ֣ית יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל מְחֻקֶּ֥ה עַל־הַקִּ֖יר סָבִ֥יב׀סָבִֽיב׃


So I went in and saw; and behold every form of creeping thing, and abominable beast, and all the idols of the house of Yisra᾽el, traced upon the wall round about.


###### Ezekiel 8:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/8:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/8:11)


וְשִׁבְעִ֣ים אִ֣ישׁ מִזִּקְנֵ֣י בֵֽית־יִ֠שְׂרָאֵ֠ל וְיַאֲזַנְיָ֨הוּ בֶן־שָׁפָ֜ן עֹמֵ֤ד בְּתוֹכָם֙ עֹמְדִ֣ים לִפְנֵיהֶ֔ם וְאִ֥ישׁ מִקְטַרְתּ֖וֹ בְּיָד֑וֹ וַעֲתַ֥ר עֲנַֽן־הַקְּטֹ֖רֶת עֹלֶֽה׃


And there stood before them seventy men of the elders of the house of Yisra᾽el and in the midst of them stood Ya᾽azanyahu, the son of Shafan, every man with his censer in his hand; and a thick cloud of incense went up.


###### Ezekiel 8:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/8:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/8:12)


וַיֹּ֣אמֶר אֵלַי֮ הֲרָאִ֣יתָ בֶן־אָדָם֒ אֲשֶׁ֨ר זִקְנֵ֤י בֵֽית־יִשְׂרָאֵל֙ עֹשִׂ֣ים בַּחֹ֔שֶׁךְ אִ֖ישׁ בְּחַדְרֵ֣י מַשְׂכִּית֑וֹ כִּ֣י אֹמְרִ֗ים אֵ֤ין יְהֹוָה֙ רֹאֶ֣ה אֹתָ֔נוּ עָזַ֥ב יְהֹוָ֖ה אֶת־הָאָֽרֶץ׃


Then he said to me, Son of man, hast thou seen what the elders of the house of Yisra᾽el are doing in the dark, every man in the chambers of his imagery? for they say, The Lord sees us not; the Lord has forsaken the land.


###### Ezekiel 8:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/8:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/8:13)


וַיֹּ֖אמֶר אֵלָ֑י ע֣וֹד תָּשׁ֥וּב תִּרְאֶ֛ה תּוֹעֵב֥וֹת גְּדֹל֖וֹת אֲשֶׁר־הֵ֥מָּה עֹשִֽׂים׃


He said also to me. Thou shalt see yet again greater abominations that they do.


###### Ezekiel 8:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/8:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/8:14)


וַיָּבֵ֣א אֹתִ֗י אֶל־פֶּ֙תַח֙ שַׁ֣עַר בֵּית־יְהֹוָ֔ה אֲשֶׁ֖ר אֶל־הַצָּפ֑וֹנָה וְהִנֵּה־שָׁם֙ הַנָּשִׁ֣ים יֹשְׁב֔וֹת מְבַכּ֖וֹת אֶת־הַתַּמּֽוּז׃{ס}


Then he brought me to the door of the gate of the Lord’s house which was towards the north; and, behold, there sat women weeping for Tammuz.


###### Ezekiel 8:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/8:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/8:15)


וַיֹּ֥אמֶר אֵלַ֖י הֲרָאִ֣יתָ בֶן־אָדָ֑ם ע֣וֹד תָּשׁ֥וּב תִּרְאֶ֛ה תּוֹעֵב֖וֹת גְּדֹל֥וֹת מֵאֵֽלֶּה׃


Then he said to me, Hast thou seen this, O son of man? thou shalt see yet again greater abominations than these.


###### Ezekiel 8:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/8:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/8:16)


וַיָּבֵ֣א אֹתִ֗י אֶל־חֲצַ֣ר בֵּית־יְהֹוָה֮ הַפְּנִימִית֒ וְהִנֵּה־פֶ֜תַח הֵיכַ֣ל יְהֹוָ֗ה בֵּ֤ין הָֽאוּלָם֙ וּבֵ֣ין הַמִּזְבֵּ֔חַ כְּעֶשְׂרִ֥ים וַחֲמִשָּׁ֖ה אִ֑ישׁ אֲחֹ֨רֵיהֶ֜ם אֶל־הֵיכַ֤ל יְהֹוָה֙ וּפְנֵיהֶ֣ם קֵ֔דְמָה וְהֵ֛מָּה מִשְׁתַּחֲוִיתֶ֥ם קֵ֖דְמָה לַשָּֽׁמֶשׁ׃


And he brought me into the inner court of the Lord’s house, and, behold, at the door of the temple of the Lord, between the porch and the altar, were about twenty five men, with their backs towards the temple of the Lord, and their faces towards the east; and they were prostrating themselves towards the sun eastwards.


###### Ezekiel 8:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/8:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/8:17)


וַיֹּ֣אמֶר אֵלַי֮ הֲרָאִ֣יתָ בֶן־אָדָם֒ הֲנָקֵל֙ לְבֵ֣ית יְהוּדָ֔ה מֵעֲשׂ֕וֹת אֶת־הַתּוֹעֵב֖וֹת אֲשֶׁ֣ר עָשׂוּ־פֹ֑ה כִּֽי־מָלְא֨וּ אֶת־הָאָ֜רֶץ חָמָ֗ס וַיָּשֻׁ֙בוּ֙ לְהַכְעִיסֵ֔נִי וְהִנָּ֛ם שֹׁלְחִ֥ים אֶת־הַזְּמוֹרָ֖ה אֶל־אַפָּֽם׃


Then he said to me, Hast thou seen this, O son of man? Is it not enough for the house of Yehuda that they commit the abominations which they commit here? but they also have filled the land with violence, and have provoked me even further to anger: and, lo, they put the branch to their nose.


###### Ezekiel 8:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/8:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/8:18)


וְגַם־אֲנִי֙ אֶעֱשֶׂ֣ה בְחֵמָ֔ה לֹא־תָח֥וֹס עֵינִ֖י וְלֹ֣א אֶחְמֹ֑ל וְקָרְא֤וּ בְאׇזְנַי֙ ק֣וֹל גָּד֔וֹל וְלֹ֥א אֶשְׁמַ֖ע אוֹתָֽם׃


Therefore I too will deal in fury: my eye shall not spare, neither will I have pity: and though they cry in my ears with a loud voice, yet will I not hear them.



## Chapter 9

###### Ezekiel 9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/9:1)


וַיִּקְרָ֣א בְאׇזְנַ֗י ק֤וֹל גָּדוֹל֙ לֵאמֹ֔ר קָרְב֖וּ פְּקֻדּ֣וֹת הָעִ֑יר וְאִ֛ישׁ כְּלִ֥י מַשְׁחֵת֖וֹ בְּיָדֽוֹ׃


And he cried in my ears with a loud voice, saying, Cause them that have charge over the city to draw near, even every man with his destroying weapon in his hand.


###### Ezekiel 9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/9:2)


וְהִנֵּ֣ה שִׁשָּׁ֣ה אֲנָשִׁ֡ים בָּאִ֣ים**׀** מִדֶּרֶךְ־שַׁ֨עַר הָעֶלְי֜וֹן אֲשֶׁ֣ר**׀** מׇפְנֶ֣ה צָפ֗וֹנָה וְאִ֨ישׁ כְּלִ֤י מַפָּצוֹ֙ בְּיָד֔וֹ וְאִישׁ־אֶחָ֤ד בְּתוֹכָם֙ לָבֻ֣שׁ בַּדִּ֔ים וְקֶ֥סֶת הַסֹּפֵ֖ר בְּמׇתְנָ֑יו וַיָּבֹ֙אוּ֙ וַיַּ֣עַמְד֔וּ אֵ֖צֶל מִזְבַּ֥ח הַנְּחֹֽשֶׁת׃


And, behold, six men came from the way of the higher gate, which lies towards the north, and every man a slaughter weapon in his hand; and one man among them was clothed in linen, with a writer’s ink well by his side: and they went in, and stood beside the brazen altar.


###### Ezekiel 9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/9:3)


וּכְב֣וֹד**׀** אֱלֹהֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֗ל נַֽעֲלָה֙ מֵעַ֤ל הַכְּרוּב֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר הָיָ֣ה עָלָ֔יו אֶ֖ל מִפְתַּ֣ן הַבָּ֑יִת וַיִּקְרָ֗א אֶל־הָאִישׁ֙ הַלָּבֻ֣שׁ הַבַּדִּ֔ים אֲשֶׁ֛ר קֶ֥סֶת הַסֹּפֵ֖ר בְּמׇתְנָֽיו׃{פ}


And the glory of the God of Yisra᾽el had ascended from the keruv, on which it was, to the threshold of the house. And he called to the man clothed in linen, who had the writer’s ink well by his side;


###### Ezekiel 9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/9:4)


וַיֹּ֤אמֶר יְהֹוָה֙ אֵלָ֔ו עֲבֹר֙ בְּת֣וֹךְ הָעִ֔יר בְּת֖וֹךְ יְרוּשָׁלָ֑͏ִם וְהִתְוִ֨יתָ תָּ֜ו עַל־מִצְח֣וֹת הָאֲנָשִׁ֗ים הַנֶּֽאֱנָחִים֙ וְהַנֶּ֣אֱנָקִ֔ים עַ֚ל כׇּל־הַתּ֣וֹעֵב֔וֹת הַֽנַּעֲשׂ֖וֹת בְּתוֹכָֽהּ׃


and the Lord said to him, Go through the midst of the city, through the midst of Yerushalayim, and set a mark upon the foreheads of the men that sigh and that cry on account of all the abominations that are done in her midst.


###### Ezekiel 9:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/9:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/9:5)


וּלְאֵ֙לֶּה֙ אָמַ֣ר בְּאׇזְנַ֔י עִבְר֥וּ בָעִ֛יר אַחֲרָ֖יו וְהַכּ֑וּ (על) [אַל־]תָּחֹ֥ס (עיניכם) [עֵינְכֶ֖ם] וְאַל־תַּחְמֹֽלוּ׃


And to the others he said in my hearing, Go after him through the city, and strike: let not your eye spare, neither have pity:


###### Ezekiel 9:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/9:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/9:6)


זָקֵ֡ן בָּח֣וּר וּבְתוּלָה֩ וְטַ֨ף וְנָשִׁ֜ים תַּהַרְג֣וּ לְמַשְׁחִ֗ית וְעַל־כׇּל־אִ֨ישׁ אֲשֶׁר־עָלָ֤יו הַתָּו֙ אַל־תִּגַּ֔שׁוּ וּמִמִּקְדָּשִׁ֖י תָּחֵ֑לּוּ וַיָּחֵ֙לּוּ֙ בָּאֲנָשִׁ֣ים הַזְּקֵנִ֔ים אֲשֶׁ֖ר לִפְנֵ֥י הַבָּֽיִת׃


slay utterly old and young, both maid, and little children, and women: but come not near any man upon whom is the mark; and begin at my sanctuary. Then they began with the old men who were before the house.


###### Ezekiel 9:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/9:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/9:7)


וַיֹּ֨אמֶר אֲלֵיהֶ֜ם טַמְּא֣וּ אֶת־הַבַּ֗יִת וּמַלְא֧וּ אֶת־הַחֲצֵר֛וֹת חֲלָלִ֖ים צֵ֑אוּ וְיָצְא֖וּ וְהִכּ֥וּ בָעִֽיר׃


And he said to them, Defile the house, and fill the courts with the slain: go out. And they went out, and slew in the city.


###### Ezekiel 9:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/9:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/9:8)


וַֽיְהִי֙ כְּהַכּוֹתָ֔ם וְנֵֽאשְׁאַ֖ר אָ֑נִי וָאֶפְּלָ֨ה עַל־פָּנַ֜י וָאֶזְעַ֗ק וָֽאֹמַר֙ אֲהָהּ֙ אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֔ה הֲמַשְׁחִ֣ית אַתָּ֗ה אֵ֚ת כׇּל־שְׁאֵרִ֣ית יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל בְּשׇׁפְכְּךָ֥ אֶת־חֲמָתְךָ֖ עַל־יְרוּשָׁלָֽ͏ִם׃


And it came to pass, while they were slaying them, and I was left, that I fell upon my face, and cried, and said, Ah Lord God! wilt Thou destroy all the remnant of Yisra᾽el in the pouring of Thy fury upon Yerushalayim?


###### Ezekiel 9:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/9:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/9:9)


וַיֹּ֣אמֶר אֵלַ֗י עֲוֺ֨ן בֵּֽית־יִשְׂרָאֵ֤ל וִֽיהוּדָה֙ גָּדוֹל֙ בִּמְאֹ֣ד מְאֹ֔ד וַתִּמָּלֵ֤א הָאָ֙רֶץ֙ דָּמִ֔ים וְהָעִ֖יר מָלְאָ֣ה מֻטֶּ֑ה כִּ֣י אָמְר֗וּ עָזַ֤ב יְהֹוָה֙ אֶת־הָאָ֔רֶץ וְאֵ֥ין יְהֹוָ֖ה רֹאֶֽה׃


Then said he to me, The iniquity of the house of Yisra᾽el and Yehuda is exceedingly great, and the land is full of blood, and the city full of injustice: for they say, The Lord has forsaken the land, and the Lord sees not.


###### Ezekiel 9:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/9:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/9:10)


וְגַ֨ם־אֲנִ֔י לֹא־תָח֥וֹס עֵינִ֖י וְלֹ֣א אֶחְמֹ֑ל דַּרְכָּ֖ם בְּרֹאשָׁ֥ם נָתָֽתִּי׃


And as for me also, my eye shall not spare, nor will I have pity, but I will recompense their way upon their head.


###### Ezekiel 9:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/9:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/9:11)


וְהִנֵּ֞ה הָאִ֣ישׁ**׀** לְבֻ֣שׁ הַבַּדִּ֗ים אֲשֶׁ֤ר הַקֶּ֙סֶת֙ בְּמׇתְנָ֔יו מֵשִׁ֥יב דָּבָ֖ר לֵאמֹ֑ר עָשִׂ֕יתִי (כאשר) [כְּכֹ֖ל אֲשֶׁ֥ר] צִוִּיתָֽנִי׃{פ}


And, behold, the man clothed in linen, who had the ink well by his side, reported the matter, saying, I have done as Thou hast commanded me.



## Chapter 10

###### Ezekiel 10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/10:1)


וָאֶרְאֶ֗ה וְהִנֵּ֤ה אֶל־הָרָקִ֙יעַ֙ אֲשֶׁר֙ עַל־רֹ֣אשׁ הַכְּרֻבִ֔ים כְּאֶ֣בֶן סַפִּ֔יר כְּמַרְאֵ֖ה דְּמ֣וּת כִּסֵּ֑א נִרְאָ֖ה עֲלֵיהֶֽם׃


Then I looked, and, behold, in the firmament that was above the head of the keruvim there appeared over them something like a sapphire stone, like what appeared to be the shape of a throne.


###### Ezekiel 10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/10:2)


וַיֹּ֜אמֶר אֶל־הָאִ֣ישׁ**׀** לְבֻ֣שׁ הַבַּדִּ֗ים וַיֹּ֡אמֶר בֹּא֩ אֶל־בֵּינ֨וֹת לַגַּלְגַּ֜ל אֶל־תַּ֣חַת לַכְּר֗וּב וּמַלֵּ֨א חׇפְנֶ֤יךָ גַֽחֲלֵי־אֵשׁ֙ מִבֵּינ֣וֹת לַכְּרֻבִ֔ים וּזְרֹ֖ק עַל־הָעִ֑יר וַיָּבֹ֖א לְעֵינָֽי׃


And he spoke to the man clothed in linen, and said, Go in between the wheelwork, under the keruv, and fill thy hands with coals of fire from between the keruvim, and scatter them over the city. And he went in in my sight.


###### Ezekiel 10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/10:3)


וְהַכְּרֻבִ֗ים עֹ֥מְדִ֛ים מִימִ֥ין לַבַּ֖יִת בְּבֹא֣וֹ הָאִ֑ישׁ וְהֶעָנָ֣ן מָלֵ֔א אֶת־הֶחָצֵ֖ר הַפְּנִימִֽית׃


Now the keruvim stood on the right side of the house, when the man went in; and the cloud filled the inner court.


###### Ezekiel 10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/10:4)


וַיָּ֤רׇם כְּבוֹד־יְהֹוָה֙ מֵעַ֣ל הַכְּר֔וּב עַ֖ל מִפְתַּ֣ן הַבָּ֑יִת וַיִּמָּלֵ֤א הַבַּ֙יִת֙ אֶת־הֶ֣עָנָ֔ן וְהֶֽחָצֵר֙ מָֽלְאָ֔ה אֶת־נֹ֖גַהּ כְּב֥וֹד יְהֹוָֽה׃


Then the glory of the Lord went up from the keruv, and stood above the threshold of the house; and the house was filled with the cloud, and the court was full of the brightness of the Lord’s glory.


###### Ezekiel 10:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/10:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/10:5)


וְקוֹל֙ כַּנְפֵ֣י הַכְּרוּבִ֔ים נִשְׁמַ֕ע עַד־הֶחָצֵ֖ר הַחִֽיצֹנָ֑ה כְּק֥וֹל אֵל־שַׁדַּ֖י בְּדַבְּרֽוֹ׃


And the sound of the wings of the keruvim was heard as far as the outer court, as the voice of the Almighty God when he speaks.


###### Ezekiel 10:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/10:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/10:6)


וַיְהִ֗י בְּצַוֺּתוֹ֙ אֶת־הָאִ֤ישׁ לְבֻֽשׁ־הַבַּדִּים֙ לֵאמֹ֔ר קַ֥ח אֵשׁ֙ מִבֵּינ֣וֹת לַגַּלְגַּ֔ל מִבֵּינ֖וֹת לַכְּרוּבִ֑ים וַיָּבֹא֙ וַֽיַּעֲמֹ֔ד אֵ֖צֶל הָאוֹפָֽן׃


And it came to pass, when he had commanded the man clothed with linen saying, Take fire from between the wheelwork, from between the keruvim; then he went in, and stood beside the wheel.


###### Ezekiel 10:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/10:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/10:7)


וַיִּשְׁלַח֩ הַכְּר֨וּב אֶת־יָד֜וֹ מִבֵּינ֣וֹת לַכְּרוּבִ֗ים אֶל־הָאֵשׁ֙ אֲשֶׁר֙ בֵּינ֣וֹת הַכְּרֻבִ֔ים וַיִּשָּׂא֙ וַיִּתֵּ֔ן אֶל־חׇפְנֵ֖י לְבֻ֣שׁ הַבַּדִּ֑ים וַיִּקַּ֖ח וַיֵּצֵֽא׃


And one keruv stretched out his hand from between the keruvim unto the fire that was between the keruvim, and took, and put it into the hands of him that was clothed in linen: who took it, and went out.


###### Ezekiel 10:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/10:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/10:8)


וַיֵּרָ֖א לַכְּרֻבִ֑ים תַּבְנִית֙ יַד־אָדָ֔ם תַּ֖חַת כַּנְפֵיהֶֽם׃


And the keruvim appeared to have the form of a man’s hand under their wings.


###### Ezekiel 10:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/10:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/10:9)


וָאֶרְאֶ֗ה וְהִנֵּ֨ה אַרְבָּעָ֣ה אוֹפַנִּים֮ אֵ֣צֶל הַכְּרוּבִים֒ אוֹפַ֣ן אֶחָ֗ד אֵ֚צֶל הַכְּר֣וּב אֶחָ֔ד וְאוֹפַ֣ן אֶחָ֔ד אֵ֖צֶל הַכְּר֣וּב אֶחָ֑ד וּמַרְאֵה֙ הָא֣וֹפַנִּ֔ים כְּעֵ֖ין אֶ֥בֶן תַּרְשִֽׁישׁ׃


And when I looked, behold the four wheels by the keruvim, one wheel by one keruv, and another wheel by another keruv: and the appearance of the wheels was like the colour of an emerald stone.


###### Ezekiel 10:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/10:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/10:10)


וּמַ֨רְאֵיהֶ֔ם דְּמ֥וּת אֶחָ֖ד לְאַרְבַּעְתָּ֑ם כַּאֲשֶׁ֛ר יִהְיֶ֥ה הָאוֹפַ֖ן בְּת֥וֹךְ הָאוֹפָֽן׃


And as for their appearances, they four had one likeness, as if a wheel had been in the midst of a wheel.


###### Ezekiel 10:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/10:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/10:11)


בְּלֶכְתָּ֗ם אֶל־אַרְבַּ֤עַת רִבְעֵיהֶם֙ יֵלֵ֔כוּ לֹ֥א יִסַּ֖בּוּ בְּלֶכְתָּ֑ם כִּ֣י הַמָּק֞וֹם אֲשֶׁר־יִפְנֶ֤ה הָרֹאשׁ֙ אַחֲרָ֣יו יֵלֵ֔כוּ לֹ֥א יִסַּ֖בּוּ בְּלֶכְתָּֽם׃


When they moved, they went towards their four sides; they turned not as they went, but to the place where the head turned they followed it; they turned not as they went.


###### Ezekiel 10:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/10:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/10:12)


וְכׇל־בְּשָׂרָם֙ וְגַבֵּהֶ֔ם וִידֵיהֶ֖ם וְכַנְפֵיהֶ֑ם וְהָאוֹפַנִּ֗ים מְלֵאִ֤ים עֵינַ֙יִם֙ סָבִ֔יב לְאַרְבַּעְתָּ֖ם אוֹפַנֵּיהֶֽם׃


And their whole body, and their backs, and their hands, and their wings, and the wheels, were full of eyes round about, even the wheels that they four had.


###### Ezekiel 10:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/10:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/10:13)


לָא֖וֹפַנִּ֑ים לָהֶ֛ם קוֹרָ֥א הַגַּלְגַּ֖ל בְּאׇזְנָֽי׃


As for the wheels, they were called in my hearing, Haggalgal (The wheelwork.)


###### Ezekiel 10:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/10:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/10:14)


וְאַרְבָּעָ֥ה פָנִ֖ים לְאֶחָ֑ד פְּנֵ֨י הָאֶחָ֜ד פְּנֵ֣י הַכְּר֗וּב וּפְנֵ֤י הַשֵּׁנִי֙ פְּנֵ֣י אָדָ֔ם וְהַשְּׁלִישִׁי֙ פְּנֵ֣י אַרְיֵ֔ה וְהָרְבִיעִ֖י פְּנֵי־נָֽשֶׁר׃


And every one had four faces: the first face was the face of a keruv, and the second face was the face of a man, and the third the face of a lion, and the fourth the face of an eagle.


###### Ezekiel 10:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/10:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/10:15)


וַיֵּרֹ֖מּוּ הַכְּרוּבִ֑ים הִ֣יא הַחַיָּ֔ה אֲשֶׁ֥ר רָאִ֖יתִי בִּֽנְהַר־כְּבָֽר׃


And the keruvim mounted up. This is the same living creature that I saw by the river Kevar.


###### Ezekiel 10:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/10:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/10:16)


וּבְלֶ֙כֶת֙ הַכְּרוּבִ֔ים יֵלְכ֥וּ הָאוֹפַנִּ֖ים אֶצְלָ֑ם וּבִשְׂאֵ֨ת הַכְּרוּבִ֜ים אֶת־כַּנְפֵיהֶ֗ם לָרוּם֙ מֵעַ֣ל הָאָ֔רֶץ לֹֽא־יִסַּ֧בּוּ הָאוֹפַנִּ֛ים גַּם־הֵ֖ם מֵאֶצְלָֽם׃
