וַיִּשְׂא֣וּ הַכְּרוּבִ֣ים אֶת־כַּ֠נְפֵיהֶ֠ם וַיֵּר֨וֹמּוּ מִן־הָאָ֤רֶץ לְעֵינַי֙ בְּצֵאתָ֔ם וְהָאוֹפַנִּ֖ים לְעֻמָּתָ֑ם וַֽיַּעֲמֹ֗ד פֶּ֣תַח שַׁ֤עַר בֵּית־יְהֹוָה֙ הַקַּדְמוֹנִ֔י וּכְב֧וֹד אֱלֹהֵֽי־יִשְׂרָאֵ֛ל עֲלֵיהֶ֖ם מִלְמָֽעְלָה׃


And the keruvim lifted up their wings, and mounted up from the earth in my sight, when they went out, with the wheels beside them; and every one stood at the door of the east gate of the Lord’s house; and the glory of the God of Yisra᾽el was over them above.

אַרְבָּעָ֨ה אַרְבָּעָ֤ה פָנִים֙ לְאֶחָ֔ד וְאַרְבַּ֥ע כְּנָפַ֖יִם לְאֶחָ֑ד וּדְמוּת֙ יְדֵ֣י אָדָ֔ם תַּ֖חַת כַּנְפֵיהֶֽם׃

## Chapter 11

###### Ezekiel 11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/11:1)


וַתִּשָּׂ֨א אֹתִ֜י ר֗וּחַ וַתָּבֵ֣א אֹ֠תִ֠י אֶל־שַׁ֨עַר בֵּית־יְהֹוָ֤ה הַקַּדְמוֹנִי֙ הַפּוֹנֶ֣ה קָדִ֔ימָה וְהִנֵּה֙ בְּפֶ֣תַח הַשַּׁ֔עַר עֶשְׂרִ֥ים וַחֲמִשָּׁ֖ה אִ֑ישׁ וָאֶרְאֶ֨ה בְתוֹכָ֜ם אֶת־יַאֲזַנְיָ֧ה בֶן־עַזֻּ֛ר וְאֶת־פְּלַטְיָ֥הוּ בֶן־בְּנָיָ֖הוּ שָׂרֵ֥י הָעָֽם׃{פ}

וַיֹּ֖אמֶר אֵלָ֑י בֶּן־אָדָ֕ם אֵ֣לֶּה הָאֲנָשִׁ֞ים הַחֹשְׁבִ֥ים אָ֛וֶן וְהַיֹּעֲצִ֥ים עֲצַת־רָ֖ע בָּעִ֥יר הַזֹּֽאת׃

And the spirit of the Lord fell upon me, and said to me, Speak; Thus says the Lord; Thus have you said, O house of Yisra᾽el: for I know the things that come into your mind, everyone of them.

Son of man, thy brethren, thy brothers, thy next of kin, and all the house of Yisra᾽el entire are they to whom the inhabitants of Yerushalayim have said, Get you far from the Lord: to us is this land given in possession.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/11:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/11:16)


לָכֵ֣ן אֱמֹ֗ר כֹּֽה־אָמַר֮ אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִה֒ כִּ֤י הִרְחַקְתִּים֙ בַּגּוֹיִ֔ם וְכִ֥י הֲפִיצוֹתִ֖ים בָּאֲרָצ֑וֹת וָאֱהִ֤י לָהֶם֙ לְמִקְדָּ֣שׁ מְעַ֔ט בָּאֲרָצ֖וֹת אֲשֶׁר־בָּ֥אוּ שָֽׁם׃{ס}


Therefore say, Thus says the Lord God; Although I have cast them far off among the nations, and although I have scattered them among the countries, and I have been to them a little sanctuary in the countries where they have come,

therefore say, Thus says the Lord God; Yet will I gather you from the peoples, and assemble you out of the countries where you have been scattered, and I will give you the land of Yisra᾽el.

###### Ezekiel 11:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/11:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/11:22)


וַיִּשְׂא֤וּ הַכְּרוּבִים֙ אֶת־כַּנְפֵיהֶ֔ם וְהָאוֹפַנִּ֖ים לְעֻמָּתָ֑ם וּכְב֧וֹד אֱלֹהֵֽי־יִשְׂרָאֵ֛ל עֲלֵיהֶ֖ם מִלְמָֽעְלָה׃


Then did the keruvim lift up their wings, and the wheels along with them; and the glory of the God of Yisra᾽el was over them above.


###### Ezekiel 11:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/11:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/11:23)


וַיַּ֙עַל֙ כְּב֣וֹד יְהֹוָ֔ה מֵעַ֖ל תּ֣וֹךְ הָעִ֑יר וַֽיַּעֲמֹד֙ עַל־הָהָ֔ר אֲשֶׁ֖ר מִקֶּ֥דֶם לָעִֽיר׃


And the glory of the Lord went up from the midst of the city, and stood upon the mountain which is on the east side of the city.


###### Ezekiel 11:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/11:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/11:24)


וְר֣וּחַ נְשָׂאַ֗תְנִי וַתְּבִאֵ֤נִי כַשְׂדִּ֙ימָה֙ אֶל־הַגּוֹלָ֔ה בַּמַּרְאֶ֖ה בְּר֣וּחַ אֱלֹהִ֑ים וַיַּ֙עַל֙ מֵֽעָלַ֔י הַמַּרְאֶ֖ה אֲשֶׁ֥ר רָאִֽיתִי׃

וָאֲדַבֵּ֖ר אֶל־הַגּוֹלָ֑ה אֵ֛ת כׇּל־דִּבְרֵ֥י יְהֹוָ֖ה אֲשֶׁ֥ר הֶרְאָֽנִי׃{פ}

## Chapter 12

###### Ezekiel 12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/12:1)

Then word of the Lord came to me, saying,


###### Ezekiel 12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/12:2)


בֶּן־אָדָ֕ם בְּת֥וֹךְ בֵּית־הַמֶּ֖רִי אַתָּ֣ה יֹשֵׁ֑ב אֲשֶׁ֣ר עֵינַ֩יִם֩ לָהֶ֨ם לִרְא֜וֹת וְלֹ֣א רָא֗וּ אׇזְנַ֨יִם לָהֶ֤ם לִשְׁמֹ֙עַ֙ וְלֹ֣א שָׁמֵ֔עוּ כִּ֛י בֵּ֥ית מְרִ֖י הֵֽם׃


Son of man, thou dwellest in the midst of a rebellious house, who have eyes to see, and see not; they have ears to hear, and hear not: for they are a rebellious house.


###### Ezekiel 12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/12:3)


וְאַתָּ֣ה בֶן־אָדָ֗ם עֲשֵׂ֤ה לְךָ֙ כְּלֵ֣י גוֹלָ֔ה וּגְלֵ֥ה יוֹמָ֖ם לְעֵינֵיהֶ֑ם וְגָלִ֨יתָ מִמְּקוֹמְךָ֜ אֶל־מָק֤וֹם אַחֵר֙ לְעֵ֣ינֵיהֶ֔ם אוּלַ֣י יִרְא֔וּ כִּ֛י בֵּ֥ית מְרִ֖י הֵֽמָּה׃


Therefore, thou son of man, prepare thee the gear for exile, and remove as though for exile by day in their sight; and thou shalt remove from thy place to another place in their sight: it may be that they will perceive, though they are a rebellious house.


###### Ezekiel 12:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/12:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/12:4)


וְהוֹצֵאתָ֨ כֵלֶ֜יךָ כִּכְלֵ֥י גוֹלָ֛ה יוֹמָ֖ם לְעֵינֵיהֶ֑ם וְאַתָּ֗ה תֵּצֵ֤א בָעֶ֙רֶב֙ לְעֵ֣ינֵיהֶ֔ם כְּמוֹצָאֵ֖י גּוֹלָֽה׃

לְעֵינֵיהֶ֖ם חֲתׇר־לְךָ֣ בַקִּ֑יר וְהוֹצֵאתָ֖ בּֽוֹ׃


Dig thou through the wall in their sight, and carry out through it.


###### Ezekiel 12:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/12:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/12:6)


לְעֵ֨ינֵיהֶ֜ם עַל־כָּתֵ֤ף תִּשָּׂא֙ בָּעֲלָטָ֣ה תוֹצִ֔יא פָּנֶ֣יךָ תְכַסֶּ֔ה וְלֹ֥א תִרְאֶ֖ה אֶת־הָאָ֑רֶץ כִּֽי־מוֹפֵ֥ת נְתַתִּ֖יךָ לְבֵ֥ית יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


In their sight shalt thou bear it upon thy shoulders, and carry it out in the dark: thou shalt cover thy face, that thou see not the ground: for I have set thee for a sign to the house of Yisra᾽el.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/12:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/12:7)


וָאַ֣עַשׂ כֵּן֮ כַּאֲשֶׁ֣ר צֻוֵּ֒יתִי֒ כֵּ֠לַ֠י הוֹצֵ֜אתִי כִּכְלֵ֤י גוֹלָה֙ יוֹמָ֔ם וּבָעֶ֛רֶב חָתַֽרְתִּי־לִ֥י בַקִּ֖יר בְּיָ֑ד בָּעֲלָטָ֥ה הוֹצֵ֛אתִי עַל־כָּתֵ֥ף נָשָׂ֖אתִי לְעֵינֵיהֶֽם׃{פ}

בֶּן־אָדָ֕ם הֲלֹ֨א אָמְר֥וּ אֵלֶ֛יךָ בֵּ֥ית יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל בֵּ֣ית הַמֶּ֑רִי מָ֖ה אַתָּ֥ה עֹשֶֽׂה׃


Son of man, has not the house of Yisra᾽el, the rebellious house, said to thee, What doest thou?


###### Ezekiel 12:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/12:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/12:10)


אֱמֹ֣ר אֲלֵיהֶ֔ם כֹּ֥ה אָמַ֖ר אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֑ה הַנָּשִׂ֞יא הַמַּשָּׂ֤א הַזֶּה֙ בִּיר֣וּשָׁלַ֔͏ִם וְכׇל־בֵּ֥ית יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל אֲשֶׁר־הֵ֥מָּה בְתוֹכָֽם׃


Say thou to them, Thus says the Lord God; This burden concerns the prince in Yerushalayim, and all the house of Yisra᾽el that are among them.


###### Ezekiel 12:11

אֱמֹ֖ר אֲנִ֣י מֽוֹפֶתְכֶ֑ם כַּאֲשֶׁ֣ר עָשִׂ֗יתִי כֵּ֚ן יֵעָשֶׂ֣ה לָהֶ֔ם בַּגּוֹלָ֥ה בַשְּׁבִ֖י יֵלֵֽכוּ׃


Say, I am your sign: as I have done, so shall it be done to them: they shall go into exile, into captivity.


###### Ezekiel 12:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/12:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/12:12)


וְהַנָּשִׂ֨יא אֲשֶׁר־בְּתוֹכָ֜ם אֶל־כָּתֵ֤ף יִשָּׂא֙ בָּעֲלָטָ֣ה וְיֵצֵ֔א בַּקִּ֥יר יַחְתְּר֖וּ לְה֣וֹצִיא ב֑וֹ פָּנָ֣יו יְכַסֶּ֔ה יַ֗עַן אֲשֶׁ֨ר לֹא־יִרְאֶ֥ה לַעַ֛יִן ה֖וּא אֶת־הָאָֽרֶץ׃


And the prince that is among them shall bear upon his shoulder in the dark, and shall go out: they shall dig through the wall to carry out thereby: he shall cover his face, that he see not the ground with his eyes.


###### Ezekiel 12:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/12:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/12:13)


וּפָרַשְׂתִּ֤י אֶת־רִשְׁתִּי֙ עָלָ֔יו וְנִתְפַּ֖שׂ בִּמְצוּדָתִ֑י וְהֵבֵאתִ֨י אֹת֤וֹ בָבֶ֙לָה֙ אֶ֣רֶץ כַּשְׂדִּ֔ים וְאוֹתָ֥הּ לֹֽא־יִרְאֶ֖ה וְשָׁ֥ם יָמֽוּת׃


My net also will I spread upon him, and he shall be taken in my snare: and I will bring him to Bavel to the land of the Kasdim; yet he shall not see it, though he shall die there.

And I will scatter towards every wind all that are about him to help him, and all his troops; and I will draw out the sword after them.


###### Ezekiel 12:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/12:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/12:15)


וְיָדְע֖וּ כִּֽי־אֲנִ֣י יְהֹוָ֑ה בַּהֲפִיצִ֤י אוֹתָם֙ בַּגּוֹיִ֔ם וְזֵרִיתִ֥י אוֹתָ֖ם בָּאֲרָצֽוֹת׃


And they shall know that I am the Lord, when I shall scatter them among the nations, and disperse them in the countries.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/12:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/12:16)


וְהוֹתַרְתִּ֤י מֵהֶם֙ אַנְשֵׁ֣י מִסְפָּ֔ר מֵחֶ֖רֶב מֵרָעָ֣ב וּמִדָּ֑בֶר לְמַ֨עַן יְסַפְּר֜וּ אֶת־כׇּל־תּוֹעֲבֽוֹתֵיהֶ֗ם בַּגּוֹיִם֙ אֲשֶׁר־בָּ֣אוּ שָׁ֔ם וְיָדְע֖וּ כִּי־אֲנִ֥י יְהֹוָֽה׃{פ}


But I will leave a few men of them over from the sword, from the famine, and from the pestilence; that they may confess all their abominations among the nations where they come; and they shall know that I am the Lord.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/12:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/12:17)


וַיְהִ֥י דְבַר־יְהֹוָ֖ה אֵלַ֥י לֵאמֹֽר׃


Moreover the word of the Lord came to me saying,


###### Ezekiel 12:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/12:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/12:18)


בֶּן־אָדָ֕ם לַחְמְךָ֖ בְּרַ֣עַשׁ תֹּאכֵ֑ל וּמֵימֶ֕יךָ בְּרׇגְזָ֥ה וּבִדְאָגָ֖ה תִּשְׁתֶּֽה׃


Son of man, eat thy bread with quaking, and drink thy water with trembling and with anxiety;


###### Ezekiel 12:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/12:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/12:19)


וְאָמַרְתָּ֣ אֶל־עַ֣ם הָאָ֡רֶץ כֹּֽה־אָמַר֩ אֲדֹנָ֨י יֱהֹוִ֜ה לְיוֹשְׁבֵ֤י יְרוּשָׁלַ֙͏ִם֙ אֶל־אַדְמַ֣ת יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל לַחְמָם֙ בִּדְאָגָ֣ה יֹאכֵ֔לוּ וּמֵימֵיהֶ֖ם בְּשִׁמָּמ֣וֹן יִשְׁתּ֑וּ לְמַ֜עַן תֵּשַׁ֤ם אַרְצָהּ֙ מִמְּלֹאָ֔הּ מֵחֲמַ֖ס כׇּֽל־הַיֹּשְׁבִ֥ים בָּֽהּ׃


and say to the people of the land, Thus says the Lord God of the inhabitants of Yerushalayim, and of the land of Yisra᾽el; They shall eat their bread with anxiety, and drink their water with appalment, that her land may be desolate, bare of its fullness, because of the violence of all that dwell in it.


###### Ezekiel 12:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/12:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/12:20)


וְהֶעָרִ֤ים הַנּֽוֹשָׁבוֹת֙ תֶּחֱרַ֔בְנָה וְהָאָ֖רֶץ שְׁמָמָ֣ה תִֽהְיֶ֑ה וִידַעְתֶּ֖ם כִּֽי־אֲנִ֥י יְהֹוָֽה׃{פ}


And the cities that are inhabited shall be laid waste, and the land shall be desolate; and you shall know that I am the Lord.


###### Ezekiel 12:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/12:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/12:21)

And the word of the Lord came to me saying,


###### Ezekiel 12:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/12:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/12:22)


בֶּן־אָדָ֗ם מָֽה־הַמָּשָׁ֤ל הַזֶּה֙ לָכֶ֔ם עַל־אַדְמַ֥ת יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל לֵאמֹ֑ר יַֽאַרְכוּ֙ הַיָּמִ֔ים וְאָבַ֖ד כׇּל־חָזֽוֹן׃


Son of man, what is that proverb that you have in the land of Yisra᾽el, saying, The days are prolonged, and every vision fails?


###### Ezekiel 12:23

לָכֵ֞ן אֱמֹ֣ר אֲלֵיהֶ֗ם כֹּה־אָמַר֮ אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִה֒ הִשְׁבַּ֙תִּי֙ אֶת־הַמָּשָׁ֣ל הַזֶּ֔ה וְלֹֽא־יִמְשְׁל֥וּ אֹת֛וֹ ע֖וֹד בְּיִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל כִּ֚י אִם־דַּבֵּ֣ר אֲלֵיהֶ֔ם קָֽרְבוּ֙ הַיָּמִ֔ים וּדְבַ֖ר כׇּל־חָזֽוֹן׃


Tell them therefore, Thus says the Lord God; I will make this proverb to cease, and they shall no more use it as a proverb in Yisra᾽el; but say to them, The days are at hand, and the word of every vision.


###### Ezekiel 12:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/12:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/12:24)


כִּ֠י לֹ֣א יִהְיֶ֥ה ע֛וֹד כׇּל־חֲז֥וֹן שָׁ֖וְא וּמִקְסַ֣ם חָלָ֑ק בְּת֖וֹךְ בֵּ֥ית יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


For there shall be no more any vain vision nor flattering divination within the house of Yisra᾽el.


###### Ezekiel 12:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/12:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/12:25)


כִּ֣י**׀** אֲנִ֣י יְהֹוָ֗ה אֲדַבֵּר֙ אֵת֩ אֲשֶׁ֨ר אֲדַבֵּ֤ר דָּבָר֙ וְיֵ֣עָשֶׂ֔ה לֹ֥א תִמָּשֵׁ֖ךְ ע֑וֹד כִּ֣י בִימֵיכֶ֞ם בֵּ֣ית הַמֶּ֗רִי אֲדַבֵּ֤ר דָּבָר֙ וַעֲשִׂיתִ֔יו נְאֻ֖ם אֲדֹנָ֥י יֱהֹוִֽה׃{פ}


For I am the Lord: I will speak, and the word that I shall speak shall come to pass; it shall be no more prolonged: for in your days, O rebellious house, will I say the word, and will perform it, says the Lord God.


###### Ezekiel 12:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/12:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/12:26)


וַיְהִ֥י דְבַר־יְהֹוָ֖ה אֵלַ֥י לֵאמֹֽר׃


And the word of the Lord came to me, saying,


###### Ezekiel 12:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/12:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/12:27)


בֶּן־אָדָ֗ם הִנֵּ֤ה בֵֽית־יִשְׂרָאֵל֙ אֹֽמְרִ֔ים הֶחָז֛וֹן אֲשֶׁר־ה֥וּא חֹזֶ֖ה לְיָמִ֣ים רַבִּ֑ים וּלְעִתִּ֥ים רְחוֹק֖וֹת ה֥וּא נִבָּֽא׃


Son of man, behold, they of the house of Yisra᾽el say, The vision that he sees is for many days to come, and he prophesies of times that are far off.


###### Ezekiel 12:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/12:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/12:28)


לָכֵ֞ן אֱמֹ֣ר אֲלֵיהֶ֗ם כֹּ֤ה אָמַר֙ אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֔ה לֹא־תִמָּשֵׁ֥ךְ ע֖וֹד כׇּל־דְּבָרָ֑י אֲשֶׁ֨ר אֲדַבֵּ֤ר דָּבָר֙ וְיֵ֣עָשֶׂ֔ה נְאֻ֖ם אֲדֹנָ֥י יֱהֹוִֽה׃{פ}


Therefore say to them, Thus says the Lord God; None of my words shall be delayed any more, but the word which I have spoken shall be done, says the Lord God.

כֹּ֤ה אָמַר֙ אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֔ה ה֖וֹי עַל־הַנְּבִיאִ֣ים הַנְּבָלִ֑ים אֲשֶׁ֥ר הֹלְכִ֛ים אַחַ֥ר רוּחָ֖ם וּלְבִלְתִּ֥י רָאֽוּ׃


thus says the Lord God; Woe to the foolish prophets, that follow their own spirit, and have seen nothing!

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/13:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/13:4)


כְּשֻׁעָלִ֖ים בׇּחֳרָב֑וֹת נְבִיאֶ֥יךָ יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל הָיֽוּ׃


O Yisra᾽el, thy prophets are like foxes among ruins.


###### Ezekiel 13:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/13:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/13:5)


לֹ֤א עֲלִיתֶם֙ בַּפְּרָצ֔וֹת וַתִּגְדְּר֥וּ גָדֵ֖ר עַל־בֵּ֣ית יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל לַעֲמֹ֥ד בַּמִּלְחָמָ֖ה בְּי֥וֹם יְהֹוָֽה׃

###### Ezekiel 13:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/13:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/13:6)


חָ֤זוּ שָׁוְא֙ וְקֶ֣סֶם כָּזָ֔ב הָאֹֽמְרִים֙ נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֔ה וַיהֹוָ֖ה לֹ֣א שְׁלָחָ֑ם וְיִחֲל֖וּ לְקַיֵּ֥ם דָּבָֽר׃


They have seen vanity and lying divination; they say, The Lord says: and the Lord has not sent them: and they hope that he will confirm the word.


###### Ezekiel 13:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/13:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/13:7)


הֲל֤וֹא מַֽחֲזֵה־שָׁוְא֙ חֲזִיתֶ֔ם וּמִקְסַ֥ם כָּזָ֖ב אֲמַרְתֶּ֑ם וְאֹֽמְרִים֙ נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֔ה וַאֲנִ֖י לֹ֥א דִבַּֽרְתִּי׃{ס}


Have you not seen a vain vision, and have you not spoken a lying divination, in that you say, The Lord says; yet I have not spoken?


###### Ezekiel 13:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/13:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/13:8)


לָכֵ֗ן כֹּ֤ה אָמַר֙ אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֔ה יַ֚עַן דַּבֶּרְכֶ֣ם שָׁ֔וְא וַחֲזִיתֶ֖ם כָּזָ֑ב לָכֵן֙ הִנְנִ֣י אֲלֵיכֶ֔ם נְאֻ֖ם אֲדֹנָ֥י יֱהֹוִֽה׃


Therefore thus says the Lord God; Because you have spoken vanity, and seen lies, therefore behold, I am against you, says the Lord God,


###### Ezekiel 13:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/13:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/13:9)


וְהָיְתָ֣ה יָדִ֗י אֶֽל־הַנְּבִיאִ֞ים הַחֹזִ֣ים שָׁוְא֮ וְהַקֹּסְמִ֣ים כָּזָב֒ בְּס֧וֹד עַמִּ֣י לֹֽא־יִֽהְי֗וּ וּבִכְתָ֤ב בֵּֽית־יִשְׂרָאֵל֙ לֹ֣א יִכָּתֵ֔בוּ וְאֶל־אַדְמַ֥ת יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל לֹ֣א יָבֹ֑אוּ וִידַעְתֶּ֕ם כִּ֥י אֲנִ֖י אֲדֹנָ֥י יֱהֹוִֽה׃


and my hand shall be against the prophets that see vanity, and that divine lies: they shall not be in the counsel of my people, neither shall they be written in the writing of the house of Yisra᾽el, neither shall they enter into the land of Yisra᾽el; and you shall know that I am the Lord God.


###### Ezekiel 13:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/13:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/13:10)


יַ֣עַן וּבְיַ֜עַן הִטְע֧וּ אֶת־עַמִּ֛י לֵאמֹ֥ר שָׁל֖וֹם וְאֵ֣ין שָׁל֑וֹם וְהוּא֙ בֹּ֣נֶה חַ֔יִץ וְהִנָּ֛ם טָחִ֥ים אֹת֖וֹ תָּפֵֽל׃


Because, even because they have led my people astray, saying, Peace; and there was no peace; and if anyone builds a wall, these daub it with whitewash:


###### Ezekiel 13:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/13:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/13:11)


אֱמֹ֛ר אֶל־טָחֵ֥י תָפֵ֖ל וְיִפֹּ֑ל הָיָ֣ה**׀** גֶּ֣שֶׁם שׁוֹטֵ֗ף וְאַתֵּ֜נָה אַבְנֵ֤י אֶלְגָּבִישׁ֙ תִּפֹּ֔לְנָה וְר֥וּחַ סְעָר֖וֹת תְּבַקֵּֽעַ׃


say to them who daub it with whitewash that it shall fall: there shall be a deluge of rain; and you, O you great hailstones, shall fall; and a stormy wind shall break out.


###### Ezekiel 13:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/13:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/13:12)


וְהִנֵּ֖ה נָפַ֣ל הַקִּ֑יר הֲלוֹא֙ יֵאָמֵ֣ר אֲלֵיכֶ֔ם אַיֵּ֥ה הַטִּ֖יחַ אֲשֶׁ֥ר טַחְתֶּֽם׃{ס}


Lo, when the wall is fallen, shall it not be said to you, Where is the daubing with which you daubed it?


###### Ezekiel 13:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/13:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/13:13)


לָכֵ֗ן כֹּ֤ה אָמַר֙ אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֔ה וּבִקַּעְתִּ֥י רוּחַ־סְעָר֖וֹת בַּחֲמָתִ֑י וְגֶ֤שֶׁם שֹׁטֵף֙ בְּאַפִּ֣י יִֽהְיֶ֔ה וְאַבְנֵ֥י אֶלְגָּבִ֖ישׁ בְּחֵמָ֥ה לְכָלָֽה׃


Therefore thus says the Lord God; I will even rend it with a stormy wind in my fury; and there shall be a deluge of rain in my anger, and great hailstones in my fury to consume it.


###### Ezekiel 13:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/13:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/13:14)


וְהָ֨רַסְתִּ֜י אֶת־הַקִּ֨יר אֲשֶׁר־טַחְתֶּ֥ם תָּפֵ֛ל וְהִגַּעְתִּ֥יהוּ אֶל־הָאָ֖רֶץ וְנִגְלָ֣ה יְסֹד֑וֹ וְנָֽפְלָה֙ וּכְלִיתֶ֣ם בְּתוֹכָ֔הּ וִידַעְתֶּ֖ם כִּֽי־אֲנִ֥י יְהֹוָֽה׃


So will I break down the wall that you have daubed with whitewash, and bring it down to the ground, so that its foundation shall be laid bare, and it shall fall and you shall be consumed in the midst of it: and you shall know that I am the Lord.


###### Ezekiel 13:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/13:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/13:15)


וְכִלֵּיתִ֤י אֶת־חֲמָתִי֙ בַּקִּ֔יר וּבַטָּחִ֥ים אֹת֖וֹ תָּפֵ֑ל וְאֹמַ֤ר לָכֶם֙ אֵ֣ין הַקִּ֔יר וְאֵ֖ין הַטָּחִ֥ים אֹתֽוֹ׃


Thus will I spend my wrath upon the wall, and upon those who have daubed it with whitewash, and I will say to you. The wall is no more, nor they that daubed it;


###### Ezekiel 13:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/13:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/13:16)


נְבִיאֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֗ל הַֽנִּבְּאִים֙ אֶל־יְר֣וּשָׁלַ֔͏ִם וְהַחֹזִ֥ים לָ֖הּ חֲז֣וֹן שָׁלֹ֑ם וְאֵ֣ין שָׁלֹ֔ם נְאֻ֖ם אֲדֹנָ֥י יֱהֹוִֽה׃{פ}


namely, the prophets of Yisra᾽el who prophesied concerning Yerushalayim, and saw visions of peace concerning her, when there was no peace, says the Lord God.


###### Ezekiel 13:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/13:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/13:17)


וְאַתָּ֣ה בֶן־אָדָ֗ם שִׂ֤ים פָּנֶ֙יךָ֙ אֶל־בְּנ֣וֹת עַמְּךָ֔ הַמִּֽתְנַבְּא֖וֹת מִֽלִּבְּהֶ֑ן וְהִנָּבֵ֖א עֲלֵיהֶֽן׃


And thou son of man, set thy face against the daughters of thy people, who prophesy out of their own heart; and prophesy thou against them,


###### Ezekiel 13:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/13:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/13:18)


וְאָמַרְתָּ֞ כֹּה־אָמַ֣ר**׀** אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֗ה הוֹי֩ לִֽמְתַפְּר֨וֹת כְּסָת֜וֹת עַ֣ל**׀** כׇּל־אַצִּילֵ֣י יָדַ֗י וְעֹשׂ֧וֹת הַמִּסְפָּח֛וֹת עַל־רֹ֥אשׁ כׇּל־קוֹמָ֖ה לְצוֹדֵ֣ד נְפָשׁ֑וֹת הַנְּפָשׁוֹת֙ תְּצוֹדֵ֣דְנָה לְעַמִּ֔י וּנְפָשׁ֖וֹת לָכֶ֥נָה תְחַיֶּֽינָה׃


and say, Thus says the Lord God; Woe to the women that sew bands upon all arms, and make veils upon the head of people of every height, for hunting souls! will you hunt the souls of my people, and will you save your own souls alive?


###### Ezekiel 13:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/13:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/13:19)


וַתְּחַלֶּלְ֨נָה אֹתִ֜י אֶל־עַמִּ֗י בְּשַׁעֲלֵ֣י שְׂעֹרִים֮ וּבִפְת֣וֹתֵי לֶחֶם֒ לְהָמִ֤ית נְפָשׁוֹת֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר לֹֽא־תְמוּתֶ֔נָה וּלְחַיּ֥וֹת נְפָשׁ֖וֹת אֲשֶׁ֣ר לֹא־תִֽחְיֶ֑ינָה בְּכַ֨זֶּבְכֶ֔ם לְעַמִּ֖י שֹׁמְעֵ֥י כָזָֽב׃{ס}


And will you profane me among my people for handfuls of barley and for pieces of bread, to slay the souls that should not die, and to save the souls alive that should not live, by your lying to my people who hear your lies?


###### Ezekiel 13:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/13:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/13:20)


לָכֵ֞ן כֹּה־אָמַ֣ר**׀** אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֗ה הִנְנִ֤י אֶל־כִּסְּתוֹתֵיכֶ֙נָה֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר אַ֠תֵּ֠נָה מְצֹדְד֨וֹת שָׁ֤ם אֶת־הַנְּפָשׁוֹת֙ לְפֹ֣רְח֔וֹת וְקָרַעְתִּ֣י אֹתָ֔ם מֵעַ֖ל זְרוֹעֹֽתֵיכֶ֑ם וְשִׁלַּחְתִּי֙ אֶת־הַנְּפָשׁ֔וֹת אֲשֶׁ֥ר אַתֶּ֛ם מְצֹדְד֥וֹת אֶת־נְפָשִׁ֖ים לְפֹרְחֹֽת׃


Therefore, thus says the Lord God; Behold, I am against your armbands, with which you hunt the souls there, whisking them off to destruction; and I will tear them from your arms, and I will let the souls go free, namely the souls that you hunt, whisking them to destruction.


###### Ezekiel 13:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/13:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/13:21)


וְקָרַעְתִּ֞י אֶת־מִסְפְּחֹֽתֵיכֶ֗ם וְהִצַּלְתִּ֤י אֶת־עַמִּי֙ מִיֶּדְכֶ֔ן וְלֹא־יִהְי֥וּ ע֛וֹד בְּיֶדְכֶ֖ן לִמְצוּדָ֑ה וִידַעְתֶּ֖ן כִּֽי־אֲנִ֥י יְהֹוָֽה׃


Your headveils also I will tear, and deliver my people out of your hand, and they shall be no more in your hand to be hunted; and you shall know that I am the Lord.


###### Ezekiel 13:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/13:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/13:22)


יַ֣עַן הַכְא֤וֹת לֵב־צַדִּיק֙ שֶׁ֔קֶר וַאֲנִ֖י לֹ֣א הִכְאַבְתִּ֑יו וּלְחַזֵּק֙ יְדֵ֣י רָשָׁ֔ע לְבִלְתִּי־שׁ֛וּב מִדַּרְכּ֥וֹ הָרָ֖ע לְהַחֲיֹתֽוֹ׃


Because with lies you have cowed the heart of the righteous whom I have not grieved; and strengthened the hands of the wicked, that he should not return from his wicked way, by promising him life:


###### Ezekiel 13:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/13:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/13:23)


לָכֵ֗ן שָׁ֚וְא לֹ֣א תֶחֱזֶ֔ינָה וְקֶ֖סֶם לֹא־תִקְסַ֣מְנָה ע֑וֹד וְהִצַּלְתִּ֤י אֶת־עַמִּי֙ מִיֶּדְכֶ֔ן וִידַעְתֶּ֖ן כִּֽי־אֲנִ֥י יְהֹוָֽה׃


therefore you shall see no more vanity, nor divine divinations: for I will deliver my people out of your hand: and you shall know that I am the Lord.



## Chapter 14

###### Ezekiel 14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/14:1)


וַיָּב֤וֹא אֵלַי֙ אֲנָשִׁ֔ים מִזִּקְנֵ֖י יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל וַיֵּשְׁב֖וּ לְפָנָֽי׃{פ}


Then came certain of the elders of Yisra᾽el to me, and sat before me.


###### Ezekiel 14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/14:2)


וַיְהִ֥י דְבַר־יְהֹוָ֖ה אֵלַ֥י לֵאמֹֽר׃


And the word of the Lord came to me, saying,


###### Ezekiel 14:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/14:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/14:3)


בֶּן־אָדָ֗ם הָאֲנָשִׁ֤ים הָאֵ֙לֶּה֙ הֶעֱל֤וּ גִלּֽוּלֵיהֶם֙ עַל־לִבָּ֔ם וּמִכְשׁ֣וֹל עֲוֺנָ֔ם נָתְנ֖וּ נֹ֣כַח פְּנֵיהֶ֑ם הַאִדָּרֹ֥שׁ אִדָּרֵ֖שׁ לָהֶֽם׃{ס}


Son of man, these men have set up their idols in their heart, and put the stumblingblock of their iniquity before their faces: should I let myself be inquired of at all by them?


###### Ezekiel 14:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/14:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/14:4)


לָכֵ֣ן דַּבֵּר־א֠וֹתָ֠ם וְאָמַרְתָּ֨ אֲלֵיהֶ֜ם כֹּה־אָמַ֣ר**׀** אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֗ה אִ֣ישׁ אִ֣ישׁ מִבֵּ֣ית יִשְׂרָאֵ֡ל אֲשֶׁר֩ יַעֲלֶ֨ה אֶת־גִּלּוּלָ֜יו אֶל־לִבּ֗וֹ וּמִכְשׁ֤וֹל עֲוֺנוֹ֙ יָשִׂים֙ נֹ֣כַח פָּנָ֔יו וּבָ֖א אֶל־הַנָּבִ֑יא אֲנִ֣י יְהֹוָ֗ה נַעֲנֵ֧יתִי ל֦וֹ (בה) [בָ֖א] בְּרֹ֥ב גִּלּוּלָֽיו׃


Therefore speak to them, and say to them, Thus says the Lord God; Every man of the house of Yisra᾽el that sets up his idols in his heart, and puts the stumblingblock of his iniquity before his face, and comes to the prophet; I the Lord will answer him that comes with the multitude of his idols;


###### Ezekiel 14:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/14:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/14:5)


לְמַ֛עַן תְּפֹ֥שׂ אֶת־בֵּֽית־יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל בְּלִבָּ֑ם אֲשֶׁ֤ר נָזֹ֙רוּ֙ מֵֽעָלַ֔י בְּגִלּוּלֵיהֶ֖ם כֻּלָּֽם׃{ס}


that I may catch the house of Yisra᾽el in their own heart, because they are all estranged from me through their idols.


###### Ezekiel 14:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/14:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/14:6)


לָכֵ֞ן אֱמֹ֣ר**׀** אֶל־בֵּ֣ית יִשְׂרָאֵ֗ל כֹּ֤ה אָמַר֙ אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֔ה שׁ֣וּבוּ וְהָשִׁ֔יבוּ מֵעַ֖ל גִּלּוּלֵיכֶ֑ם וּמֵעַ֥ל כׇּל־תּוֹעֲבֹתֵיכֶ֖ם הָשִׁ֥יבוּ פְנֵיכֶֽם׃


Therefore say to the house of Yisra᾽el, Thus says the Lord God; Repent, and turn yourselves from your idols; and turn away your faces from all your abominations.


###### Ezekiel 14:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/14:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/14:7)


כִּי֩ אִ֨ישׁ אִ֜ישׁ מִבֵּ֣ית יִשְׂרָאֵ֗ל וּמֵהַגֵּר֮ אֲשֶׁר־יָג֣וּר בְּיִשְׂרָאֵל֒ וְיִנָּזֵ֣ר מֵאַחֲרַ֗י וְיַ֤עַל גִּלּוּלָיו֙ אֶל־לִבּ֔וֹ וּמִכְשׁ֣וֹל עֲוֺנ֔וֹ יָשִׂ֖ים נֹ֣כַח פָּנָ֑יו וּבָ֤א אֶל־הַנָּבִיא֙ לִדְרׇשׁ־ל֣וֹ בִ֔י אֲנִ֣י יְהֹוָ֔ה נַעֲנֶה־לּ֖וֹ בִּֽי׃


For everyone of the house of Yisra᾽el, and of the stranger that sojourns in Yisra᾽el, who separates himself from me, and sets up his idols in his heart, and puts the stumblingblock of his iniquity before his face, and comes to a prophet to inquire of him concerning me; I the Lord will answer him by myself:


###### Ezekiel 14:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/14:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/14:8)


וְנָתַתִּ֨י פָנַ֜י בָּאִ֣ישׁ הַה֗וּא וַהֲשִׂמֹתִ֙יהוּ֙ לְא֣וֹת וְלִמְשָׁלִ֔ים וְהִכְרַתִּ֖יו מִתּ֣וֹךְ עַמִּ֑י וִידַעְתֶּ֖ם כִּי־אֲנִ֥י יְהֹוָֽה׃{ס}


and I will set my face against that man, and will make him a sign and a proverb, and I will cut him off from the midst of my people; and you shall know that I am the Lord.


###### Ezekiel 14:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/14:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/14:9)


וְהַנָּבִ֤יא כִֽי־יְפֻתֶּה֙ וְדִבֶּ֣ר דָּבָ֔ר אֲנִ֤י יְהֹוָה֙ פִּתֵּ֔יתִי אֵ֖ת הַנָּבִ֣יא הַה֑וּא וְנָטִ֤יתִי אֶת־יָדִי֙ עָלָ֔יו וְהִ֨שְׁמַדְתִּ֔יו מִתּ֖וֹךְ עַמִּ֥י יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


And if the prophet be deceived when he has spoken a thing, I the Lord have deceived that prophet, and I will stretch out my hand upon him, and will destroy him from the midst of my people Yisra᾽el.


###### Ezekiel 14:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/14:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/14:10)


וְנָשְׂא֖וּ עֲוֺנָ֑ם כַּעֲוֺן֙ הַדֹּרֵ֔שׁ כַּעֲוֺ֥ן הַנָּבִ֖יא יִהְיֶֽה׃


And they shall bear the punishment of their iniquity: the punishment of the prophet shall be even as the punishment of him that seeks to him;


###### Ezekiel 14:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/14:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/14:11)


לְ֠מַ֠עַן לֹא־יִתְע֨וּ ע֤וֹד בֵּֽית־יִשְׂרָאֵל֙ מֵֽאַחֲרַ֔י וְלֹא־יִטַּמְּא֥וּ ע֖וֹד בְּכׇל־פִּשְׁעֵיהֶ֑ם וְהָיוּ־לִ֣י לְעָ֗ם וַאֲנִי֙ אֶהְיֶ֤ה לָהֶם֙ לֵֽאלֹהִ֔ים נְאֻ֖ם אֲדֹנָ֥י יֱהֹוִֽה׃{פ}


that the house of Yisra᾽el may no more go astray from me, neither be defiled any more by all their transgressions; but that they may be my people, and I may be their God, says the Lord God.


###### Ezekiel 14:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/14:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/14:12)


וַיְהִ֥י דְבַר־יְהֹוָ֖ה אֵלַ֥י לֵאמֹֽר׃


The word of the Lord came again to me, saying,


###### Ezekiel 14:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/14:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/14:13)


בֶּן־אָדָ֗ם אֶ֚רֶץ כִּ֤י תֶֽחֱטָא־לִי֙ לִמְעׇל־מַ֔עַל וְנָטִ֤יתִי יָדִי֙ עָלֶ֔יהָ וְשָׁבַ֥רְתִּי לָ֖הּ מַטֵּה־לָ֑חֶם וְהִשְׁלַחְתִּי־בָ֣הּ רָעָ֔ב וְהִכְרַתִּ֥י מִמֶּ֖נָּה אָדָ֥ם וּבְהֵמָֽה׃


Son of man, when the land sins against me by trespassing grievously, then will I stretch out my hand upon it, and will break its staff of bread, and will send famine upon it, and will cut off man and beast from it:


###### Ezekiel 14:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/14:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/14:14)


וְ֠הָי֠וּ שְׁלֹ֨שֶׁת הָאֲנָשִׁ֤ים הָאֵ֙לֶּה֙ בְּתוֹכָ֔הּ נֹ֖חַ (דנאל) [דָּנִיֵּ֣אל] וְאִיּ֑וֹב הֵ֤מָּה בְצִדְקָתָם֙ יְנַצְּל֣וּ נַפְשָׁ֔ם נְאֻ֖ם אֲדֹנָ֥י יֱהֹוִֽה׃


though these three men, Noaĥ, Dani᾽el, and Iyyov, were in it, they should only save their own lives by their righteousness, says the Lord God.


###### Ezekiel 14:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/14:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/14:15)


לוּ־חַיָּ֥ה רָעָ֛ה אַעֲבִ֥יר בָּאָ֖רֶץ וְשִׁכְּלָ֑תָּה וְהָיְתָ֤ה שְׁמָמָה֙ מִבְּלִ֣י עוֹבֵ֔ר מִפְּנֵ֖י הַחַיָּֽה׃


If I cause evil beasts to pass through the land, and they spoil it, so that it be desolate, that no man may pass through because of the beasts:


###### Ezekiel 14:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/14:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/14:16)


שְׁלֹ֨שֶׁת הָאֲנָשִׁ֣ים הָאֵלֶּה֮ בְּתוֹכָהּ֒ חַי־אָ֗נִי נְאֻם֙ אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֔ה אִם־בָּנִ֥ים וְאִם־בָּנ֖וֹת יַצִּ֑ילוּ הֵ֤מָּה לְבַדָּם֙ יִנָּצֵ֔לוּ וְהָאָ֖רֶץ תִּהְיֶ֥ה שְׁמָמָֽה׃


though these three men were in it, as I live, says the Lord God, they shall deliver neither sons nor daughters; they alone shall be saved, but the land shall be desolate.


###### Ezekiel 14:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/14:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/14:17)


א֛וֹ חֶ֥רֶב אָבִ֖יא עַל־הָאָ֣רֶץ הַהִ֑יא וְאָמַרְתִּ֗י חֶ֚רֶב תַּעֲבֹ֣ר בָּאָ֔רֶץ וְהִכְרַתִּ֥י מִמֶּ֖נָּה אָדָ֥ם וּבְהֵמָֽה׃


Or if I bring a sword upon that land, and say, Sword, go through the land; so that I cut off man and beast from it:


###### Ezekiel 14:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/14:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/14:18)


וּשְׁלֹ֨שֶׁת הָאֲנָשִׁ֣ים הָאֵ֘לֶּה֮ בְּתוֹכָהּ֒ חַי־אָ֗נִי נְאֻם֙ אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֔ה לֹ֥א יַצִּ֖ילוּ בָּנִ֣ים וּבָנ֑וֹת כִּ֛י הֵ֥ם לְבַדָּ֖ם יִנָּצֵֽלוּ׃


though these three men were in it, as I live, says the Lord God, they shall deliver neither sons nor daughters, but they alone shall be saved.


###### Ezekiel 14:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/14:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/14:19)


א֛וֹ דֶּ֥בֶר אֲשַׁלַּ֖ח אֶל־הָאָ֣רֶץ הַהִ֑יא וְשָׁפַכְתִּ֨י חֲמָתִ֤י עָלֶ֙יהָ֙ בְּדָ֔ם לְהַכְרִ֥ית מִמֶּ֖נָּה אָדָ֥ם וּבְהֵמָֽה׃


Or if I send a pestilence into that land, and pour out my fury upon it in blood, to cut off from it man and beast:


###### Ezekiel 14:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/14:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/14:20)


וְנֹ֨חַ (דנאל) [דָּנִיֵּ֣אל] וְאִיּוֹב֮ בְּתוֹכָהּ֒ חַי־אָ֗נִי נְאֻם֙ אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֔ה אִם־בֵּ֥ן אִם־בַּ֖ת יַצִּ֑ילוּ הֵ֥מָּה בְצִדְקָתָ֖ם יַצִּ֥ילוּ נַפְשָֽׁם׃{פ}


though Noaĥ, Dani᾽el, and Iyyov, were in it, as I live, says the Lord God, they shall deliver neither son nor daughter; they shall but save their own lives by their righteousness.


###### Ezekiel 14:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/14:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/14:21)


כִּי֩ כֹ֨ה אָמַ֜ר אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֗ה אַ֣ף כִּֽי־אַרְבַּ֣עַת שְׁפָטַ֣י׀הָרָעִ֡ים חֶ֠רֶב וְרָעָ֞ב וְחַיָּ֤ה רָעָה֙ וָדֶ֔בֶר שִׁלַּ֖חְתִּי אֶל־יְרוּשָׁלָ֑͏ִם לְהַכְרִ֥ית מִמֶּ֖נָּה אָדָ֥ם וּבְהֵמָֽה׃


For thus says the Lord God; How much more when I send my four severe judgments upon Yerushalayim, namely the sword, and the famine, and the evil beast, and the pestilence, to cut off from it man and beast?


###### Ezekiel 14:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/14:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/14:22)


וְהִנֵּ֨ה נֽוֹתְרָה־בָּ֜הּ פְּלֵטָ֗ה הַֽמּוּצָאִים֮ בָּנִ֣ים וּבָנוֹת֒ הִנָּם֙ יוֹצְאִ֣ים אֲלֵיכֶ֔ם וּרְאִיתֶ֥ם אֶת־דַּרְכָּ֖ם וְאֶת־עֲלִילוֹתָ֑ם וְנִחַמְתֶּ֗ם עַל־הָרָעָה֙ אֲשֶׁ֤ר הֵבֵ֙אתִי֙ עַל־יְר֣וּשָׁלַ֔͏ִם אֵ֛ת כׇּל־אֲשֶׁ֥ר הֵבֵ֖אתִי עָלֶֽיהָ׃


Yet, behold, therein shall be left a remnant to be brought out, both sons and daughters: behold, they shall come out to you, and you shall see their way and their doings: and you shall be comforted concerning the evil that I have brought upon Yerushalayim, even concerning all that I have brought upon it.


###### Ezekiel 14:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/14:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/14:23)


וְנִחֲמ֣וּ אֶתְכֶ֔ם כִּֽי־תִרְא֥וּ אֶת־דַּרְכָּ֖ם וְאֶת־עֲלִילוֹתָ֑ם וִידַעְתֶּ֗ם כִּי֩ לֹ֨א חִנָּ֤ם עָשִׂ֙יתִי֙ אֵ֣ת כׇּל־אֲשֶׁר־עָשִׂ֣יתִי בָ֔הּ נְאֻ֖ם אֲדֹנָ֥י יֱהֹוִֽה׃{פ}


And they shall console you, when you see their ways and their doings: and you shall know that not without cause have I done all that I have done in it, says the Lord God.



## Chapter 15

###### Ezekiel 15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/15:1)


וַיְהִ֥י דְבַר־יְהֹוָ֖ה אֵלַ֥י לֵאמֹֽר׃


And the word of the Lord came to me, saying,


###### Ezekiel 15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/15:2)


בֶּן־אָדָ֕ם מַה־יִּהְיֶ֥ה עֵץ־הַגֶּ֖פֶן מִכׇּל־עֵ֑ץ הַזְּמוֹרָ֕ה אֲשֶׁ֥ר הָיָ֖ה בַּעֲצֵ֥י הַיָּֽעַר׃


Son of man, What is the vine tree more than any tree, that vine branch which grew up among the trees of the forest?


###### Ezekiel 15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/15:3)


הֲיֻקַּ֤ח מִמֶּ֙נּוּ֙ עֵ֔ץ לַעֲשׂ֖וֹת לִמְלָאכָ֑ה אִם־יִקְח֤וּ מִמֶּ֙נּוּ֙ יָתֵ֔ד לִתְל֥וֹת עָלָ֖יו כׇּל־כֶּֽלִי׃


Can wood be taken from it to do any work? or will men take a peg of it for hanging on it any vessel?


###### Ezekiel 15:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/15:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/15:4)


הִנֵּ֥ה לָאֵ֖שׁ נִתַּ֣ן לְאׇכְלָ֑ה אֵת֩ שְׁנֵ֨י קְצוֹתָ֜יו אָכְלָ֤ה הָאֵשׁ֙ וְתוֹכ֣וֹ נָחָ֔ר הֲיִצְלַ֖ח לִמְלָאכָֽה׃


Behold, it is cast into the fire for fuel; the fire devours both ends of it, and the middle of it is burned. Is it fit for any work?


###### Ezekiel 15:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/15:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/15:5)


הִנֵּה֙ בִּֽהְיוֹת֣וֹ תָמִ֔ים לֹ֥א יֵעָשֶׂ֖ה לִמְלָאכָ֑ה אַ֣ף כִּי־אֵ֤שׁ אֲכָלַ֙תְהוּ֙ וַיֵּחָ֔ר וְנַעֲשָׂ֥ה ע֖וֹד לִמְלָאכָֽה׃{ס}


Behold, when it was whole, it was meet for no work: how much less shall it be meet yet for any work, when the fire has devoured it, and it is burned?


###### Ezekiel 15:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/15:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/15:6)


לָכֵ֗ן כֹּ֤ה אָמַר֙ אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֔ה כַּאֲשֶׁ֤ר עֵץ־הַגֶּ֙פֶן֙ בְּעֵ֣ץ הַיַּ֔עַר אֲשֶׁר־נְתַתִּ֥יו לָאֵ֖שׁ לְאׇכְלָ֑ה כֵּ֣ן נָתַ֔תִּי אֶת־יֹשְׁבֵ֖י יְרוּשָׁלָֽ͏ִם׃


Therefore thus says the Lord God; As the vine tree among the trees of the forest, which I have given to the fire for fuel, so will I give the inhabitants of Yerushalayim.


###### Ezekiel 15:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/15:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/15:7)


וְנָתַתִּ֤י אֶת־פָּנַי֙ בָּהֶ֔ם מֵהָאֵ֣שׁ יָצָ֔אוּ וְהָאֵ֖שׁ תֹּאכְלֵ֑ם וִֽידַעְתֶּם֙ כִּֽי־אֲנִ֣י יְהֹוָ֔ה בְּשׂוּמִ֥י אֶת־פָּנַ֖י בָּהֶֽם׃


And I will set my face against them; they shall go out from one fire, and another fire shall devour them; and you shall know that I am the Lord, when I set my face against them.


###### Ezekiel 15:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/15:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/15:8)


וְנָתַתִּ֥י אֶת־הָאָ֖רֶץ שְׁמָמָ֑ה יַ֚עַן מָ֣עֲלוּ מַ֔עַל נְאֻ֖ם אֲדֹנָ֥י יֱהֹוִֽה׃{פ}


And I will make the land desolate, because they have acted faithlessly, says the Lord God.



## Chapter 16

###### Ezekiel 16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:1)


וַיְהִ֥י דְבַר־יְהֹוָ֖ה אֵלַ֥י לֵאמֹֽר׃


And the word of the Lord came to me, saying,


###### Ezekiel 16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:2)


בֶּן־אָדָ֕ם הוֹדַ֥ע אֶת־יְרוּשָׁלַ֖͏ִם אֶת־תּוֹעֲבֹתֶֽיהָ׃


Son of man, cause Yerushalayim to know her abominations,


###### Ezekiel 16:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:3)


וְאָמַרְתָּ֞ כֹּה־אָמַ֨ר אֲדֹנָ֤י יֱהֹוִה֙ לִיר֣וּשָׁלַ֔͏ִם מְכֹרֹתַ֙יִךְ֙ וּמֹ֣לְדֹתַ֔יִךְ מֵאֶ֖רֶץ הַֽכְּנַעֲנִ֑י אָבִ֥יךְ הָאֱמֹרִ֖י וְאִמֵּ֥ךְ חִתִּֽית׃


and say, Thus says the Lord God to Yerushalayim; Thy birth and thy nativity is of the land of Kena῾an; thy father was an Emorite, and thy mother a Ḥittite,


###### Ezekiel 16:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:4)


וּמוֹלְדוֹתַ֗יִךְ בְּי֨וֹם הוּלֶּ֤דֶת אוֹתָךְ֙ לֹא־כׇרַּ֣ת שׇׁרֵּ֔ךְ וּבְמַ֥יִם לֹא־רֻחַ֖צְתְּ לְמִשְׁעִ֑י וְהׇמְלֵ֙חַ֙ לֹ֣א הֻמְלַ֔חַתְּ וְהׇחְתֵּ֖ל לֹ֥א חֻתָּֽלְתְּ׃


and as for thy birth, in the day thou wast born thy navel was not cut, neither wast thou washed in water for cleansing; thou wast not salted at all, nor swaddled at all.


###### Ezekiel 16:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:5)


לֹא־חָ֨סָה עָלַ֜יִךְ עַ֗יִן לַעֲשׂ֥וֹת לָ֛ךְ אַחַ֥ת מֵאֵ֖לֶּה לְחֻמְלָ֣ה עָלָ֑יִךְ וַֽתֻּשְׁלְכִ֞י אֶל־פְּנֵ֤י הַשָּׂדֶה֙ בְּגֹ֣עַל נַפְשֵׁ֔ךְ בְּי֖וֹם הֻלֶּ֥דֶת אֹתָֽךְ׃


No eyes pitied thee, to do any of these to thee, to have compassion upon thee; but thou wast cast out in the open field, in thy loathsomeness, in the day that thou wast born.


###### Ezekiel 16:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:6)


וָאֶעֱבֹ֤ר עָלַ֙יִךְ֙ וָֽאֶרְאֵ֔ךְ מִתְבּוֹסֶ֖סֶת בְּדָמָ֑יִךְ וָאֹ֤מַר לָךְ֙ בְּדָמַ֣יִךְ חֲיִ֔י וָאֹ֥מַר לָ֖ךְ בְּדָמַ֥יִךְ חֲיִֽי׃


And when I passed by thee, and saw thee weltering in thy blood, I said to thee, In thy blood live! yea, I said to thee, In thy blood live!


###### Ezekiel 16:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:7)


רְבָבָ֗ה כְּצֶ֤מַח הַשָּׂדֶה֙ נְתַתִּ֔יךְ וַתִּרְבִּי֙ וַֽתִּגְדְּלִ֔י וַתָּבֹ֖אִי בַּעֲדִ֣י עֲדָיִ֑ים שָׁדַ֤יִם נָכֹ֙נוּ֙ וּשְׂעָרֵ֣ךְ צִמֵּ֔חַ וְאַ֖תְּ עֵרֹ֥ם וְעֶרְיָֽה׃


I will cause thee to increase like the plant of the field. And thou didst increase, and grow big, and thou didst come to possess great attractions: thy breasts were firm, and thy hair was grown, and yet thou wast poorly clad and bare.


###### Ezekiel 16:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:8)


וָאֶעֱבֹ֨ר עָלַ֜יִךְ וָאֶרְאֵ֗ךְ וְהִנֵּ֤ה עִתֵּךְ֙ עֵ֣ת דֹּדִ֔ים וָאֶפְרֹ֤שׂ כְּנָפִי֙ עָלַ֔יִךְ וָאֲכַסֶּ֖ה עֶרְוָתֵ֑ךְ וָאֶשָּׁ֣בַֽע לָ֠ךְ וָאָב֨וֹא בִבְרִ֜ית אֹתָ֗ךְ נְאֻ֛ם אֲדֹנָ֥י יֱהֹוִ֖ה וַתִּֽהְיִי־לִֽי׃


Now when I passed by thee, and looked upon thee, behold, thy time was the time of love; and I spread my skirt over thee, and covered thy nakedness; yea, I swore to thee, and entered into a covenant with thee, says the Lord God, and thou didst become mine.


###### Ezekiel 16:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:9)


וָאֶרְחָצֵ֣ךְ בַּמַּ֔יִם וָאֶשְׁטֹ֥ף דָּמַ֖יִךְ מֵעָלָ֑יִךְ וָאֲסֻכֵ֖ךְ בַּשָּֽׁמֶן׃


Then I washed thee with water; indeed, I rinsed away thy blood from thee, and I anointed thee with oil.


###### Ezekiel 16:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:10)


וָאַלְבִּישֵׁ֣ךְ רִקְמָ֔ה וָאֶנְעֲלֵ֖ךְ תָּ֑חַשׁ וָאֶחְבְּשֵׁ֣ךְ בַּשֵּׁ֔שׁ וַאֲכַסֵּ֖ךְ מֶֽשִׁי׃


I clothed thee also with embroidered cloth, and shod thee with taĥash skin, and I girded thee about with fine linen, and I covered thee with silk.


###### Ezekiel 16:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:11)


וָאֶעְדֵּ֖ךְ עֶ֑דִי וָאֶתְּנָ֤ה צְמִידִים֙ עַל־יָדַ֔יִךְ וְרָבִ֖יד עַל־גְּרוֹנֵֽךְ׃


I decked thee also with ornaments, and I put bracelets upon thy hands, and a chain on thy neck,


###### Ezekiel 16:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:12)


וָ֥אֶתֵּֽן־נֶ֙זֶם֙ עַל־אַפֵּ֔ךְ וַעֲגִילִ֖ים עַל־אׇזְנָ֑יִךְ וַעֲטֶ֥רֶת תִּפְאֶ֖רֶת בְּרֹאשֵֽׁךְ׃


and I put a ring upon thy nose, and earrings in thy ears, and a beautiful crown upon thy head.


###### Ezekiel 16:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:13)


וַתַּעְדִּ֞י זָהָ֣ב וָכֶ֗סֶף וּמַלְבּוּשֵׁךְ֙ (ששי) [שֵׁ֤שׁ] וָמֶ֙שִׁי֙ וְרִקְמָ֔ה סֹ֧לֶת וּדְבַ֛שׁ וָשֶׁ֖מֶן (אכלתי) [אָכָ֑לְתְּ] וַתִּ֙יפִי֙ בִּמְאֹ֣ד מְאֹ֔ד וַֽתִּצְלְחִ֖י לִמְלוּכָֽה׃


Thus wast thou decked with gold and silver; and thy raiment was of fine linen, and silk, and embroidered cloth; thou didst eat fine flour, and honey, and oil: and thou wast very very beautiful, and thou wast fit for royal estate.


###### Ezekiel 16:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:14)


וַיֵּ֨צֵא לָ֥ךְ שֵׁ֛ם בַּגּוֹיִ֖ם בְּיׇפְיֵ֑ךְ כִּ֣י**׀** כָּלִ֣יל ה֗וּא בַּהֲדָרִי֙ אֲשֶׁר־שַׂ֣מְתִּי עָלַ֔יִךְ נְאֻ֖ם אֲדֹנָ֥י יֱהֹוִֽה׃


And thy renown went forth among the nations for thy beauty: for it was perfect through my splendour which I had bestowed upon thee, says the Lord God.


###### Ezekiel 16:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:15)


וַתִּבְטְחִ֣י בְיׇפְיֵ֔ךְ וַתִּזְנִ֖י עַל־שְׁמֵ֑ךְ וַתִּשְׁפְּכִ֧י אֶת־תַּזְנוּתַ֛יִךְ עַל־כׇּל־עוֹבֵ֖ר לוֹ־יֶֽהִי׃


But thou didst trust in thy own beauty, and didst play the harlot because of thy renown, and didst lavish thy fornications on everyone that passed by; his it was.


###### Ezekiel 16:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:16)


וַתִּקְחִ֣י מִבְּגָדַ֗יִךְ וַתַּֽעֲשִׂי־לָךְ֙ בָּמ֣וֹת טְלֻא֔וֹת וַתִּזְנִ֖י עֲלֵיהֶ֑ם לֹ֥א בָא֖וֹת וְלֹ֥א יִהְיֶֽה׃


And thou didst take some of thy garments, and didst make for thyself high places decked out in various colours, and on those thou didst play the harlot: such things may not come about, nor shall it continue.


###### Ezekiel 16:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:17)


וַתִּקְחִ֞י כְּלֵ֣י תִפְאַרְתֵּ֗ךְ מִזְּהָבִ֤י וּמִכַּסְפִּי֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר נָתַ֣תִּי לָ֔ךְ וַתַּעֲשִׂי־לָ֖ךְ צַלְמֵ֣י זָכָ֑ר וַתִּזְנִי־בָֽם׃


Thou hast also taken thy fair implements, made of my gold and my silver, and hast made for thyself male images, and hast committed lewdness with them,


###### Ezekiel 16:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:18)


וַתִּקְחִ֛י אֶת־בִּגְדֵ֥י רִקְמָתֵ֖ךְ וַתְּכַסִּ֑ים וְשַׁמְנִי֙ וּקְטׇרְתִּ֔י (נתתי) [נָתַ֖תְּ] לִפְנֵיהֶֽם׃


and thou hast taken thy embroidered garments, to cover them: and thou hast set my oil and my incense before them.


###### Ezekiel 16:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:19)


וְלַחְמִי֩ אֲשֶׁר־נָתַ֨תִּי לָ֜ךְ סֹ֣לֶת וָשֶׁ֤מֶן וּדְבַשׁ֙ הֶאֱכַלְתִּ֔יךְ וּנְתַתִּ֧יהוּ לִפְנֵיהֶ֛ם לְרֵ֥יחַ נִיחֹ֖חַ וַיֶּ֑הִי נְאֻ֖ם אֲדֹנָ֥י יֱהֹוִֽה׃


My bread also which I gave thee, fine flour, and oil, and honey, with which I fed thee, thou hast even set it before them for a sweet savour: and thus it was, says the Lord God.


###### Ezekiel 16:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:20)


וַתִּקְחִ֞י אֶת־בָּנַ֤יִךְ וְאֶת־בְּנוֹתַ֙יִךְ֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר יָלַ֣דְתְּ לִ֔י וַתִּזְבָּחִ֥ים לָהֶ֖ם לֶאֱכ֑וֹל הַמְעַ֖ט (מתזנותך) [מִתַּזְנוּתָֽיִךְ]׃


Moreover thou hast taken thy sons and thy daughters, whom thou hast born to me, and these hast thou sacrificed to them to be devoured. Were thy harlotries so small a matter?


###### Ezekiel 16:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:21)


וַֽתִּשְׁחֲטִ֖י אֶת־בָּנָ֑י וַֽתִּתְּנִ֔ים בְּהַעֲבִ֥יר אוֹתָ֖ם לָהֶֽם׃


And thou hast slain my children, and delivered them to cause them to pass through the fire for them?


###### Ezekiel 16:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:22)


וְאֵ֤ת כׇּל־תּוֹעֲבֹתַ֙יִךְ֙ וְתַזְנֻתַ֔יִךְ לֹ֥א (זכרתי) [זָכַ֖רְתְּ] אֶת־יְמֵ֣י נְעוּרָ֑יִךְ בִּֽהְיוֹתֵךְ֙ עֵירֹ֣ם וְעֶרְיָ֔ה מִתְבּוֹסֶ֥סֶת בְּדָמֵ֖ךְ הָיִֽית׃


And in all thy abominations and thy harlotries thou hast not remembered the days of thy youth, when thou was poorly clad and bare, weltering in thy blood.


###### Ezekiel 16:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:23)


וַיְהִ֕י אַחֲרֵ֖י כׇּל־רָעָתֵ֑ךְ א֣וֹי א֣וֹי לָ֔ךְ נְאֻ֖ם אֲדֹנָ֥י יֱהֹוִֽה׃


And it came to pass after all thy wickedness, (woe, woe to thee! says the Lord God;)


###### Ezekiel 16:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:24)


וַתִּבְנִי־לָ֖ךְ גָּ֑ב וַתַּעֲשִׂי־לָ֥ךְ רָמָ֖ה בְּכׇל־רְחֽוֹב׃


that thou hast also built to thee a platform, and hast made thee a high place in every street.


###### Ezekiel 16:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:25)


אֶל־כׇּל־רֹ֣אשׁ דֶּ֗רֶךְ בָּנִית֙ רָמָתֵ֔ךְ וַתְּתַֽעֲבִי֙ אֶת־יׇפְיֵ֔ךְ וַתְּפַשְּׂקִ֥י אֶת־רַגְלַ֖יִךְ לְכׇל־עוֹבֵ֑ר וַתַּרְבִּ֖י אֶת־[תַּזְנוּתָֽיִךְ] (תזנותך)׃


Thou hast built thy high place on every main street, and hast made thy beauty to be abhorred, and hast offered thyself to every one that passed by, and multiplied thy harlotries.


###### Ezekiel 16:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:26)


וַתִּזְנִ֧י אֶל־בְּנֵי־מִצְרַ֛יִם שְׁכֵנַ֖יִךְ גִּדְלֵ֣י בָשָׂ֑ר וַתַּרְבִּ֥י אֶת־תַּזְנֻתֵ֖ךְ לְהַכְעִיסֵֽנִי׃


Thou hast also played the harlot with the children of Miżrayim, thy neighbours of large flesh; and hast multiplied thy harlotry, to provoke me to anger.


###### Ezekiel 16:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:27)


וְהִנֵּ֨ה נָטִ֤יתִי יָדִי֙ עָלַ֔יִךְ וָאֶגְרַ֖ע חֻקֵּ֑ךְ וָאֶתְּנֵ֞ךְ בְּנֶ֤פֶשׁ שֹׂנְאוֹתַ֙יִךְ֙ בְּנ֣וֹת פְּלִשְׁתִּ֔ים הַנִּכְלָמ֖וֹת מִדַּרְכֵּ֥ךְ זִמָּֽה׃


Behold, therefore I have stretched out my hand over thee, and have diminished thy allotment, and delivered thee to the will of them that hate thee, the daughters of the Pelishtim, who are ashamed of thy lewd way.


###### Ezekiel 16:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:28)


וַתִּזְנִי֙ אֶל־בְּנֵ֣י אַשּׁ֔וּר מִבִּלְתִּ֖י שׇׂבְעָתֵ֑ךְ וַתִּזְנִ֕ים וְגַ֖ם לֹ֥א שָׂבָֽעַתְּ׃


Thou hast played the harlot also with the children of Ashshur, because thou wast insatiable; yea, thou hast played the harlot with them, and yet thou couldst not be satisfied.


###### Ezekiel 16:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:29)


וַתַּרְבִּ֧י אֶת־תַּזְנוּתֵ֛ךְ אֶל־אֶ֥רֶץ כְּנַ֖עַן כַּשְׂדִּ֑ימָה וְגַם־בְּזֹ֖את לֹ֥א שָׂבָֽעַתְּ׃


Thou hast moreover multiplied thy harlotry in the land of Kena῾an as far as Kasdim; and yet thou wast not satisfied with that.


###### Ezekiel 16:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:30)


מָ֤ה אֲמֻלָה֙ לִבָּתֵ֔ךְ נְאֻ֖ם אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֑ה בַּֽעֲשׂוֹתֵךְ֙ אֶת־כׇּל־אֵ֔לֶּה מַעֲשֵׂ֥ה אִשָּֽׁה־זוֹנָ֖ה שַׁלָּֽטֶת׃


How weak is thy heart, says the Lord God, seeing thou doest all these things, the work of an importunate harlot;


###### Ezekiel 16:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:31)


בִּבְנוֹתַ֤יִךְ גַּבֵּךְ֙ בְּרֹ֣אשׁ כׇּל־דֶּ֔רֶךְ וְרָמָתֵ֥ךְ (עשיתי) [עָשִׂ֖ית] בְּכׇל־רְח֑וֹב וְלֹא־[הָיִ֥ית] (הייתי) כַּזּוֹנָ֖ה לְקַלֵּ֥ס אֶתְנָֽן׃


in that thou didst build thy platform in every main street, and didst make thy high place in every street; yet thou hast not been like a harlot, for thou didst scorn payment,

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:32)


הָאִשָּׁ֖ה הַמְּנָאָ֑פֶת תַּ֣חַת אִישָׁ֔הּ תִּקַּ֖ח אֶת־זָרִֽים׃


but rather like a wife that commits adultery, who takes strangers instead of her husband!


###### Ezekiel 16:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:33)


לְכׇל־זֹנ֖וֹת יִתְּנוּ־נֵ֑דֶה וְאַ֨תְּ נָתַ֤תְּ אֶת־נְדָנַ֙יִךְ֙ לְכׇל־מְאַֽהֲבַ֔יִךְ וַתִּשְׁחֳדִ֣י אוֹתָ֗ם לָב֥וֹא אֵלַ֛יִךְ מִסָּבִ֖יב בְּתַזְנוּתָֽיִךְ׃


Men give gifts to all harlots: but thou givest thy gifts to all thy lovers, and hirest them, that they may come to thee on every side for thy lewd satisfaction.


###### Ezekiel 16:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:34)


וַיְהִי־בָ֨ךְ הֵ֤פֶךְ מִן־הַנָּשִׁים֙ בְּתַזְנוּתַ֔יִךְ וְאַחֲרַ֖יִךְ לֹ֣א זוּנָּ֑ה וּבְתִתֵּ֣ךְ אֶתְנָ֗ן וְאֶתְנַ֛ן לֹ֥א נִתַּן־לָ֖ךְ וַתְּהִ֥י לְהֶֽפֶךְ׃


And the contrary is in thee from other women, in that thou didst solicit to harlotry, and wast not solicited; and in that thou didst give hire, and no hire was given thee: thus thou art contrary.

Wherefore, O harlot, hear the word of the Lord:


###### Ezekiel 16:36

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:36)


כֹּה־אָמַ֞ר אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֗ה יַ֣עַן הִשָּׁפֵ֤ךְ נְחֻשְׁתֵּךְ֙ וַתִּגָּלֶ֣ה עֶרְוָתֵ֔ךְ בְּתַזְנוּתַ֖יִךְ עַל־מְאַֽהֲבָ֑יִךְ וְעַל֙ כׇּל־גִּלּוּלֵ֣י תוֹעֲבוֹתַ֔יִךְ וְכִדְמֵ֣י בָנַ֔יִךְ אֲשֶׁ֥ר נָתַ֖תְּ לָהֶֽם׃


Thus says the Lord God; Because thy shame was displayed, and thy nakedness laid bare through thy harlotries with thy lovers, and with all the idols of thy abominations, and because of the blood of thy children, which thou didst give to them;


###### Ezekiel 16:37

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:37](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:37)


לָ֠כֵ֠ן הִנְנִ֨י מְקַבֵּ֤ץ אֶת־כׇּל־מְאַהֲבַ֙יִךְ֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר עָרַ֣בְתְּ עֲלֵיהֶ֔ם וְאֵת֙ כׇּל־אֲשֶׁ֣ר אָהַ֔בְתְּ עַ֖ל כׇּל־אֲשֶׁ֣ר שָׂנֵ֑את וְקִבַּצְתִּי֩ אֹתָ֨ם עָלַ֜יִךְ מִסָּבִ֗יב וְגִלֵּיתִ֤י עֶרְוָתֵךְ֙ אֲלֵהֶ֔ם וְרָא֖וּ אֶת־כׇּל־עֶרְוָתֵֽךְ׃


behold, therefore I will gather all thy lovers, with whom thou hast taken pleasure, and all them that thou hast loved, with all them that thou hast hated; I will even gather them round about against thee, and will uncover thy nakedness to them, that they may see all thy nakedness.


###### Ezekiel 16:38

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:38](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:38)


וּשְׁפַטְתִּיךְ֙ מִשְׁפְּטֵ֣י נֹאֲפ֔וֹת וְשֹׁפְכֹ֖ת דָּ֑ם וּנְתַתִּ֕יךְ דַּ֥ם חֵמָ֖ה וְקִנְאָֽה׃


And I will judge thee, as women are judged that break wedlock or shed blood; and I will bring upon thee the blood of anger and jealousy.


###### Ezekiel 16:39

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:39](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:39)


וְנָתַתִּ֨י אֹתָ֜ךְ בְּיָדָ֗ם וְהָרְס֤וּ גַבֵּךְ֙ וְנִתְּצ֣וּ רָמֹתַ֔יִךְ וְהִפְשִׁ֤יטוּ אוֹתָךְ֙ בְּגָדַ֔יִךְ וְלָקְח֖וּ כְּלֵ֣י תִפְאַרְתֵּ֑ךְ וְהִנִּיח֖וּךְ עֵירֹ֥ם וְעֶרְיָֽה׃


And I will also give thee into their hand, and they shall throw down thy platform, and shall break down thy high places: they shall strip thee also of thy clothes, and shall take thy fair jewels, and leave thee naked and bare.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:40](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:40)

They shall also bring up a company against thee, and they shall stone thee with stones, and thrust thee through with their swords.

וְשָׂרְפ֤וּ בָתַּ֙יִךְ֙ בָּאֵ֔שׁ וְעָשׂוּ־בָ֣ךְ שְׁפָטִ֔ים לְעֵינֵ֖י נָשִׁ֣ים רַבּ֑וֹת וְהִשְׁבַּתִּיךְ֙ מִזּוֹנָ֔ה וְגַם־אֶתְנַ֖ן לֹ֥א תִתְּנִי־עֽוֹד׃

וַהֲנִחֹתִ֤י חֲמָתִי֙ בָּ֔ךְ וְסָ֥רָה קִנְאָתִ֖י מִמֵּ֑ךְ וְשָׁ֣קַטְתִּ֔י וְלֹ֥א אֶכְעַ֖ס עֽוֹד׃


So will I relieve my fury upon thee, and my jealousy shall depart from thee, and I will be quiet, and will be no more angry.

בַּת־אִמֵּ֣ךְ אַ֔תְּ גֹּעֶ֥לֶת אִישָׁ֖הּ וּבָנֶ֑יהָ וַאֲח֨וֹת אֲחוֹתֵ֜ךְ אַ֗תְּ אֲשֶׁ֤ר גָּֽעֲלוּ֙ אַנְשֵׁיהֶ֣ן וּבְנֵיהֶ֔ן אִמְּכֶ֣ן חִתִּ֔ית וַאֲבִיכֶ֖ן אֱמֹרִֽי׃


Thou art thy mother’s daughter, who loathed her husband and her children; and thou art the sister of thy sisters, who loathed their husbands and their children: your mother was a Ḥittite, and your father an Emorite.


###### Ezekiel 16:46

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:46](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:46)


וַאֲחוֹתֵ֨ךְ הַגְּדוֹלָ֤ה שֹׁמְרוֹן֙ הִ֣יא וּבְנוֹתֶ֔יהָ הַיּוֹשֶׁ֖בֶת עַל־שְׂמֹאולֵ֑ךְ וַאֲחוֹתֵ֞ךְ הַקְּטַנָּ֣ה מִמֵּ֗ךְ הַיּוֹשֶׁ֙בֶת֙ מִֽימִינֵ֔ךְ סְדֹ֖ם וּבְנוֹתֶֽיהָ׃

###### Ezekiel 16:47

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:47](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:47)


וְלֹ֤א בְדַרְכֵיהֶן֙ הָלַ֔כְתְּ וּכְתוֹעֲבוֹתֵיהֶ֖ן (עשיתי) [עָשִׂ֑ית] כִּמְעַ֣ט קָ֔ט וַתַּשְׁחִ֥תִי מֵהֵ֖ן בְּכׇל־דְּרָכָֽיִךְ׃


Yet hast thou not walked after their ways, nor done after their abominations: for after a very little while, thou wast more corrupt than they in all thy ways.


###### Ezekiel 16:48

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:48](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:48)


חַי־אָ֗נִי נְאֻם֙ אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֔ה אִם־עָֽשְׂתָה֙ סְדֹ֣ם אֲחוֹתֵ֔ךְ הִ֖יא וּבְנוֹתֶ֑יהָ כַּאֲשֶׁ֣ר עָשִׂ֔ית אַ֖תְּ וּבְנוֹתָֽיִךְ׃


As I live, says the Lord God, Sedom thy sister has not done, neither she nor her daughters, as thou hast done, thou and thy daughters.


###### Ezekiel 16:49

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:49](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:49)


הִנֵּה־זֶ֣ה הָיָ֔ה עֲוֺ֖ן סְדֹ֣ם אֲחוֹתֵ֑ךְ גָּא֨וֹן שִׂבְעַת־לֶ֜חֶם וְשַׁלְוַ֣ת הַשְׁקֵ֗ט הָ֤יָה לָהּ֙ וְלִבְנוֹתֶ֔יהָ וְיַד־עָנִ֥י וְאֶבְי֖וֹן לֹ֥א הֶחֱזִֽיקָה׃


Behold this was the iniquity of thy sister Sedom: she and her daughters had pride, surfeit of bread, and abundance of idleness, and yet she did not strengthen the hand of the poor and needy.


###### Ezekiel 16:50

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:50](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:50)


וַֽתִּגְבְּהֶ֔ינָה וַתַּעֲשֶׂ֥ינָה תוֹעֵבָ֖ה לְפָנָ֑י וָאָסִ֥יר אֶתְהֶ֖ן כַּאֲשֶׁ֥ר רָאִֽיתִי׃{ס}

###### Ezekiel 16:51

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:51](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:51)


וְשֹׁ֣מְר֔וֹן כַּחֲצִ֥י חַטֹּאתַ֖יִךְ לֹ֣א חָטָ֑אָה וַתַּרְבִּ֤י אֶת־תּוֹעֲבוֹתַ֙יִךְ֙ מֵהֵ֔נָּה וַתְּצַדְּקִי֙ אֶת־[אֲחוֹתַ֔יִךְ] (אחותך) בְּכׇל־תּוֹעֲבֹתַ֖יִךְ אֲשֶׁ֥ר (עשיתי) [עָשִֽׂית]׃


Nor did Shomeron commit half of thy sins; but thou hast multiplied thy abominations more than they, and hast justified thy sisters by all thy abominations which thou hast done.


###### Ezekiel 16:52

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:52](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:52)


גַּם־אַ֣תְּ**׀** שְׂאִ֣י כְלִמָּתֵ֗ךְ אֲשֶׁ֤ר פִּלַּלְתְּ֙ לַאֲחוֹתֵ֔ךְ בְּחַטֹּאתַ֛יִךְ אֲשֶׁר־הִתְעַ֥בְתְּ מֵהֵ֖ן תִּצְדַּ֣קְנָה מִמֵּ֑ךְ וְגַם־אַ֥תְּ בּ֙וֹשִׁי֙ וּשְׂאִ֣י כְלִמָּתֵ֔ךְ בְּצַדֶּקְתֵּ֖ךְ אַחְיוֹתֵֽךְ׃


Thou also, bear thy own disgrace, in that thou hast given judgment for thy sisters: through thy sins that thou hast committed more abominable than they, they are become more righteous than thou: yea, be thou confounded also, and bear thy shame, in that thou hast made thy sisters seem righteous.


###### Ezekiel 16:53

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:53](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:53)


וְשַׁבְתִּי֙ אֶת־שְׁבִ֣יתְהֶ֔ן אֶת־[שְׁב֤וּת] (שבית) סְדֹם֙ וּבְנוֹתֶ֔יהָ וְאֶת־[שְׁב֥וּת] (שבית) שֹׁמְר֖וֹן וּבְנוֹתֶ֑יהָ (ושבית) [וּשְׁב֥וּת] שְׁבִיתַ֖יִךְ בְּתוֹכָֽהְנָה׃


When I shall bring back their captivity, the captivity of Sedom and her daughters, and the captivity of Shomeron and her daughters, then will I bring back the captivity of thy captives in the midst of them:


###### Ezekiel 16:54

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:54](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:54)


לְמַ֙עַן֙ תִּשְׂאִ֣י כְלִמָּתֵ֔ךְ וְנִכְלַ֕מְתְּ מִכֹּ֖ל אֲשֶׁ֣ר עָשִׂ֑ית בְּנַחֲמֵ֖ךְ אֹתָֽן׃


that thou mayst bear thy own disgrace, and mayst be confounded in all that thou hast done, in that thou art a comfort to them.


###### Ezekiel 16:55

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:55](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:55)


וַאֲחוֹתַ֗יִךְ סְדֹ֤ם וּבְנוֹתֶ֙יהָ֙ תָּשֹׁ֣בְןָ לְקַדְמָתָ֔ן וְשֹֽׁמְרוֹן֙ וּבְנוֹתֶ֔יהָ תָּשֹׁ֖בְןָ לְקַדְמָתָ֑ן וְאַתְּ֙ וּבְנוֹתַ֔יִךְ תְּשֻׁבֶ֖ינָה לְקַדְמַתְכֶֽן׃


When thy sisters, Sedom and her daughters, shall return to their former estate, and Shomeron and her daughters shall return to their former estate, then thou and thy daughters shall return to your former estate.


###### Ezekiel 16:56

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:56](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:56)


וְל֤וֹא הָֽיְתָה֙ סְדֹ֣ם אֲחוֹתֵ֔ךְ לִשְׁמוּעָ֖ה בְּפִ֑יךְ בְּי֖וֹם גְּאוֹנָֽיִךְ׃


For was not thy sister Sedom a byword in thy mouth in the day of thy pride,


###### Ezekiel 16:57

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:57](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:57)


בְּטֶ֘רֶם֮ תִּגָּלֶ֣ה רָעָתֵךְ֒ כְּמ֗וֹ עֵ֚ת חֶרְפַּ֣ת בְּנוֹת־אֲרָ֔ם וְכׇל־סְבִיבוֹתֶ֖יהָ בְּנ֣וֹת פְּלִשְׁתִּ֑ים הַשָּׁאט֥וֹת אוֹתָ֖ךְ מִסָּבִֽיב׃


before thy wickedness was laid bare, it was at the time of the taunt of the daughters of Aram, and all that are round about her, the daughters of the Pelishtim, who despise thee round about?


###### Ezekiel 16:58

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:58](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:58)


אֶת־זִמָּתֵ֥ךְ וְאֶת־תּוֹעֲבוֹתַ֖יִךְ אַ֣תְּ נְשָׂאתִ֑ים נְאֻ֖ם יְהֹוָֽה׃{ס}


Thou hast borne thy lewdness and thy abominations, says the Lord.


###### Ezekiel 16:59

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:59](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:59)


כִּ֣י כֹ֤ה אָמַר֙ אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֔ה (ועשית) [וְעָשִׂ֥יתִי] אוֹתָ֖ךְ כַּאֲשֶׁ֣ר עָשִׂ֑ית אֲשֶׁר־בָּזִ֥ית אָלָ֖ה לְהָפֵ֥ר בְּרִֽית׃


For thus says the Lord God; I will even deal with thee as thou hast done, for thou hast despised the oath in breaking the covenant.


###### Ezekiel 16:60

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:60](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:60)


וְזָכַרְתִּ֨י אֲנִ֧י אֶת־בְּרִיתִ֛י אוֹתָ֖ךְ בִּימֵ֣י נְעוּרָ֑יִךְ וַהֲקִימוֹתִ֥י לָ֖ךְ בְּרִ֥ית עוֹלָֽם׃


Nevertheless I will remember my covenant with thee in the days of thy youth, and I will establish unto thee an everlasting covenant.


###### Ezekiel 16:61

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:61](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:61)


וְזָכַ֣רְתְּ אֶת־דְּרָכַ֘יִךְ֮ וְנִכְלַמְתְּ֒ בְּקַחְתֵּ֗ךְ אֶת־אֲחוֹתַ֙יִךְ֙ הַגְּדֹל֣וֹת מִמֵּ֔ךְ אֶל־הַקְּטַנּ֖וֹת מִמֵּ֑ךְ וְנָתַתִּ֨י אֶתְהֶ֥ן לָ֛ךְ לְבָנ֖וֹת וְלֹ֥א מִבְּרִיתֵֽךְ׃


Then thou shalt remember thy ways, and be ashamed, when thou shalt receive thy sisters, thy elder and thy younger: and I will give them to thee for daughters, but not on account of the covenant with thee.


###### Ezekiel 16:62

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:62](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:62)


וַהֲקִימֹתִ֥י אֲנִ֛י אֶת־בְּרִיתִ֖י אִתָּ֑ךְ וְיָדַ֖עַתְּ כִּֽי־אֲנִ֥י יְהֹוָֽה׃


And I will establish my covenant with thee; and thou shalt know that I am the Lord:


###### Ezekiel 16:63

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:63](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/16:63)


לְמַ֤עַן תִּזְכְּרִי֙ וָבֹ֔שְׁתְּ וְלֹ֨א יִֽהְיֶה־לָּ֥ךְ עוֹד֙ פִּתְח֣וֹן פֶּ֔ה מִפְּנֵ֖י כְּלִמָּתֵ֑ךְ בְּכַפְּרִי־לָךְ֙ לְכׇל־אֲשֶׁ֣ר עָשִׂ֔ית נְאֻ֖ם אֲדֹנָ֥י יֱהֹוִֽה׃{פ}


that thou mayst remember, and be put to shame, and never open thy mouth any more because of thy shame, when I have forgiven thee for all that thou hast done, says the Lord God.



## Chapter 17

###### Ezekiel 17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/17:1)


וַיְהִ֥י דְבַר־יְהֹוָ֖ה אֵלַ֥י לֵאמֹֽר׃


And the word of the Lord came to me, saying,


###### Ezekiel 17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/17:2)


בֶּן־אָדָ֕ם ח֥וּד חִידָ֖ה וּמְשֹׁ֣ל מָשָׁ֑ל אֶל־בֵּ֖ית יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


Son of man, propound a riddle, and speak a parable to the house of Yisra᾽el;


###### Ezekiel 17:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/17:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/17:3)


וְאָמַרְתָּ֞ כֹּה־אָמַ֣ר**׀** אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֗ה הַנֶּ֤שֶׁר הַגָּדוֹל֙ גְּד֤וֹל הַכְּנָפַ֙יִם֙ אֶ֣רֶךְ הָאֵ֔בֶר מָלֵא֙ הַנּוֹצָ֔ה אֲשֶׁר־ל֖וֹ הָרִקְמָ֑ה בָּ֚א אֶל־הַלְּבָנ֔וֹן וַיִּקַּ֖ח אֶת־צַמֶּ֥רֶת הָאָֽרֶז׃


and say, Thus says the Lord God; A great eagle with great wings and long pinions, rich in plumage of many colours, came to the Levanon, and took the top of the cedar:


###### Ezekiel 17:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/17:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/17:4)


אֵ֛ת רֹ֥אשׁ יְנִיקוֹתָ֖יו קָטָ֑ף וַיְבִיאֵ֙הוּ֙ אֶל־אֶ֣רֶץ כְּנַ֔עַן בְּעִ֥יר רֹכְלִ֖ים שָׂמֽוֹ׃


he broke off the topmost of its young twigs, and carried it into a land of trade; he set it in a city of merchants.


###### Ezekiel 17:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/17:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/17:5)


וַיִּקַּח֙ מִזֶּ֣רַע הָאָ֔רֶץ וַֽיִּתְּנֵ֖הוּ בִּשְׂדֵה־זָ֑רַע קָ֚ח עַל־מַ֣יִם רַבִּ֔ים צַפְצָפָ֖ה שָׂמֽוֹ׃


He took also of the seed of the land, and planted it in a fruitful field; he placed it by great waters, and set it like a willow tree.


###### Ezekiel 17:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/17:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/17:6)


וַיִּצְמַ֡ח וַיְהִי֩ לְגֶ֨פֶן סֹרַ֜חַת שִׁפְלַ֣ת קוֹמָ֗ה לִפְנ֤וֹת דָּֽלִיּוֹתָיו֙ אֵלָ֔יו וְשׇׁרָשָׁ֖יו תַּחְתָּ֣יו יִהְי֑וּ וַתְּהִ֣י לְגֶ֔פֶן וַתַּ֣עַשׂ בַּדִּ֔ים וַתְּשַׁלַּ֖ח פֹּרֹֽאות׃


And it grew, and became a spreading vine of low stature, whose tendrils turned towards him, and the roots of which were under him: so it became a vine, and brought forth branches, and sent out sprigs.


###### Ezekiel 17:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/17:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/17:7)


וַיְהִ֤י נֶֽשֶׁר־אֶחָד֙ גָּד֔וֹל גְּד֥וֹל כְּנָפַ֖יִם וְרַב־נוֹצָ֑ה וְהִנֵּה֩ הַגֶּ֨פֶן הַזֹּ֜את כָּפְנָ֧ה שׇׁרָשֶׁ֣יהָ עָלָ֗יו וְדָֽלִיּוֹתָיו֙ שִׁלְחָה־לּ֔וֹ לְהַשְׁק֣וֹת אוֹתָ֔הּ מֵעֲרֻג֖וֹת מַטָּעָֽהּ׃


But there was another great eagle with great wings and many feathers: and, behold, this vine did bend her roots towards him, and send out her tendrils towards him, from its plantations, that he might water it.


###### Ezekiel 17:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/17:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/17:8)


אֶל־שָׂ֥דֶה טּ֛וֹב אֶל־מַ֥יִם רַבִּ֖ים הִ֣יא שְׁתוּלָ֑ה לַעֲשׂ֤וֹת עָנָף֙ וְלָשֵׂ֣את פֶּ֔רִי לִהְי֖וֹת לְגֶ֥פֶן אַדָּֽרֶת׃


It was planted in a good soil by many waters, that it might bring forth branches, and that it might bear fruit, that it might be a goodly vine.


###### Ezekiel 17:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/17:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/17:9)


אֱמֹ֗ר כֹּ֥ה אָמַ֛ר אֲדֹנָ֥י יֱהֹוִ֖ה תִּצְלָ֑ח הֲלוֹא֩ אֶת־שׇׁרָשֶׁ֨יהָ יְנַתֵּ֜ק וְאֶת־פִּרְיָ֣הּ**׀** יְקוֹסֵ֣ס וְיָבֵ֗שׁ כׇּל־טַרְפֵּ֤י צִמְחָהּ֙ תִּיבָ֔שׁ וְלֹֽא־בִזְרֹ֤עַ גְּדוֹלָה֙ וּבְעַם־רָ֔ב לְמַשְׂא֥וֹת אוֹתָ֖הּ מִשׇּׁרָשֶֽׁיהָ׃


Say thou, Thus says the Lord God; Shall it prosper? shall he not pull up its roots, and cut off its fruit, that it wither? it shall wither in all its sprouting leaves; it will not need great power or many people to pluck it up by its roots.


###### Ezekiel 17:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/17:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/17:10)


וְהִנֵּ֥ה שְׁתוּלָ֖ה הֲתִצְלָ֑ח הֲלֹא֩ כְגַ֨עַת בָּ֜הּ ר֤וּחַ הַקָּדִים֙ תִּיבַ֣שׁ יָבֹ֔שׁ עַל־עֲרֻגֹ֥ת צִמְחָ֖הּ תִּיבָֽשׁ׃{פ}


Yea, behold, planted though it is, shall it prosper? shall it not utterly wither, when the east wind touches it? it shall wither in the plantations where it grew.


###### Ezekiel 17:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/17:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/17:11)


וַיְהִ֥י דְבַר־יְהֹוָ֖ה אֵלַ֥י לֵאמֹֽר׃


And the word of the Lord came to me, saying,


###### Ezekiel 17:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/17:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/17:12)


אֱמׇר־נָא֙ לְבֵ֣ית הַמֶּ֔רִי הֲלֹ֥א יְדַעְתֶּ֖ם מָה־אֵ֑לֶּה אֱמֹ֗ר הִנֵּה־בָ֨א מֶלֶךְ־בָּבֶ֤ל יְרוּשָׁלַ֙͏ִם֙ וַיִּקַּ֤ח אֶת־מַלְכָּהּ֙ וְאֶת־שָׂרֶ֔יהָ וַיָּבֵ֥א אוֹתָ֛ם אֵלָ֖יו בָּבֶֽלָה׃


Say now to the rebellious house, Know you not what these things mean? tell them, Behold, the king of Bavel is come to Yerushalayim, and has taken its king, and its princes, and has led them with him to Bavel;


###### Ezekiel 17:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/17:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/17:13)


וַיִּקַּח֙ מִזֶּ֣רַע הַמְּלוּכָ֔ה וַיִּכְרֹ֥ת אִתּ֖וֹ בְּרִ֑ית וַיָּבֵ֤א אֹתוֹ֙ בְּאָלָ֔ה וְאֶת־אֵילֵ֥י הָאָ֖רֶץ לָקָֽח׃


and has taken of the king’s seed, and made a covenant with him, and has put him on oath: he has also taken away the mighty of the land:


###### Ezekiel 17:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/17:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/17:14)


לִֽהְיוֹת֙ מַמְלָכָ֣ה שְׁפָלָ֔ה לְבִלְתִּ֖י הִתְנַשֵּׂ֑א לִשְׁמֹ֥ר אֶת־בְּרִית֖וֹ לְעׇמְדָֽהּ׃


that the kingdom might be lowly, that it might not lift itself up, but that by keeping his covenant it might stand.


###### Ezekiel 17:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/17:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/17:15)


וַיִּמְרׇד־בּ֗וֹ לִשְׁלֹ֤חַ מַלְאָכָיו֙ מִצְרַ֔יִם לָתֶת־ל֥וֹ סוּסִ֖ים וְעַם־רָ֑ב הֲיִצְלָ֤ח הֲיִמָּלֵט֙ הָעֹשֵׂ֣ה אֵ֔לֶּה וְהֵפֵ֥ר בְּרִ֖ית וְנִמְלָֽט׃


But he rebelled against him by sending his ambassadors to Miżrayim, that they might give him horses and much people. Shall he prosper? shall he that does such things escape? or shall he break the covenant, and yet be saved?


###### Ezekiel 17:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/17:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/17:16)


חַי־אָ֗נִי נְאֻם֮ אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִה֒ אִם־לֹ֗א בִּמְקוֹם֙ הַמֶּ֙לֶךְ֙ הַמַּמְלִ֣יךְ אֹת֔וֹ אֲשֶׁ֤ר בָּזָה֙ אֶת־אָ֣לָת֔וֹ וַאֲשֶׁ֥ר הֵפֵ֖ר אֶת־בְּרִית֑וֹ אִתּ֥וֹ בְתוֹךְ־בָּבֶ֖ל יָמֽוּת׃


As I live, says the Lord God, surely in the place where the king dwells that made him king, whose oath he despised, and whose covenant he broke, even with him in the midst of Bavel he shall die.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/17:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/17:17)


וְלֹא֩ בְחַ֨יִל גָּד֜וֹל וּבְקָהָ֣ל רָ֗ב יַעֲשֶׂ֨ה אוֹת֤וֹ פַרְעֹה֙ בַּמִּלְחָמָ֔ה בִּשְׁפֹּ֥ךְ סֹלְלָ֖ה וּבִבְנ֣וֹת דָּיֵ֑ק לְהַכְרִ֖ית נְפָשׁ֥וֹת רַבּֽוֹת׃

וּבָזָ֥ה אָלָ֖ה לְהָפֵ֣ר בְּרִ֑ית וְהִנֵּ֨ה נָתַ֥ן יָד֛וֹ וְכׇל־אֵ֥לֶּה עָשָׂ֖ה לֹ֥א יִמָּלֵֽט׃{ס}

לָכֵ֞ן כֹּה־אָמַ֨ר אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִה֮ חַי־אָ֒נִי֒ אִם־לֹ֗א אָֽלָתִי֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר בָּזָ֔ה וּבְרִיתִ֖י אֲשֶׁ֣ר הֵפִ֑יר וּנְתַתִּ֖יו בְּרֹאשֽׁוֹ׃


Therefore thus says the Lord God; As I live, surely my oath that he has despised, and my covenant that he has broken, even that will I recompense upon his own head.


###### Ezekiel 17:20

וּפָרַשְׂתִּ֤י עָלָיו֙ רִשְׁתִּ֔י וְנִתְפַּ֖שׂ בִּמְצוּדָתִ֑י וַהֲבִיאוֹתִ֣יהוּ בָבֶ֗לָה וְנִשְׁפַּטְתִּ֤י אִתּוֹ֙ שָׁ֔ם מַעֲל֖וֹ אֲשֶׁ֥ר מָעַל־בִּֽי׃

###### Ezekiel 18:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/18:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/18:20)


הַנֶּ֥פֶשׁ הַחֹטֵ֖את הִ֣יא תָמ֑וּת בֵּ֞ן לֹא־יִשָּׂ֣א**׀** בַּעֲוֺ֣ן הָאָ֗ב וְאָב֙ לֹ֤א יִשָּׂא֙ בַּעֲוֺ֣ן הַבֵּ֔ן צִדְקַ֤ת הַצַּדִּיק֙ עָלָ֣יו תִּֽהְיֶ֔ה וְרִשְׁעַ֥ת (רשע) [הָרָשָׁ֖ע] עָלָ֥יו תִּֽהְיֶֽה׃{ס}


The soul that sins, it shall die. The son shall not bear the iniquity of the father, neither shall the father bear the iniquity of the son: the righteousness of the righteous shall be upon him, and the wickedness of the wicked shall be upon him.


###### Ezekiel 18:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/18:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/18:21)


וְהָרָשָׁ֗ע כִּ֤י יָשׁוּב֙ מִכׇּל־חַטֹּאתָו֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר עָשָׂ֔ה וְשָׁמַר֙ אֶת־כׇּל־חֻקּוֹתַ֔י וְעָשָׂ֥ה מִשְׁפָּ֖ט וּצְדָקָ֑ה חָיֹ֥ה יִֽחְיֶ֖ה לֹ֥א יָמֽוּת׃


But if the wicked will turn from all his sins that he has committed, and keep all my statutes, and do that which is lawful and right, he shall surely live, he shall not die.


###### Ezekiel 18:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/18:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/18:22)


כׇּל־פְּשָׁעָיו֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר עָשָׂ֔ה לֹ֥א יִזָּכְר֖וּ ל֑וֹ בְּצִדְקָת֥וֹ אֲשֶׁר־עָשָׂ֖ה יִֽחְיֶֽה׃


None of his transgressions that he has committed shall be remembered against him: in his righteousness that he has done he shall live.


###### Ezekiel 18:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/18:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/18:23)


הֶחָפֹ֤ץ אֶחְפֹּץ֙ מ֣וֹת רָשָׁ֔ע נְאֻ֖ם אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֑ה הֲל֛וֹא בְּשׁוּב֥וֹ מִדְּרָכָ֖יו וְחָיָֽה׃{ס}


Have I any pleasure at all that the wicked should die? says the Lord God: and not that he should return from his ways, and live?


###### Ezekiel 18:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/18:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/18:24)


וּבְשׁ֨וּב צַדִּ֤יק מִצִּדְקָתוֹ֙ וְעָ֣שָׂה עָ֔וֶל כְּכֹ֨ל הַתּוֹעֵב֜וֹת אֲשֶׁר־עָשָׂ֧ה הָרָשָׁ֛ע יַעֲשֶׂ֖ה וָחָ֑י כׇּל־צִדְקֹתָ֤ו אֲשֶׁר־עָשָׂה֙ לֹ֣א תִזָּכַ֔רְנָה בְּמַעֲל֧וֹ אֲשֶׁר־מָעַ֛ל וּבְחַטָּאת֥וֹ אֲשֶׁר־חָטָ֖א בָּ֥ם יָמֽוּת׃


But when the righteous turns away from his righteousness, and commits iniquity, and does according to all the abominations that the wicked man does, shall he live? All his righteousness that he has done shall not be remembered: in his trespass that he has trespassed, and in his sin that he has sinned, in them shall he die.


###### Ezekiel 18:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/18:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/18:25)


וַאֲמַרְתֶּ֕ם לֹ֥א יִתָּכֵ֖ן דֶּ֣רֶךְ אֲדֹנָ֑י שִׁמְעוּ־נָא֙ בֵּ֣ית יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל הֲדַרְכִּי֙ לֹ֣א יִתָּכֵ֔ן הֲלֹ֥א דַרְכֵיכֶ֖ם לֹ֥א יִתָּכֵֽנוּ׃


Yet you say, The way of the Lord is unfair. Hear now, O house of Yisra᾽el; Is my way unfair? surely your ways are unfair!


###### Ezekiel 18:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/18:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/18:26)


בְּשׁוּב־צַדִּ֧יק מִצִּדְקָת֛וֹ וְעָ֥שָׂה עָ֖וֶל וּמֵ֣ת עֲלֵיהֶ֑ם בְּעַוְל֥וֹ אֲשֶׁר־עָשָׂ֖ה יָמֽוּת׃{ס}


When a righteous man turns away from his righteousness, and commits iniquity, and dies in them; for his iniquity that he has done he shall die.


###### Ezekiel 18:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/18:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/18:27)


וּבְשׁ֣וּב רָשָׁ֗ע מֵֽרִשְׁעָתוֹ֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר עָשָׂ֔ה וַיַּ֥עַשׂ מִשְׁפָּ֖ט וּצְדָקָ֑ה ה֖וּא אֶת־נַפְשׁ֥וֹ יְחַיֶּֽה׃


Again, when the wicked man turns away from his wickedness that he has committed, and does that which is lawful and right, he shall save his soul alive.


###### Ezekiel 18:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/18:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/18:28)


וַיִּרְאֶ֣ה (וישוב) [וַיָּ֔שׇׁב] מִכׇּל־פְּשָׁעָ֖יו אֲשֶׁ֣ר עָשָׂ֑ה חָי֥וֹ יִֽחְיֶ֖ה לֹ֥א יָמֽוּת׃


Because he considers, and turns away from all his transgressions that he has committed, he shall surely live, he shall not die.


###### Ezekiel 18:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/18:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/18:29)


וְאָֽמְרוּ֙ בֵּ֣ית יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל לֹ֥א יִתָּכֵ֖ן דֶּ֣רֶךְ אֲדֹנָ֑י הַדְּרָכַ֞י לֹ֤א יִתָּֽכְנוּ֙ בֵּ֣ית יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל הֲלֹ֥א דַרְכֵיכֶ֖ם לֹ֥א יִתָּכֵֽן׃


Yet the house of Yisra᾽el says, The way of the Lord is not fair. O house of Yisra᾽el, are my ways unfair? surely your ways are unfair!


###### Ezekiel 18:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/18:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/18:30)


לָכֵן֩ אִ֨ישׁ כִּדְרָכָ֜יו אֶשְׁפֹּ֤ט אֶתְכֶם֙ בֵּ֣ית יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל נְאֻ֖ם אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֑ה שׁ֤וּבוּ וְהָשִׁ֙יבוּ֙ מִכׇּל־פִּשְׁעֵיכֶ֔ם וְלֹא־יִהְיֶ֥ה לָכֶ֛ם לְמִכְשׁ֖וֹל עָוֺֽן׃


Therefore I will judge you, O house of Yisra᾽el, every one according to his ways, says the Lord God. Repent, and turn yourselves from all your transgressions; so that iniquity shall not be your ruin.


###### Ezekiel 18:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/18:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/18:31)


הַשְׁלִ֣יכוּ מֵעֲלֵיכֶ֗ם אֶת־כׇּל־פִּשְׁעֵיכֶם֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר פְּשַׁעְתֶּ֣ם בָּ֔ם וַעֲשׂ֥וּ לָכֶ֛ם לֵ֥ב חָדָ֖שׁ וְר֣וּחַ חֲדָשָׁ֑ה וְלָ֥מָּה תָמֻ֖תוּ בֵּ֥ית יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


Cast away from you all your transgressions, in which you have transgressed; and make for yourselves a new heart and a new spirit: for why will you die, O house of Yisra᾽el?


###### Ezekiel 18:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/18:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/18:32)


כִּ֣י לֹ֤א אֶחְפֹּץ֙ בְּמ֣וֹת הַמֵּ֔ת נְאֻ֖ם אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֑ה וְהָשִׁ֖יבוּ וִֽחְיֽוּ׃{פ}


For I have no pleasure in the death of him that dies, says the Lord God: therefore turn, and live.



## Chapter 19

###### Ezekiel 19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/19:1)


וְאַתָּה֙ שָׂ֣א קִינָ֔ה אֶל־נְשִׂיאֵ֖י יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


And you, take up a lamentation for the princes of Yisra᾽el,


###### Ezekiel 19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/19:2)


וְאָמַרְתָּ֗ מָ֤ה אִמְּךָ֙ לְבִיָּ֔א בֵּ֥ין אֲרָי֖וֹת רָבָ֑צָה בְּת֥וֹךְ כְּפִרִ֖ים רִבְּתָ֥ה גוּרֶֽיהָ׃


and say, What a lioness was thy mother! she lay down among lions, she nourished her whelps among young lions.


###### Ezekiel 19:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/19:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/19:3)


וַתַּ֛עַל אֶחָ֥ד מִגֻּרֶ֖יהָ כְּפִ֣יר הָיָ֑ה וַיִּלְמַ֥ד לִטְרׇף־טֶ֖רֶף אָדָ֥ם אָכָֽל׃


And she brought up one of her whelps: it became a young lion, and it learned to catch the prey; it devoured men.


###### Ezekiel 19:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/19:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/19:4)


וַיִּשְׁמְע֥וּ אֵלָ֛יו גּוֹיִ֖ם בְּשַׁחְתָּ֣ם נִתְפָּ֑שׂ וַיְבִאֻ֥הוּ בַחַחִ֖ים אֶל־אֶ֥רֶץ מִצְרָֽיִם׃


The nations also heard of him; he was taken in their pit, and they brought him with hooks to the land of Miżrayim.


###### Ezekiel 19:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/19:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/19:5)


וַתֵּ֙רֶא֙ כִּ֣י נֽוֹחֲלָ֔ה אָבְדָ֖ה תִּקְוָתָ֑הּ וַתִּקַּ֛ח אֶחָ֥ד מִגֻּרֶ֖יהָ כְּפִ֥יר שָׂמָֽתְהוּ׃


Now when she saw that though she had waited, her hope was lost, then she took another of her whelps, and made him a young lion.


###### Ezekiel 19:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/19:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/19:6)


וַיִּתְהַלֵּ֥ךְ בְּתוֹךְ־אֲרָי֖וֹת כְּפִ֣יר הָיָ֑ה וַיִּלְמַ֥ד לִטְרׇף־טֶ֖רֶף אָדָ֥ם אָכָֽל׃


And he went up and down among the lions, he became a young lion, and learned to catch the prey, and devoured men.


###### Ezekiel 19:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/19:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/19:7)


וַיֵּ֙דַע֙ אַלְמְנוֹתָ֔יו וְעָרֵיהֶ֖ם הֶחֱרִ֑יב וַתֵּ֤שַׁם אֶ֙רֶץ֙ וּמְלֹאָ֔הּ מִקּ֖וֹל שַׁאֲגָתֽוֹ׃


And he knew their palaces, and he laid waste their cities; and the land and all that was in it was appalled by the noise of his roaring.


###### Ezekiel 19:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/19:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/19:8)


וַיִּתְּנ֨וּ עָלָ֥יו גּוֹיִ֛ם סָבִ֖יב מִמְּדִינ֑וֹת וַֽיִּפְרְשׂ֥וּ עָלָ֛יו רִשְׁתָּ֖ם בְּשַׁחְתָּ֥ם נִתְפָּֽשׂ׃


Then the nations set on him on every side from the provinces, and spread their net over him: he was taken in their pit.


###### Ezekiel 19:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/19:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/19:9)


וַֽיִּתְּנֻ֤הוּ בַסּ֙וּגַר֙ בַּחַחִ֔ים וַיְבִאֻ֖הוּ אֶל־מֶ֣לֶךְ בָּבֶ֑ל יְבִאֻ֙הוּ֙ בַּמְּצֹד֔וֹת לְמַ֗עַן לֹא־יִשָּׁמַ֥ע קוֹל֛וֹ ע֖וֹד אֶל־הָרֵ֥י יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃{פ}


And they put him in a cage with hooks, and brought him to the king of Bavel: they brought him into strongholds, that his voice should no more be heard upon the mountains of Yisra᾽el.


###### Ezekiel 19:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/19:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/19:10)


אִמְּךָ֥ כַגֶּ֛פֶן בְּדָמְךָ֖ עַל־מַ֣יִם שְׁתוּלָ֑ה פֹּֽרִיָּה֙ וַעֲנֵפָ֔ה הָיְתָ֖ה מִמַּ֥יִם רַבִּֽים׃


Thy mother is like the vine in thy likeness, planted by the waters: she was fruitful and full of branches by reason of many waters.


###### Ezekiel 19:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/19:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/19:11)


וַיִּֽהְיוּ־לָ֞הּ מַטּ֣וֹת עֹ֗ז אֶל־שִׁבְטֵי֙ מֹֽשְׁלִ֔ים וַתִּגְבַּ֥הּ קוֹמָת֖וֹ עַל־בֵּ֣ין עֲבֹתִ֑ים וַיֵּרָ֣א בְגׇבְה֔וֹ בְּרֹ֖ב דָּלִיֹּתָֽיו׃


And she had strong rods for the sceptres of them that bore rule, and its stature was exalted among the thick branches, and it appeared in its height with the multitude of its tendrils.


###### Ezekiel 19:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/19:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/19:12)


וַתֻּתַּ֤שׁ בְּחֵמָה֙ לָאָ֣רֶץ הֻשְׁלָ֔כָה וְר֥וּחַ הַקָּדִ֖ים הוֹבִ֣ישׁ פִּרְיָ֑הּ הִתְפָּרְק֧וּ וְיָבֵ֛שׁוּ מַטֵּ֥ה עֻזָּ֖הּ אֵ֥שׁ אֲכָלָֽתְהוּ׃


But she was plucked up in fury, she was cast down to the ground, and the east wind dried up her fruit: her strong rods were broken and withered; the fire consumed it.


###### Ezekiel 19:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/19:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/19:13)


וְעַתָּ֖ה שְׁתוּלָ֣ה בַמִּדְבָּ֑ר בְּאֶ֖רֶץ צִיָּ֥ה וְצָמָֽא׃


And now she is planted in the wilderness, in a dry and thirsty ground.


###### Ezekiel 19:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/19:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/19:14)


וַתֵּצֵ֨א אֵ֜שׁ מִמַּטֵּ֤ה בַדֶּ֙יהָ֙ פִּרְיָ֣הּ אָכָ֔לָה וְלֹא־הָ֥יָה בָ֛הּ מַטֵּה־עֹ֖ז שֵׁ֣בֶט לִמְשׁ֑וֹל קִ֥ינָה הִ֖יא וַתְּהִ֥י לְקִינָֽה׃{פ}


And fire is gone out of the rod of her branches; it has devoured her fruit, so that she has no strong rod to be a sceptre to rule. This is a lamentation, and a lamentation it has become.



## Chapter 20

###### Ezekiel 20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/20:1)


וַיְהִ֣י**׀** בַּשָּׁנָ֣ה הַשְּׁבִיעִ֗ית בַּחֲמִשִׁי֙ בֶּעָשׂ֣וֹר לַחֹ֔דֶשׁ בָּ֧אוּ אֲנָשִׁ֛ים מִזִּקְנֵ֥י יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל לִדְרֹ֣שׁ אֶת־יְהֹוָ֑ה וַיֵּשְׁב֖וּ לְפָנָֽי׃{ס}


And it came to pass in the seventh year, in the fifth month, on the tenth day of the month, that certain of the elders of Yisra᾽el came to inquire of the Lord, and sat before me.


###### Ezekiel 20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/20:2)


וַיְהִ֥י דְבַר־יְהֹוָ֖ה אֵלַ֥י לֵאמֹֽר׃


Then the word of the Lord came to me, saying,


###### Ezekiel 20:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/20:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/20:3)


בֶּן־אָדָ֗ם דַּבֵּ֞ר אֶת־זִקְנֵ֤י יִשְׂרָאֵל֙ וְאָמַרְתָּ֣ אֲלֵהֶ֗ם כֹּ֤ה אָמַר֙ אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֔ה הֲלִדְרֹ֥שׁ אֹתִ֖י אַתֶּ֣ם בָּאִ֑ים חַי־אָ֙נִי֙ אִם־אִדָּרֵ֣שׁ לָכֶ֔ם נְאֻ֖ם אֲדֹנָ֥י יֱהֹוִֽה׃


Son of man, speak to the elders of Yisra᾽el, and say to them, Thus says the Lord God; Are you come to inquire of me? As I live, says the Lord God, I will not be inquired of by you.


###### Ezekiel 20:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/20:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/20:4)

וְאָמַרְתָּ֣ אֲלֵיהֶ֗ם כֹּה־אָמַר֮ אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִה֒ בְּיוֹם֙ בׇּחֳרִ֣י בְיִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל וָאֶשָּׂ֣א יָדִ֗י לְזֶ֙רַע֙ בֵּ֣ית יַעֲקֹ֔ב וָאִוָּדַ֥ע לָהֶ֖ם בְּאֶ֣רֶץ מִצְרָ֑יִם וָאֶשָּׂ֨א יָדִ֤י לָהֶם֙ לֵאמֹ֔ר אֲנִ֖י יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֵיכֶֽם׃


and say to them, Thus says the Lord God; In the day when I chose Yisra᾽el and lifted up my hand to the seed of the house of Ya῾aqov, and made myself known to them in the land of Miżrayim, when I lifted up my hand to them, saying, I am the Lord your God;


###### Ezekiel 20:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/20:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/20:6)


בַּיּ֣וֹם הַה֗וּא נָשָׂ֤אתִי יָדִי֙ לָהֶ֔ם לְהוֹצִיאָ֖ם מֵאֶ֣רֶץ מִצְרָ֑יִם אֶל־אֶ֜רֶץ אֲשֶׁר־תַּ֣רְתִּי לָהֶ֗ם זָבַ֤ת חָלָב֙ וּדְבַ֔שׁ צְבִ֥י הִ֖יא לְכׇל־הָאֲרָצֽוֹת׃
