###### Ezekiel 20:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/20:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/20:7)


וָאֹמַ֣ר אֲלֵהֶ֗ם אִ֣ישׁ שִׁקּוּצֵ֤י עֵינָיו֙ הַשְׁלִ֔יכוּ וּבְגִלּוּלֵ֥י מִצְרַ֖יִם אַל־תִּטַּמָּ֑אוּ אֲנִ֖י יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֵיכֶֽם׃


then I said to them, Cast away every man the abominations of his eyes, and do not defile yourselves with the idols of Miżrayim: I am the Lord your God.


###### Ezekiel 20:8

וַיַּמְרוּ־בִ֗י וְלֹ֤א אָבוּ֙ לִשְׁמֹ֣עַ אֵלַ֔י אִ֣ישׁ אֶת־שִׁקּוּצֵ֤י עֵֽינֵיהֶם֙ לֹ֣א הִשְׁלִ֔יכוּ וְאֶת־גִּלּוּלֵ֥י מִצְרַ֖יִם לֹ֣א עָזָ֑בוּ וָאֹמַ֞ר לִשְׁפֹּ֧ךְ חֲמָתִ֣י עֲלֵיהֶ֗ם לְכַלּ֤וֹת אַפִּי֙ בָּהֶ֔ם בְּת֖וֹךְ אֶ֥רֶץ מִצְרָֽיִם׃


But they rebelled against me, and would not hearken to me: they did not cast away every man the abominations of their eyes, neither did they forsake the idols of Miżrayim: then I said, I will pour out my fury upon them, to accomplish my anger against them in the midst of the land of Miżrayim.

But the house of Yisra᾽el rebelled against me in the wilderness: they did not follow my statutes, and they rejected my judgments, which if a man do, he may live by them; and my sabbaths they greatly profaned: then I said, I would pour out my fury upon them in the wilderness, to consume them.

###### Ezekiel 20:21

וַיַּמְרוּ־בִ֣י הַבָּנִ֗ים בְּחֻקּוֹתַ֣י לֹֽא־הָ֠לָ֠כוּ וְאֶת־מִשְׁפָּטַ֨י לֹֽא־שָׁמְר֜וּ לַעֲשׂ֣וֹת אוֹתָ֗ם אֲשֶׁר֩ יַעֲשֶׂ֨ה אוֹתָ֤ם הָאָדָם֙ וָחַ֣י בָּהֶ֔ם אֶת־שַׁבְּתוֹתַ֖י חִלֵּ֑לוּ וָאֹמַ֞ר לִשְׁפֹּ֧ךְ חֲמָתִ֣י עֲלֵיהֶ֗ם לְכַלּ֥וֹת אַפִּ֛י בָּ֖ם בַּמִּדְבָּֽר׃

###### Ezekiel 20:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/20:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/20:28)


וָאֲבִיאֵם֙ אֶל־הָאָ֔רֶץ אֲשֶׁ֤ר נָשָׂ֙אתִי֙ אֶת־יָדִ֔י לָתֵ֥ת אוֹתָ֖הּ לָהֶ֑ם וַיִּרְאוּ֩ כׇל־גִּבְעָ֨ה רָמָ֜ה וְכׇל־עֵ֣ץ עָבֹ֗ת וַיִּזְבְּחוּ־שָׁ֤ם אֶת־זִבְחֵיהֶם֙ וַיִּתְּנוּ־שָׁם֙ כַּ֣עַס קׇרְבָּנָ֔ם וַיָּשִׂ֣ימוּ שָׁ֗ם רֵ֚יחַ נִיח֣וֹחֵיהֶ֔ם וַיַּסִּ֥יכוּ שָׁ֖ם אֶת־נִסְכֵּיהֶֽם׃


For when I had brought them into the land, about which I lifted up my hand to give it to them, then they saw every high hill, and all the thick leaved trees, and there they offered their sacrifices, and there they presented the provocations of their offering: there also they made their sweet savour, and there they poured out their drink offerings.


###### Ezekiel 20:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/20:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/20:29)


וָאֹמַ֣ר אֲלֵהֶ֔ם מָ֣ה הַבָּמָ֔ה אֲשֶׁר־אַתֶּ֥ם הַבָּאִ֖ים שָׁ֑ם וַיִּקָּרֵ֤א שְׁמָהּ֙ בָּמָ֔ה עַ֖ד הַיּ֥וֹם הַזֶּֽה׃{ס}


Then I said to them, What is the high place to which you go? And its name is called Bama to this day.


###### Ezekiel 20:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/20:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/20:30)


לָכֵ֞ן אֱמֹ֣ר**׀** אֶל־בֵּ֣ית יִשְׂרָאֵ֗ל כֹּ֤ה אָמַר֙ אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֔ה הַבְּדֶ֥רֶךְ אֲבוֹתֵיכֶ֖ם אַתֶּ֣ם נִטְמְאִ֑ים וְאַחֲרֵ֥י שִׁקּֽוּצֵיהֶ֖ם אַתֶּ֥ם זֹנִֽים׃


Therefore say to the house of Yisra᾽el, Thus says the Lord God; Are you polluted after the manner of your fathers? and do you go astray after their abominations?


###### Ezekiel 20:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/20:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/20:31)


וּבִשְׂאֵ֣ת מַתְּנֹתֵיכֶ֡ם בְּֽהַעֲבִיר֩ בְּנֵיכֶ֨ם בָּאֵ֜שׁ אַתֶּם֩ נִטְמְאִ֤֨ים לְכׇל־גִּלּֽוּלֵיכֶם֙ עַד־הַיּ֔וֹם{ס}וַאֲנִ֛י אִדָּרֵ֥שׁ לָכֶ֖ם בֵּ֣ית יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל חַי־אָ֗נִי נְאֻם֙ אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֔ה אִם־אִדָּרֵ֖שׁ לָכֶֽם׃


For when you offer your gifts, and make your sons pass through the fire, you defile yourselves with all your idols, to this day: and shall I be inquired of by you, O house of Yisra᾽el? As I live, says the Lord God, I will not be inquired of by you.


###### Ezekiel 20:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/20:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/20:32)


וְהָֽעֹלָה֙ עַל־ר֣וּחֲכֶ֔ם הָי֖וֹ לֹ֣א תִֽהְיֶ֑ה אֲשֶׁ֣ר**׀** אַתֶּ֣ם אֹמְרִ֗ים נִֽהְיֶ֤ה כַגּוֹיִם֙ כְּמִשְׁפְּח֣וֹת הָאֲרָצ֔וֹת לְשָׁרֵ֖ת עֵ֥ץ וָאָֽבֶן׃


And that which comes into your mind shall never come about, that you say, We will be like the nations, like the families of the countries, to serve wood and stone.


###### Ezekiel 20:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/20:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/20:33)


חַי־אָ֕נִי נְאֻ֖ם אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֑ה אִם־לֹ֠א בְּיָ֨ד חֲזָקָ֜ה וּבִזְר֧וֹעַ נְטוּיָ֛ה וּבְחֵמָ֥ה שְׁפוּכָ֖ה אֶמְל֥וֹךְ עֲלֵיכֶֽם׃


As I live, says the Lord God, surely with a mighty hand, and with a stretched out arm, and with anger poured out, will I be king over you:


###### Ezekiel 20:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/20:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/20:34)


וְהוֹצֵאתִ֤י אֶתְכֶם֙ מִן־הָ֣עַמִּ֔ים וְקִבַּצְתִּ֣י אֶתְכֶ֔ם מִן־הָ֣אֲרָצ֔וֹת אֲשֶׁ֥ר נְפוֹצֹתֶ֖ם בָּ֑ם בְּיָ֤ד חֲזָקָה֙ וּבִזְר֣וֹעַ נְטוּיָ֔ה וּבְחֵמָ֖ה שְׁפוּכָֽה׃


and I will bring you out from the peoples and will gather you out of the countries in which you are scattered, with a mighty hand, and with a stretched out arm, and with anger poured out.


###### Ezekiel 20:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/20:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/20:35)


וְהֵבֵאתִ֣י אֶתְכֶ֔ם אֶל־מִדְבַּ֖ר הָעַמִּ֑ים וְנִשְׁפַּטְתִּ֤י אִתְּכֶם֙ שָׁ֔ם פָּנִ֖ים אֶל־פָּנִֽים׃

כַּאֲשֶׁ֤ר נִשְׁפַּ֙טְתִּי֙ אֶת־אֲב֣וֹתֵיכֶ֔ם בְּמִדְבַּ֖ר אֶ֣רֶץ מִצְרָ֑יִם כֵּ֚ן אִשָּׁפֵ֣ט אִתְּכֶ֔ם נְאֻ֖ם אֲדֹנָ֥י יֱהֹוִֽה׃

וּבָרוֹתִ֣י מִכֶּ֗ם הַמֹּרְדִ֤ים וְהַפּֽוֹשְׁעִים֙ בִּ֔י מֵאֶ֤רֶץ מְגֽוּרֵיהֶם֙ אוֹצִ֣יא אוֹתָ֔ם וְאֶל־אַדְמַ֥ת יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל לֹ֣א יָב֑וֹא וִידַעְתֶּ֖ם כִּי־אֲנִ֥י יְהֹוָֽה׃


and I will purge out from among you the rebels, and them that transgress against me: I will bring them out of the country where they sojourn, and they shall not enter into the land of Yisra᾽el: and you shall know that I am the Lord.


###### Ezekiel 20:39

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/20:39](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/20:39)


וְאַתֶּ֨ם בֵּֽית־יִשְׂרָאֵ֜ל כֹּה־אָמַ֣ר**׀** אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֗ה אִ֤ישׁ גִּלּוּלָיו֙ לְכ֣וּ עֲבֹ֔דוּ וְאַחַ֕ר אִם־אֵינְכֶ֖ם שֹׁמְעִ֣ים אֵלָ֑י וְאֶת־שֵׁ֤ם קׇדְשִׁי֙ לֹ֣א תְחַלְּלוּ־ע֔וֹד בְּמַתְּנוֹתֵיכֶ֖ם וּבְגִלּוּלֵיכֶֽם׃


As for you, O house of Yisra᾽el, thus says the Lord God; Go serve every man his idols; but afterwards you will surely hearken to me, and profane my holy name no more with your gifts, and with your idols.


###### Ezekiel 20:40

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/20:40](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/20:40)


כִּ֣י בְהַר־קׇדְשִׁ֞י בְּהַ֣ר**׀** מְר֣וֹם יִשְׂרָאֵ֗ל נְאֻם֙ אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֔ה שָׁ֣ם יַעַבְדֻ֜נִי כׇּל־בֵּ֧ית יִשְׂרָאֵ֛ל כֻּלֹּ֖ה בָּאָ֑רֶץ שָׁ֣ם אֶרְצֵ֔ם וְשָׁ֞ם אֶדְר֣וֹשׁ אֶת־תְּרוּמֹֽתֵיכֶ֗ם וְאֶת־רֵאשִׁ֛ית מַשְׂאוֹתֵיכֶ֖ם בְּכׇל־קׇדְשֵׁיכֶֽם׃


For in my holy mountain, in the mountain of the height of Yisra᾽el, says the Lord God, there shall all the house of Yisra᾽el all of them in the land, serve me: there will I accept them, and there will I require your offerings, and the firstfruits of your gifts, with all your holy things.

בְּרֵ֣יחַ נִיחֹ֘חַ֮ אֶרְצֶ֣ה אֶתְכֶם֒ בְּהוֹצִיאִ֤י אֶתְכֶם֙ מִן־הָ֣עַמִּ֔ים וְקִבַּצְתִּ֣י אֶתְכֶ֔ם מִן־הָ֣אֲרָצ֔וֹת אֲשֶׁ֥ר נְפֹצֹתֶ֖ם בָּ֑ם וְנִקְדַּשְׁתִּ֥י בָכֶ֖ם לְעֵינֵ֥י הַגּוֹיִֽם׃

###### Ezekiel 20:42

וִֽידַעְתֶּם֙ כִּֽי־אֲנִ֣י יְהֹוָ֔ה בַּהֲבִיאִ֥י אֶתְכֶ֖ם אֶל־אַדְמַ֣ת יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל אֶל־הָאָ֗רֶץ אֲשֶׁ֤ר נָשָׂ֙אתִי֙ אֶת־יָדִ֔י לָתֵ֥ת אוֹתָ֖הּ לַאֲבוֹתֵיכֶֽם׃


And you shall know that I am the Lord, when I shall bring you into the land of Yisra᾽el, into the country about which I lifted up my hand to give it to your fathers.

וּזְכַרְתֶּם־שָׁ֗ם אֶת־דַּרְכֵיכֶם֙ וְאֵת֙ כׇּל־עֲלִיל֣וֹתֵיכֶ֔ם אֲשֶׁ֥ר נִטְמֵאתֶ֖ם בָּ֑ם וּנְקֹֽטֹתֶם֙ בִּפְנֵיכֶ֔ם בְּכׇל־רָעוֹתֵיכֶ֖ם אֲשֶׁ֥ר עֲשִׂיתֶֽם׃

וִֽידַעְתֶּם֙ כִּֽי־אֲנִ֣י יְהֹוָ֔ה בַּעֲשׂוֹתִ֥י אִתְּכֶ֖ם לְמַ֣עַן שְׁמִ֑י לֹא֩ כְדַרְכֵיכֶ֨ם הָרָעִ֜ים וְכַעֲלִילוֹתֵיכֶ֤ם הַנִּשְׁחָתוֹת֙ בֵּ֣ית יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל נְאֻ֖ם אֲדֹנָ֥י יֱהֹוִֽה׃{פ}


And you shall know that I am the Lord, when I have acted with you for my name’s sake, not according to your wicked ways, nor according to your corrupt doings, O house of Yisra᾽el, says the Lord God.



## Chapter 21

###### Ezekiel 21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/21:1)


וַיְהִ֥י דְבַר־יְהֹוָ֖ה אֵלַ֥י לֵאמֹֽר׃


Moreover the word of the Lord came to me, saying,


###### Ezekiel 21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/21:2)


בֶּן־אָדָ֗ם שִׂ֤ים פָּנֶ֙יךָ֙ דֶּ֣רֶךְ תֵּימָ֔נָה וְהַטֵּ֖ף אֶל־דָּר֑וֹם וְהִנָּבֵ֛א אֶל־יַ֥עַר הַשָּׂדֶ֖ה נֶֽגֶב׃


Son of man, set thy face towards the south, and preach towards the south, and prophesy against the forest land of the Negev;


###### Ezekiel 21:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/21:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/21:3)


וְאָֽמַרְתָּ֙ לְיַ֣עַר הַנֶּ֔גֶב שְׁמַ֖ע דְּבַר־יְהֹוָ֑ה כֹּה־אָמַ֣ר אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֡ה הִנְנִ֣י מַֽצִּית־בְּךָ֣׀אֵ֡שׁ וְאָכְלָ֣ה בְךָ֣ כׇל־עֵֽץ־לַח֩ וְכׇל־עֵ֨ץ יָבֵ֤שׁ לֹֽא־תִכְבֶּה֙ לַהֶ֣בֶת שַׁלְהֶ֔בֶת וְנִצְרְבוּ־בָ֥הּ כׇּל־פָּנִ֖ים מִנֶּ֥גֶב צָפֽוֹנָה׃


and say to the forest of the Negev, Hear the word of the Lord; Thus says the Lord God; Behold, I will kindle a fire in thee, and it shall devour every green tree in thee, and every dry tree; it shall not be quenched; it shall be a flaming flame, and all faces from the Negev northwards shall be scorched by it.


###### Ezekiel 21:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/21:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/21:4)


וְרָאוּ֙ כׇּל־בָּשָׂ֔ר כִּ֛י אֲנִ֥י יְהֹוָ֖ה בִּֽעַרְתִּ֑יהָ לֹ֖א תִּכְבֶּֽה׃


And all flesh shall see that I the Lord have kindled it: it shall not be quenched.


###### Ezekiel 21:5

וָאֹמַ֕ר אֲהָ֖הּ אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֑ה הֵ֚מָּה אֹמְרִ֣ים לִ֔י הֲלֹ֛א מְמַשֵּׁ֥ל מְשָׁלִ֖ים הֽוּא׃{פ}

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/21:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/21:7)


בֶּן־אָדָ֗ם שִׂ֤ים פָּנֶ֙יךָ֙ אֶל־יְר֣וּשָׁלַ֔͏ִם וְהַטֵּ֖ף אֶל־מִקְדָּשִׁ֑ים וְהִנָּבֵ֖א אֶל־אַדְמַ֥ת יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃

###### Ezekiel 21:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/21:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/21:8)


וְאָמַרְתָּ֞ לְאַדְמַ֣ת יִשְׂרָאֵ֗ל כֹּ֚ה אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֔ה הִנְנִ֣י אֵלַ֔יִךְ וְהוֹצֵאתִ֥י חַרְבִּ֖י מִתַּעְרָ֑הּ וְהִכְרַתִּ֥י מִמֵּ֖ךְ צַדִּ֥יק וְרָשָֽׁע׃


and say to the land of Yisra᾽el, Thus says the Lord; Behold, I am against thee, and will draw my sword out of its sheath, and will cut off from thee the righteous and the wicked.

And it shall be, when they say to thee, Why dost thou sigh? then thou shalt answer, For the tidings; because it comes: and every heart shall melt, and all hands shall be feeble, and every spirit shall faint, and all knees shall drip with water: behold, it comes, and shall be brought to pass, says the Lord God.

###### Ezekiel 21:37

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/21:37](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/21:37)


לָאֵ֤שׁ תִּֽהְיֶה֙ לְאׇכְלָ֔ה דָּמֵ֥ךְ יִהְיֶ֖ה בְּת֣וֹךְ הָאָ֑רֶץ לֹ֣א תִזָּכֵ֔רִי כִּ֛י אֲנִ֥י יְהֹוָ֖ה דִּבַּֽרְתִּי׃{פ}


Thou shalt be for fuel to the fire; thy blood shall be in the midst of the land; thou shalt be no more remembered: for I the Lord have spoken it.



## Chapter 22

###### Ezekiel 22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/22:1)


וַיְהִ֥י דְבַר־יְהֹוָ֖ה אֵלַ֥י לֵאמֹֽר׃


Then the word of the Lord came to me, saying,


###### Ezekiel 22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/22:2)


וְאַתָּ֣ה בֶן־אָדָ֔ם הֲתִשְׁפֹּ֥ט הֲתִשְׁפֹּ֖ט אֶת־עִ֣יר הַדָּמִ֑ים וְה֣וֹדַעְתָּ֔הּ אֵ֖ת כׇּל־תּוֹעֲבוֹתֶֽיהָ׃


Now, thou son of man, wilt thou judge, wilt thou judge the bloody city? then make known to her all her abominations.


###### Ezekiel 22:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/22:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/22:3)


וְאָמַרְתָּ֗ כֹּ֤ה אָמַר֙ אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֔ה עִ֣יר שֹׁפֶ֥כֶת דָּ֛ם בְּתוֹכָ֖הּ לָב֣וֹא עִתָּ֑הּ וְעָשְׂתָ֧ה גִלּוּלִ֛ים עָלֶ֖יהָ לְטׇמְאָֽה׃


And thou shalt say, Thus says the Lord God, A city that sheds blood in the midst of it, that her time may come, and makes idols against herself to defile herself.


###### Ezekiel 22:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/22:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/22:4)


בְּדָמֵ֨ךְ אֲשֶׁר־שָׁפַ֜כְתְּ אָשַׁ֗מְתְּ וּבְגִלּוּלַ֤יִךְ אֲשֶׁר־עָשִׂית֙ טָמֵ֔את וַתַּקְרִ֣יבִי יָמַ֔יִךְ וַתָּב֖וֹא עַד־שְׁנוֹתָ֑יִךְ עַל־כֵּ֗ן נְתַתִּ֤יךְ חֶרְפָּה֙ לַגּוֹיִ֔ם וְקַלָּסָ֖ה לְכׇל־הָאֲרָצֽוֹת׃


Thou art become guilty in thy blood that thou hast shed; and art defiled in thy idols which thou hast made; and thou hast caused thy days to draw near, and art come to thy years: therefore have I made thee a reproach to the nations, and a mocking to all countries.


###### Ezekiel 22:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/22:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/22:5)


הַקְּרֹב֛וֹת וְהָרְחֹק֥וֹת מִמֵּ֖ךְ יִתְקַלְּסוּ־בָ֑ךְ טְמֵאַ֣ת הַשֵּׁ֔ם רַבַּ֖ת הַמְּהוּמָֽה׃


Those that are near, and those that are far from thee, shall mock thee, infamous as thou art, and full of tumult.


###### Ezekiel 22:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/22:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/22:6)


הִנֵּה֙ נְשִׂיאֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל אִ֥ישׁ לִזְרֹע֖וֹ הָ֣יוּ בָ֑ךְ לְמַ֖עַן שְׁפׇךְ־דָּֽם׃


Behold, the princes of Yisra᾽el, each of them amongst thee, putting out his full force, for shedding blood.


###### Ezekiel 22:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/22:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/22:7)


אָ֤ב וָאֵם֙ הֵקַ֣לּוּ בָ֔ךְ לַגֵּ֛ר עָשׂ֥וּ בַעֹ֖שֶׁק בְּתוֹכֵ֑ךְ יָת֥וֹם וְאַלְמָנָ֖ה ה֥וֹנוּ בָֽךְ׃


Amongst thee, they have made light of father and mother: in the midst of thee have they dealt by oppression with the stranger: in thee they have wronged the fatherless and the widow.


###### Ezekiel 22:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/22:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/22:8)


קׇדָשַׁ֖י בָּזִ֑ית וְאֶת־שַׁבְּתֹתַ֖י חִלָּֽלְתְּ׃


Thou hast despised my holy things, and hast profaned my sabbaths.


###### Ezekiel 22:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/22:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/22:9)


אַנְשֵׁ֥י רָכִ֛יל הָ֥יוּ בָ֖ךְ לְמַ֣עַן שְׁפׇךְ־דָּ֑ם וְאֶל־הֶֽהָרִים֙ אָ֣כְלוּ בָ֔ךְ זִמָּ֖ה עָשׂ֥וּ בְתוֹכֵֽךְ׃


In thee are men that did carry slander to shed blood: and in thee they did eat upon the mountains: in the midst of thee they have committed lewdness.


###### Ezekiel 22:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/22:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/22:10)


עֶרְוַת־אָ֖ב גִּלָּה־בָ֑ךְ טְמֵאַ֥ת הַנִּדָּ֖ה עִנּוּ־בָֽךְ׃


In thee they have uncovered their father’s nakedness: in thee they have abused her who is unclean of her menstrual flow.


###### Ezekiel 22:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/22:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/22:11)


וְאִ֣ישׁ**׀** אֶת־אֵ֣שֶׁת רֵעֵ֗הוּ עָשָׂה֙ תּוֹעֵבָ֔ה וְאִ֥ישׁ אֶת־כַּלָּת֖וֹ טִמֵּ֣א בְזִמָּ֑ה וְאִ֛ישׁ אֶת־אֲחֹת֥וֹ בַת־אָבִ֖יו עִנָּה־בָֽךְ׃


One has committed a disgusting act with his neighbour’s wife; and another has lewdly defiled his daughter in law; and another in thee has abused his sister, his father’s daughter.


###### Ezekiel 22:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/22:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/22:12)


שֹׁ֥חַד לָֽקְחוּ־בָ֖ךְ לְמַ֣עַן שְׁפׇךְ־דָּ֑ם נֶ֧שֶׁךְ וְתַרְבִּ֣ית לָקַ֗חַתְּ וַתְּבַצְּעִ֤י רֵעַ֙יִךְ֙ בַּעֹ֔שֶׁק וְאֹתִ֣י שָׁכַ֔חַתְּ נְאֻ֖ם אֲדֹנָ֥י יֱהֹוִֽה׃


In thee they have taken gifts to shed blood; thou hast taken usury and interest, and thou hast taken unjust gain of thy neighbours by extortion, and hast forgotten me, says the Lord God.


###### Ezekiel 22:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/22:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/22:13)


וְהִנֵּה֙ הִכֵּ֣יתִי כַפִּ֔י אֶל־בִּצְעֵ֖ךְ אֲשֶׁ֣ר עָשִׂ֑ית וְעַ֨ל־דָּמֵ֔ךְ אֲשֶׁ֥ר הָי֖וּ בְּתוֹכֵֽךְ׃


Behold, therefore I have struck my hand at thy dishonest gain which thou hast made, and at thy blood which has been in the midst of thee.


###### Ezekiel 22:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/22:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/22:14)


הֲיַעֲמֹ֤ד לִבֵּךְ֙ אִם־תֶּחֱזַ֣קְנָה יָדַ֔יִךְ לַיָּמִ֕ים אֲשֶׁ֥ר אֲנִ֖י עֹשֶׂ֣ה אוֹתָ֑ךְ אֲנִ֥י יְהֹוָ֖ה דִּבַּ֥רְתִּי וְעָשִֽׂיתִי׃


Can thy heart endure, or can thy hands be strong, in the days that I shall deal with thee? I the Lord have spoken it, and will do it.


###### Ezekiel 22:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/22:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/22:15)


וַהֲפִיצוֹתִ֤י אוֹתָךְ֙ בַּגּוֹיִ֔ם וְזֵרִיתִ֖יךְ בָּאֲרָצ֑וֹת וַהֲתִמֹּתִ֥י טֻמְאָתֵ֖ךְ מִמֵּֽךְ׃


And I will scatter thee among the nations, and disperse thee in the countries, and will consume thy uncleanliness out of thee.


###### Ezekiel 22:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/22:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/22:16)


וְנִחַ֥לְתְּ בָּ֖ךְ לְעֵינֵ֣י גוֹיִ֑ם וְיָדַ֖עַתְּ כִּי־אֲנִ֥י יְהֹוָֽה׃{פ}


And thou shalt be profaned by thyself in the sight of the nations, and thou shalt know that I am the Lord.


###### Ezekiel 22:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/22:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/22:17)


וַיְהִ֥י דְבַר־יְהֹוָ֖ה אֵלַ֥י לֵאמֹֽר׃


And the word of the Lord came to me, saying,


###### Ezekiel 22:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/22:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/22:18)


בֶּן־אָדָ֕ם הָיוּ־לִ֥י בֵֽית־יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל (לסוג) [לְסִ֑יג] כֻּלָּ֡ם נְ֠חֹ֠שֶׁת וּבְדִ֨יל וּבַרְזֶ֤ל וְעוֹפֶ֙רֶת֙ בְּת֣וֹךְ כּ֔וּר סִגִ֥ים כֶּ֖סֶף הָיֽוּ׃{ס}


Son of man, the house of Yisra᾽el is to me become dross: all of them, brass, and tin, and iron, and lead, in the midst of the furnace; they are even the dross of silver.


###### Ezekiel 22:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/22:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/22:19)


לָכֵ֗ן כֹּ֤ה אָמַר֙ אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֔ה יַ֛עַן הֱי֥וֹת כֻּלְּכֶ֖ם לְסִגִ֑ים לָכֵן֙ הִנְנִ֣י קֹבֵ֣ץ אֶתְכֶ֔ם אֶל־תּ֖וֹךְ יְרוּשָׁלָֽ͏ִם׃


Therefore thus says the Lord God; Because you are all become dross, behold, therefore I will gather you into the midst of Yerushalayim.


###### Ezekiel 22:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/22:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/22:20)


קְבֻ֣צַת כֶּ֡סֶף וּ֠נְחֹ֠שֶׁת וּבַרְזֶ֨ל וְעוֹפֶ֤רֶת וּבְדִיל֙ אֶל־תּ֣וֹךְ כּ֔וּר לָפַֽחַת־עָלָ֥יו אֵ֖שׁ לְהַנְתִּ֑יךְ כֵּ֤ן אֶקְבֹּץ֙ בְּאַפִּ֣י וּבַחֲמָתִ֔י וְהִנַּחְתִּ֥י וְהִתַּכְתִּ֖י אֶתְכֶֽם׃


As they gather silver, and brass, and iron, and lead, and tin, into the midst of the furnace, to blow the fire upon it, to melt it; so will I gather you in my anger and in my fury, and I will leave you there, and melt you.


###### Ezekiel 22:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/22:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/22:21)


וְכִנַּסְתִּ֣י אֶתְכֶ֔ם וְנָפַחְתִּ֥י עֲלֵיכֶ֖ם בְּאֵ֣שׁ עֶבְרָתִ֑י וְנִתַּכְתֶּ֖ם בְּתוֹכָֽהּ׃


And I will gather you, and blow upon you in the fire of my wrath, and you shall be melted in the midst of it.


###### Ezekiel 22:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/22:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/22:22)


כְּהִתּ֥וּךְ כֶּ֙סֶף֙ בְּת֣וֹךְ כּ֔וּר כֵּ֖ן תֻּתְּכ֣וּ בְתוֹכָ֑הּ וִֽידַעְתֶּם֙ כִּֽי־אֲנִ֣י יְהֹוָ֔ה שָׁפַ֥כְתִּי חֲמָתִ֖י עֲלֵיכֶֽם׃{פ}


As silver is melted in the midst of the furnace, so shall you be melted in the midst of it; and you shall know that I the Lord have poured out my fury upon you.


###### Ezekiel 22:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/22:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/22:23)


וַיְהִ֥י דְבַר־יְהֹוָ֖ה אֵלַ֥י לֵאמֹֽר׃


And the word of the Lord came to me, saying,


###### Ezekiel 22:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/22:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/22:24)


בֶּן־אָדָ֕ם אֱמׇר־לָ֕הּ אַ֣תְּ אֶ֔רֶץ לֹ֥א מְטֹהָרָ֖ה הִ֑יא לֹ֥א גֻשְׁמָ֖הּ בְּי֥וֹם זָֽעַם׃


Son of man, say to her, Thou art a land that is not cleansed, nor rained upon in the day of indignation.


###### Ezekiel 22:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/22:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/22:25)


קֶ֤שֶׁר נְבִיאֶ֙יהָ֙ בְּתוֹכָ֔הּ כַּאֲרִ֥י שׁוֹאֵ֖ג טֹ֣רֵֽף טָ֑רֶף נֶ֣פֶשׁ אָכָ֗לוּ חֹ֤סֶן וִיקָר֙ יִקָּ֔חוּ אַלְמְנוֹתֶ֖יהָ הִרְבּ֥וּ בְתוֹכָֽהּ׃


There is a conspiracy of her prophets in the midst thereof, like a roaring lion ravening the prey; they have devoured souls; they take treasure and precious things; they have made her widows many in the midst of her.


###### Ezekiel 22:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/22:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/22:26)


כֹּהֲנֶ֜יהָ חָמְס֣וּ תוֹרָתִי֮ וַיְחַלְּל֣וּ קׇדָשַׁי֒ בֵּֽין־קֹ֤דֶשׁ לְחֹל֙ לֹ֣א הִבְדִּ֔ילוּ וּבֵין־הַטָּמֵ֥א לְטָה֖וֹר לֹ֣א הוֹדִ֑יעוּ וּמִשַּׁבְּתוֹתַי֙ הֶעְלִ֣ימוּ עֵינֵיהֶ֔ם וָאֵחַ֖ל בְּתוֹכָֽם׃


Her priests have violated my Tora, and have profaned my holy things: they have put no difference between the holy and common, neither have they taught the difference between the unclean and the clean, and they have hidden their eyes from my sabbaths, and I am profaned among them.


###### Ezekiel 22:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/22:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/22:27)


שָׂרֶ֣יהָ בְקִרְבָּ֔הּ כִּזְאֵבִ֖ים טֹ֣רְפֵי טָ֑רֶף לִשְׁפׇּךְ־דָּם֙ לְאַבֵּ֣ד נְפָשׁ֔וֹת לְמַ֖עַן בְּצֹ֥עַ בָּֽצַע׃


Her princes in the midst of her are like wolves ravening the prey, to shed blood, and to destroy souls, to get dishonest gain.


###### Ezekiel 22:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/22:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/22:28)


וּנְבִיאֶ֗יהָ טָח֤וּ לָהֶם֙ תָּפֵ֔ל חֹזִ֣ים שָׁ֔וְא וְקֹסְמִ֥ים לָהֶ֖ם כָּזָ֑ב אֹמְרִ֗ים כֹּ֤ה אָמַר֙ אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֔ה וַיהֹוָ֖ה לֹ֥א דִבֵּֽר׃


And her prophets have daubed them with whitewash, seeing false visions, and divining lies to them, saying, Thus says the Lord God, when the Lord has not spoken.


###### Ezekiel 22:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/22:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/22:29)


עַ֤ם הָאָ֙רֶץ֙ עָ֣שְׁקוּ עֹ֔שֶׁק וְגָזְל֖וּ גָּזֵ֑ל וְעָנִ֤י וְאֶבְיוֹן֙ הוֹנ֔וּ וְאֶת־הַגֵּ֥ר עָשְׁק֖וּ בְּלֹ֥א מִשְׁפָּֽט׃


The people of the land have used oppression, and committed robbery, and have wronged the poor and needy: indeed, they have oppressed the stranger wrongfully.


###### Ezekiel 22:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/22:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/22:30)


וָאֲבַקֵּ֣שׁ מֵהֶ֡ם אִ֣ישׁ גֹּֽדֵר־גָּדֵר֩ וְעֹמֵ֨ד בַּפֶּ֧רֶץ לְפָנַ֛י בְּעַ֥ד הָאָ֖רֶץ לְבִלְתִּ֣י שַׁחֲתָ֑הּ וְלֹ֖א מָצָֽאתִי׃


And I sought for a man among them, that should make up the hedge, and stand in the breach before me the land, that I should not destroy it: but I found none.


###### Ezekiel 22:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/22:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/22:31)


וָאֶשְׁפֹּ֤ךְ עֲלֵיהֶם֙ זַעְמִ֔י בְּאֵ֥שׁ עֶבְרָתִ֖י כִּלִּיתִ֑ים דַּרְכָּם֙ בְּרֹאשָׁ֣ם נָתַ֔תִּי נְאֻ֖ם אֲדֹנָ֥י יֱהֹוִֽה׃{פ}


Therefore I have poured out my indignation upon them; I have consumed them with the fire of my wrath: their own way have I recompensed upon their heads, says the Lord God.



## Chapter 23

###### Ezekiel 23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/23:1)


וַיְהִ֥י דְבַר־יְהֹוָ֖ה אֵלַ֥י לֵאמֹֽר׃


The word of the Lord came again to me, saying,


###### Ezekiel 23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/23:2)


בֶּן־אָדָ֑ם שְׁתַּ֣יִם נָשִׁ֔ים בְּנ֥וֹת אֵם־אַחַ֖ת הָיֽוּ׃


Son of man, there were two women, the daughters of one mother:


###### Ezekiel 23:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/23:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/23:3)


וַתִּזְנֶ֣ינָה בְמִצְרַ֔יִם בִּנְעוּרֵיהֶ֖ן זָנ֑וּ שָׁ֚מָּה מֹעֲכ֣וּ שְׁדֵיהֶ֔ן וְשָׁ֣ם עִשּׂ֔וּ דַּדֵּ֖י בְּתוּלֵיהֶֽן׃


And they committed harlotry in Miżrayim; they committed harlotry in their youth: there were their breasts pressed, and there their virgin teats were handled.


###### Ezekiel 23:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/23:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/23:4)


וּשְׁמוֹתָ֗ן אׇהֳלָ֤ה הַגְּדוֹלָה֙ וְאׇהֳלִיבָ֣ה אֲחוֹתָ֔הּ וַתִּהְיֶ֣ינָה לִ֔י וַתֵּלַ֖דְנָה בָּנִ֣ים וּבָנ֑וֹת וּשְׁמוֹתָ֕ן שֹׁמְר֣וֹן אׇהֳלָ֔ה וִירוּשָׁלַ֖͏ִם אׇהֳלִיבָֽה׃


And the names of them were Ohola the elder, and Oholiva her sister: and they were mine, and they bore sons and daughters. Thus were their names; Shomeron is Ohola, and Yerushalayim is Oholiva.


###### Ezekiel 23:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/23:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/23:5)


וַתִּ֥זֶן אׇהֳלָ֖ה תַּחְתָּ֑י וַתֶּעְגַּב֙ עַֽל־מְאַהֲבֶ֔יהָ אֶל־אַשּׁ֖וּר קְרוֹבִֽים׃


And Ohola played the harlot when she was mine; and she doted on her lovers, on those of Ashshur her neighbours,


###### Ezekiel 23:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/23:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/23:6)


לְבֻשֵׁ֤י תְכֵ֙לֶת֙ פַּח֣וֹת וּסְגָנִ֔ים בַּח֥וּרֵי חֶ֖מֶד כֻּלָּ֑ם פָּרָשִׁ֕ים רֹכְבֵ֖י סוּסִֽים׃


who were clothed with blue, captains and rulers, all of them charming young men, horsemen riding upon horses.


###### Ezekiel 23:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/23:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/23:7)


וַתִּתֵּ֤ן תַּזְנוּתֶ֙יהָ֙ עֲלֵיהֶ֔ם מִבְחַ֥ר בְּנֵֽי־אַשּׁ֖וּר כֻּלָּ֑ם וּבְכֹ֧ל אֲשֶׁר־עָגְבָ֛ה בְּכׇל־גִּלּוּלֵיהֶ֖ם נִטְמָֽאָה׃


Thus she committed her lewd practices with them, with all them that were the choice men of Ashshur, and with all on whom she doted, with all their idols she defiled herself.


###### Ezekiel 23:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/23:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/23:8)


וְאֶת־תַּזְנוּתֶ֤יהָ מִמִּצְרַ֙יִם֙ לֹ֣א עָזָ֔בָה כִּ֤י אוֹתָהּ֙ שָׁכְב֣וּ בִנְעוּרֶ֔יהָ וְהֵ֥מָּה עִשּׂ֖וּ דַּדֵּ֣י בְתוּלֶ֑יהָ וַיִּשְׁפְּכ֥וּ תַזְנוּתָ֖ם עָלֶֽיהָ׃


Neither did she give up her lewd practices brought from Miżrayim: for in her youth they lay with her, and they handled her virgin breasts, and poured out their lust upon her.


###### Ezekiel 23:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/23:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/23:9)


לָכֵ֥ן נְתַתִּ֖יהָ בְּיַד־מְאַהֲבֶ֑יהָ בְּיַד֙ בְּנֵ֣י אַשּׁ֔וּר אֲשֶׁ֥ר עָגְבָ֖ה עֲלֵיהֶֽם׃


Therefore I have delivered her into the hand of her lovers, into the hand of the children of Ashshur, upon whom she doted.


###### Ezekiel 23:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/23:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/23:10)


הֵ֘מָּה֮ גִּלּ֣וּ עֶרְוָתָהּ֒ בָּנֶ֤יהָ וּבְנוֹתֶ֙יהָ֙ לָקָ֔חוּ וְאוֹתָ֖הּ בַּחֶ֣רֶב הָרָ֑גוּ וַתְּהִי־שֵׁם֙ לַנָּשִׁ֔ים וּשְׁפוּטִ֖ים עָ֥שׂוּ בָֽהּ׃{ס}


These uncovered her nakedness: they took her sons and her daughters, and slew her with the sword: and she became notorious among women; for they had executed judgment upon her.


###### Ezekiel 23:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/23:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/23:11)


וַתֵּ֙רֶא֙ אֲחוֹתָ֣הּ אׇהֳלִיבָ֔ה וַתַּשְׁחֵ֥ת עַגְבָתָ֖הּ מִמֶּ֑נָּה וְאֶ֨ת־תַּזְנוּתֶ֔יהָ מִזְּנוּנֵ֖י אֲחוֹתָֽהּ׃


And her sister Oholiva saw this, yet she was more corrupt in her inordinate love than she, and in her harlotries more than her sister in her harlotries.


###### Ezekiel 23:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/23:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/23:12)


אֶל־בְּנֵי֩ אַשּׁ֨וּר עָגָ֜בָה פַּח֨וֹת וּסְגָנִ֤ים קְרֹבִים֙ לְבֻשֵׁ֣י מִכְל֔וֹל פָּרָשִׁ֖ים רֹכְבֵ֣י סוּסִ֑ים בַּח֥וּרֵי חֶ֖מֶד כֻּלָּֽם׃


She doted upon the children of Ashshur her neighbours, captains and rulers clothed most gorgeously, horsemen riding upon horses, all of the charming young men.


###### Ezekiel 23:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/23:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/23:13)


וָאֵ֖רֶא כִּ֣י נִטְמָ֑אָה דֶּ֥רֶךְ אֶחָ֖ד לִשְׁתֵּיהֶֽן׃


Then I saw that she was defiled, that they took both of them the same way,


###### Ezekiel 23:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/23:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/23:14)


וַתּ֖וֹסֶף אֶל־תַּזְנוּתֶ֑יהָ וַתֵּ֗רֶא אַנְשֵׁי֙ מְחֻקֶּ֣ה עַל־הַקִּ֔יר צַלְמֵ֣י (כשדיים) [כַשְׂדִּ֔ים] חֲקֻקִ֖ים בַּשָּׁשַֽׁר׃


and that she increased her harlotries: for when she saw men portrayed upon the wall, the images of the Kasdim portrayed with vermilion,


###### Ezekiel 23:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/23:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/23:15)


חֲגוֹרֵ֨י אֵז֜וֹר בְּמׇתְנֵיהֶ֗ם סְרוּחֵ֤י טְבוּלִים֙ בְּרָ֣אשֵׁיהֶ֔ם מַרְאֵ֥ה שָׁלִשִׁ֖ים כֻּלָּ֑ם דְּמ֤וּת בְּנֵֽי־בָבֶל֙ כַּשְׂדִּ֔ים אֶ֖רֶץ מוֹלַדְתָּֽם׃


girded with girdles upon their loins, with flowing turbans upon their heads, all of them looking like captains, after the manner of the children of Bavel in Kasdim, the land of their nativity:


###### Ezekiel 23:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/23:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/23:16)


(ותעגב) [וַתַּעְגְּבָ֥ה] עֲלֵיהֶ֖ם לְמַרְאֵ֣ה עֵינֶ֑יהָ וַתִּשְׁלַ֧ח מַלְאָכִ֛ים אֲלֵיהֶ֖ם כַּשְׂדִּֽימָה׃


and as soon as she saw them with her eyes, she doted upon them, and sent messengers to them to Kasdim.


###### Ezekiel 23:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/23:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/23:17)


וַיָּבֹ֨אוּ אֵלֶ֤יהָ בְנֵֽי־בָבֶל֙ לְמִשְׁכַּ֣ב דֹּדִ֔ים וַיְטַמְּא֥וּ אוֹתָ֖הּ בְּתַזְנוּתָ֑ם וַתִּ֨טְמָא־בָ֔ם וַתֵּ֥קַע נַפְשָׁ֖הּ מֵהֶֽם׃


And the children of Bavel came to her into the bed of love, and they defiled her with their lust, and she was polluted with them, and her mind was alienated from them.


###### Ezekiel 23:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/23:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/23:18)


וַתְּגַל֙ תַּזְנוּתֶ֔יהָ וַתְּגַ֖ל אֶת־עֶרְוָתָ֑הּ וַתֵּ֤קַע נַפְשִׁי֙ מֵעָלֶ֔יהָ כַּאֲשֶׁ֛ר נָקְעָ֥ה נַפְשִׁ֖י מֵעַ֥ל אֲחוֹתָֽהּ׃


So she uncovered her harlotries, and uncovered her nakedness: then my mind was alienated from her, just as my mind was alienated from her sister.


###### Ezekiel 23:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/23:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/23:19)


וַתַּרְבֶּ֖ה אֶת־תַּזְנוּתֶ֑יהָ לִזְכֹּר֙ אֶת־יְמֵ֣י נְעוּרֶ֔יהָ אֲשֶׁ֥ר זָנְתָ֖ה בְּאֶ֥רֶץ מִצְרָֽיִם׃


Yet she multiplied her harlotries, recalling the days of her youth, when she had played the harlot in the land of Miżrayim.


###### Ezekiel 23:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/23:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/23:20)


וַֽתַּעְגְּבָ֔ה עַ֖ל פִּֽלַגְשֵׁיהֶ֑ם אֲשֶׁ֤ר בְּשַׂר־חֲמוֹרִים֙ בְּשָׂרָ֔ם וְזִרְמַ֥ת סוּסִ֖ים זִרְמָתָֽם׃


For she doted upon her paramours there, whose members were like those of asses, and whose issue was like that of horses.


###### Ezekiel 23:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/23:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/23:21)


וַֽתִּפְקְדִ֔י אֵ֖ת זִמַּ֣ת נְעוּרָ֑יִךְ בַּעְשׂ֤וֹת מִמִּצְרַ֙יִם֙ דַּדַּ֔יִךְ לְמַ֖עַן שְׁדֵ֥י נְעוּרָֽיִךְ׃{ס}


Thus thou didst call to remembrance the lewdness of thy youth, when those of Miżrayim handled thy teats, for the breasts of thy youth.


###### Ezekiel 23:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/23:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/23:22)


לָכֵ֣ן אׇהֳלִיבָ֗ה כֹּה־אָמַר֮ אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִה֒ הִנְנִ֨י מֵעִ֤יר אֶת־מְאַהֲבַ֙יִךְ֙ עָלַ֔יִךְ אֵ֛ת אֲשֶׁר־נָקְעָ֥ה נַפְשֵׁ֖ךְ מֵהֶ֑ם וַהֲבֵאתִ֥ים עָלַ֖יִךְ מִסָּבִֽיב׃


Therefore, O Oholiva, thus says the Lord God; Behold, I will raise up thy lovers against thee, from whom thy mind is alienated, and I will bring them against thee on every side;


###### Ezekiel 23:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/23:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/23:23)


בְּנֵ֧י בָבֶ֣ל וְכׇל־כַּשְׂדִּ֗ים פְּק֤וֹד וְשׁ֙וֹעַ֙ וְק֔וֹעַ כׇּל־בְּנֵ֥י אַשּׁ֖וּר אוֹתָ֑ם בַּח֨וּרֵי חֶ֜מֶד פַּח֤וֹת וּסְגָנִים֙ כֻּלָּ֔ם שָֽׁלִשִׁים֙ וּקְרוּאִ֔ים רֹכְבֵ֥י סוּסִ֖ים כֻּלָּֽם׃


the children of Bavel, and all Kasdim, Peqod, and Shoa, and Qoa, all the children of Ashshur with them: all of them charming young men, captains, and rulers, great lords and renowned, all of them riding upon horses.


###### Ezekiel 23:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/23:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/23:24)


וּבָ֣אוּ עָלַ֡יִךְ הֹ֠צֶן רֶ֤כֶב וְגַלְגַּל֙ וּבִקְהַ֣ל עַמִּ֔ים צִנָּ֤ה וּמָגֵן֙ וְקוֹבַ֔ע יָשִׂ֥ימוּ עָלַ֖יִךְ סָבִ֑יב וְנָתַתִּ֤י לִפְנֵיהֶם֙ מִשְׁפָּ֔ט וּשְׁפָט֖וּךְ בְּמִשְׁפְּטֵיהֶֽם׃


And they shall come against thee, a multitude of wagons, and wheels, and with an assembly of people, who shall set against thee buckler and shield and helmet round about: and I will put the case before them, and they shall judge thee according to their judgments.


###### Ezekiel 23:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/23:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/23:25)


וְנָתַתִּ֨י קִנְאָתִ֜י בָּ֗ךְ וְעָשׂ֤וּ אוֹתָךְ֙ בְּחֵמָ֔ה אַפֵּ֤ךְ וְאׇזְנַ֙יִךְ֙ יָסִ֔ירוּ וְאַחֲרִיתֵ֖ךְ בַּחֶ֣רֶב תִּפּ֑וֹל הֵ֗מָּה בָּנַ֤יִךְ וּבְנוֹתַ֙יִךְ֙ יִקָּ֔חוּ וְאַחֲרִיתֵ֖ךְ תֵּאָכֵ֥ל בָּאֵֽשׁ׃


And I will set my jealousy against thee, and they shall deal furiously with thee: they shall take away thy nose and thy ears; and thy remnant shall fall by the sword: they shall take thy sons and thy daughters; and thy residue shall be devoured by the fire.


###### Ezekiel 23:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/23:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/23:26)


וְהִפְשִׁיט֖וּךְ אֶת־בְּגָדָ֑יִךְ וְלָקְח֖וּ כְּלֵ֥י תִפְאַרְתֵּֽךְ׃


They shall also strip thee of thy clothes, and take away thy fair jewels.


###### Ezekiel 23:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/23:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/23:27)


וְהִשְׁבַּתִּ֤י זִמָּתֵךְ֙ מִמֵּ֔ךְ וְאֶת־זְנוּתֵ֖ךְ מֵאֶ֣רֶץ מִצְרָ֑יִם וְלֹֽא־תִשְׂאִ֤י עֵינַ֙יִךְ֙ אֲלֵיהֶ֔ם וּמִצְרַ֖יִם לֹ֥א תִזְכְּרִי־עֽוֹד׃{פ}


Thus will I make thy lewdness to cease from thee, and thy harlotry brought from the land of Miżrayim: so that thou shalt not lift up thy eyes to them, nor remember Miżrayim any more.


###### Ezekiel 23:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/23:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/23:28)


כִּ֣י כֹ֤ה אָמַר֙ אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֔ה הִנְנִי֙ נֹֽתְנָ֔ךְ בְּיַ֖ד אֲשֶׁ֣ר שָׂנֵ֑את בְּיַ֛ד אֲשֶׁר־נָקְעָ֥ה נַפְשֵׁ֖ךְ מֵהֶֽם׃


For thus says the Lord God; Behold, I will deliver thee into the hand of them whom thou hatest, into the hand of them from whom thy mind is alienated:


###### Ezekiel 23:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/23:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/23:29)


וְעָשׂ֨וּ אוֹתָ֜ךְ בְּשִׂנְאָ֗ה וְלָֽקְחוּ֙ כׇּל־יְגִיעֵ֔ךְ וַעֲזָב֖וּךְ עֵירֹ֣ם וְעֶרְיָ֑ה וְנִגְלָה֙ עֶרְוַ֣ת זְנוּנַ֔יִךְ וְזִמָּתֵ֖ךְ וְתַזְנוּתָֽיִךְ׃

כִּ֣י נִאֵ֗פוּ וְדָם֙ בִּֽידֵיהֶ֔ן וְאֶת־גִּלּוּלֵיהֶ֖ן נִאֵ֑פוּ וְגַ֤ם אֶת־בְּנֵיהֶן֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר יָֽלְדוּ־לִ֔י הֶעֱבִ֥ירוּ לָהֶ֖ם לְאׇכְלָֽה׃

## Chapter 24

###### Ezekiel 24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/24:1)


וַיְהִי֩ דְבַר־יְהֹוָ֨ה אֵלַ֜י בַּשָּׁנָ֤ה הַתְּשִׁיעִית֙ בַּחֹ֣דֶשׁ הָעֲשִׂירִ֔י בֶּעָשׂ֥וֹר לַחֹ֖דֶשׁ לֵאמֹֽר׃


And in the ninth year, in the tenth month, on the tenth day of the month, the word of the Lord came to me, saying,


###### Ezekiel 24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/24:2)


בֶּן־אָדָ֗ם (כתוב) [כְּתׇב־]לְךָ֙ אֶת־שֵׁ֣ם הַיּ֔וֹם אֶת־עֶ֖צֶם הַיּ֣וֹם הַזֶּ֑ה סָמַ֤ךְ מֶֽלֶךְ־בָּבֶל֙ אֶל־יְר֣וּשָׁלַ֔͏ִם בְּעֶ֖צֶם הַיּ֥וֹם הַזֶּֽה׃


Son of man, write the name of the day, of this same day: the king of Bavel has invested Yerushalayim on this very day.


###### Ezekiel 24:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/24:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/24:3)


וּמְשֹׁ֤ל אֶל־בֵּית־הַמֶּ֙רִי֙ מָשָׁ֔ל וְאָמַרְתָּ֣ אֲלֵיהֶ֔ם כֹּ֥ה אָמַ֖ר אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֑ה שְׁפֹ֤ת הַסִּיר֙ שְׁפֹ֔ת וְגַם־יְצֹ֥ק בּ֖וֹ מָֽיִם׃


And utter a parable to the rebellious house, and say to them, Thus says the Lord God; Set on the pot, set it on, and also pour water into it:


###### Ezekiel 24:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/24:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/24:4)


אֱסֹ֤ף נְתָחֶ֙יהָ֙ אֵלֶ֔יהָ כׇּל־נֵ֥תַח ט֖וֹב יָרֵ֣ךְ וְכָתֵ֑ף מִבְחַ֥ר עֲצָמִ֖ים מַלֵּֽא׃


gather the pieces of meat into it, every good piece, the thigh, and the shoulder; fill it with choice bones.


###### Ezekiel 24:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/24:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/24:5)


מִבְחַ֤ר הַצֹּאן֙ לָק֔וֹחַ וְגַ֛ם דּ֥וּר הָעֲצָמִ֖ים תַּחְתֶּ֑יהָ רַתַּ֣ח רְתָחֶ֔יהָ גַּם־בָּשְׁל֥וּ עֲצָמֶ֖יהָ בְּתוֹכָֽהּ׃{ס}


Take the choice of the flock, and also pile the bones under it; make it boil well, and let the bones of it be cooked in it.


###### Ezekiel 24:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/24:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/24:6)


לָכֵ֞ן כֹּה־אָמַ֣ר**׀** אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֗ה אוֹי֮ עִ֣יר הַדָּמִים֒ סִ֚יר אֲשֶׁ֣ר חֶלְאָתָ֣הֿ בָ֔הּ וְחֶ֨לְאָתָ֔הּ לֹ֥א יָצְאָ֖ה מִמֶּ֑נָּה לִנְתָחֶ֤יהָ לִנְתָחֶ֙יהָ֙ הֽוֹצִיאָ֔הּ לֹא־נָפַ֥ל עָלֶ֖יהָ גּוֹרָֽל׃


Therefore thus says the Lord God; Woe to the bloody city, to the pot in which there is filth, for its filth is not gone out of it! bring it out piece by piece; no lot has fallen upon it.


###### Ezekiel 24:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/24:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/24:7)


כִּ֤י דָמָהּ֙ בְּתוֹכָ֣הּ הָיָ֔ה עַל־צְחִ֥יחַ סֶ֖לַע שָׂמָ֑תְהוּ לֹ֤א שְׁפָכַ֙תְהוּ֙ עַל־הָאָ֔רֶץ לְכַסּ֥וֹת עָלָ֖יו עָפָֽר׃


For her blood is in the midst of her; she set it upon the bare rock; she poured it not upon the ground, to cover it with dust;


###### Ezekiel 24:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/24:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/24:8)


לְהַעֲל֤וֹת חֵמָה֙ לִנְקֹ֣ם נָקָ֔ם נָתַ֥תִּי אֶת־דָּמָ֖הּ עַל־צְחִ֣יחַ סָ֑לַע לְבִלְתִּ֖י הִכָּסֽוֹת׃{פ}


that it might cause fury to come, that vengeance might be taken, for I have set her blood upon the bare rock, that it should not be covered.


###### Ezekiel 24:9

Therefore thus says the Lord God; Woe to the bloody city! I too will make the pile great.

Futile is the toil, for her great filth went not out of her: her filth is noisome.


###### Ezekiel 24:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/24:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/24:13)


בְּטֻמְאָתֵ֖ךְ זִמָּ֑ה יַ֤עַן טִֽהַרְתִּיךְ֙ וְלֹ֣א טָהַ֔רְתְּ מִטֻּמְאָתֵךְ֙ לֹ֣א תִטְהֲרִי־ע֔וֹד עַד־הֲנִיחִ֥י אֶת־חֲמָתִ֖י בָּֽךְ׃


Because of thy lewd uncleanliness, because I have purged thee, and thou wast not purged, thou shalt not be purged from thy filthiness any more, till I have relieved my fury upon thee.


###### Ezekiel 24:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/24:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/24:14)


אֲנִ֨י יְהֹוָ֤ה דִּבַּ֙רְתִּי֙ בָּאָ֣ה וְעָשִׂ֔יתִי לֹא־אֶפְרַ֥ע וְלֹא־אָח֖וּס וְלֹ֣א אֶנָּחֵ֑ם כִּדְרָכַ֤יִךְ וְכַעֲלִילוֹתַ֙יִךְ֙ שְׁפָט֔וּךְ נְאֻ֖ם אֲדֹנָ֥י יֱהֹוִֽה׃{פ}


I the Lord have spoken it: it shall come to pass, and I will do it; I will not go back, neither will I spare, neither will I repent; according to thy ways, and according to thy doings, shall they judge thee, says the Lord God.

בֶּן־אָדָ֕ם הִנְנִ֨י לֹקֵ֧חַ מִמְּךָ֛ אֶת־מַחְמַ֥ד עֵינֶ֖יךָ בְּמַגֵּפָ֑ה וְלֹ֤א תִסְפֹּד֙ וְלֹ֣א תִבְכֶּ֔ה וְל֥וֹא תָב֖וֹא דִּמְעָתֶֽךָ׃


Son of man, behold, I am about to take away from thee the delight of thy eyes at a stroke: yet thou shalt neither mourn nor weep, nor shall thy tears run down.


###### Ezekiel 24:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/24:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/24:17)


הֵאָנֵ֣ק**׀** דֹּ֗ם מֵתִים֙ אֵ֣בֶל לֹא־תַֽעֲשֶׂ֔ה פְּאֵֽרְךָ֙ חֲב֣וֹשׁ עָלֶ֔יךָ וּנְעָלֶ֖יךָ תָּשִׂ֣ים בְּרַגְלֶ֑יךָ וְלֹ֤א תַעְטֶה֙ עַל־שָׂפָ֔ם וְלֶ֥חֶם אֲנָשִׁ֖ים לֹ֥א תֹאכֵֽל׃

אֱמֹ֣ר**׀** לְבֵ֣ית יִשְׂרָאֵ֗ל כֹּה־אָמַר֮ אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִה֒ הִנְנִ֨י מְחַלֵּ֤ל אֶת־מִקְדָּשִׁי֙ גְּא֣וֹן עֻזְּכֶ֔ם מַחְמַ֥ד עֵינֵיכֶ֖ם וּמַחְמַ֣ל נַפְשְׁכֶ֑ם וּבְנֵיכֶ֧ם וּבְנוֹתֵיכֶ֛ם אֲשֶׁ֥ר עֲזַבְתֶּ֖ם בַּחֶ֥רֶב יִפֹּֽלוּ׃


Speak to the house of Yisra᾽el, Thus says the Lord God; Behold, I will profane my sanctuary, the pride of your strength, the delight of your eyes, and the longing of your soul; and your sons and your daughters whom you have left behind shall fall by the sword.


###### Ezekiel 24:22

וּפְאֵרֵכֶ֣ם עַל־רָאשֵׁיכֶ֗ם וְנַֽעֲלֵיכֶם֙ בְּרַגְלֵיכֶ֔ם לֹ֥א תִסְפְּד֖וּ וְלֹ֣א תִבְכּ֑וּ וּנְמַקֹּתֶם֙ בַּעֲוֺנֹ֣תֵיכֶ֔ם וּנְהַמְתֶּ֖ם אִ֥ישׁ אֶל־אָחִֽיו׃

וְאַתָּ֣ה בֶן־אָדָ֔ם הֲל֗וֹא בְּי֨וֹם קַחְתִּ֤י מֵהֶם֙ אֶת־מָ֣עוּזָּ֔ם מְשׂ֖וֹשׂ תִּפְאַרְתָּ֑ם אֶת־מַחְמַ֤ד עֵינֵיהֶם֙ וְאֶת־מַשָּׂ֣א נַפְשָׁ֔ם בְּנֵיהֶ֖ם וּבְנוֹתֵיהֶֽם׃

that on that day he that escapes shall come to thee, to cause thee to hear it with thy ears?


###### Ezekiel 24:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/24:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/24:27)

and say to the children of ῾Ammon, Hear the word of the Lord God; Because thou didst say Aha, against my sanctuary, when it was profaned; and against the land of Yisra᾽el, when it was made desolate; and against the house of Yehuda, when they went into exile;


###### Ezekiel 25:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/25:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/25:4)


לָכֵ֡ן הִנְנִי֩ נֹתְנָ֨ךְ לִבְנֵי־קֶ֜דֶם לְמוֹרָשָׁ֗ה וְיִשְּׁב֤וּ טִירֽוֹתֵיהֶם֙ בָּ֔ךְ וְנָ֥תְנוּ בָ֖ךְ מִשְׁכְּנֵיהֶ֑ם הֵ֚מָּה יֹאכְל֣וּ פִרְיֵ֔ךְ וְהֵ֖מָּה יִשְׁתּ֥וּ חֲלָבֵֽךְ׃

###### Ezekiel 25:6

כִּ֣י כֹ֤ה אָמַר֙ אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֔ה יַ֚עַן מַחְאֲךָ֣ יָ֔ד וְרַקְעֲךָ֖ בְּרָ֑גֶל וַתִּשְׂמַ֤ח בְּכׇל־שָֽׁאטְךָ֙ בְּנֶ֔פֶשׁ אֶל־אַדְמַ֖ת יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


For thus says the Lord God; Because thou hast clapped thy hands, and stamped with the feet, and rejoiced with all the disdain of thy soul against the land of Yisra᾽el;


###### Ezekiel 25:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/25:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/25:7)


לָכֵ֡ן הִנְנִי֩ נָטִ֨יתִי אֶת־יָדִ֜י עָלֶ֗יךָ וּנְתַתִּ֤יךָֽ (לבג) [לְבַז֙] לַגּוֹיִ֔ם וְהִכְרַתִּ֙יךָ֙ מִן־הָ֣עַמִּ֔ים וְהַאֲבַדְתִּ֖יךָ מִן־הָאֲרָצ֑וֹת אַשְׁמִ֣ידְךָ֔ וְיָדַעְתָּ֖ כִּֽי־אֲנִ֥י יְהֹוָֽה׃{פ}


behold, therefore I have stretched out my hand upon thee, and I will deliver thee for a spoil to the nations; and I will cut thee off from the peoples, and I will cause thee to perish out of the countries: I will destroy thee; and thou shalt know that I am the Lord.


###### Ezekiel 25:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/25:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/25:8)


כֹּ֥ה אָמַ֖ר אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֑ה יַ֗עַן אֲמֹ֤ר מוֹאָב֙ וְשֵׂעִ֔יר הִנֵּ֥ה כְּכׇֽל־הַגּוֹיִ֖ם בֵּ֥ית יְהוּדָֽה׃

###### Ezekiel 25:9

לָכֵן֩ הִנְנִ֨י פֹתֵ֜חַ אֶת־כֶּ֤תֶף מוֹאָב֙ מֵהֶ֣עָרִ֔ים מֵעָרָ֖יו מִקָּצֵ֑הוּ צְבִ֗י אֶ֚רֶץ בֵּ֣ית הַיְשִׁימֹ֔ת בַּ֥עַל מְע֖וֹן (וקריתמה) [וְקִרְיָתָֽיְמָה]׃


therefore, behold, I will open the flank of Mo᾽av on the side of the cities, on the side of his cities which are on his frontiers, the beautiful country of Bet-hayshimot, Ba῾al-me῾on, and Qiryatayim,


###### Ezekiel 25:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/25:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/25:10)


לִבְנֵי־קֶ֙דֶם֙ עַל־בְּנֵ֣י עַמּ֔וֹן וּנְתַתִּ֖יהָ לְמוֹרָשָׁ֑ה לְמַ֛עַן לֹא־תִזָּכֵ֥ר בְּנֵֽי־עַמּ֖וֹן בַּגּוֹיִֽם׃


to the children of the east I will give it (along with the children of ῾Ammon) as a possession, so that the children of ῾Ammon may not be remembered among the nations.


###### Ezekiel 25:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/25:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/25:11)


וּבְמוֹאָ֖ב אֶעֱשֶׂ֣ה שְׁפָטִ֑ים וְיָדְע֖וּ כִּי־אֲנִ֥י יְהֹוָֽה׃{פ}


And I will execute judgments upon Mo᾽av; and they shall know that I am the Lord.


###### Ezekiel 25:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/25:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/25:12)


כֹּ֤ה אָמַר֙ אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֔ה יַ֣עַן עֲשׂ֥וֹת אֱד֛וֹם בִּנְקֹ֥ם נָקָ֖ם לְבֵ֣ית יְהוּדָ֑ה וַיֶּאְשְׁמ֥וּ אָשׁ֖וֹם וְנִקְּמ֥וּ בָהֶֽם׃


Thus says the Lord God; Because Edom has dealt against the house of Yehuda by taking vengeance, and has greatly offended, and revenged himself upon them;


###### Ezekiel 25:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/25:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/25:13)


לָכֵ֗ן כֹּ֤ה אָמַר֙ אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֔ה וְנָטִ֤תִי יָדִי֙ עַל־אֱד֔וֹם וְהִכְרַתִּ֥י מִמֶּ֖נָּה אָדָ֣ם וּבְהֵמָ֑ה וּנְתַתִּ֤יהָ חׇרְבָּה֙ מִתֵּימָ֔ן וּדְדָ֖נֶה בַּחֶ֥רֶב יִפֹּֽלוּ׃


therefore thus says the Lord God; I will also stretch out my hand upon Edom, and will cut off man and beast from it; and I will make it desolate from Teman; and they of Dedan shall fall by the sword.


###### Ezekiel 25:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/25:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/25:14)


וְנָתַתִּ֨י אֶת־נִקְמָתִ֜י בֶּאֱד֗וֹם בְּיַד֙ עַמִּ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל וְעָשׂ֣וּ בֶאֱד֔וֹם כְּאַפִּ֖י וְכַחֲמָתִ֑י וְיָֽדְעוּ֙ אֶת־נִקְמָתִ֔י נְאֻ֖ם אֲדֹנָ֥י יֱהֹוִֽה׃


And I will lay my vengeance upon Edom by the hand of my people Yisra᾽el: and they shall do in Edom according to my anger and according to my fury; and they shall know my vengeance, says the Lord God.


###### Ezekiel 25:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/25:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/25:15)


כֹּ֤ה אָמַר֙ אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֔ה יַ֛עַן עֲשׂ֥וֹת פְּלִשְׁתִּ֖ים בִּנְקָמָ֑ה וַיִּנָּקְמ֤וּ נָקָם֙ בִּשְׁאָ֣ט בְּנֶ֔פֶשׁ לְמַשְׁחִ֖ית אֵיבַ֥ת עוֹלָֽם׃


Thus says the Lord God; Because the Pelishtim have dealt by revenge, and have taken vengeance with disdain of soul, to destroy with everlasting hatred;


###### Ezekiel 25:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/25:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/25:16)


לָכֵ֗ן כֹּ֤ה אָמַר֙ אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֔ה הִנְנִ֨י נוֹטֶ֤ה יָדִי֙ עַל־פְּלִשְׁתִּ֔ים וְהִכְרַתִּ֖י אֶת־כְּרֵתִ֑ים וְהַ֣אֲבַדְתִּ֔י אֶת־שְׁאֵרִ֖ית ח֥וֹף הַיָּֽם׃


therefore thus says the Lord God; Behold, I stretch out my hand upon the Pelishtim, and I will cut off the Keretim, and destroy the remnant of the sea coast;


###### Ezekiel 25:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/25:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/25:17)


וְעָשִׂ֤יתִי בָם֙ נְקָמ֣וֹת גְּדֹל֔וֹת בְּתוֹכְח֖וֹת חֵמָ֑ה וְיָֽדְעוּ֙ כִּֽי־אֲנִ֣י יְהֹוָ֔ה בְּתִתִּ֥י אֶת־נִקְמָתִ֖י בָּֽם׃{פ}


and I will execute great vengeance upon them with furious rebukes; and they shall know that I am the Lord, when I shall lay my vengeance upon them.



## Chapter 26

###### Ezekiel 26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/26:1)


וַיְהִ֛י בְּעַשְׁתֵּֽי־עֶשְׂרֵ֥ה שָׁנָ֖ה בְּאֶחָ֣ד לַחֹ֑דֶשׁ הָיָ֥ה דְבַר־יְהֹוָ֖ה אֵלַ֥י לֵאמֹֽר׃


And it came to pass in the eleventh year, on the first day of the month, that the word of the Lord came to me, saying,


###### Ezekiel 26:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/26:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/26:2)


בֶּן־אָדָ֗ם יַ֠עַן אֲשֶׁר־אָ֨מְרָה צֹּ֤ר עַל־יְרוּשָׁלַ֙͏ִם֙ הֶאָ֔ח נִשְׁבְּרָ֛ה דַּלְת֥וֹת הָעַמִּ֖ים נָסֵ֣בָּה אֵלָ֑י אִמָּלְאָ֖ה הׇחֳרָֽבָה׃


Son of man, because Żor has said against Yerushalayim, Aha, she is broken that was the gates of the peoples: she is turned to me: I shall be filled with her that is laid waste:


###### Ezekiel 26:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/26:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/26:3)


לָכֵ֗ן כֹּ֤ה אָמַר֙ אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֔ה הִנְנִ֥י עָלַ֖יִךְ צֹ֑ר וְהַעֲלֵיתִ֤י עָלַ֙יִךְ֙ גּוֹיִ֣ם רַבִּ֔ים כְּהַעֲל֥וֹת הַיָּ֖ם לְגַלָּֽיו׃


therefore thus says the Lord God; Behold, I am against thee, O Żor, and will cause many nations to come up against thee, as the sea causes his waves to come up.


###### Ezekiel 26:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/26:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/26:4)


וְשִׁחֲת֞וּ חֹמ֣וֹת צֹ֗ר וְהָֽרְסוּ֙ מִגְדָּלֶ֔יהָ וְסִחֵיתִ֥י עֲפָרָ֖הּ מִמֶּ֑נָּה וְנָתַתִּ֥י אוֹתָ֖הּ לִצְחִ֥יחַ סָֽלַע׃


And they shall destroy the walls of Żor, and break down her towers: I will also scrape her dust from her, and make her like a bare rock.


###### Ezekiel 26:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/26:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/26:5)


מִשְׁטַ֨ח חֲרָמִ֤ים תִּֽהְיֶה֙ בְּת֣וֹךְ הַיָּ֔ם כִּ֚י אֲנִ֣י דִבַּ֔רְתִּי נְאֻ֖ם אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֑ה וְהָיְתָ֥ה לְבַ֖ז לַגּוֹיִֽם׃


It shall be a place for the spreading of nets in the midst of the sea: for I have spoken it, says the Lord God: and it shall become a spoil to the nations.


###### Ezekiel 26:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/26:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/26:6)


וּבְנוֹתֶ֙יהָ֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר בַּשָּׂדֶ֔ה בַּחֶ֖רֶב תֵּהָרַ֑גְנָה וְיָדְע֖וּ כִּֽי־אֲנִ֥י יְהֹוָֽה׃{פ}


And her daughters who are in the field shall be slain by the sword; and they shall know that I am the Lord.


###### Ezekiel 26:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/26:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/26:7)


כִּ֣י כֹ֤ה אָמַר֙ אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֔ה הִנְנִ֧י מֵבִ֣יא אֶל־צֹ֗ר נְבוּכַדְרֶאצַּ֧ר מֶלֶךְ־בָּבֶ֛ל מִצָּפ֖וֹן מֶ֣לֶךְ מְלָכִ֑ים בְּס֛וּס וּבְרֶ֥כֶב וּבְפָרָשִׁ֖ים וְקָהָ֥ל וְעַם־רָֽב׃


For thus says the Lord God; Behold, I will bring upon Żor Nevukhadreżżar king of Bavel, a king of kings, from the north, with horses, and with chariots, and with horsemen, and a company, and much people.


###### Ezekiel 26:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/26:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/26:8)


בְּנוֹתַ֥יִךְ בַּשָּׂדֶ֖ה בַּחֶ֣רֶב יַהֲרֹ֑ג וְנָתַ֨ן עָלַ֜יִךְ דָּיֵ֗ק וְשָׁפַ֤ךְ עָלַ֙יִךְ֙ סֹֽלְלָ֔ה וְהֵקִ֥ים עָלַ֖יִךְ צִנָּֽה׃


He shall slay thy daughters in the field with the sword: and he shall lay a siege work against thee, and cast a mound against thee, and lift up the buckler against thee.


###### Ezekiel 26:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/26:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/26:9)


וּמְחִ֣י קׇֽבׇלּ֔וֹ יִתֵּ֖ן בְּחֹמוֹתָ֑יִךְ וּמִ֨גְדְּלֹתַ֔יִךְ יִתֹּ֖ץ בְּחַרְבוֹתָֽיו׃


And he shall set his engine of war against thy walls, and with his axes he shall break down thy towers.


###### Ezekiel 26:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/26:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/26:10)


מִשִּׁפְעַ֥ת סוּסָ֖יו יְכַסֵּ֣ךְ אֲבָקָ֑ם מִקּוֹל֩ פָּרַ֨שׁ וְגַלְגַּ֜ל וָרֶ֗כֶב תִּרְעַ֙שְׁנָה֙ חוֹמוֹתַ֔יִךְ בְּבֹאוֹ֙ בִּשְׁעָרַ֔יִךְ כִּמְבוֹאֵ֖י עִ֥יר מְבֻקָּעָֽה׃


By reason of the abundance of his horses their dust shall cover thee: thy walls shall shake at the noise of the horsemen, and of the wheels, and of the chariots, when he shall enter into thy gates, as men enter into a city in which a breach is made.


###### Ezekiel 26:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/26:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/26:11)


בְּפַרְס֣וֹת סוּסָ֔יו יִרְמֹ֖ס אֶת־כׇּל־חוּצוֹתָ֑יִךְ עַמֵּךְ֙ בַּחֶ֣רֶב יַהֲרֹ֔ג וּמַצְּב֥וֹת עֻזֵּ֖ךְ לָאָ֥רֶץ תֵּרֵֽד׃


With the hoofs of his horses shall he tread down all thy streets: he shall slay thy people by the sword, and thy strong garrisons shall go down to the ground.


###### Ezekiel 26:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/26:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/26:12)


וְשָׁלְל֣וּ חֵילֵ֗ךְ וּבָֽזְזוּ֙ רְכֻלָּתֵ֔ךְ וְהָֽרְסוּ֙ חוֹמוֹתַ֔יִךְ וּבָתֵּ֥י חֶמְדָּתֵ֖ךְ יִתֹּ֑צוּ וַאֲבָנַ֤יִךְ וְעֵצַ֙יִךְ֙ וַעֲפָרֵ֔ךְ בְּת֥וֹךְ מַ֖יִם יָשִֽׂימוּ׃


And they shall make a spoil of thy riches, and make a prey of thy merchandise: and they shall break down thy walls, and destroy the houses of thy delight: and they shall lay thy stones and thy timber and thy dust in the midst of the water.


###### Ezekiel 26:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/26:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/26:13)


וְהִשְׁבַּתִּ֖י הֲמ֣וֹן שִׁירָ֑יִךְ וְק֣וֹל כִּנּוֹרַ֔יִךְ לֹ֥א יִשָּׁמַ֖ע עֽוֹד׃


And I will cause the noise of thy songs to cease; and the sound of thy lyres shall be heard no more.


###### Ezekiel 26:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/26:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/26:14)


וּנְתַתִּ֞יךְ לִצְחִ֣יחַ סֶ֗לַע מִשְׁטַ֤ח חֲרָמִים֙ תִּֽהְיֶ֔ה לֹ֥א תִבָּנֶ֖ה ע֑וֹד כִּ֣י אֲנִ֤י יְהֹוָה֙ דִּבַּ֔רְתִּי נְאֻ֖ם אֲדֹנָ֥י יֱהֹוִֽה׃{ס}


And I will make thee like a bare rock: thou shalt be a place to spread nets upon; thou shalt be built no more: for I the Lord have spoken it, says the Lord God.


###### Ezekiel 26:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/26:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/26:15)


כֹּ֥ה אָמַ֛ר אֲדֹנָ֥י יֱהֹוִ֖ה לְצ֑וֹר הֲלֹ֣א**׀** מִקּ֣וֹל מַפַּלְתֵּ֗ךְ בֶּאֱנֹ֨ק חָלָ֜ל בֵּהָ֤רֵֽג הֶ֙רֶג֙ בְּתוֹכֵ֔ךְ יִרְעֲשׁ֖וּ הָאִיִּֽים׃


Thus says the Lord God to Żor; Shall not the islands shake at the sound of thy fall, when the wounded cry, when the slaughter is made in the midst of thee?


###### Ezekiel 26:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/26:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/26:16)


וְֽיָרְד֞וּ מֵעַ֣ל כִּסְאוֹתָ֗ם כֹּ֚ל נְשִׂיאֵ֣י הַיָּ֔ם וְהֵסִ֙ירוּ֙ אֶת־מְעִ֣ילֵיהֶ֔ם וְאֶת־בִּגְדֵ֥י רִקְמָתָ֖ם יִפְשֹׁ֑טוּ חֲרָד֤וֹת׀יִלְבָּ֙שׁוּ֙ עַל־הָאָ֣רֶץ יֵשֵׁ֔בוּ וְחָֽרְדוּ֙ לִרְגָעִ֔ים וְשָׁמְמ֖וּ עָלָֽיִךְ׃


Then all the princes of the sea shall come down from their thrones, and lay away their robes, and strip off their embroidered garments. They shall clothe themselves with trembling; they shall sit upon the ground, and shall tremble every moment, and be appalled at thee.


###### Ezekiel 26:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/26:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/26:17)


וְנָשְׂא֨וּ עָלַ֤יִךְ קִינָה֙ וְאָ֣מְרוּ לָ֔ךְ אֵ֣יךְ אָבַ֔דְתְּ נוֹשֶׁ֖בֶת מִיַּמִּ֑ים הָעִ֣יר הַהֻלָּ֗לָה אֲשֶׁר֩ הָיְתָ֨ה חֲזָקָ֤ה בַיָּם֙ הִ֣יא וְיֹשְׁבֶ֔יהָ אֲשֶׁר־נָתְנ֥וּ חִתִּיתָ֖ם לְכׇל־יוֹשְׁבֶֽיהָ׃


And they shall take up a lamentation for thee, and say to thee, How art thou destroyed, that was peopled from the seas, the renowned city, that was strong in the sea, she and her inhabitants, who cause their terror to be on all those round about.


###### Ezekiel 26:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/26:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/26:18)


עַתָּה֙ יֶחְרְד֣וּ הָאִיִּ֔ן י֖וֹם מַפַּלְתֵּ֑ךְ וְנִבְהֲל֛וּ הָאִיִּ֥ים אֲשֶׁר־בַּיָּ֖ם מִצֵּאתֵֽךְ׃{ס}


Now shall the islands tremble in the day of thy fall; yea, the islands that are in the sea shall be alarmed at thy departure.


###### Ezekiel 26:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/26:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/26:19)


כִּ֣י כֹ֤ה אָמַר֙ אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֔ה בְּתִתִּ֤י אֹתָךְ֙ עִ֣יר נֶחֱרֶ֔בֶת כֶּעָרִ֖ים אֲשֶׁ֣ר לֹֽא־נוֹשָׁ֑בוּ בְּהַעֲל֤וֹת עָלַ֙יִךְ֙ אֶת־תְּה֔וֹם וְכִסּ֖וּךְ הַמַּ֥יִם הָרַבִּֽים׃


For thus says the Lord God; When I shall make thee a desolate city, like the cities that are not inhabited; when I shall bring up the deep upon thee, and great waters shall cover thee;


###### Ezekiel 26:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/26:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/26:20)

when I shall bring thee down with them that descend into the pit, to the people of old time and shall set thee in the lowest parts of the earth (like places desolate of old,) with them that go down to the pit, that thou be not inhabited; and I shall set up my ornament in the land of living;

I will make thee a terror, and thou shalt be no more; though thou be sought for, yet shalt thou never be found again, says the Lord God.

###### Ezekiel 27:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/27:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/27:30)


וְהִשְׁמִ֤יעוּ עָלַ֙יִךְ֙ בְּקוֹלָ֔ם וְיִזְעֲק֖וּ מָרָ֑ה וְיַעֲל֤וּ עָפָר֙ עַל־רָ֣אשֵׁיהֶ֔ם בָּאֵ֖פֶר יִתְפַּלָּֽשׁוּ׃


and they shall cause their voice to be heard over thee, and shall cry bitterly, and shall cast up dust upon their heads, they shall wallow in the ashes:

וְהִקְרִ֤יחוּ אֵלַ֙יִךְ֙ קׇרְחָ֔ה וְחָגְר֖וּ שַׂקִּ֑ים וּבָכ֥וּ אֵלַ֛יִךְ בְּמַר־נֶ֖פֶשׁ מִסְפֵּ֥ד מָֽר׃


and they shall make themselves bald for thee, and gird themselves with sackcloth, and they shall weep for thee in bitterness of soul, a bitter mourning.

בְּצֵ֤את עִזְבוֹנַ֙יִךְ֙ מִיַּמִּ֔ים הִשְׂבַּ֖עַתְּ עַמִּ֣ים רַבִּ֑ים בְּרֹ֤ב הוֹנַ֙יִךְ֙ וּמַ֣עֲרָבַ֔יִךְ הֶעֱשַׁ֖רְתְּ מַלְכֵי־אָֽרֶץ׃

בֶּן־אָדָ֡ם אֱמֹר֩ לִנְגִ֨יד צֹ֜ר כֹּה־אָמַ֣ר**׀** אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֗ה יַ֣עַן גָּבַ֤הּ לִבְּךָ֙ וַתֹּ֙אמֶר֙ אֵ֣ל אָ֔נִי מוֹשַׁ֧ב אֱלֹהִ֛ים יָשַׁ֖בְתִּי בְּלֵ֣ב יַמִּ֑ים וְאַתָּ֤ה אָדָם֙ וְֽלֹא־אֵ֔ל וַתִּתֵּ֥ן לִבְּךָ֖ כְּלֵ֥ב אֱלֹהִֽים׃


Son of man, say to the prince of Żor, Thus says the Lord God; Because thy heart is lifted up, and thou hast said, I am a God, I sit in the seat of God, in the heart of the seas; yet thou art a man, and not God, though thou hast set thy heart as the heart of God:


###### Ezekiel 28:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/28:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/28:3)

behold, thou art wiser than Dani᾽el; there is no secret that they can hide from thee:

behold, therefore I will bring strangers upon thee, the most terrible of the nations: and they shall draw their swords against the beauty of thy wisdom, and they shall defile thy brightness.

לַשַּׁ֖חַת יוֹרִד֑וּךָ וָמַ֛תָּה מְמוֹתֵ֥י חָלָ֖ל בְּלֵ֥ב יַמִּֽים׃


They shall bring thee down to the pit, and thou shalt die the deaths of them that are slain in the heart of the seas.


###### Ezekiel 28:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/28:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/28:9)


הֶאָמֹ֤ר תֹּאמַר֙ אֱלֹהִ֣ים אָ֔נִי לִפְנֵ֖י הֹרְגֶ֑ךָ וְאַתָּ֥ה אָדָ֛ם וְלֹא־אֵ֖ל בְּיַ֥ד מְחַלְלֶֽיךָ׃

בֶּן־אָדָ֕ם שָׂ֥א קִינָ֖ה עַל־מֶ֣לֶךְ צ֑וֹר וְאָמַ֣רְתָּ לּ֗וֹ כֹּ֤ה אָמַר֙ אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֔ה אַתָּה֙ חוֹתֵ֣ם תׇּכְנִ֔ית מָלֵ֥א חׇכְמָ֖ה וּכְלִ֥יל יֹֽפִי׃


Son of man, take up a lamentation for the king of Żor, and say to him, Thus says the Lord God; Thou art a seal and a paragon, full of wisdom, and perfect in beauty.


###### Ezekiel 28:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/28:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/28:13)


בְּעֵ֨דֶן גַּן־אֱלֹהִ֜ים הָיִ֗יתָ כׇּל־אֶ֨בֶן יְקָרָ֤ה מְסֻכָתֶ֙ךָ֙ אֹ֣דֶם פִּטְדָ֞ה וְיָהֲלֹ֗ם תַּרְשִׁ֥ישׁ שֹׁ֙הַם֙ וְיָ֣שְׁפֵ֔ה סַפִּ֣יר נֹ֔פֶךְ וּבָרְקַ֖ת וְזָהָ֑ב מְלֶ֨אכֶת תֻּפֶּ֤יךָ וּנְקָבֶ֙יךָ֙ בָּ֔ךְ בְּי֥וֹם הִבָּרַאֲךָ֖ כּוֹנָֽנוּ׃


Thou has been in ῾Eden the garden of God; every precious stone was thy covering, the ruby, the crysolithe, and the diamond, the emerald, the shoham, and the jade, the sapphire, the turquoise, and the beryl, and gold; the workmanship of thy settings and thy sockets was in thee, in the day that thou wast created they were prepared.

Thou wast perfect in thy ways from the day that thou wast created, till iniquity was found in thee.


###### Ezekiel 28:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/28:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/28:16)


בְּרֹ֣ב רְכֻלָּתְךָ֗ מָל֧וּ תוֹכְךָ֛ חָמָ֖ס וַֽתֶּחֱטָ֑א וָאֲחַלֶּלְךָ֩ מֵהַ֨ר אֱלֹהִ֤ים וָֽאַבֶּדְךָ֙ כְּר֣וּב הַסֹּכֵ֔ךְ מִתּ֖וֹךְ אַבְנֵי־אֵֽשׁ׃

###### Ezekiel 28:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/28:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/28:17)


גָּבַ֤הּ לִבְּךָ֙ בְּיׇפְיֶ֔ךָ שִׁחַ֥תָּ חׇכְמָתְךָ֖ עַל־יִפְעָתֶ֑ךָ עַל־אֶ֣רֶץ הִשְׁלַכְתִּ֗יךָ לִפְנֵ֧י מְלָכִ֛ים נְתַתִּ֖יךָ לְרַ֥אֲוָה בָֽךְ׃

מֵרֹ֣ב עֲוֺנֶ֗יךָ בְּעֶ֙וֶל֙ רְכֻלָּ֣תְךָ֔ חִלַּ֖לְתָּ מִקְדָּשֶׁ֑יךָ וָאוֹצִא־אֵ֤שׁ מִתּֽוֹכְךָ֙ הִ֣יא אֲכָלַ֔תְךָ וָאֶתֶּנְךָ֤ לְאֵ֙פֶר֙ עַל־הָאָ֔רֶץ לְעֵינֵ֖י כׇּל־רֹאֶֽיךָ׃


Thou hast defiled thy sanctuaries by the multitude of thy iniquities, by the iniquity of thy traffic; therefore have I brought forth a fire from the midst of thee, it has devoured thee, and I have turned thee to ashes upon the earth in the sight of all them that behold thee.


###### Ezekiel 28:19

###### Ezekiel 28:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/28:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/28:22)


וְאָמַרְתָּ֗ כֹּ֤ה אָמַר֙ אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֔ה הִנְנִ֤י עָלַ֙יִךְ֙ צִיד֔וֹן וְנִכְבַּדְתִּ֖י בְּתוֹכֵ֑ךְ וְֽיָדְע֞וּ כִּֽי־אֲנִ֣י יְהֹוָ֗ה בַּעֲשׂ֥וֹתִי בָ֛הּ שְׁפָטִ֖ים וְנִקְדַּ֥שְׁתִּי בָֽהּ׃


and say, Thus says the Lord God; Behold, I am against thee, O Żidon; and I will be glorified in the midst of thee: and they shall know that I am the Lord, when I shall have executed judgments in her, and shall be sanctified in her.


###### Ezekiel 28:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/28:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/28:23)


וְשִׁלַּחְתִּי־בָ֞הּ דֶּ֤בֶר וָדָם֙ בְּח֣וּצוֹתֶ֔יהָ וְנִפְלַ֤ל חָלָל֙ בְּתוֹכָ֔הּ בְּחֶ֥רֶב עָלֶ֖יהָ מִסָּבִ֑יב וְיָדְע֖וּ כִּי־אֲנִ֥י יְהֹוָֽה׃


For I will send into her pestilence, and blood into her streets; and the wounded shall fall in heaps in the midst of her by the sword upon her on every side; and they shall know that I am the Lord.


###### Ezekiel 28:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/28:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/28:24)


וְלֹא־יִהְיֶ֨ה ע֜וֹד לְבֵ֣ית יִשְׂרָאֵ֗ל סִלּ֤וֹן מַמְאִיר֙ וְק֣וֹץ מַכְאִ֔ב מִכֹּל֙ סְבִ֣יבֹתָ֔ם הַשָּׁאטִ֖ים אוֹתָ֑ם וְיָ֣דְע֔וּ כִּ֥י אֲנִ֖י אֲדֹנָ֥י יֱהֹוִֽה׃{פ}


And there shall be no more a pricking brier to the house of Yisra᾽el, nor a thorn to give pain, among all those that are round about that disdain them; and they shall know that I am the Lord.


###### Ezekiel 28:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/28:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/28:25)


כֹּה־אָמַר֮ אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִה֒ בְּקַבְּצִ֣י**׀** אֶת־בֵּ֣ית יִשְׂרָאֵ֗ל מִן־הָֽעַמִּים֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר נָפֹ֣צוּ בָ֔ם וְנִקְדַּ֥שְׁתִּי בָ֖ם לְעֵינֵ֣י הַגּוֹיִ֑ם וְיָֽשְׁבוּ֙ עַל־אַדְמָתָ֔ם אֲשֶׁ֥ר נָתַ֖תִּי לְעַבְדִּ֥י לְיַעֲקֹֽב׃


Thus says the Lord God; When I shall have gathered the house of Yisra᾽el from the peoples among whom they are scattered, and shall be sanctified in them in the sight of the nations, then shall they dwell in their land that I have given to my servant Ya῾aqov.


###### Ezekiel 28:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/28:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/28:26)


וְיָשְׁב֣וּ עָלֶ֘יהָ֮ לָבֶ֒טַח֒ וּבָנ֤וּ בָתִּים֙ וְנָטְע֣וּ כְרָמִ֔ים וְיָשְׁב֖וּ לָבֶ֑טַח בַּעֲשׂוֹתִ֣י שְׁפָטִ֗ים בְּכֹ֨ל הַשָּׁאטִ֤ים אֹתָם֙ מִסְּבִ֣יבוֹתָ֔ם וְיָ֣דְע֔וּ כִּ֛י אֲנִ֥י יְהֹוָ֖ה אֱלֹהֵיהֶֽם׃{פ}


And they shall dwell safely there, and shall build houses, and plant vineyards, and they shall dwell in security, when I have executed judgments upon all those that disdain them round about them; and they shall know that I am the Lord their God.



## Chapter 29

###### Ezekiel 29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/29:1)


בַּשָּׁנָה֙ הָעֲשִׂרִ֔ית בָּעֲשִׂרִ֕י בִּשְׁנֵ֥ים עָשָׂ֖ר לַחֹ֑דֶשׁ הָיָ֥ה דְבַר־יְהֹוָ֖ה אֵלַ֥י לֵאמֹֽר׃


In the tenth year, in the tenth month, on the twelfth day of the month, the word of the Lord came to me, saying,


###### Ezekiel 29:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/29:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/29:2)


בֶּן־אָדָ֕ם שִׂ֣ים פָּנֶ֔יךָ עַל־פַּרְעֹ֖ה מֶ֣לֶךְ מִצְרָ֑יִם וְהִנָּבֵ֣א עָלָ֔יו וְעַל־מִצְרַ֖יִם כֻּלָּֽהּ׃


Son of man, set thy face against Par῾o king of Miżrayim, and prophesy against him, and against all Miżrayim:


###### Ezekiel 29:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/29:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/29:3)


דַּבֵּ֨ר וְאָמַרְתָּ֜ כֹּה־אָמַ֣ר**׀** אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֗ה הִנְנִ֤י עָלֶ֙יךָ֙ פַּרְעֹ֣ה מֶלֶךְ־מִצְרַ֔יִם הַתַּנִּים֙ הַגָּד֔וֹל הָרֹבֵ֖ץ בְּת֣וֹךְ יְאֹרָ֑יו אֲשֶׁ֥ר אָמַ֛ר לִ֥י יְאֹרִ֖י וַאֲנִ֥י עֲשִׂיתִֽנִי׃


speak, and say, Thus says the Lord God; Behold, I am against thee, Par῾o king of Miżrayim, the great crocodile that couches in the midst of his streams, who has said, My River is my own, and I have made it for myself.


###### Ezekiel 29:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/29:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/29:4)


וְנָתַתִּ֤י (חחיים) [חַחִים֙] בִּלְחָיֶ֔יךָ וְהִדְבַּקְתִּ֥י דְגַת־יְאֹרֶ֖יךָ בְּקַשְׂקְשֹׂתֶ֑יךָ וְהַעֲלִיתִ֙יךָ֙ מִתּ֣וֹךְ יְאֹרֶ֔יךָ וְאֵת֙ כׇּל־דְּגַ֣ת יְאֹרֶ֔יךָ בְּקַשְׂקְשֹׂתֶ֖יךָ תִּדְבָּֽק׃


But I will put hooks in thy jaws, and I will cause the fish of thy streams to stick to thy scales, and I will bring thee up out of the midst of thy streams, and all the fish of thy streams shall stick to thy scales.


###### Ezekiel 29:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/29:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/29:5)


וּנְטַשְׁתִּ֣יךָ הַמִּדְבָּ֗רָה אוֹתְךָ֙ וְאֵת֙ כׇּל־דְּגַ֣ת יְאֹרֶ֔יךָ עַל־פְּנֵ֤י הַשָּׂדֶה֙ תִּפּ֔וֹל לֹ֥א תֵאָסֵ֖ף וְלֹ֣א תִקָּבֵ֑ץ לְחַיַּ֥ת הָאָ֛רֶץ וּלְע֥וֹף הַשָּׁמַ֖יִם נְתַתִּ֥יךָ לְאׇכְלָֽה׃


And I will cast thee unto the wilderness, thee and all the fish of thy rivers: thou shalt fall upon the open fields; thou shalt not be brought together, nor gathered: I have given thee for food to the beasts of the earth and to the birds of the sky.


###### Ezekiel 29:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/29:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/29:6)


וְיָֽדְעוּ֙ כׇּל־יֹשְׁבֵ֣י מִצְרַ֔יִם כִּ֖י אֲנִ֣י יְהֹוָ֑ה יַ֧עַן הֱיוֹתָ֛ם מִשְׁעֶ֥נֶת קָנֶ֖ה לְבֵ֥ית יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


And all the inhabitants of Miżrayim shall know that I am the Lord, because they have been a staff of reed to the house of Yisra᾽el.


###### Ezekiel 29:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/29:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/29:7)


בְּתׇפְשָׂ֨ם בְּךָ֤ (בכפך) [בַכַּף֙] תֵּר֔וֹץ וּבָקַעְתָּ֥ לָהֶ֖ם כׇּל־כָּתֵ֑ף וּבְהִֽשָּׁעֲנָ֤ם עָלֶ֙יךָ֙ תִּשָּׁבֵ֔ר וְהַעֲמַדְתָּ֥ לָהֶ֖ם כׇּל־מׇתְנָֽיִם׃{ס}


When they took hold of thee by the hand, thou didst break, and tore all their shoulder: and when they leaned upon thee, thou didst break, and didst make all their loins to be at a stand.


###### Ezekiel 29:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/29:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/29:8)


לָכֵ֗ן כֹּ֤ה אָמַר֙ אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֔ה הִנְנִ֛י מֵבִ֥יא עָלַ֖יִךְ חָ֑רֶב וְהִכְרַתִּ֥י מִמֵּ֖ךְ אָדָ֥ם וּבְהֵמָֽה׃


Therefore thus says the Lord God; Behold, I will bring a sword upon thee, and cut off man and beast out of thee.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/29:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/29:9)


וְהָיְתָ֤ה אֶרֶץ־מִצְרַ֙יִם֙ לִשְׁמָמָ֣ה וְחׇרְבָּ֔ה וְיָדְע֖וּ כִּי־אֲנִ֣י יְהֹוָ֑ה יַ֧עַן אָמַ֛ר יְאֹ֥ר לִ֖י וַאֲנִ֥י עָשִֽׂיתִי׃

###### Ezekiel 29:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/29:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/29:10)


לָכֵ֛ן הִנְנִ֥י אֵלֶ֖יךָ וְאֶל־יְאֹרֶ֑יךָ וְנָתַתִּ֞י אֶת־אֶ֣רֶץ מִצְרַ֗יִם לְחׇרְבוֹת֙ חֹ֣רֶב שְׁמָמָ֔ה מִמִּגְדֹּ֥ל סְוֵנֵ֖ה וְעַד־גְּב֥וּל כּֽוּשׁ׃


Behold, therefore I am against thee, and against thy streams, and I will make the land of Miżrayim utterly waste and desolate, from Migdol to Seven, as far as the border of Kush.


###### Ezekiel 29:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/29:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/29:11)


לֹ֤א תַֽעֲבׇר־בָּהּ֙ רֶ֣גֶל אָדָ֔ם וְרֶ֥גֶל בְּהֵמָ֖ה לֹ֣א תַֽעֲבׇר־בָּ֑הּ וְלֹ֥א תֵשֵׁ֖ב אַרְבָּעִ֥ים שָׁנָֽה׃


No foot of man shall pass through it, nor foot of beast shall pass through it, neither shall it be inhabited for forty years.


###### Ezekiel 29:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/29:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/29:12)


וְנָתַתִּ֣י אֶת־אֶ֩רֶץ֩ מִצְרַ֨יִם שְׁמָמָ֜ה בְּת֣וֹךְ**׀** אֲרָצ֣וֹת נְשַׁמּ֗וֹת וְעָרֶ֙יהָ֙ בְּת֨וֹךְ עָרִ֤ים מׇֽחֳרָבוֹת֙ תִּהְיֶ֣יןָ שְׁמָמָ֔ה אַרְבָּעִ֖ים שָׁנָ֑ה וַהֲפִצֹתִ֤י אֶת־מִצְרַ֙יִם֙ בַּגּוֹיִ֔ם וְזֵרִיתִ֖ים בָּאֲרָצֽוֹת׃{ס}


And I will make the land of Miżrayim desolate in the midst of waste lands, and her cities shall be desolate among ruined cities for forty years: and I will scatter Miżrayim among the nations, and will disperse them through the lands.


###### Ezekiel 29:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/29:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/29:13)


כִּ֛י כֹּ֥ה אָמַ֖ר אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֑ה מִקֵּ֞ץ אַרְבָּעִ֤ים שָׁנָה֙ אֲקַבֵּ֣ץ אֶת־מִצְרַ֔יִם מִן־הָעַמִּ֖ים אֲשֶׁר־נָפֹ֥צוּ שָֽׁמָּה׃


Yet thus says the Lord God; At the end of forty years I will gather Miżrayim from the people where they were scattered:


###### Ezekiel 29:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/29:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/29:14)


וְשַׁבְתִּי֙ אֶת־שְׁב֣וּת מִצְרַ֔יִם וַהֲשִׁבֹתִ֤י אֹתָם֙ אֶ֣רֶץ פַּתְר֔וֹס עַל־אֶ֖רֶץ מְכוּרָתָ֑ם וְהָ֥יוּ שָׁ֖ם מַמְלָכָ֥ה שְׁפָלָֽה׃


and I will bring back the captivity of Miżrayim, and will cause them to return to the land of Patros, into the land of their origin; and they shall be there an abject kingdom.


###### Ezekiel 29:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/29:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/29:15)


מִן־הַמַּמְלָכוֹת֙ תִּֽהְיֶ֣ה שְׁפָלָ֔ה וְלֹֽא־תִתְנַשֵּׂ֥א ע֖וֹד עַל־הַגּוֹיִ֑ם וְהִ֨מְעַטְתִּ֔ים לְבִלְתִּ֖י רְד֥וֹת בַּגּוֹיִֽם׃


It shall be the most abject of the kingdoms; and it shall not exalt itself any more above the nations: for I will diminish them, that they shall no more rule over the nations.


###### Ezekiel 29:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/29:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/29:16)


וְלֹ֣א יִֽהְיֶה־עוֹד֩ לְבֵ֨ית יִשְׂרָאֵ֤ל לְמִבְטָח֙ מַזְכִּ֣יר עָוֺ֔ן בִּפְנוֹתָ֖ם אַחֲרֵיהֶ֑ם וְיָ֣דְע֔וּ כִּ֥י אֲנִ֖י אֲדֹנָ֥י יֱהֹוִֽה׃{פ}


And it shall be no more a safe standby for the house of Yisra᾽el, bringing their iniquity to remembrance, when they shall turn after them: but they shall know that I am the Lord God.


###### Ezekiel 29:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/29:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/29:17)


וַיְהִ֗י בְּעֶשְׂרִ֤ים וָשֶׁ֙בַע֙ שָׁנָ֔ה בָּרִאשׁ֖וֹן בְּאֶחָ֣ד לַחֹ֑דֶשׁ הָיָ֥ה דְבַר־יְהֹוָ֖ה אֵלַ֥י לֵאמֹֽר׃


And it came to pass in the twenty seventh year, in the first month, on the first day of the month, that the word of the Lord came to me, saying,


###### Ezekiel 29:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/29:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/29:18)


בֶּן־אָדָ֗ם נְבוּכַדְרֶאצַּ֣ר מֶֽלֶךְ־בָּ֠בֶ֠ל הֶעֱבִ֨יד אֶת־חֵיל֜וֹ עֲבֹדָ֤ה גְדוֹלָה֙ אֶל־צֹ֔ר כׇּל־רֹ֣אשׁ מֻקְרָ֔ח וְכׇל־כָּתֵ֖ף מְרוּטָ֑ה וְ֠שָׂכָ֠ר לֹא־הָ֨יָה ל֤וֹ וּלְחֵילוֹ֙ מִצֹּ֔ר עַל־הָעֲבֹדָ֖ה אֲשֶׁר־עָבַ֥ד עָלֶֽיהָ׃{ס}


Son of man, Nevukhadreżżar king of Bavel made his army labour hard against Żor: every head was made bald, and every shoulder was peeled: yet he had no wages, nor his army, for Żor, for the labour that he performed against it:


###### Ezekiel 29:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/29:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/29:19)


לָכֵ֗ן כֹּ֤ה אָמַר֙ אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֔ה הִנְנִ֥י נֹתֵ֛ן לִנְבוּכַדְרֶאצַּ֥ר מֶלֶךְ־בָּבֶ֖ל אֶת־אֶ֣רֶץ מִצְרָ֑יִם וְנָשָׂ֨א הֲמֹנָ֜הּ וְשָׁלַ֤ל שְׁלָלָהּ֙ וּבָזַ֣ז בִּזָּ֔הּ וְהָיְתָ֥ה שָׂכָ֖ר לְחֵילֽוֹ׃


therefore thus says the Lord God; Behold, I will give the land of Miżrayim to Nevukhadreżżar king of Bavel; and he shall take her multitude, and take her spoil, and take her prey; and it shall be the wages for his army.


###### Ezekiel 29:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/29:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/29:20)


פְּעֻלָּתוֹ֙ אֲשֶׁר־עָ֣בַד בָּ֔הּ נָתַ֥תִּי ל֖וֹ אֶת־אֶ֣רֶץ מִצְרָ֑יִם אֲשֶׁר֙ עָ֣שׂוּ לִ֔י נְאֻ֖ם אֲדֹנָ֥י יֱהֹוִֽה׃


I have given him the land of Miżrayim for his labour with which he served against it, which they did for me, says the Lord God.


###### Ezekiel 29:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/29:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/29:21)


בַּיּ֣וֹם הַה֗וּא אַצְמִ֤יחַ קֶ֙רֶן֙ לְבֵ֣ית יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל וּלְךָ֛ אֶתֵּ֥ן פִּתְחֽוֹן־פֶּ֖ה בְּתוֹכָ֑ם וְיָדְע֖וּ כִּֽי־אֲנִ֥י יְהֹוָֽה׃{פ}


On that day will I cause the horn of the house of Yisra᾽el to put out shoots, and I will open thy mouth in the midst of them; and they shall know that I am the Lord.



## Chapter 30

###### Ezekiel 30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/30:1)


וַיְהִ֥י דְבַר־יְהֹוָ֖ה אֵלַ֥י לֵאמֹֽר׃


And the word of the Lord came to me, saying,


###### Ezekiel 30:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/30:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/30:2)

וְחִזַּקְתִּ֗י אֶת־זְרֹעוֹת֙ מֶ֣לֶךְ בָּבֶ֔ל וְנָתַתִּ֥י אֶת־חַרְבִּ֖י בְּיָד֑וֹ וְשָֽׁבַרְתִּי֙ אֶת־זְרֹע֣וֹת פַּרְעֹ֔ה וְנָאַ֛ק נַאֲק֥וֹת חָלָ֖ל לְפָנָֽיו׃

## Chapter 31

###### Ezekiel 31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/31:1)


וַיְהִ֗י בְּאַחַ֤ת עֶשְׂרֵה֙ שָׁנָ֔ה בַּשְּׁלִישִׁ֖י בְּאֶחָ֣ד לַחֹ֑דֶשׁ הָיָ֥ה דְבַר־יְהֹוָ֖ה אֵלַ֥י לֵאמֹֽר׃
