###### Ezekiel 31:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/31:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/31:2)


בֶּן־אָדָ֕ם אֱמֹ֛ר אֶל־פַּרְעֹ֥ה מֶלֶךְ־מִצְרַ֖יִם וְאֶל־הֲמוֹנ֑וֹ אֶל־מִ֖י דָּמִ֥יתָ בְגׇדְלֶֽךָ׃


Son of man, speak to Par῾o king of Miżrayim, and to his multitude; Whom art thou like in thy greatness?


###### Ezekiel 31:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/31:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/31:3)


הִנֵּ֨ה אַשּׁ֜וּר אֶ֣רֶז בַּלְּבָנ֗וֹן יְפֵ֥ה עָנָ֛ף וְחֹ֥רֶשׁ מֵצַ֖ל וּגְבַ֣הּ קוֹמָ֑ה וּבֵ֣ין עֲבֹתִ֔ים הָיְתָ֖ה צַמַּרְתּֽוֹ׃


Behold, Ashshur was a cedar in the Levanon with fair branches, amid a shadowing forest, and of a high stature; and its top was among the thick boughs.


###### Ezekiel 31:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/31:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/31:4)


מַ֣יִם גִּדְּל֔וּהוּ תְּה֖וֹם רֹמְמָ֑תְהוּ אֶת־נַהֲרֹתֶ֗יהָ הֹלֵךְ֙ סְבִיב֣וֹת מַטָּעָ֔הּ וְאֶת־תְּעָלֹתֶ֣יהָ שִׁלְחָ֔ה אֶ֖ל כׇּל־עֲצֵ֥י הַשָּׂדֶֽה׃


The waters made it great, the deep set it up on high, with her rivers running round about her plants, and she sent out her little canals to all the trees of the field.


###### Ezekiel 31:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/31:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/31:5)


עַל־כֵּן֙ גָּבְהָ֣א קֹמָת֔וֹ מִכֹּ֖ל עֲצֵ֣י הַשָּׂדֶ֑ה וַתִּרְבֶּ֨ינָה סַֽרְעַפֹּתָ֜יו וַתֶּאֱרַ֧כְנָה פֹארֹתָ֛ו מִמַּ֥יִם רַבִּ֖ים בְּשַׁלְּחֽוֹ׃


Therefore its height was exalted above all the trees of the field, and its boughs were multiplied, and its branches became long because of the multitude of waters, when it shot them forth.


###### Ezekiel 31:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/31:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/31:6)


בִּסְעַפֹּתָ֤יו קִֽנְנוּ֙ כׇּל־ע֣וֹף הַשָּׁמַ֔יִם וְתַ֤חַת פֹּֽארֹתָיו֙ יָֽלְד֔וּ כֹּ֖ל חַיַּ֣ת הַשָּׂדֶ֑ה וּבְצִלּוֹ֙ יֵֽשְׁב֔וּ כֹּ֖ל גּוֹיִ֥ם רַבִּֽים׃


All the birds of the sky made their nests in its boughs, and under its branches all the beasts of the field brought forth their young, and under its shadow all great nations dwelt.


###### Ezekiel 31:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/31:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/31:7)


וַיְּיִ֣ף בְּגׇדְל֔וֹ בְּאֹ֖רֶךְ דָּלִיּוֹתָ֑יו כִּֽי־הָיָ֥ה שׇׁרְשׁ֖וֹ אֶל־מַ֥יִם רַבִּֽים׃


Thus was it fair in its greatness, in the length of its branches: for its root was by many waters.


###### Ezekiel 31:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/31:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/31:8)


אֲרָזִ֣ים לֹֽא־עֲמָמֻ֘הוּ֮ בְּגַן־אֱלֹהִים֒ בְּרוֹשִׁ֗ים לֹ֤א דָמוּ֙ אֶל־סְעַפֹּתָ֔יו וְעַרְמֹנִ֥ים לֹא־הָי֖וּ כְּפֹרֹאתָ֑יו כׇּל־עֵץ֙ בְּגַן־אֱלֹהִ֔ים לֹא־דָמָ֥ה אֵלָ֖יו בְּיׇפְיֽוֹ׃


The cedars in the garden of God could not obscure it: the cypress trees were not like its boughs, and the plane trees were not like its branches; nor did any tree in the garden of God compare with it in beauty.


###### Ezekiel 31:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/31:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/31:9)


יָפֶ֣ה עֲשִׂיתִ֔יו בְּרֹ֖ב דָּלִיּוֹתָ֑יו וַיְקַנְאֻ֙הוּ֙ כׇּל־עֲצֵי־עֵ֔דֶן אֲשֶׁ֖ר בְּגַ֥ן הָאֱלֹהִֽים׃{פ}


I have made it fair by the multitude of its branches: so that all the trees of ῾Eden, that were in the garden of God, envied him.


###### Ezekiel 31:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/31:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/31:10)


לָכֵ֗ן כֹּ֤ה אָמַר֙ אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֔ה יַ֕עַן אֲשֶׁ֥ר גָּבַ֖הְתָּ בְּקוֹמָ֑ה וַיִּתֵּ֤ן צַמַּרְתּוֹ֙ אֶל־בֵּ֣ין עֲבוֹתִ֔ים וְרָ֥ם לְבָב֖וֹ בְּגׇבְהֽוֹ׃


Therefore thus says the Lord God; Because thou hast lifted up thyself in height, and he has shot up his top among the thick boughs, and his heart is lifted up in his height;


###### Ezekiel 31:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/31:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/31:11)


וְאֶ֨תְּנֵ֔הוּ בְּיַ֖ד אֵ֣יל גּוֹיִ֑ם עָשׂ֤וֹ יַֽעֲשֶׂה֙ ל֔וֹ כְּרִשְׁע֖וֹ גֵּרַשְׁתִּֽהוּ׃


I have therefore delivered him into the hand of the mighty one of the nations; he shall surely deal with him: I have driven him out for his wickedness.


###### Ezekiel 31:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/31:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/31:12)


וַיִּכְרְתֻ֧הוּ זָרִ֛ים עָרִיצֵ֥י גוֹיִ֖ם וַֽיִּטְּשֻׁ֑הוּ אֶל־הֶ֠הָרִ֠ים וּבְכׇל־גֵּ֨אָי֜וֹת נָפְל֣וּ דָלִיּוֹתָ֗יו וַתִּשָּׁבַ֤רְנָה פֹֽרֹאתָיו֙ בְּכֹל֙ אֲפִיקֵ֣י הָאָ֔רֶץ וַיֵּרְד֧וּ מִצִּלּ֛וֹ כׇּל־עַמֵּ֥י הָאָ֖רֶץ וַֽיִּטְּשֻֽׁהוּ׃


And strangers, the terrible of the nations, have cut him off, and have left him: upon the mountains and in all the valleys his branches are fallen, and his boughs were broken by all the water courses of the land; and all the peoples of the earth are gone down from his shadow, and have left him.


###### Ezekiel 31:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/31:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/31:13)


עַל־מַפַּלְתּ֥וֹ יִשְׁכְּנ֖וּ כׇּל־ע֣וֹף הַשָּׁמָ֑יִם וְאֶל־פֹּרֹאתָ֣יו הָי֔וּ כֹּ֖ל חַיַּ֥ת הַשָּׂדֶֽה׃


Upon his ruin shall dwell all the birds of the sky, and all the beasts of the field shall be upon his branches:


###### Ezekiel 31:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/31:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/31:14)


לְמַ֡עַן אֲשֶׁר֩ לֹא־יִגְבְּה֨וּ בְקוֹמָתָ֜ם כׇּל־עֲצֵי־מַ֗יִם וְלֹֽא־יִתְּנ֤וּ אֶת־צַמַּרְתָּם֙ אֶל־בֵּ֣ין עֲבֹתִ֔ים וְלֹֽא־יַעַמְד֧וּ אֵלֵיהֶ֛ם בְּגׇבְהָ֖ם כׇּל־שֹׁ֣תֵי מָ֑יִם כִּֽי־כֻלָּם֩ נִתְּנ֨וּ לַמָּ֜וֶת אֶל־אֶ֣רֶץ תַּחְתִּ֗ית בְּת֛וֹךְ בְּנֵ֥י אָדָ֖ם אֶל־י֥וֹרְדֵי בֽוֹר׃{פ}


so that none of all the trees by the waters exalt themselves in their height, nor shoot up their top among the thick boughs, and that their trunks do not tower on high, all that drink water: for they are all delivered to death, to the nether parts of the earth, in the midst of the children of men, with them that go down to the pit.


###### Ezekiel 31:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/31:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/31:15)


כֹּֽה־אָמַ֞ר אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֗ה בְּי֨וֹם רִדְתּ֤וֹ שְׁא֙וֹלָה֙ הֶאֱבַ֜לְתִּי כִּסֵּ֤תִי עָלָיו֙ אֶת־תְּה֔וֹם וָֽאֶמְנַע֙ נַהֲרוֹתֶ֔יהָ וַיִּכָּלְא֖וּ מַ֣יִם רַבִּ֑ים וָאַקְדִּ֤ר עָלָיו֙ לְבָנ֔וֹן וְכׇל־עֲצֵ֥י הַשָּׂדֶ֖ה עָלָ֥יו עֻלְפֶּֽה׃


Thus says the Lord God; In the day when he went down to She᾽ol I caused the deep to mourn, to cover itself for him, I restrained the floods thereof, and the great waters were stayed: and I wrapped Levanon in gloom for him, and all the trees of the field fainted for him.


###### Ezekiel 31:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/31:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/31:16)


מִקּ֤וֹל מַפַּלְתּוֹ֙ הִרְעַ֣שְׁתִּי גוֹיִ֔ם בְּהוֹרִדִ֥י אֹת֛וֹ שְׁא֖וֹלָה אֶת־י֣וֹרְדֵי ב֑וֹר וַיִּנָּ֨חֲמ֜וּ בְּאֶ֤רֶץ תַּחְתִּית֙ כׇּל־עֲצֵי־עֵ֔דֶן מִבְחַ֥ר וְטוֹב־לְבָנ֖וֹן כׇּל־שֹׁ֥תֵי מָֽיִם׃


I made the nations shake at the sound of his fall, when I cast him down to She᾽ol with them that descend into the pit: and all the trees of ῾Eden, the choice and best of Levanon, all that drink water were comforted in the nether parts of the earth.


###### Ezekiel 31:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/31:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/31:17)


גַּם־הֵ֗ם אִתּ֛וֹ יָרְד֥וּ שְׁא֖וֹלָה אֶל־חַלְלֵי־חָ֑רֶב וּזְרֹע֛וֹ יָשְׁב֥וּ בְצִלּ֖וֹ בְּת֥וֹךְ גּוֹיִֽם׃


They also went down into She᾽ol with him, to them that are slain with the sword; and they that were his arm, that dwelt under his shadow in the midst of nations.


###### Ezekiel 31:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/31:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/31:18)


אֶל־מִ֨י דָמִ֥יתָ כָּ֛כָה בְּכָב֥וֹד וּבְגֹ֖דֶל בַּעֲצֵי־עֵ֑דֶן וְהוּרַדְתָּ֨ אֶת־עֲצֵי־עֵ֜דֶן אֶל־אֶ֣רֶץ תַּחְתִּ֗ית בְּת֨וֹךְ עֲרֵלִ֤ים תִּשְׁכַּב֙ אֶת־חַלְלֵי־חֶ֔רֶב ה֤וּא פַרְעֹה֙ וְכׇל־הֲמוֹנֹ֔ה נְאֻ֖ם אֲדֹנָ֥י יֱהֹוִֽה׃{פ}


To whom art thou thus like in glory and in greatness among the trees of ῾Eden? yet shalt thou be brought down with the trees of ῾Eden to the nether parts of the earth: thou shalt lie in the midst of the uncircumcised, with them that are slain by the sword. This is Par῾o and all his multitude, says the Lord God.



## Chapter 32

###### Ezekiel 32:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/32:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/32:1)


וַֽיְהִי֙ בִּשְׁתֵּ֣י עֶשְׂרֵ֣ה שָׁנָ֔ה בִּשְׁנֵֽי־עָשָׂ֥ר חֹ֖דֶשׁ בְּאֶחָ֣ד לַחֹ֑דֶשׁ הָיָ֥ה דְבַר־יְהֹוָ֖ה אֵלַ֥י לֵאמֹֽר׃


And it came to pass in the twelfth year, in the twelfth month, on the first day of the month, that the word of the Lord came to me, saying,


###### Ezekiel 32:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/32:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/32:2)


בֶּן־אָדָ֗ם שָׂ֤א קִינָה֙ עַל־פַּרְעֹ֣ה מֶלֶךְ־מִצְרַ֔יִם וְאָמַרְתָּ֣ אֵלָ֔יו כְּפִ֥יר גּוֹיִ֖ם נִדְמֵ֑יתָ וְאַתָּה֙ כַּתַּנִּ֣ים בַּיַּמִּ֔ים וַתָּ֣גַח בְּנַהֲרוֹתֶ֗יךָ וַתִּדְלַח־מַ֙יִם֙ בְּרַגְלֶ֔יךָ וַתִּרְפֹּ֖ס נַהֲרוֹתָֽם׃{ס}


Son of man, take up a lamentation for Par῾o king of Miżrayim, and say to him, Thou didst liken thyself to a young lion among the nations; but thou art rather a crocodile in the seas, and thou dost thrust forward in thy streams, churning the water with thy feet, and fouling their channels.


###### Ezekiel 32:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/32:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/32:3)


כֹּ֤ה אָמַר֙ אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֔ה וּפָרַשְׂתִּ֤י עָלֶ֙יךָ֙ אֶת־רִשְׁתִּ֔י בִּקְהַ֖ל עַמִּ֣ים רַבִּ֑ים וְהֶעֱל֖וּךָ בְּחֶרְמִֽי׃


Thus says the Lord God; I will therefore spread out my net over thee with a company of many peoples, and they will bring thee up in my net.


###### Ezekiel 32:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/32:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/32:4)


וּנְטַשְׁתִּ֣יךָ בָאָ֔רֶץ עַל־פְּנֵ֥י הַשָּׂדֶ֖ה אֲטִילֶ֑ךָ וְהִשְׁכַּנְתִּ֤י עָלֶ֙יךָ֙ כׇּל־ע֣וֹף הַשָּׁמַ֔יִם וְהִשְׂבַּעְתִּ֥י מִמְּךָ֖ חַיַּ֥ת כׇּל־הָאָֽרֶץ׃


Then will I throw thee upon the land, I will cast thee forth upon the open field, and will cause all the birds of the sky to remain upon thee, and I will fill the beasts of the whole earth with thee.


###### Ezekiel 32:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/32:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/32:5)


וְנָתַתִּ֥י אֶת־בְּשָׂרְךָ֖ עַל־הֶהָרִ֑ים וּמִלֵּאתִ֥י הַגֵּאָי֖וֹת רָמוּתֶֽךָ׃


And I will lay thy flesh upon the mountains, and fill the valleys with thy height.


###### Ezekiel 32:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/32:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/32:6)


וְהִשְׁקֵיתִ֨י אֶ֧רֶץ צָפָֽתְךָ֛ מִדָּמְךָ֖ אֶל־הֶהָרִ֑ים וַאֲפִקִ֖ים יִמָּלְא֥וּן מִמֶּֽךָּ׃


I will also water with thy blood the land over which thou overflowest as far as the mountains; and the rivers shall be full of thee.


###### Ezekiel 32:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/32:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/32:7)


וְכִסֵּיתִ֤י בְכַבּֽוֹתְךָ֙ שָׁמַ֔יִם וְהִקְדַּרְתִּ֖י אֶת־כֹּֽכְבֵיהֶ֑ם שֶׁ֚מֶשׁ בֶּעָנָ֣ן אֲכַסֶּ֔נּוּ וְיָרֵ֖חַ לֹא־יָאִ֥יר אוֹרֽוֹ׃


And when I shall extinguish thee, I will cover the heaven, and make dark the stars thereof; I will cover the sun with a cloud, and the moon shall not give her light.


###### Ezekiel 32:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/32:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/32:8)


כׇּל־מְא֤וֹרֵי אוֹר֙ בַּשָּׁמַ֔יִם אַקְדִּירֵ֖ם עָלֶ֑יךָ וְנָתַ֤תִּֽי חֹ֙שֶׁךְ֙ עַֽל־אַרְצְךָ֔ נְאֻ֖ם אֲדֹנָ֥י יֱהֹוִֽה׃


All the bright lights of heaven will I make dark over thee, and set darkness upon thy land, says the Lord God.


###### Ezekiel 32:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/32:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/32:9)


וְהִ֨כְעַסְתִּ֔י לֵ֖ב עַמִּ֣ים רַבִּ֑ים בַּהֲבִיאִ֤י שִׁבְרְךָ֙ בַּגּוֹיִ֔ם עַל־אֲרָצ֖וֹת אֲשֶׁ֥ר לֹא־יְדַעְתָּֽם׃


I will also vex the hearts of many peoples, when I shall bring thy destruction among the nations, into the countries which thou hast not known.


###### Ezekiel 32:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/32:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/32:10)


וַהֲשִׁמּוֹתִ֨י עָלֶ֜יךָ עַמִּ֣ים רַבִּ֗ים וּמַלְכֵיהֶם֙ יִשְׂעֲר֤וּ עָלֶ֙יךָ֙ שַׂ֔עַר בְּעוֹפְפִ֥י חַרְבִּ֖י עַל־פְּנֵיהֶ֑ם וְחָרְד֤וּ לִרְגָעִים֙ אִ֣ישׁ לְנַפְשׁ֔וֹ בְּי֖וֹם מַפַּלְתֶּֽךָ׃{פ}


Yea, I will make many peoples appalled at thee, and their kings shall be horribly afraid for thee, when I shall brandish my sword before them; and they shall tremble at every moment, every man for his own life, in the day of thy fall.


###### Ezekiel 32:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/32:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/32:11)


כִּ֛י כֹּ֥ה אָמַ֖ר אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֑ה חֶ֥רֶב מֶלֶךְ־בָּבֶ֖ל תְּבוֹאֶֽךָ׃


For thus says the Lord God; The sword of the king of Bavel shall come upon thee.


###### Ezekiel 32:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/32:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/32:12)


בְּחַרְב֤וֹת גִּבּוֹרִים֙ אַפִּ֣יל הֲמוֹנֶ֔ךָ עָרִיצֵ֥י גוֹיִ֖ם כֻּלָּ֑ם וְשָֽׁדְדוּ֙ אֶת־גְּא֣וֹן מִצְרַ֔יִם וְנִשְׁמַ֖ד כׇּל־הֲמוֹנָֽהּ׃


By the swords of the mighty will I cause thy multitude to fall, the terrible of the nations, all of them: and they shall spoil the pride of Miżrayim, and all its multitude shall be destroyed.


###### Ezekiel 32:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/32:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/32:13)


וְהַֽאֲבַדְתִּי֙ אֶת־כׇּל־בְּהֶמְתָּ֔הּ מֵעַ֖ל מַ֣יִם רַבִּ֑ים וְלֹ֨א תִדְלָחֵ֤ם רֶֽגֶל־אָדָם֙ ע֔וֹד וּפַרְס֥וֹת בְּהֵמָ֖ה לֹ֥א תִדְלָחֵֽם׃

בֶּן־אָדָ֕ם נְהֵ֛ה עַל־הֲמ֥וֹן מִצְרַ֖יִם וְהוֹרִדֵ֑הוּ א֠וֹתָ֠הּ וּבְנ֨וֹת גּוֹיִ֧ם אַדִּרִ֛ם אֶל־אֶ֥רֶץ תַּחְתִּיּ֖וֹת אֶת־י֥וֹרְדֵי בֽוֹר׃

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/32:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/32:20)

###### Ezekiel 32:21

יְדַבְּרוּ־ל֞וֹ אֵלֵ֧י גִבּוֹרִ֛ים מִתּ֥וֹךְ שְׁא֖וֹל אֶת־עֹֽזְרָ֑יו יָ֥רְד֛וּ שָׁכְב֥וּ הָעֲרֵלִ֖ים חַלְלֵי־חָֽרֶב׃

###### Ezekiel 32:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/32:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/32:23)


אֲשֶׁ֨ר נִתְּנ֤וּ קִבְרֹתֶ֙יהָ֙ בְּיַרְכְּתֵי־ב֔וֹר וַיְהִ֣י קְהָלָ֔הּ סְבִיב֖וֹת קְבֻרָתָ֑הּ כֻּלָּ֤ם חֲלָלִים֙ נֹפְלִ֣ים בַּחֶ֔רֶב אֲשֶׁר־נָתְנ֥וּ חִתִּ֖ית בְּאֶ֥רֶץ חַיִּֽים׃

###### Ezekiel 32:24

שָׁ֤ם עֵילָם֙ וְכׇל־הֲמוֹנָ֔הּ סְבִיב֖וֹת קְבֻרָתָ֑הּ כֻּלָּ֣ם חֲלָלִים֩ הַנֹּפְלִ֨ים בַּחֶ֜רֶב אֲֽשֶׁר־יָרְד֥וּ עֲרֵלִ֣ים**׀** אֶל־אֶ֣רֶץ תַּחְתִּיּ֗וֹת אֲשֶׁ֨ר נָתְנ֤וּ חִתִּיתָם֙ בְּאֶ֣רֶץ חַיִּ֔ים וַיִּשְׂא֥וּ כְלִמָּתָ֖ם אֶת־י֥וֹרְדֵי בֽוֹר׃

###### Ezekiel 32:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/32:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/32:25)


בְּת֣וֹךְ חֲ֠לָלִ֠ים נָתְנ֨וּ מִשְׁכָּ֥ב לָהּ֙ בְּכׇל־הֲמוֹנָ֔הּ סְבִיבוֹתָ֖יו קִבְרֹתֶ֑הָ כֻּלָּ֣ם עֲרֵלִ֣ים חַלְלֵי־חֶ֡רֶב כִּֽי־נִתַּ֨ן חִתִּיתָ֜ם בְּאֶ֣רֶץ חַיִּ֗ים וַיִּשְׂא֤וּ כְלִמָּתָם֙ אֶת־י֣וֹרְדֵי ב֔וֹר בְּת֥וֹךְ חֲלָלִ֖ים נִתָּֽן׃


They have set her a bed in the midst of the slain with all her multitude: her graves are round about him: all of them uncircumcised, slain by the sword: though the terror of them was spread in the land of the living, yet have they borne their shame with them that go down to the pit: he is put in the midst of them that are slain.

And shall they not lie with the fallen mighty of the uncircumcised, who are gone down to She᾽ol with their weapons of war, their swords laid under their heads, and their iniquities having come upon their bones? For the terror of the mighty was in the land of the living.

And thou shalt be broken in the midst of the uncircumcised, and shalt lie with them that are slain with the sword.

שָׁ֣מָּה אֱד֗וֹם מְלָכֶ֙יהָ֙ וְכׇל־נְשִׂיאֶ֔יהָ אֲשֶׁר־נִתְּנ֥וּ בִגְבוּרָתָ֖ם אֶת־חַלְלֵי־חָ֑רֶב הֵ֛מָּה אֶת־עֲרֵלִ֥ים יִשְׁכָּ֖בוּ וְאֶת־יֹ֥רְדֵי בֽוֹר׃


Edom is there, her kings, and all her princes, who for all their might are laid by them that were slain by the sword: they shall lie with the uncircumcised, and with them that go down to the pit.


###### Ezekiel 32:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/32:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/32:30)


שָׁ֣מָּה נְסִיכֵ֥י צָפ֛וֹן כֻּלָּ֖ם וְכׇל־צִדֹנִ֑י אֲשֶׁר־יָרְד֣וּ אֶת־חֲלָלִ֗ים בְּחִתִּיתָ֤ם מִגְֿבֽוּרָתָם֙ בּוֹשִׁ֔ים וַיִּשְׁכְּב֤וּ עֲרֵלִים֙ אֶת־חַלְלֵי־חֶ֔רֶב וַיִּשְׂא֥וּ כְלִמָּתָ֖ם אֶת־י֥וֹרְדֵי בֽוֹר׃


There are the princes of the north, all of them, and all the Żidonians, who are gone down with the slain; ashamed for the terror caused by their might; and they lie uncircumcised with them that are slain by the sword, and bear their shame with them that go down to the pit.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/32:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/32:31)


אוֹתָם֙ יִרְאֶ֣ה פַרְעֹ֔ה וְנִחַ֖ם עַל־כׇּל־הֲמוֹנֹ֑ה חַלְלֵי־חֶ֙רֶב֙ פַּרְעֹ֣ה וְכׇל־חֵיל֔וֹ נְאֻ֖ם אֲדֹנָ֥י יֱהֹוִֽה׃


Par῾o shall see them, and shall be comforted over all his multitude, namely Par῾o and all his army slain by the sword, says the Lord God.


###### Ezekiel 32:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/32:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/32:32)


כִּֽי־נָתַ֥תִּי אֶת־[חִתִּיתִ֖י] (חתיתו) בְּאֶ֣רֶץ חַיִּ֑ים וְהֻשְׁכַּב֩ בְּת֨וֹךְ עֲרֵלִ֜ים אֶת־חַלְלֵי־חֶ֗רֶב פַּרְעֹה֙ וְכׇל־הֲמוֹנֹ֔ה נְאֻ֖ם אֲדֹנָ֥י יֱהֹוִֽה׃{פ}

## Chapter 33

###### Ezekiel 33:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/33:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/33:1)


וַיְהִ֥י דְבַר־יְהֹוָ֖ה אֵלַ֥י לֵאמֹֽר׃


And the word of the Lord came to me, saying,


###### Ezekiel 33:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/33:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/33:2)


בֶּן־אָדָ֗ם דַּבֵּ֤ר אֶל־בְּנֵֽי־עַמְּךָ֙ וְאָמַרְתָּ֣ אֲלֵיהֶ֔ם אֶ֕רֶץ כִּֽי־אָבִ֥יא עָלֶ֖יהָ חָ֑רֶב וְלָקְח֨וּ עַם־הָאָ֜רֶץ אִ֤ישׁ אֶחָד֙ מִקְצֵיהֶ֔ם וְנָתְנ֥וּ אֹת֛וֹ לָהֶ֖ם לְצֹפֶֽה׃


Son of man, speak to the children of thy people, and say to them, When I bring the sword upon a land, if the people of the land take a man from among them, and set him for their watchman:


###### Ezekiel 33:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/33:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/33:3)


וְרָאָ֥ה אֶת־הַחֶ֖רֶב בָּאָ֣ה עַל־הָאָ֑רֶץ וְתָקַ֥ע בַּשּׁוֹפָ֖ר וְהִזְהִ֥יר אֶת־הָעָֽם׃


if when he sees the sword come upon the land, he blow the shofar, and warn the people;


###### Ezekiel 33:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/33:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/33:4)


וְשָׁמַ֨ע הַשֹּׁמֵ֜עַ אֶת־ק֤וֹל הַשּׁוֹפָר֙ וְלֹ֣א נִזְהָ֔ר וַתָּ֥בוֹא חֶ֖רֶב וַתִּקָּחֵ֑הוּ דָּמ֖וֹ בְּרֹאשׁ֥וֹ יִֽהְיֶֽה׃


then whoever hears the sound of the shofar, and takes no warning; if the sword comes, and takes him away, his blood shall be upon his own head.


###### Ezekiel 33:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/33:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/33:5)


אֵת֩ ק֨וֹל הַשּׁוֹפָ֤ר שָׁמַע֙ וְלֹ֣א נִזְהָ֔ר דָּמ֖וֹ בּ֣וֹ יִֽהְיֶ֑ה וְה֥וּא נִזְהָ֖ר נַפְשׁ֥וֹ מִלֵּֽט׃


He heard the sound of the shofar, and took no warning; his blood shall be upon him. But if he had taken warning, he would have saved his life.


###### Ezekiel 33:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/33:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/33:6)


וְ֠הַצֹּפֶ֠ה כִּי־יִרְאֶ֨ה אֶת־הַחֶ֜רֶב בָּאָ֗ה וְלֹֽא־תָקַ֤ע בַּשּׁוֹפָר֙ וְהָעָ֣ם לֹֽא־נִזְהָ֔ר וַתָּב֣וֹא חֶ֔רֶב וַתִּקַּ֥ח מֵהֶ֖ם נָ֑פֶשׁ ה֚וּא בַּעֲוֺנ֣וֹ נִלְקָ֔ח וְדָמ֖וֹ מִיַּֽד־הַצֹּפֶ֥ה אֶדְרֹֽשׁ׃{פ}

###### Ezekiel 33:31
