וְיָב֣וֹאוּ אֵ֠לֶ֠יךָ כִּמְבוֹא־עָ֞ם וְיֵשְׁב֤וּ לְפָנֶ֙יךָ֙ עַמִּ֔י וְשָֽׁמְעוּ֙ אֶת־דְּבָרֶ֔יךָ וְאוֹתָ֖ם לֹ֣א יַעֲשׂ֑וּ כִּֽי־עֲגָבִ֤ים בְּפִיהֶם֙ הֵ֣מָּה עֹשִׂ֔ים אַחֲרֵ֥י בִצְעָ֖ם לִבָּ֥ם הֹלֵֽךְ׃


And they come to thee as the people come, and they sit before thee, my people, and they hear thy words, but they do not carry them out; for it is become flute music in their mouths, whilst their heart is set on unjust gain.


###### Ezekiel 33:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/33:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/33:32)


וְהִנְּךָ֤ לָהֶם֙ כְּשִׁ֣יר עֲגָבִ֔ים יְפֵ֥ה ק֖וֹל וּמֵטִ֣ב נַגֵּ֑ן וְשָֽׁמְעוּ֙ אֶת־דְּבָרֶ֔יךָ וְעֹשִׂ֥ים אֵינָ֖ם אוֹתָֽם׃


And, lo, thou art to them like a song for flutes by one who has a pleasant voice, and can play the instrument well: for they hear the words, but they do not carry them out.


###### Ezekiel 33:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/33:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/33:33)


וּבְבֹאָ֑הּ הִנֵּ֣ה בָאָ֔ה וְיָ֣דְע֔וּ כִּ֥י נָבִ֖יא הָיָ֥ה בְתוֹכָֽם׃{פ}


But when it comes to pass, (see, it is coming,) then they shall know that a prophet has been among them.



## Chapter 34

###### Ezekiel 34:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/34:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/34:1)


וַיְהִ֥י דְבַר־יְהֹוָ֖ה אֵלַ֥י לֵאמֹֽר׃


And the word of the Lord came to me, saying,


###### Ezekiel 34:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/34:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/34:2)


בֶּן־אָדָ֕ם הִנָּבֵ֖א עַל־רוֹעֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל הִנָּבֵ֣א וְאָמַרְתָּ֩ אֲלֵיהֶ֨ם לָרֹעִ֜ים כֹּה־אָמַ֣ר**׀** אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֗ה ה֤וֹי רֹעֵֽי־יִשְׂרָאֵל֙ אֲשֶׁ֤ר הָיוּ֙ רֹעִ֣ים אוֹתָ֔ם הֲל֣וֹא הַצֹּ֔אן יִרְע֖וּ הָרֹעִֽים׃


Son of man, prophesy against the shepherds of Yisra᾽el, prophesy, and say to them, to the shepherds, Thus says the Lord God; Woe to the shepherds of Yisra᾽el that have fed themselves! should not the shepherds feed the flocks?


###### Ezekiel 34:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/34:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/34:3)


אֶת־הַחֵ֤לֶב תֹּאכֵ֙לוּ֙ וְאֶת־הַצֶּ֣מֶר תִּלְבָּ֔שׁוּ הַבְּרִיאָ֖ה תִּזְבָּ֑חוּ הַצֹּ֖אן לֹ֥א תִרְעֽוּ׃


You eat the fat, and clothe yourselves with the wool, you kill the fallings: but you do not feed the sheep.


###### Ezekiel 34:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/34:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/34:4)


אֶֽת־הַנַּחְלוֹת֩ לֹ֨א חִזַּקְתֶּ֜ם וְאֶת־הַחוֹלָ֣ה לֹֽא־רִפֵּאתֶ֗ם וְלַנִּשְׁבֶּ֙רֶת֙ לֹ֣א חֲבַשְׁתֶּ֔ם וְאֶת־הַנִּדַּ֙חַת֙ לֹ֣א הֲשֵׁבֹתֶ֔ם וְאֶת־הָאֹבֶ֖דֶת לֹ֣א בִקַּשְׁתֶּ֑ם וּבְחׇזְקָ֛ה רְדִיתֶ֥ם אֹתָ֖ם וּבְפָֽרֶךְ׃


You have not strengthened the weak, nor have you healed the sick, nor have you bound the crippled, nor have you brought back the strayed, nor have you sought that which was lost; but with force and with cruelty have you ruled them.


###### Ezekiel 34:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/34:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/34:5)


וַתְּפוּצֶ֖ינָה מִבְּלִ֣י רֹעֶ֑ה וַתִּהְיֶ֧ינָה לְאׇכְלָ֛ה לְכׇל־חַיַּ֥ת הַשָּׂדֶ֖ה וַתְּפוּצֶֽינָה׃


And they were scattered, because there was no shepherd: and they became food for all the beasts of the field, when they were scattered.


###### Ezekiel 34:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/34:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/34:6)


יִשְׁגּ֤וּ צֹאנִי֙ בְּכׇל־הֶ֣הָרִ֔ים וְעַ֖ל כׇּל־גִּבְעָ֣ה רָמָ֑ה וְעַ֨ל כׇּל־פְּנֵ֤י הָאָ֙רֶץ֙ נָפֹ֣צוּ צֹאנִ֔י וְאֵ֥ין דּוֹרֵ֖שׁ וְאֵ֥ין מְבַקֵּֽשׁ׃


My sheep wandered through all the mountains, and upon every high hill: for my flock was dispersed upon all the face of the earth, and none did search or seek after them.


###### Ezekiel 34:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/34:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/34:7)


לָכֵ֣ן רֹעִ֔ים שִׁמְע֖וּ אֶת־דְּבַ֥ר יְהֹוָֽה׃


Therefore, shepherds, hear the word of the Lord;


###### Ezekiel 34:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/34:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/34:8)


חַי־אָ֜נִי נְאֻ֣ם**׀** אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֗ה אִם־לֹ֣א יַ֣עַן הֱיֽוֹת־צֹאנִ֣י׀לָבַ֡ז וַתִּהְיֶ֩ינָה֩ צֹאנִ֨י לְאׇכְלָ֜ה לְכׇל־חַיַּ֤ת הַשָּׂדֶה֙ מֵאֵ֣ין רֹעֶ֔ה וְלֹא־דָרְשׁ֥וּ רֹעַ֖י אֶת־צֹאנִ֑י וַיִּרְע֤וּ הָרֹעִים֙ אוֹתָ֔ם וְאֶת־צֹאנִ֖י לֹ֥א רָעֽוּ׃


As I live, says the Lord God, surely since my flock has become a prey, and my sheep have become food for every beast of the field, because there was no shepherd, nor did my shepherds search for my flock, but the shepherds fed themselves, and fed not my flock;


###### Ezekiel 34:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/34:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/34:9)


לָכֵן֙ הָרֹעִ֔ים שִׁמְע֖וּ דְּבַר־יְהֹוָֽה׃


therefore, O shepherds, hear the word of the Lord;


###### Ezekiel 34:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/34:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/34:10)


כֹּה־אָמַ֞ר אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֗ה הִנְנִ֨י אֶֽל־הָרֹעִ֜ים וְֽדָרַשְׁתִּ֧י אֶת־צֹאנִ֣י מִיָּדָ֗ם וְהִשְׁבַּתִּים֙ מֵרְע֣וֹת צֹ֔אן וְלֹא־יִרְע֥וּ ע֛וֹד הָרֹעִ֖ים אוֹתָ֑ם וְהִצַּלְתִּ֤י צֹאנִי֙ מִפִּיהֶ֔ם וְלֹא־תִֽהְיֶ֥יןָ לָהֶ֖ם לְאׇכְלָֽה׃{ס}


thus says the Lord God; Behold, I am against the shepherds; and I will require my flock at their hand, and I will put an end to their feeding the sheep; nor shall the shepherds feed themselves any more; for I will deliver my flock from their mouth, that they may not be food for them.


###### Ezekiel 34:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/34:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/34:11)


כִּ֛י כֹּ֥ה אָמַ֖ר אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֑ה הִנְנִי־אָ֕נִי וְדָרַשְׁתִּ֥י אֶת־צֹאנִ֖י וּבִקַּרְתִּֽים׃


For thus says the Lord God; Behold, I will both search my sheep, and seek them out.


###### Ezekiel 34:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/34:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/34:12)


כְּבַקָּרַת֩ רֹעֶ֨ה עֶדְר֜וֹ בְּיוֹם־הֱיוֹת֤וֹ בְתוֹךְ־צֹאנוֹ֙ נִפְרָשׁ֔וֹת כֵּ֖ן אֲבַקֵּ֣ר אֶת־צֹאנִ֑י וְהִצַּלְתִּ֣י אֶתְהֶ֗ם מִכׇּל־הַמְּקוֹמֹת֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר נָפֹ֣צוּ שָׁ֔ם בְּי֥וֹם עָנָ֖ן וַעֲרָפֶֽל׃


As a shepherd seeks out his flock in the day that he is among his sheep that are strayed; so will I seek out my sheep, and will deliver them out of all places where they have been dispersed in the cloudy and dark day.


###### Ezekiel 34:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/34:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/34:13)


וְהוֹצֵאתִ֣ים מִן־הָעַמִּ֗ים וְקִבַּצְתִּים֙ מִן־הָ֣אֲרָצ֔וֹת וַהֲבִיאוֹתִ֖ים אֶל־אַדְמָתָ֑ם וּרְעִיתִים֙ אֶל־הָרֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל בָּאֲפִיקִ֕ים וּבְכֹ֖ל מוֹשְׁבֵ֥י הָאָֽרֶץ׃


And I will bring them out from among the peoples, and gather them from the countries, and will bring them to their own land, and pasture them upon the mountains of Yisra᾽el by the water courses, and in all the inhabited places of the country.


###### Ezekiel 34:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/34:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/34:14)


בְּמִרְעֶה־טּוֹב֙ אֶרְעֶ֣ה אֹתָ֔ם וּבְהָרֵ֥י מְרֽוֹם־יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל יִהְיֶ֣ה נְוֵהֶ֑ם שָׁ֤ם תִּרְבַּ֙צְנָה֙ בְּנָ֣וֶה טּ֔וֹב וּמִרְעֶ֥ה שָׁמֵ֛ן תִּרְעֶ֖ינָה אֶל־הָרֵ֥י יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


I will feed them in a good pasture, and upon the high mountains of Yisra᾽el shall their fold be: there shall they lie in a good pasture, and in a fat grazing land shall they feed upon the mountains of Yisra᾽el.


###### Ezekiel 34:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/34:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/34:15)


אֲנִ֨י אֶרְעֶ֤ה צֹאנִי֙ וַאֲנִ֣י אַרְבִּיצֵ֔ם נְאֻ֖ם אֲדֹנָ֥י יֱהֹוִֽה׃


I will feed my flock, and I will cause them to lie down, says the Lord God.


###### Ezekiel 34:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/34:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/34:16)


אֶת־הָאֹבֶ֤דֶת אֲבַקֵּשׁ֙ וְאֶת־הַנִּדַּ֣חַת אָשִׁ֔יב וְלַנִּשְׁבֶּ֣רֶת אֶֽחֱבֹ֔שׁ וְאֶת־הַחוֹלָ֖ה אֲחַזֵּ֑ק וְאֶת־הַשְּׁמֵנָ֧ה וְאֶת־הַחֲזָקָ֛ה אַשְׁמִ֖יד אֶרְעֶ֥נָּה בְמִשְׁפָּֽט׃


I will seek that which was lost, and bring back the strayed, and will bind up the crippled, and will strengthen the sick: but I will destroy the fat and the strong; I will make them graze upon justice.


###### Ezekiel 34:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/34:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/34:17)


וְאַתֵּ֣נָה צֹאנִ֔י כֹּ֥ה אָמַ֖ר אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֑ה הִנְנִ֤י שֹׁפֵט֙ בֵּֽין־שֶׂ֣ה לָשֶׂ֔ה לָאֵילִ֖ים וְלָעַתּוּדִֽים׃


And as for you, O my flock, thus says the Lord God; Behold, I judge between one lamb and another, the rams and the he goats.


###### Ezekiel 34:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/34:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/34:18)


הַמְעַ֣ט מִכֶּ֗ם הַמִּרְעֶ֤ה הַטּוֹב֙ תִּרְע֔וּ וְיֶ֙תֶר֙ מִרְעֵיכֶ֔ם תִּרְמְס֖וּ בְּרַגְלֵיכֶ֑ם וּמִשְׁקַע־מַ֣יִם תִּשְׁתּ֔וּ וְאֵת֙ הַנּ֣וֹתָרִ֔ים בְּרַגְלֵיכֶ֖ם תִּרְפֹּשֽׂוּן׃


Is it so small a thing to you that you have eaten up the good pasture, but you must also tread down with your feet the residue of your pasture lands? and to have drunk of the clear waters, but you must also foul the residue with your feet?


###### Ezekiel 34:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/34:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/34:19)


וְצֹאנִ֑י מִרְמַ֤ס רַגְלֵיכֶם֙ תִּרְעֶ֔ינָה וּמִרְפַּ֥שׂ רַגְלֵיכֶ֖ם תִּשְׁתֶּֽינָה׃{פ}


And as for my sheep they eat that which you have trodden with your feet; and they drink that which you have fouled with your feet.


###### Ezekiel 34:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/34:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/34:20)


לָכֵ֗ן כֹּ֥ה אָמַ֛ר אֲדֹנָ֥י יֱהֹוִ֖ה אֲלֵיהֶ֑ם הִנְנִי־אָ֕נִי וְשָֽׁפַטְתִּי֙ בֵּֽין־שֶׂ֣ה בִרְיָ֔ה וּבֵ֥ין שֶׂ֖ה רָזָֽה׃


Therefore thus says the Lord God to them: Behold, I will judge between the fat cattle and between the lean cattle.


###### Ezekiel 34:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/34:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/34:21)


יַ֗עַן בְּצַ֤ד וּבְכָתֵף֙ תֶּהְדֹּ֔פוּ וּבְקַרְנֵיכֶ֥ם תְּנַגְּח֖וּ כׇּל־הַנַּחְל֑וֹת עַ֣ד אֲשֶׁ֧ר הֲפִיצוֹתֶ֛ם אוֹתָ֖נָה אֶל־הַחֽוּצָה׃


Because you have thrust with side and with shoulder, and pushed all the weak ones with your horns, till you have scattered them abroad;


###### Ezekiel 34:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/34:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/34:22)


וְהוֹשַׁעְתִּ֣י לְצֹאנִ֔י וְלֹא־תִהְיֶ֥ינָה ע֖וֹד לָבַ֑ז וְשָׁ֣פַטְתִּ֔י בֵּ֥ין שֶׂ֖ה לָשֶֽׂה׃


therefore will I save my flock, and they shall no more be a prey; and I will judge between cattle and cattle.


###### Ezekiel 34:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/34:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/34:23)


וַהֲקִמֹתִ֨י עֲלֵיהֶ֜ם רֹעֶ֤ה אֶחָד֙ וְרָעָ֣ה אֶתְהֶ֔ן אֵ֖ת עַבְדִּ֣י דָוִ֑יד ה֚וּא יִרְעֶ֣ה אֹתָ֔ם וְהֽוּא־יִהְיֶ֥ה לָהֶ֖ן לְרֹעֶֽה׃


And I will set up one shepherd over them, and he shall feed them, namely my servant David; he shall feed them, and he shall be their shepherd.


###### Ezekiel 34:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/34:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/34:24)


וַאֲנִ֣י יְהֹוָ֗ה אֶֽהְיֶ֤ה לָהֶם֙ לֵֽאלֹהִ֔ים וְעַבְדִּ֥י דָוִ֖ד נָשִׂ֣יא בְתוֹכָ֑ם אֲנִ֥י יְהֹוָ֖ה דִּבַּֽרְתִּי׃


And I the Lord will be their God, and my servant David will be a prince among them; I the Lord have spoken it.


###### Ezekiel 34:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/34:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/34:25)


וְכָרַתִּ֤י לָהֶם֙ בְּרִ֣ית שָׁל֔וֹם וְהִשְׁבַּתִּ֥י חַיָּֽה־רָעָ֖ה מִן־הָאָ֑רֶץ וְיָשְׁב֤וּ בַמִּדְבָּר֙ לָבֶ֔טַח וְיָשְׁנ֖וּ בַּיְּעָרִֽים׃


And I will make with them a covenant of peace, and will cause the evil beasts to cease out of the land: and they shall dwell safely in the wilderness, and sleep in the woods.


###### Ezekiel 34:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/34:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/34:26)


וְנָתַתִּ֥י אוֹתָ֛ם וּסְבִיב֥וֹת גִּבְעָתִ֖י בְּרָכָ֑ה וְהוֹרַדְתִּ֤י הַגֶּ֙שֶׁם֙ בְּעִתּ֔וֹ גִּשְׁמֵ֥י בְרָכָ֖ה יִֽהְיֽוּ׃


And I will make them and the places round about my hill a blessing; and I will cause the shower to come down in its season; they will be showers of blessing.


###### Ezekiel 34:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/34:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/34:27)


וְנָתַן֩ עֵ֨ץ הַשָּׂדֶ֜ה אֶת־פִּרְי֗וֹ וְהָאָ֙רֶץ֙ תִּתֵּ֣ן יְבוּלָ֔הּ וְהָי֥וּ עַל־אַדְמָתָ֖ם לָבֶ֑טַח וְֽיָדְע֞וּ כִּֽי־אֲנִ֣י יְהֹוָ֗ה בְּשִׁבְרִי֙ אֶת־מֹט֣וֹת עֻלָּ֔ם וְהִ֨צַּלְתִּ֔ים מִיַּ֖ד הָעֹבְדִ֥ים בָּהֶֽם׃


And the tree of the field shall yield her fruit, and the earth shall yield her increase, and they shall be secure in their land, and shall know that I am the Lord, when I have broken the bars of their yoke, and delivered them out of the hand of those that enslaved them.


###### Ezekiel 34:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/34:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/34:28)


וְלֹא־יִהְי֨וּ ע֥וֹד בַּז֙ לַגּוֹיִ֔ם וְחַיַּ֥ת הָאָ֖רֶץ לֹ֣א תֹאכְלֵ֑ם וְיָשְׁב֥וּ לָבֶ֖טַח וְאֵ֥ין מַחֲרִֽיד׃


And they shall no more be a prey to the nations, nor shall the beast of the land devour them; but they shall dwell in safety, and none shall make them afraid.


###### Ezekiel 34:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/34:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/34:29)


וַהֲקִמֹתִ֥י לָהֶ֛ם מַטָּ֖ע לְשֵׁ֑ם וְלֹא־יִהְי֨וּ ע֜וֹד אֲסֻפֵ֤י רָעָב֙ בָּאָ֔רֶץ וְלֹא־יִשְׂא֥וּ ע֖וֹד כְּלִמַּ֥ת הַגּוֹיִֽם׃


And I will raise up for them a plantation for renown, and they shall be no more consumed with hunger in the land, nor suffer any more the insult of the nations.


###### Ezekiel 34:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/34:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/34:30)


וְיָדְע֗וּ כִּ֣י אֲנִ֧י יְהֹוָ֛ה אֱלֹהֵיהֶ֖ם אִתָּ֑ם וְהֵ֗מָּה עַמִּי֙ בֵּ֣ית יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל נְאֻ֖ם אֲדֹנָ֥י יֱהֹוִֽה׃


Thus shall they know that I the Lord their God am with them, and that they, the house of Yisra᾽el are my people, says the Lord God.


###### Ezekiel 34:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/34:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/34:31)


וְאַתֵּ֥ן צֹאנִ֛י צֹ֥אן מַרְעִיתִ֖י אָדָ֣ם אַתֶּ֑ם אֲנִי֙ אֱלֹ֣הֵיכֶ֔ם נְאֻ֖ם אֲדֹנָ֥י יֱהֹוִֽה׃{פ}


But you my flock, the flock of my pasture, are men, and I am your God, says the Lord God.



## Chapter 35

###### Ezekiel 35:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/35:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/35:1)


וַיְהִ֥י דְבַר־יְהֹוָ֖ה אֵלַ֥י לֵאמֹֽר׃


And the word of the Lord came to me, saying,


###### Ezekiel 35:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/35:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/35:2)


בֶּן־אָדָ֕ם שִׂ֥ים פָּנֶ֖יךָ עַל־הַ֣ר שֵׂעִ֑יר וְהִנָּבֵ֖א עָלָֽיו׃


Son of man, set thy face against mount Se῾ir, and prophesy against it,


###### Ezekiel 35:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/35:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/35:3)


וְאָמַ֣רְתָּ לּ֗וֹ כֹּ֤ה אָמַר֙ אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֔ה הִנְנִ֥י אֵלֶ֖יךָ הַר־שֵׂעִ֑יר וְנָטִ֤יתִי יָדִי֙ עָלֶ֔יךָ וּנְתַתִּ֖יךָ שְׁמָמָ֥ה וּמְשַׁמָּֽה׃


and say to it, Thus says the Lord God; Behold, O mount Se῾ir, I am against thee, and I will stretch out my hand against thee, and I will make thee a total blight.


###### Ezekiel 35:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/35:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/35:4)


עָרֶ֙יךָ֙ חׇרְבָּ֣ה אָשִׂ֔ים וְאַתָּ֖ה שְׁמָמָ֣ה תִֽהְיֶ֑ה וְיָדַעְתָּ֖ כִּֽי־אֲנִ֥י יְהֹוָֽה׃


I will lay thy cities waste, and thou shalt be desolate, and thou shalt know that I am the Lord.


###### Ezekiel 35:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/35:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/35:5)


יַ֗עַן הֱי֤וֹת לְךָ֙ אֵיבַ֣ת עוֹלָ֔ם וַתַּגֵּ֥ר אֶת־בְּנֵֽי־יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל עַל־יְדֵי־חָ֑רֶב בְּעֵ֣ת אֵידָ֔ם בְּעֵ֖ת עֲוֺ֥ן קֵֽץ׃


Because thou hast had a perpetual hatred and hast hurled the children of Yisra᾽el to the power of the sword in the time of their calamity, in the time of their final punishment:


###### Ezekiel 35:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/35:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/35:6)


לָכֵ֣ן חַי־אָ֗נִי נְאֻם֙ אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֔ה כִּֽי־לְדָ֥ם אֶעֶשְׂךָ֖ וְדָ֣ם יִרְדְּפֶ֑ךָ אִם־לֹ֥א דָ֛ם שָׂנֵ֖אתָ וְדָ֥ם יִרְדְּפֶֽךָ׃


therefore, as I live, says the Lord God, I will prepare thee to blood, and blood shall pursue thee: surely thou hast hated thy own blood; therefore blood shall pursue thee.


###### Ezekiel 35:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/35:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/35:7)


וְנָֽתַתִּי֙ אֶת־הַ֣ר שֵׂעִ֔יר לְשִֽׁמְמָ֖ה וּשְׁמָמָ֑ה וְהִכְרַתִּ֥י מִמֶּ֖נּוּ עֹבֵ֥ר וָשָֽׁב׃


Thus will I make mount Se῾ir most desolate, and cut off from it all who come and go.


###### Ezekiel 35:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/35:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/35:8)


וּמִלֵּאתִ֥י אֶת־הָרָ֖יו חֲלָלָ֑יו גִּבְעוֹתֶ֤יךָ וְגֵיאוֹתֶ֙יךָ֙ וְכׇל־אֲפִיקֶ֔יךָ חַלְלֵי־חֶ֖רֶב יִפְּל֥וּ בָהֶֽם׃


And I will fill its mountains with its slain men: in thy hills, and in thy valleys, and in all thy water courses shall they fall that are slain with the sword.


###### Ezekiel 35:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/35:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/35:9)


שִֽׁמְמ֤וֹת עוֹלָם֙ אֶתֶּנְךָ֔ וְעָרֶ֖יךָ לֹ֣א (תישבנה) [תָשֹׁ֑בְנָה] וִידַעְתֶּ֖ם כִּֽי־אֲנִ֥י יְהֹוָֽה׃


I will make thee perpetual desolations, and thy cities shall not have a restoration: and you shall know that I am the Lord.


###### Ezekiel 35:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/35:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/35:10)


יַ֣עַן אֲ֠מׇרְךָ֠ אֶת־שְׁנֵ֨י הַגּוֹיִ֜ם וְאֶת־שְׁתֵּ֧י הָאֲרָצ֛וֹת לִ֥י תִהְיֶ֖ינָה וִֽירַשְׁנ֑וּהָ וַיהֹוָ֖ה שָׁ֥ם הָיָֽה׃{ס}


Because thou hast said, These two nations and these two countries shall be mine, and we will possess it; though the Lord was there:


###### Ezekiel 35:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/35:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/35:11)


לָכֵ֣ן חַי־אָ֗נִי נְאֻם֮ אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִה֒ וְעָשִׂ֗יתִי כְּאַפְּךָ֙ וּכְקִנְאָ֣תְךָ֔ אֲשֶׁ֣ר עָשִׂ֔יתָה מִשִּׂנְאָתֶ֖יךָ בָּ֑ם וְנוֹדַ֥עְתִּי בָ֖ם כַּאֲשֶׁ֥ר אֶשְׁפְּטֶֽךָ׃


therefore, as I live, says the Lord God, I will do according to thy anger, and according to thy envy which thou hast used out of thy hatred against them; and I will make myself known among them, when I shall judge thee.


###### Ezekiel 35:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/35:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/35:12)


וְֽיָדַעְתָּ֮ כִּ֣י אֲנִ֣י יְהֹוָה֒ שָׁמַ֣עְתִּי**׀** אֶת־כׇּל־נָאָֽצוֹתֶ֗יךָ אֲשֶׁ֥ר אָמַ֛רְתָּ עַל־הָרֵ֥י יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל לֵאמֹ֣ר׀[שָׁמֵ֑מוּ] (שממה) לָ֥נוּ נִתְּנ֖וּ לְאׇכְלָֽה׃


And thou shalt know that I am the Lord, and that I have heard all thy blasphemies which thou hast spoken against the mountains of Yisra᾽el saying, They are laid desolate, they are given us to consume.


###### Ezekiel 35:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/35:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/35:13)


וַתַּגְדִּ֤ילוּ עָלַי֙ בְּפִיכֶ֔ם וְהַעְתַּרְתֶּ֥ם עָלַ֖י דִּבְרֵיכֶ֑ם אֲנִ֖י שָׁמָֽעְתִּי׃{ס}


Thus with your mouth you have boasted against me, and have multiplied your words against me: I have heard them.


###### Ezekiel 35:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/35:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/35:14)


כֹּ֥ה אָמַ֖ר אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֑ה כִּשְׂמֹ֙חַ֙ כׇּל־הָאָ֔רֶץ שְׁמָמָ֖ה אֶעֱשֶׂה־לָּֽךְ׃


Thus says the Lord God; When the whole earth rejoices, I will make thee desolate.


###### Ezekiel 35:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/35:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/35:15)


כְּשִׂמְחָ֨תְךָ֜ לְנַחֲלַ֧ת בֵּֽית־יִשְׂרָאֵ֛ל עַ֥ל אֲשֶׁר־שָׁמֵ֖מָה כֵּ֣ן אֶעֱשֶׂה־לָּ֑ךְ שְׁמָמָ֨ה תִהְיֶ֤ה הַר־שֵׂעִיר֙ וְכׇל־אֱד֣וֹם כֻּלָּ֔הּ וְיָדְע֖וּ כִּי־אֲנִ֥י יְהֹוָֽה׃{פ}


As thou didst rejoice at the inheritance of the house of Yisra᾽el because it was desolate, so will I do to thee: thou shalt be desolate, O mount Se῾ir, and all Edom, all of it: and they shall know that I am the Lord.



## Chapter 36

###### Ezekiel 36:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:1)


וְאַתָּ֣ה בֶן־אָדָ֔ם הִנָּבֵ֖א אֶל־הָרֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל וְאָ֣מַרְתָּ֔ הָרֵי֙ יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל שִׁמְע֖וּ דְּבַר־יְהֹוָֽה׃


And thou, son of man, prophesy to the mountains of Yisra᾽el, and say, You mountains of Yisra᾽el hear the word of the Lord:


###### Ezekiel 36:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:2)


כֹּ֤ה אָמַר֙ אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֔ה יַ֣עַן אָמַ֧ר הָאוֹיֵ֛ב עֲלֵיכֶ֖ם הֶאָ֑ח וּבָמ֣וֹת עוֹלָ֔ם לְמוֹרָשָׁ֖ה הָ֥יְתָה לָּֽנוּ׃


thus says the Lord God; Because the enemy has said against you, Aha, even the ancient high places are ours in possession:


###### Ezekiel 36:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:3)


לָכֵן֙ הִנָּבֵ֣א וְאָמַרְתָּ֔ כֹּ֥ה אָמַ֖ר אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֑ה יַ֣עַן בְּיַ֡עַן שַׁמּוֹת֩ וְשָׁאֹ֨ף אֶתְכֶ֜ם מִסָּבִ֗יב לִֽהְיֽוֹתְכֶ֤ם מֽוֹרָשָׁה֙ לִשְׁאֵרִ֣ית הַגּוֹיִ֔ם וַתֵּ֥עֲל֛וּ עַל־שְׂפַ֥ת לָשׁ֖וֹן וְדִבַּת־עָֽם׃


therefore prophesy and say, Thus says the Lord God; Because they have made you desolate, and swallowed you up on every side, that you might be a possession to the rest of the nations, and you are taken up in the lips of talkers, and are become the gossip of the people:


###### Ezekiel 36:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:4)


לָכֵן֙ הָרֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל שִׁמְע֖וּ דְּבַר־אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֑ה כֹּה־אָמַ֣ר אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱ֠הֹוִ֠ה לֶהָרִ֨ים וְלַגְּבָע֜וֹת לָאֲפִיקִ֣ים וְלַגֵּאָי֗וֹת וְלֶחֳרָב֤וֹת הַשֹּֽׁמְמוֹת֙ וְלֶעָרִ֣ים הַנֶּעֱזָב֔וֹת אֲשֶׁ֨ר הָי֤וּ לְבַז֙ וּלְלַ֔עַג לִשְׁאֵרִ֥ית הַגּוֹיִ֖ם אֲשֶׁ֥ר מִסָּבִֽיב׃


therefore, you mountains of Yisra᾽el, hear the word of the Lord God; Thus says the Lord God to the mountains, and to the hills, to the water courses, and to the valleys, to the desolate wastes, and to the cities that are forsaken, which have become a prey and a derision to the residue of the nations that are round about;


###### Ezekiel 36:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:5)


לָכֵ֗ן כֹּה־אָמַר֮ אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִה֒ אִם־לֹ֠א בְּאֵ֨שׁ קִנְאָתִ֥י דִבַּ֛רְתִּי עַל־שְׁאֵרִ֥ית הַגּוֹיִ֖ם וְעַל־אֱד֣וֹם כֻּלָּ֑א אֲשֶׁ֣ר נָתְנֽוּ־אֶת־אַרְצִ֣י׀לָ֠הֶ֠ם לְמ֨וֹרָשָׁ֜ה בְּשִׂמְחַ֤ת כׇּל־לֵבָב֙ בִּשְׁאָ֣ט נֶ֔פֶשׁ לְמַ֥עַן מִגְרָשָׁ֖הּ לָבַֽז׃


therefore thus says the Lord God; Surely in the fire of my jealousy have I spoken against the residue of the nations, and against all Edom, who have appointed my land to themselves for a possession with the joy of all their heart, with disdainful minds, to cast it out for a prey.


###### Ezekiel 36:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:6)


לָכֵ֕ן הִנָּבֵ֖א עַל־אַדְמַ֣ת יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל וְאָמַרְתָּ֡ לֶהָרִ֣ים וְ֠לַגְּבָע֠וֹת לָאֲפִיקִ֨ים וְלַגֵּאָי֜וֹת כֹּה־אָמַ֣ר**׀** אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֗ה הִנְנִ֨י בְקִנְאָתִ֤י וּבַֽחֲמָתִי֙ דִּבַּ֔רְתִּי יַ֛עַן כְּלִמַּ֥ת גּוֹיִ֖ם נְשָׂאתֶֽם׃


Prophesy therefore concerning the land of Yisra᾽el, and say to the mountains, and to the hills, to the water courses, and to the valleys saying, Thus says the Lord God; Behold, I have spoken in my jealousy and in my fury, because you have suffered the insult of the nations:


###### Ezekiel 36:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:7)


לָכֵ֗ן כֹּ֤ה אָמַר֙ אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֔ה אֲנִ֖י נָשָׂ֣אתִי אֶת־יָדִ֑י אִם־לֹ֤א הַגּוֹיִם֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר לָכֶ֣ם מִסָּבִ֔יב הֵ֖מָּה כְּלִמָּתָ֥ם יִשָּֽׂאוּ׃


therefore thus says the Lord God; I have lifted up my hand, saying, Surely the nations that are about you, they shall bear their own insult.


###### Ezekiel 36:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:8)


וְאַתֶּ֞ם הָרֵ֤י יִשְׂרָאֵל֙ עַנְפְּכֶ֣ם תִּתֵּ֔נוּ וּפֶרְיְכֶ֥ם תִּשְׂא֖וּ לְעַמִּ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל כִּ֥י קֵרְב֖וּ לָבֽוֹא׃


But you, O mountains of Yisra᾽el, you shall shoot forth your branches, and yield your fruit to my people of Yisra᾽el; for they will soon be coming.


###### Ezekiel 36:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:9)


כִּ֖י הִנְנִ֣י אֲלֵיכֶ֑ם וּפָנִ֣יתִי אֲלֵיכֶ֔ם וְנֶעֱבַדְתֶּ֖ם וְנִזְרַעְתֶּֽם׃


For, behold, I am for you, and I will turn to you, and you shall be tilled and sown:


###### Ezekiel 36:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:10)


וְהִרְבֵּיתִ֤י עֲלֵיכֶם֙ אָדָ֔ם כׇּל־בֵּ֥ית יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל כֻּלֹּ֑ה וְנֹֽשְׁבוּ֙ הֶעָרִ֔ים וְהֶחֳרָב֖וֹת תִּבָּנֶֽינָה׃


and I will multiply men upon you, all the house of Yisra᾽el, all of it: and the cities shall be inhabited, and the waste places rebuilt:


###### Ezekiel 36:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:11)


וְהִרְבֵּיתִ֧י עֲלֵיכֶ֛ם אָדָ֥ם וּבְהֵמָ֖ה וְרָב֣וּ וּפָר֑וּ וְהוֹשַׁבְתִּ֨י אֶתְכֶ֜ם כְּקַדְמֽוֹתֵיכֶ֗ם וְהֵיטִֽבֹתִי֙ מֵרִאשֹׁ֣תֵיכֶ֔ם וִידַעְתֶּ֖ם כִּֽי־אֲנִ֥י יְהֹוָֽה׃


and I will multiply upon you man and beast; and they shall increase and bring fruit: and I will cause you to be inhabited as in your former times, and I will do better to you than at your beginnings: and you shall know that I am the Lord.


###### Ezekiel 36:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:12)


וְהוֹלַכְתִּי֩ עֲלֵיכֶ֨ם אָדָ֜ם אֶת־עַמִּ֤י יִשְׂרָאֵל֙ וִירֵשׁ֔וּךָ וְהָיִ֥יתָ לָהֶ֖ם לְנַחֲלָ֑ה וְלֹא־תוֹסִ֥ף ע֖וֹד לְשַׁכְּלָֽם׃{ס}


And I will cause men to walk upon you, my people Yisra᾽el; and they shall possess thee, and thou shalt be their inheritance, and thou shalt no longer bereave them of children.


###### Ezekiel 36:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:13)


כֹּ֤ה אָמַר֙ אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֔ה יַ֚עַן אֹמְרִ֣ים לָכֶ֔ם אֹכֶ֥לֶת אָדָ֖ם (אתי) [אָ֑תְּ] וּמְשַׁכֶּ֥לֶת (גויך) [גּוֹיַ֖יִךְ] הָיִֽית׃


Thus says the Lord God: Because they say to you, Thou art a land that devourest men, and hast bereaved thy nations;


###### Ezekiel 36:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:14)


לָכֵ֗ן אָדָם֙ לֹא־תֹ֣אכְלִי ע֔וֹד (וגויך) [וְגוֹיַ֖יִךְ] לֹ֣א (תכשלי) [תְשַׁכְּלִי־]ע֑וֹד נְאֻ֖ם אֲדֹנָ֥י יֱהֹוִֽה׃


therefore thou shalt devour men no longer, nor any more bereave thy nations, says the Lord God.


###### Ezekiel 36:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:15)


וְלֹא־אַשְׁמִ֨יעַ אֵלַ֤יִךְ עוֹד֙ כְּלִמַּ֣ת הַגּוֹיִ֔ם וְחֶרְפַּ֥ת עַמִּ֖ים לֹ֣א תִשְׂאִי־ע֑וֹד (וגויך) [וְגוֹיַ֙יִךְ֙] לֹא־תַכְשִׁ֣לִי ע֔וֹד נְאֻ֖ם אֲדֹנָ֥י יֱהֹוִֽה׃{פ}


Neither will I allow the insult of the nations to be heard any more against thee, nor shalt thou bear the reproach of peoples, any longer, nor shalt thou cause thy nations to stumble any more, says the Lord God.


###### Ezekiel 36:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:16)


וַיְהִ֥י דְבַר־יְהֹוָ֖ה אֵלַ֥י לֵאמֹֽר׃


And the word of the Lord came to me, saying,


###### Ezekiel 36:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:17)


בֶּן־אָדָ֗ם בֵּ֤ית יִשְׂרָאֵל֙ יֹשְׁבִ֣ים עַל־אַדְמָתָ֔ם וַיְטַמְּא֣וּ אוֹתָ֔הּ בְּדַרְכָּ֖ם וּבַעֲלִֽילוֹתָ֑ם כְּטֻמְאַת֙ הַנִּדָּ֔ה הָיְתָ֥ה דַרְכָּ֖ם לְפָנָֽי׃


Son of man, when the house of Yisra᾽el dwelt in their own land, they defiled it by their way and by their doings: their way was before me as the uncleanness of a menstruous woman.


###### Ezekiel 36:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:18)


וָאֶשְׁפֹּ֤ךְ חֲמָתִי֙ עֲלֵיהֶ֔ם עַל־הַדָּ֖ם אֲשֶׁר־שָׁפְכ֣וּ עַל־הָאָ֑רֶץ וּבְגִלּוּלֵיהֶ֖ם טִמְּאֽוּהָ׃


So I poured my fury upon them for the blood that they had shed upon the land, and for their idols with which they had defiled it:


###### Ezekiel 36:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:19)


וָאָפִ֤יץ אֹתָם֙ בַּגּוֹיִ֔ם וַיִּזָּר֖וּ בָּאֲרָצ֑וֹת כְּדַרְכָּ֥ם וְכַעֲלִילוֹתָ֖ם שְׁפַטְתִּֽים׃


and I scattered them among the nations, and they were dispersed through the countries: according to their way and according to their doings I judged them.


###### Ezekiel 36:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:20)


וַיָּב֗וֹא אֶל־הַגּוֹיִם֙ אֲשֶׁר־בָּ֣אוּ שָׁ֔ם וַֽיְחַלְּל֖וּ אֶת־שֵׁ֣ם קׇדְשִׁ֑י בֶּאֱמֹ֤ר לָהֶם֙ עַם־יְהֹוָ֣ה אֵ֔לֶּה וּמֵאַרְצ֖וֹ יָצָֽאוּ׃


And when they came to the nations, into which they came, they profaned my holy name, in that men said of them, These are the people of the Lord, and they are gone out of his land.


###### Ezekiel 36:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:21)


וָאֶחְמֹ֖ל עַל־שֵׁ֣ם קׇדְשִׁ֑י אֲשֶׁ֤ר חִלְּלֻ֙הוּ֙ בֵּ֣ית יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל בַּגּוֹיִ֖ם אֲשֶׁר־בָּ֥אוּ שָֽׁמָּה׃{פ}


But I had concern for my holy name, which the house of Yisra᾽el had profaned among the nations, into which they came.


###### Ezekiel 36:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:22)


לָכֵ֞ן אֱמֹ֣ר לְבֵֽית־יִשְׂרָאֵ֗ל כֹּ֤ה אָמַר֙ אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֔ה לֹ֧א לְמַעַנְכֶ֛ם אֲנִ֥י עֹשֶׂ֖ה בֵּ֣ית יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל כִּ֤י אִם־לְשֵׁם־קׇדְשִׁי֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר חִלַּלְתֶּ֔ם בַּגּוֹיִ֖ם אֲשֶׁר־בָּ֥אתֶם שָֽׁם׃


Therefore say to the house of Yisra᾽el, Thus says the Lord God; I do not do this for your sakes, O house of Yisra᾽el, but for my holy name’s sake, which you have profaned among the nations, to which you came.


###### Ezekiel 36:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:23)


וְקִדַּשְׁתִּ֞י אֶת־שְׁמִ֣י הַגָּד֗וֹל הַֽמְחֻלָּל֙ בַּגּוֹיִ֔ם אֲשֶׁ֥ר חִלַּלְתֶּ֖ם בְּתוֹכָ֑ם וְיָדְע֨וּ הַגּוֹיִ֜ם כִּֽי־אֲנִ֣י יְהֹוָ֗ה נְאֻם֙ אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֔ה בְּהִקָּדְשִׁ֥י בָכֶ֖ם לְעֵינֵיהֶֽם׃


And I will sanctify my great name, which was profaned among the nations, which you have profaned in the midst of them; and the nations shall know that I am the Lord, says the Lord God, when I shall be sanctified in you before their eyes.


###### Ezekiel 36:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:24)


וְלָקַחְתִּ֤י אֶתְכֶם֙ מִן־הַגּוֹיִ֔ם וְקִבַּצְתִּ֥י אֶתְכֶ֖ם מִכׇּל־הָאֲרָצ֑וֹת וְהֵבֵאתִ֥י אֶתְכֶ֖ם אֶל־אַדְמַתְכֶֽם׃


For I will take you from among the nations, and gather you out of all countries, and will bring you into your own land.


###### Ezekiel 36:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:25)


וְזָרַקְתִּ֧י עֲלֵיכֶ֛ם מַ֥יִם טְהוֹרִ֖ים וּטְהַרְתֶּ֑ם מִכֹּ֧ל טֻמְאוֹתֵיכֶ֛ם וּמִכׇּל־גִּלּ֥וּלֵיכֶ֖ם אֲטַהֵ֥ר אֶתְכֶֽם׃


Then will I sprinkle clean water upon you, and you shall be clean: from all your uncleannesses, and from all your idols, will I cleanse you.


###### Ezekiel 36:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:26)


וְנָתַתִּ֤י לָכֶם֙ לֵ֣ב חָדָ֔שׁ וְר֥וּחַ חֲדָשָׁ֖ה אֶתֵּ֣ן בְּקִרְבְּכֶ֑ם וַהֲסִ֨רֹתִ֜י אֶת־לֵ֤ב הָאֶ֙בֶן֙ מִבְּשַׂרְכֶ֔ם וְנָתַתִּ֥י לָכֶ֖ם לֵ֥ב בָּשָֽׂר׃


A new heart also will I give you, and a new spirit will I put within you: and I will take away the stony heart out of your flesh, and I will give you a heart of flesh.


###### Ezekiel 36:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:27)


וְאֶת־רוּחִ֖י אֶתֵּ֣ן בְּקִרְבְּכֶ֑ם וְעָשִׂ֗יתִי אֵ֤ת אֲשֶׁר־בְּחֻקַּי֙ תֵּלֵ֔כוּ וּמִשְׁפָּטַ֥י תִּשְׁמְר֖וּ וַעֲשִׂיתֶֽם׃


And I will put my spirit within you, and cause you to follow my statutes, and you shall keep my judgments, and do them.


###### Ezekiel 36:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:28)


וִישַׁבְתֶּ֣ם בָּאָ֔רֶץ אֲשֶׁ֥ר נָתַ֖תִּי לַאֲבֹֽתֵיכֶ֑ם וִהְיִ֤יתֶם לִי֙ לְעָ֔ם וְאָ֣נֹכִ֔י אֶהְיֶ֥ה לָכֶ֖ם לֵאלֹהִֽים׃


And you shall dwell in the land that I gave to your fathers; and you shall be my people, and I will be your God.


###### Ezekiel 36:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:29)


וְהוֹשַׁעְתִּ֣י אֶתְכֶ֔ם מִכֹּ֖ל טֻמְאֽוֹתֵיכֶ֑ם וְקָרָ֤אתִי אֶל־הַדָּגָן֙ וְהִרְבֵּיתִ֣י אֹת֔וֹ וְלֹא־אֶתֵּ֥ן עֲלֵיכֶ֖ם רָעָֽב׃


And I will save you from all your uncleannesses: and I will summon the corn, and make it abundant, and lay no famine upon you.


###### Ezekiel 36:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:30)


וְהִרְבֵּיתִי֙ אֶת־פְּרִ֣י הָעֵ֔ץ וּתְנוּבַ֖ת הַשָּׂדֶ֑ה לְמַ֗עַן אֲ֠שֶׁ֠ר לֹ֣א תִקְח֥וּ ע֛וֹד חֶרְפַּ֥ת רָעָ֖ב בַּגּוֹיִֽם׃


And I will make the fruit of the tree, and the increase of the field abundant, that you shall receive no longer the reproach of famine among the nations.


###### Ezekiel 36:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:31)


וּזְכַרְתֶּם֙ אֶת־דַּרְכֵיכֶ֣ם הָרָעִ֔ים וּמַעַלְלֵיכֶ֖ם אֲשֶׁ֣ר לֹֽא־טוֹבִ֑ים וּנְקֹֽטֹתֶם֙ בִּפְנֵיכֶ֔ם עַ֚ל עֲוֺנֹ֣תֵיכֶ֔ם וְעַ֖ל תּוֹעֲבוֹתֵיכֶֽם׃


Then shall you remember your evil ways, and your doings that were not good, and will loathe yourselves in your own sight for your iniquities and for your disgusting deeds.


###### Ezekiel 36:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:32)


לֹ֧א לְמַעַנְכֶ֣ם אֲנִֽי־עֹשֶׂ֗ה נְאֻם֙ אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֔ה יִוָּדַ֖ע לָכֶ֑ם בּ֧וֹשׁוּ וְהִכָּֽלְמ֛וּ מִדַּרְכֵיכֶ֖ם בֵּ֥ית יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃{ס}


Not for your sakes shall I do this, says the Lord God; let that be known to you. Be ashamed and confounded for your ways, O house of Yisra᾽el.


###### Ezekiel 36:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:33)


כֹּ֤ה אָמַר֙ אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֔ה בְּיוֹם֙ טַהֲרִ֣י אֶתְכֶ֔ם מִכֹּ֖ל עֲוֺנֽוֹתֵיכֶ֑ם וְהֽוֹשַׁבְתִּי֙ אֶת־הֶ֣עָרִ֔ים וְנִבְנ֖וּ הֶחֳרָבֽוֹת׃


Thus says the Lord God; On the day that I shall have cleansed you from all your iniquities I will also cause the cities to be inhabited, and the wastes shall be rebuilt.


###### Ezekiel 36:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:34)


וְהָאָ֥רֶץ הַנְּשַׁמָּ֖ה תֵּעָבֵ֑ד תַּ֚חַת אֲשֶׁ֣ר הָיְתָ֣ה שְׁמָמָ֔ה לְעֵינֵ֖י כׇּל־עוֹבֵֽר׃


And the desolate land shall be tilled, instead of the desolation that it was in the sight of all that passed by.


###### Ezekiel 36:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:35)


וְאָמְר֗וּ הָאָ֤רֶץ הַלֵּ֙זוּ֙ הַנְּשַׁמָּ֔ה הָיְתָ֖ה כְּגַן־עֵ֑דֶן וְהֶעָרִ֧ים הֶחֳרֵב֛וֹת וְהַֽנְשַׁמּ֥וֹת וְהַנֶּהֱרָס֖וֹת בְּצוּר֥וֹת יָשָֽׁבוּ׃


And they shall say, This land that was blighted is become like the garden of ῾Eden; and the waste and blighted and ruined cities are fortified, and inhabited.


###### Ezekiel 36:36

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:36)


וְיָדְע֣וּ הַגּוֹיִ֗ם אֲשֶׁ֣ר יִֽשָּׁאֲרוּ֮ סְבִיבֽוֹתֵיכֶם֒ כִּ֣י**׀** אֲנִ֣י יְהֹוָ֗ה בָּנִ֙יתִי֙ הַנֶּ֣הֱרָס֔וֹת נָטַ֖עְתִּי הַנְּשַׁמָּ֑ה אֲנִ֥י יְהֹוָ֖ה דִּבַּ֥רְתִּי וְעָשִֽׂיתִי׃{ס}


Then the nations that are left round about you shall know that I the Lord have rebuilt the ruined places, and have replanted that which was blighted: I the Lord have spoken it, and I will do it.


###### Ezekiel 36:37

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:37](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:37)


כֹּ֤ה אָמַר֙ אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֔ה ע֗וֹד זֹ֛את אִדָּרֵ֥שׁ לְבֵֽית־יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל לַעֲשׂ֣וֹת לָהֶ֑ם אַרְבֶּ֥ה אֹתָ֛ם כַּצֹּ֖אן אָדָֽם׃


Thus says the Lord God; moreover, this request I will grant to the house of Yisra᾽el to do it for them; I will increase them with men like a flock.


###### Ezekiel 36:38

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:38](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/36:38)


כְּצֹ֣אן קׇדָשִׁ֗ים כְּצֹ֤אן יְרוּשָׁלַ֙͏ִם֙ בְּמ֣וֹעֲדֶ֔יהָ כֵּ֤ן תִּֽהְיֶ֙ינָה֙ הֶעָרִ֣ים הֶחֳרֵב֔וֹת מְלֵא֖וֹת צֹ֣אן אָדָ֑ם וְיָדְע֖וּ כִּֽי־אֲנִ֥י יְהֹוָֽה׃{פ}


Like the flock of sacrifices, like the flock of Yerushalayim in her appointed times; so shall the waste cities be filled with flocks of men: and they shall know that I am the Lord.



## Chapter 37

###### Ezekiel 37:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/37:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/37:1)


הָיְתָ֣ה עָלַי֮ יַד־יְהֹוָה֒ וַיּוֹצִאֵ֤נִֽי בְר֙וּחַ֙ יְהֹוָ֔ה וַיְנִיחֵ֖נִי בְּת֣וֹךְ הַבִּקְעָ֑ה וְהִ֖יא מְלֵאָ֥ה עֲצָמֽוֹת׃


The hand of the Lord was upon me, and carried me out in the spirit of the Lord, and set me down in the midst of the valley which was full of bones,


###### Ezekiel 37:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/37:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/37:2)


וְהֶעֱבִירַ֥נִי עֲלֵיהֶ֖ם סָבִ֣יב׀סָבִ֑יב וְהִנֵּ֨ה רַבּ֤וֹת מְאֹד֙ עַל־פְּנֵ֣י הַבִּקְעָ֔ה וְהִנֵּ֖ה יְבֵשׁ֥וֹת מְאֹֽד׃


and he caused me to pass by them round about: and, behold, there were very many in the open valley; and, lo, they were very dry.


###### Ezekiel 37:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/37:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/37:3)


וַיֹּ֣אמֶר אֵלַ֔י בֶּן־אָדָ֕ם הֲתִֽחְיֶ֖ינָה הָעֲצָמ֣וֹת הָאֵ֑לֶּה וָאֹמַ֕ר אֲדֹנָ֥י יֱהֹוִ֖ה אַתָּ֥ה יָדָֽעְתָּ׃


And he said to me, Son of man, can these bones live? And I answered, O Lord God, thou knowst.


###### Ezekiel 37:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/37:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/37:4)


וַיֹּ֣אמֶר אֵלַ֔י הִנָּבֵ֖א עַל־הָעֲצָמ֣וֹת הָאֵ֑לֶּה וְאָמַרְתָּ֣ אֲלֵיהֶ֔ם הָֽעֲצָמוֹת֙ הַיְבֵשׁ֔וֹת שִׁמְע֖וּ דְּבַר־יְהֹוָֽה׃


Again he said to me, Prophesy over these bones, and say to them, O dry bones, hear the word of the Lord.


###### Ezekiel 37:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/37:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/37:5)


כֹּ֤ה אָמַר֙ אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֔ה לָעֲצָמ֖וֹת הָאֵ֑לֶּה הִנֵּ֨ה אֲנִ֜י מֵבִ֥יא בָכֶ֛ם ר֖וּחַ וִחְיִיתֶֽם׃


Thus says the Lord God to these bones; Behold, I will cause breath to enter into you, and you shall live:


###### Ezekiel 37:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/37:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/37:6)


וְנָתַתִּי֩ עֲלֵיכֶ֨ם גִּידִ֜ים וְֽהַעֲלֵתִ֧י עֲלֵיכֶ֣ם בָּשָׂ֗ר וְקָרַמְתִּ֤י עֲלֵיכֶם֙ ע֔וֹר וְנָתַתִּ֥י בָכֶ֛ם ר֖וּחַ וִחְיִיתֶ֑ם וִידַעְתֶּ֖ם כִּֽי־אֲנִ֥י יְהֹוָֽה׃


and I will lay sinews upon you, and I will bring up flesh upon you, and cover you with skin, and put breath in you, and you shall live; and you shall know that I am the Lord.


###### Ezekiel 37:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/37:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/37:7)


וְנִבֵּ֖אתִי כַּאֲשֶׁ֣ר צֻוֵּ֑יתִי וַֽיְהִי־ק֤וֹל כְּהִנָּֽבְאִי֙ וְהִנֵּה־רַ֔עַשׁ וַתִּקְרְב֣וּ עֲצָמ֔וֹת עֶ֖צֶם אֶל־עַצְמֽוֹ׃


So I prophesied as I was commanded: and as I prophesied, there was a noise, and behold a rattling, and the bones came together, bone to its bone.


###### Ezekiel 37:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/37:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/37:8)


וְרָאִ֜יתִי וְהִנֵּֽה־עֲלֵיהֶ֤ם גִּדִים֙ וּבָשָׂ֣ר עָלָ֔ה וַיִּקְרַ֧ם עֲלֵיהֶ֛ם ע֖וֹר מִלְמָ֑עְלָה וְר֖וּחַ אֵ֥ין בָּהֶֽם׃


And as I beheld, lo, the sinews and the flesh came up on them, and the skin covered them above: but there was no breath in them.


###### Ezekiel 37:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/37:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/37:9)


וַיֹּ֣אמֶר אֵלַ֔י הִנָּבֵ֖א אֶל־הָר֑וּחַ הִנָּבֵ֣א בֶן־אָ֠דָ֠ם וְאָמַרְתָּ֨ אֶל־הָר֜וּחַ{ס}כֹּה־אָמַ֣ר**׀** אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֗ה מֵאַרְבַּ֤ע רוּחוֹת֙ בֹּ֣אִי הָר֔וּחַ וּפְחִ֛י בַּהֲרוּגִ֥ים הָאֵ֖לֶּה וְיִֽחְיֽוּ׃


Then he said to me, Prophesy to the breath, prophesy, son of man, and say to the breath, Thus says the Lord God; Come from the four winds, O breath, and breathe upon these slain, that they may live.


###### Ezekiel 37:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/37:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/37:10)


וְהִנַּבֵּ֖אתִי כַּאֲשֶׁ֣ר צִוָּ֑נִי וַתָּבוֹא֩ בָהֶ֨ם הָר֜וּחַ וַיִּֽחְי֗וּ וַיַּֽעַמְדוּ֙ עַל־רַגְלֵיהֶ֔ם חַ֖יִל גָּד֥וֹל מְאֹד־מְאֹֽד׃


So I prophesied as he commanded me, and the breath came into them, and they lived, and stood up on their feet, an exceeding great army.


###### Ezekiel 37:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/37:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/37:11)


וַיֹּ֘אמֶר֮ אֵלַי֒ בֶּן־אָדָ֕ם הָעֲצָמ֣וֹת הָאֵ֔לֶּה כׇּל־בֵּ֥ית יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל הֵ֑מָּה הִנֵּ֣ה אֹמְרִ֗ים יָבְשׁ֧וּ עַצְמוֹתֵ֛ינוּ וְאָבְדָ֥ה תִקְוָתֵ֖נוּ נִגְזַ֥רְנוּ לָֽנוּ׃


Then he said to me, Son of man, these bones are the whole house of Yisra᾽el: behold, they say, Our bones are dried, and our hope is lost: we are clean cut off.


###### Ezekiel 37:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/37:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/37:12)


לָכֵן֩ הִנָּבֵ֨א וְאָמַרְתָּ֜ אֲלֵיהֶ֗ם כֹּה־אָמַר֮ אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִה֒ הִנֵּה֩ אֲנִ֨י פֹתֵ֜חַ אֶת־קִבְרֽוֹתֵיכֶ֗ם וְהַעֲלֵיתִ֥י אֶתְכֶ֛ם מִקִּבְרוֹתֵיכֶ֖ם עַמִּ֑י וְהֵבֵאתִ֥י אֶתְכֶ֖ם אֶל־אַדְמַ֥ת יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


Therefore prophesy and say to them, Thus says the Lord God; Behold, O my people, I will open your graves, and cause you to come up out of your graves, and bring you into the land of Yisra᾽el.


###### Ezekiel 37:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/37:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/37:13)


וִידַעְתֶּ֖ם כִּֽי־אֲנִ֣י יְהֹוָ֑ה בְּפִתְחִ֣י אֶת־קִבְרֽוֹתֵיכֶ֗ם וּבְהַעֲלוֹתִ֥י אֶתְכֶ֛ם מִקִּבְרוֹתֵיכֶ֖ם עַמִּֽי׃


And you shall know that I am the Lord, when I have opened your graves, O my people, and have brought you up out of your graves,


###### Ezekiel 37:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/37:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/37:14)


וְנָתַתִּ֨י רוּחִ֤י בָכֶם֙ וִחְיִיתֶ֔ם וְהִנַּחְתִּ֥י אֶתְכֶ֖ם עַל־אַדְמַתְכֶ֑ם וִידַעְתֶּ֞ם כִּֽי־אֲנִ֧י יְהֹוָ֛ה דִּבַּ֥רְתִּי וְעָשִׂ֖יתִי נְאֻם־יְהֹוָֽה׃{פ}


and I shall put my spirit in you, and you shall live, and I shall place you in your own land: then you shall know that I the Lord have spoken, and performed it, says the Lord.


###### Ezekiel 37:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/37:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/37:15)


וַיְהִ֥י דְבַר־יְהֹוָ֖ה אֵלַ֥י לֵאמֹֽר׃


And the word of the Lord came to me saying,


###### Ezekiel 37:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/37:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/37:16)


וְאַתָּ֣ה בֶן־אָדָ֗ם קַח־לְךָ֙ עֵ֣ץ אֶחָ֔ד וּכְתֹ֤ב עָלָיו֙ לִֽיהוּדָ֔ה וְלִבְנֵ֥י יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל חֲבֵרָ֑ו וּלְקַח֙ עֵ֣ץ אֶחָ֔ד וּכְת֣וֹב עָלָ֗יו לְיוֹסֵף֙ עֵ֣ץ אֶפְרַ֔יִם וְכׇל־בֵּ֥ית יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל חֲבֵרָֽו׃


And thou, son of man, take thee one stick, and write upon it. For Yehuda, and for the children of Yisra᾽el his companions: then take another stick and write upon it. For Yosef, the stick of Efrayim, and for all the house of Yisra᾽el his companions:


###### Ezekiel 37:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/37:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/37:17)


וְקָרַ֨ב אֹתָ֜ם אֶחָ֧ד אֶל־אֶחָ֛ד לְךָ֖ לְעֵ֣ץ אֶחָ֑ד וְהָי֥וּ לַאֲחָדִ֖ים בְּיָדֶֽךָ׃


and join them one to the other to make one stick; and they shall become one in thy hand.


###### Ezekiel 37:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/37:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/37:18)


וְכַֽאֲשֶׁר֙ יֹאמְר֣וּ אֵלֶ֔יךָ בְּנֵ֥י עַמְּךָ֖ לֵאמֹ֑ר הֲלֽוֹא־תַגִּ֥יד לָ֖נוּ מָה־אֵ֥לֶּה לָּֽךְ׃


And when the children of thy people shall speak to thee, saying, Wilt thou not tell us what thou meanest by these?


###### Ezekiel 37:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/37:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/37:19)


דַּבֵּ֣ר אֲלֵהֶ֗ם כֹּה־אָמַר֮ אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִה֒ הִנֵּה֩ אֲנִ֨י לֹקֵ֜חַ אֶת־עֵ֤ץ יוֹסֵף֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר בְּיַד־אֶפְרַ֔יִם וְשִׁבְטֵ֥י יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל חֲבֵרָ֑ו וְנָתַתִּי֩ אוֹתָ֨ם עָלָ֜יו אֶת־עֵ֣ץ יְהוּדָ֗ה וַֽעֲשִׂיתִם֙ לְעֵ֣ץ אֶחָ֔ד וְהָי֥וּ אֶחָ֖ד בְּיָדִֽי׃


say to them, Thus says the Lord God; Behold I will take the stick of Yosef, which is in the hand of Efrayim, and the tribes of Yisra᾽el his companions, and will put them and it together with the stick of Yehuda to form one stick, and they shall be one in my hand.


###### Ezekiel 37:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/37:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/37:20)


וְהָי֨וּ הָעֵצִ֜ים אֲֽשֶׁר־תִּכְתֹּ֧ב עֲלֵיהֶ֛ם בְּיָדְךָ֖ לְעֵינֵיהֶֽם׃


And the sticks on which thou writest shall be in thy hand before their eyes.


###### Ezekiel 37:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/37:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/37:21)


וְדַבֵּ֣ר אֲלֵיהֶ֗ם כֹּה־אָמַר֮ אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִה֒ הִנֵּ֨ה אֲנִ֤י לֹקֵ֙חַ֙ אֶת־בְּנֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל מִבֵּ֥ין הַגּוֹיִ֖ם אֲשֶׁ֣ר הָֽלְכוּ־שָׁ֑ם וְקִבַּצְתִּ֤י אֹתָם֙ מִסָּבִ֔יב וְהֵבֵאתִ֥י אוֹתָ֖ם אֶל־אַדְמָתָֽם׃


And say to them, Thus says the Lord God; Behold, I will take the children of Yisra᾽el from among the nations, into which they are gone, and will gather them on every side, and bring them into their own land:


###### Ezekiel 37:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/37:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/37:22)


וְעָשִׂ֣יתִי אֹ֠תָ֠ם לְג֨וֹי אֶחָ֤ד בָּאָ֙רֶץ֙ בְּהָרֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל וּמֶ֧לֶךְ אֶחָ֛ד יִהְיֶ֥ה לְכֻלָּ֖ם לְמֶ֑לֶךְ וְלֹ֤א (יהיה) [יִֽהְיוּ־]עוֹד֙ לִשְׁנֵ֣י גוֹיִ֔ם וְלֹ֨א יֵחָ֥צוּ ע֛וֹד לִשְׁתֵּ֥י מַמְלָכ֖וֹת עֽוֹד׃


and I will make them one nation in the land upon the mountains of Yisra᾽el; and one king shall be king over them all: and they shall no more be two nations, nor shall they be divided into two kingdoms any more at all:


###### Ezekiel 37:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/37:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/37:23)


וְלֹ֧א יִֽטַּמְּא֣וּ ע֗וֹד בְּגִלּֽוּלֵיהֶם֙ וּבְשִׁקּ֣וּצֵיהֶ֔ם וּבְכֹ֖ל פִּשְׁעֵיהֶ֑ם וְהוֹשַׁעְתִּ֣י אֹתָ֗ם מִכֹּ֤ל מוֹשְׁבֹֽתֵיהֶם֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר חָטְא֣וּ בָהֶ֔ם וְטִהַרְתִּ֤י אוֹתָם֙ וְהָיוּ־לִ֣י לְעָ֔ם וַאֲנִ֕י אֶהְיֶ֥ה לָהֶ֖ם לֵאלֹהִֽים׃


nor shall they defile themselves any more with their idols, nor with their detestable things, nor with any of their transgressions: but I will save them in all their dwellingplaces, where they have sinned, and will cleanse them: so shall they be my people, and I will be their God.


###### Ezekiel 37:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/37:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/37:24)


וְעַבְדִּ֤י דָוִד֙ מֶ֣לֶךְ עֲלֵיהֶ֔ם וְרוֹעֶ֥ה אֶחָ֖ד יִהְיֶ֣ה לְכֻלָּ֑ם וּבְמִשְׁפָּטַ֣י יֵלֵ֔כוּ וְחֻקּוֹתַ֥י יִשְׁמְר֖וּ וְעָשׂ֥וּ אוֹתָֽם׃


And David my servant shall be king over them; and they all shall have one shepherd: they shall also follow my judgments, and observe my statutes, and do them.


###### Ezekiel 37:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/37:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/37:25)


וְיָשְׁב֣וּ עַל־הָאָ֗רֶץ אֲשֶׁ֤ר נָתַ֙תִּי֙ לְעַבְדִּ֣י לְיַֽעֲקֹ֔ב אֲשֶׁ֥ר יָֽשְׁבוּ־בָ֖הּ אֲבֽוֹתֵיכֶ֑ם וְיָשְׁב֣וּ עָלֶ֡יהָ הֵ֠מָּה וּבְנֵיהֶ֞ם וּבְנֵ֤י בְנֵיהֶם֙ עַד־עוֹלָ֔ם וְדָוִ֣ד עַבְדִּ֔י נָשִׂ֥יא לָהֶ֖ם לְעוֹלָֽם׃


And they shall dwell in the land that I have given to Ya῾aqov my servant, in which your fathers have dwelt; and they shall dwell there, they, and their children, and their children’s children for ever: and my servant David shall be their prince for ever.


###### Ezekiel 37:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/37:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/37:26)


וְכָרַתִּ֤י לָהֶם֙ בְּרִ֣ית שָׁל֔וֹם בְּרִ֥ית עוֹלָ֖ם יִהְיֶ֣ה אוֹתָ֑ם וּנְתַתִּים֙ וְהִרְבֵּיתִ֣י אוֹתָ֔ם וְנָתַתִּ֧י אֶת־מִקְדָּשִׁ֛י בְּתוֹכָ֖ם לְעוֹלָֽם׃


Moreover I will make a covenant of peace with them; it shall be an everlasting covenant with them, which I will give them; and I will multiply them, and will set my sanctuary in the midst of them for evermore.


###### Ezekiel 37:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/37:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/37:27)


וְהָיָ֤ה מִשְׁכָּנִי֙ עֲלֵיהֶ֔ם וְהָיִ֥יתִי לָהֶ֖ם לֵאלֹהִ֑ים וְהֵ֖מָּה יִהְיוּ־לִ֥י לְעָֽם׃


And my tabernacle shall be with them: and I will be their God, and they shall be my people.


###### Ezekiel 37:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/37:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/37:28)


וְיָֽדְעוּ֙ הַגּוֹיִ֔ם כִּ֚י אֲנִ֣י יְהֹוָ֔ה מְקַדֵּ֖שׁ אֶת־יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל בִּהְי֧וֹת מִקְדָּשִׁ֛י בְּתוֹכָ֖ם לְעוֹלָֽם׃{פ}


Then the nations shall know that I the Lord do sanctify Yisra᾽el, when my sanctuary shall be in the midst of them for evermore.



## Chapter 38

###### Ezekiel 38:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/38:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/38:1)


וַיְהִ֥י דְבַר־יְהֹוָ֖ה אֵלַ֥י לֵאמֹֽר׃


And the word of the Lord came to me, saying,


###### Ezekiel 38:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/38:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/38:2)


בֶּן־אָדָ֗ם שִׂ֤ים פָּנֶ֙יךָ֙ אֶל־גּוֹג֙ אֶ֣רֶץ הַמָּג֔וֹג נְשִׂ֕יא רֹ֖אשׁ מֶ֣שֶׁךְ וְתֻבָ֑ל וְהִנָּבֵ֖א עָלָֽיו׃


Son of man, set thy face against Gog, of the land of Magog, the chief prince of Meshekh and Tuval, and prophesy against him,


###### Ezekiel 38:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/38:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/38:3)


וְאָ֣מַרְתָּ֔ כֹּ֥ה אָמַ֖ר אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֑ה הִנְנִ֤י אֵלֶ֙יךָ֙ גּ֔וֹג נְשִׂ֕יא רֹ֖אשׁ מֶ֥שֶׁךְ וְתֻבָֽל׃


and say, Thus says the Lord God; Behold, I am against thee, O Gog, the chief prince of Meshekh and Tuval:


###### Ezekiel 38:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/38:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/38:4)


וְשׁ֣וֹבַבְתִּ֔יךָ וְנָתַתִּ֥י חַחִ֖ים בִּלְחָיֶ֑יךָ וְהוֹצֵאתִי֩ אוֹתְךָ֨ וְאֶת־כׇּל־חֵילֶ֜ךָ סוּסִ֣ים וּפָרָשִׁ֗ים לְבֻשֵׁ֤י מִכְלוֹל֙ כֻּלָּ֔ם קָהָ֥ל רָב֙ צִנָּ֣ה וּמָגֵ֔ן תֹּפְשֵׂ֥י חֲרָב֖וֹת כֻּלָּֽם׃


and I will turn thee about and put hooks into thy jaws, and I will bring thee out, and all thy army, horses and horsemen, all of them in complete attire, a great company with buckler and shield, all of them handling swords:


###### Ezekiel 38:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/38:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/38:5)


פָּרַ֛ס כּ֥וּשׁ וּפ֖וּט אִתָּ֑ם כֻּלָּ֖ם מָגֵ֥ן וְכוֹבָֽע׃


Paras, Kush, and Put with them; all of them with shield and helmet:


###### Ezekiel 38:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/38:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/38:6)


גֹּ֚מֶר וְכׇל־אֲגַפֶּ֔יהָ בֵּ֚ית תּֽוֹגַרְמָ֔ה יַרְכְּתֵ֥י צָפ֖וֹן וְאֶת־כׇּל־אֲגַפָּ֑יו עַמִּ֥ים רַבִּ֖ים אִתָּֽךְ׃


Gomer, and all his bands; the house of Togarma of the far sides of the north, and all his bands: and many peoples with thee.


###### Ezekiel 38:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/38:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/38:7)


הִכֹּן֙ וְהָכֵ֣ן לְךָ֔ אַתָּ֕ה וְכׇל־קְהָלֶ֖ךָ הַנִּקְהָלִ֣ים עָלֶ֑יךָ וְהָיִ֥יתָ לָהֶ֖ם לְמִשְׁמָֽר׃


Be thou prepared, and prepare for thyself, thou, and all thy company that are assembled unto thee, and be thou a guard to them.


###### Ezekiel 38:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/38:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/38:8)


מִיָּמִ֣ים רַבִּים֮ תִּפָּקֵד֒ בְּאַחֲרִ֨ית הַשָּׁנִ֜ים תָּב֣וֹא**׀** אֶל־אֶ֣רֶץ**׀** מְשׁוֹבֶ֣בֶת מֵחֶ֗רֶב מְקֻבֶּ֙צֶת֙ מֵעַמִּ֣ים רַבִּ֔ים עַ֚ל הָרֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל אֲשֶׁר־הָי֥וּ לְחׇרְבָּ֖ה תָּמִ֑יד וְהִיא֙ מֵעַמִּ֣ים הוּצָ֔אָה וְיָשְׁב֥וּ לָבֶ֖טַח כֻּלָּֽם׃


After many days thou shalt be called upon: in the latter years thou shalt come against the land that is brought back from the sword, and is gathered out of many peoples against the mountains of Yisra᾽el, which have been a continual waste: but it is brought out of the nations, and they dwell safely all of them.


###### Ezekiel 38:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/38:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/38:9)


וְעָלִ֙יתָ֙ כַּשֹּׁאָ֣ה תָב֔וֹא כֶּעָנָ֛ן לְכַסּ֥וֹת הָאָ֖רֶץ תִּֽהְיֶ֑ה אַתָּה֙ וְכׇל־אֲגַפֶּ֔יךָ וְעַמִּ֥ים רַבִּ֖ים אוֹתָֽךְ׃{ס}


Thou shalt ascend and come like a storm, thou shalt be like a cloud to cover the land, thou, and all thy bands, and many peoples with thee.


###### Ezekiel 38:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/38:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/38:10)


כֹּ֥ה אָמַ֖ר אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֑ה וְהָיָ֣ה**׀** בַּיּ֣וֹם הַה֗וּא יַעֲל֤וּ דְבָרִים֙ עַל־לְבָבֶ֔ךָ וְחָשַׁבְתָּ֖ מַחֲשֶׁ֥בֶת רָעָֽה׃


Thus says the Lord God; It shall come to pass on that day, that things shall come into thy mind, and thou shalt think an evil scheme:


###### Ezekiel 38:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/38:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/38:11)


וְאָמַרְתָּ֗ אֶֽעֱלֶה֙ עַל־אֶ֣רֶץ פְּרָז֔וֹת אָבוֹא֙ הַשֹּׁ֣קְטִ֔ים יֹשְׁבֵ֖י לָבֶ֑טַח כֻּלָּ֗ם יֹֽשְׁבִים֙ בְּאֵ֣ין חוֹמָ֔ה וּבְרִ֥יחַ וּדְלָתַ֖יִם אֵ֥ין לָהֶֽם׃


and thou shalt say, I will go up to the land of unwalled villages; I will go to them that are at quiet, that dwell in safety, all of them dwelling without walls, and having neither bars nor gates;


###### Ezekiel 38:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/38:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/38:12)


לִשְׁלֹ֥ל שָׁלָ֖ל וְלָבֹ֣ז בַּ֑ז לְהָשִׁ֨יב יָדְךָ֜ עַל־חֳרָב֣וֹת נוֹשָׁב֗וֹת וְאֶל־עַם֙ מְאֻסָּ֣ף מִגּוֹיִ֔ם עֹשֶׂה֙ מִקְנֶ֣ה וְקִנְיָ֔ן יֹשְׁבֵ֖י עַל־טַבּ֥וּר הָאָֽרֶץ׃


to take a spoil, and to take a prey; to turn thy hand against the waste places that are now inhabited, and against a people that are gathered out of the nations, that have acquired cattle and goods, and that dwell at the centre of the earth.


###### Ezekiel 38:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/38:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/38:13)


שְׁבָ֡א וּ֠דְדָ֠ן וְסֹחֲרֵ֨י תַרְשִׁ֤ישׁ וְכׇל־כְּפִירֶ֙יהָ֙ יֹאמְר֣וּ לְךָ֔ הֲלִשְׁלֹ֤ל שָׁלָל֙ אַתָּ֣ה בָ֔א הֲלָבֹ֥ז בַּ֖ז הִקְהַ֣לְתָּ קְהָלֶ֑ךָ לָשֵׂ֣את**׀** כֶּ֣סֶף וְזָהָ֗ב לָקַ֙חַת֙ מִקְנֶ֣ה וְקִנְיָ֔ן לִשְׁלֹ֖ל שָׁלָ֥ל גָּדֽוֹל׃{ס}


Sheva, and Dedan, and the merchants of Tarshish, with all its young lions, shall say to thee. Art thou come to take a spoil? hast thou gathered thy company to take a prey? to carry away silver and gold, to take away cattle and goods, to take a great spoil?


###### Ezekiel 38:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/38:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/38:14)


לָכֵן֙ הִנָּבֵ֣א בֶן־אָדָ֔ם וְאָמַרְתָּ֣ לְג֔וֹג כֹּ֥ה אָמַ֖ר אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֑ה הֲל֣וֹא**׀** בַּיּ֣וֹם הַה֗וּא בְּשֶׁ֨בֶת עַמִּ֧י יִשְׂרָאֵ֛ל לָבֶ֖טַח תֵּדָֽע׃


Therefore, son of man, prophesy and say to Gog, Thus says the Lord God; In that day when my people of Yisra᾽el dwell safely, shalt thou not know it?


###### Ezekiel 38:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/38:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/38:15)


וּבָ֤אתָ מִמְּקֽוֹמְךָ֙ מִיַּרְכְּתֵ֣י צָפ֔וֹן אַתָּ֕ה וְעַמִּ֥ים רַבִּ֖ים אִתָּ֑ךְ רֹכְבֵ֤י סוּסִים֙ כֻּלָּ֔ם קָהָ֥ל גָּד֖וֹל וְחַ֥יִל רָֽב׃


And thou shalt come from thy place out of the far sides of the north, thou, and many peoples with thee, all of them riding upon horses, a great company, and a mighty army:


###### Ezekiel 38:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/38:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/38:16)


וְעָלִ֙יתָ֙ עַל־עַמִּ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל כֶּעָנָ֖ן לְכַסּ֣וֹת הָאָ֑רֶץ בְּאַחֲרִ֨ית הַיָּמִ֜ים תִּהְיֶ֗ה וַהֲבִאוֹתִ֙יךָ֙ עַל־אַרְצִ֔י לְמַ֩עַן֩ דַּ֨עַת הַגּוֹיִ֜ם אֹתִ֗י בְּהִקָּדְשִׁ֥י בְךָ֛ לְעֵינֵיהֶ֖ם גּֽוֹג׃{ס}


and thou shalt come up against my people of Yisra᾽el, like a cloud to cover the land; it shall be in the latter days, and I will bring thee against my land, that the nations may know me, when I shall be sanctified by thee, O Gog, before their eyes.


###### Ezekiel 38:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/38:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/38:17)


כֹּה־אָמַ֞ר אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֗ה הַאַתָּה־ה֨וּא אֲשֶׁר־דִּבַּ֜רְתִּי בְּיָמִ֣ים קַדְמוֹנִ֗ים בְּיַד֙ עֲבָדַי֙ נְבִיאֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל הַֽנִּבְּאִ֛ים בַּיָּמִ֥ים הָהֵ֖ם שָׁנִ֑ים לְהָבִ֥יא אֹתְךָ֖ עֲלֵיהֶֽם׃{ס}


Thus says the Lord God; Art thou he of whom I have spoken in old time by my servants the prophets of Yisra᾽el, who prophesied in those days for many years that I would bring thee against them?


###### Ezekiel 38:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/38:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/38:18)


וְהָיָ֣ה**׀** בַּיּ֣וֹם הַה֗וּא בְּי֨וֹם בּ֥וֹא גוֹג֙ עַל־אַדְמַ֣ת יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל נְאֻ֖ם אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֑ה תַּעֲלֶ֥ה חֲמָתִ֖י בְּאַפִּֽי׃


And it shall come to pass on that day, when Gog shall come against the land of Yisra᾽el, says the Lord God, that my fury shall glare out.


###### Ezekiel 38:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/38:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/38:19)


וּבְקִנְאָתִ֥י בְאֵשׁ־עֶבְרָתִ֖י דִּבַּ֑רְתִּי אִם־לֹ֣א**׀** בַּיּ֣וֹם הַה֗וּא יִֽהְיֶה֙ רַ֣עַשׁ גָּד֔וֹל עַ֖ל אַדְמַ֥ת יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


For in my jealousy and in the fire of my anger have I spoken saying, Surely in that day there shall be a great shaking in the land of Yisra᾽el;


###### Ezekiel 38:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/38:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/38:20)


וְרָעֲשׁ֣וּ מִפָּנַ֡י דְּגֵ֣י הַיָּם֩ וְע֨וֹף הַשָּׁמַ֜יִם וְחַיַּ֣ת הַשָּׂדֶ֗ה וְכׇל־הָרֶ֙מֶשׂ֙ הָרֹמֵ֣שׂ עַל־הָאֲדָמָ֔ה וְכֹל֙ הָאָדָ֔ם אֲשֶׁ֖ר עַל־פְּנֵ֣י הָאֲדָמָ֑ה וְנֶהֶרְס֣וּ הֶהָרִ֗ים וְנָֽפְלוּ֙ הַמַּדְרֵג֔וֹת וְכׇל־חוֹמָ֖ה לָאָ֥רֶץ תִּפּֽוֹל׃


so that the fishes of the sea, and the birds of the sky, and the beasts of the field, and all creeping things that creep upon the earth, and all the men that are upon the face of the earth, shall shake at my presence, and the mountains shall be thrown down, and the steep places shall fall, and every wall shall fall to the ground.


###### Ezekiel 38:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/38:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/38:21)


וְקָרָ֨אתִי עָלָ֤יו לְכׇל־הָרַי֙ חֶ֔רֶב נְאֻ֖ם אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֑ה חֶ֥רֶב אִ֖ישׁ בְּאָחִ֥יו תִּהְיֶֽה׃


And I will call for a sword against him throughout all my mountains, says the Lord God: every man’s sword shall be against his brother.


###### Ezekiel 38:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/38:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/38:22)


וְנִשְׁפַּטְתִּ֥י אִתּ֖וֹ בְּדֶ֣בֶר וּבְדָ֑ם וְגֶ֣שֶׁם שׁוֹטֵף֩ וְאַבְנֵ֨י אֶלְגָּבִ֜ישׁ אֵ֣שׁ וְגׇפְרִ֗ית אַמְטִ֤יר עָלָיו֙ וְעַל־אֲגַפָּ֔יו וְעַל־עַמִּ֥ים רַבִּ֖ים אֲשֶׁ֥ר אִתּֽוֹ׃


And I will contend with him by pestilence and by blood; and I will rain down upon him, and upon his bands, and upon the many peoples that are with him, a torrential rain, and great hailstones, fire, and brimstone.


###### Ezekiel 38:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/38:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/38:23)


וְהִתְגַּדִּלְתִּי֙ וְהִתְקַדִּשְׁתִּ֔י וְנ֣וֹדַעְתִּ֔י לְעֵינֵ֖י גּוֹיִ֣ם רַבִּ֑ים וְיָדְע֖וּ כִּֽי־אֲנִ֥י יְהֹוָֽה׃{ס}


Thus will I magnify myself and sanctify myself; and I will make myself known in the eyes of many nations, and they shall know that I am the Lord.



## Chapter 39

###### Ezekiel 39:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/39:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/39:1)


וְאַתָּ֤ה בֶן־אָדָם֙ הִנָּבֵ֣א עַל־גּ֔וֹג וְאָ֣מַרְתָּ֔ כֹּ֥ה אָמַ֖ר אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֑ה הִנְנִ֤י אֵלֶ֙יךָ֙ גּ֔וֹג נְשִׂ֕יא רֹ֖אשׁ מֶ֥שֶׁךְ וְתֻבָֽל׃


And thou, son of man, prophesy against Gog, and say, Thus says the Lord God; Behold, I am against thee, O Gog, the chief prince of Meshekh and Tuval:


###### Ezekiel 39:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/39:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/39:2)


וְשֹׁבַבְתִּ֙יךָ֙ וְשִׁשֵּׁאתִ֔יךָ וְהַעֲלִיתִ֖יךָ מִיַּרְכְּתֵ֣י צָפ֑וֹן וַהֲבִאוֹתִ֖ךָ עַל־הָרֵ֥י יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


and I will turn thee about, and entice thee on, and will cause thee to come up from the far sides of the north, and will bring thee against the mountains of Yisra᾽el:


###### Ezekiel 39:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/39:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/39:3)


וְהִכֵּיתִ֥י קַשְׁתְּךָ֖ מִיַּ֣ד שְׂמֹאולֶ֑ךָ וְחִצֶּ֕יךָ מִיַּ֥ד יְמִינְךָ֖ אַפִּֽיל׃


and I will smite thy bow out of thy left hand, and will cause thy arrows to fall out of thy right hand.


###### Ezekiel 39:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/39:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/39:4)


עַל־הָרֵ֨י יִשְׂרָאֵ֜ל תִּפּ֗וֹל אַתָּה֙ וְכׇל־אֲגַפֶּ֔יךָ וְעַמִּ֖ים אֲשֶׁ֣ר אִתָּ֑ךְ לְעֵ֨יט צִפּ֧וֹר כׇּל־כָּנָ֛ף וְחַיַּ֥ת הַשָּׂדֶ֖ה נְתַתִּ֥יךָ לְאׇכְלָֽה׃


Thou shalt fall upon the mountains of Yisra᾽el, thou, and all thy bands, and the peoples that are with thee: I will give thee to the ravenous birds of every sort, and to the beasts of the field to be devoured.


###### Ezekiel 39:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/39:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/39:5)


עַל־פְּנֵ֥י הַשָּׂדֶ֖ה תִּפּ֑וֹל כִּ֚י אֲנִ֣י דִבַּ֔רְתִּי נְאֻ֖ם אֲדֹנָ֥י יֱהֹוִֽה׃


Thou shalt fall upon the open field: for I have spoken it, says the Lord God.


###### Ezekiel 39:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/39:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/39:6)


וְשִׁלַּחְתִּי־אֵ֣שׁ בְּמָג֔וֹג וּבְיֹשְׁבֵ֥י הָאִיִּ֖ים לָבֶ֑טַח וְיָדְע֖וּ כִּי־אֲנִ֥י יְהֹוָֽה׃


And I will send a fire on Magog, and among them that dwell securely in the coastlines: and they shall know that I am the Lord.


###### Ezekiel 39:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/39:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/39:7)


וְאֶת־שֵׁ֨ם קׇדְשִׁ֜י אוֹדִ֗יעַ בְּתוֹךְ֙ עַמִּ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל וְלֹא־אַחֵ֥ל אֶת־שֵׁם־קׇדְשִׁ֖י ע֑וֹד וְיָדְע֤וּ הַגּוֹיִם֙ כִּֽי־אֲנִ֣י יְהֹוָ֔ה קָד֖וֹשׁ בְּיִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


So will I make my holy name known in the midst of my people Yisra᾽el; and I will not allow my holy name to be profaned any more: and the nations shall know that I am the Lord, the Holy One in Yisra᾽el.


###### Ezekiel 39:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/39:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/39:8)


הִנֵּ֤ה בָאָה֙ וְנִֽהְיָ֔תָה נְאֻ֖ם אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֑ה ה֥וּא הַיּ֖וֹם אֲשֶׁ֥ר דִּבַּֽרְתִּי׃


Behold, it comes, and it is accomplished, says the Lord God; this is the day of which I have spoken.


###### Ezekiel 39:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/39:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/39:9)


וְֽיָצְא֞וּ יֹשְׁבֵ֣י**׀** עָרֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֗ל וּבִעֲר֡וּ וְ֠הִשִּׂ֠יקוּ בְּנֶ֨שֶׁק וּמָגֵ֤ן וְצִנָּה֙ בְּקֶ֣שֶׁת וּבְחִצִּ֔ים וּבְמַקֵּ֥ל יָ֖ד וּבְרֹ֑מַח וּבִעֲר֥וּ בָהֶ֛ם אֵ֖שׁ שֶׁ֥בַע שָׁנִֽים׃


And they that dwell in the cities of Yisra᾽el shall go forth, and shall set fire to the weapons, and burn them, both shield and buckler, bow and arrows, and the staves, and the spears, and they shall make fires with them for seven years.


###### Ezekiel 39:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/39:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/39:10)


וְלֹא־יִשְׂא֨וּ עֵצִ֜ים מִן־הַשָּׂדֶ֗ה וְלֹ֤א יַחְטְבוּ֙ מִן־הַיְּעָרִ֔ים כִּ֥י בַנֶּ֖שֶׁק יְבַעֲרוּ־אֵ֑שׁ וְשָׁלְל֣וּ אֶת־שֹׁלְלֵיהֶ֗ם וּבָֽזְזוּ֙ אֶת־בֹּ֣זְזֵיהֶ֔ם נְאֻ֖ם אֲדֹנָ֥י יֱהֹוִֽה׃{ס}


So that they shall take no wood out of the field, nor cut down any out of the forests; for they shall make fire with the weapons: and they shall spoil those that spoiled them, and rob those that robbed them, says the Lord God.


###### Ezekiel 39:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/39:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/39:11)


וְהָיָ֣ה בַיּ֣וֹם הַה֡וּא אֶתֵּ֣ן לְגוֹג֩׀מְקֽוֹם־שָׁ֨ם קֶ֜בֶר בְּיִשְׂרָאֵ֗ל גֵּ֤י הָעֹֽבְרִים֙ קִדְמַ֣ת הַיָּ֔ם וְחֹסֶ֥מֶת הִ֖יא אֶת־הָעֹֽבְרִ֑ים וְקָ֣בְרוּ שָׁ֗ם אֶת־גּוֹג֙ וְאֶת־כׇּל־הֲמוֹנֹ֔ה וְקָ֣רְא֔וּ גֵּ֖יא הֲמ֥וֹן גּֽוֹג׃


And it shall come to pass on that day, that I will give to Gog a place for burial in Yisra᾽el, the valley of those who travel to the east of the sea: and it shall block the path of the travellers: and there shall they bury Gog and all his multitude: and they shall call it The Valley of Hamon-gog (the Multitude of Gog).


###### Ezekiel 39:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/39:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/39:12)


וּקְבָרוּם֙ בֵּ֣ית יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל לְמַ֖עַן טַהֵ֣ר אֶת־הָאָ֑רֶץ שִׁבְעָ֖ה חֳדָשִֽׁים׃


And seven months shall the house of Yisra᾽el be burying them, that they may cleanse the land.


###### Ezekiel 39:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/39:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/39:13)


וְקָֽבְרוּ֙ כׇּל־עַ֣ם הָאָ֔רֶץ וְהָיָ֥ה לָהֶ֖ם לְשֵׁ֑ם י֚וֹם הִכָּ֣בְדִ֔י נְאֻ֖ם אֲדֹנָ֥י יֱהֹוִֽה׃


And all the people of the land shall bury them; and it shall be to them a renown on the day that I shall be glorified, says the Lord God.


###### Ezekiel 39:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/39:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/39:14)


וְאַנְשֵׁ֨י תָמִ֤יד יַבְדִּ֙ילוּ֙ עֹבְרִ֣ים בָּאָ֔רֶץ מְקַבְּרִ֣ים אֶת־הָעֹבְרִ֗ים אֶת־הַנּוֹתָרִ֛ים עַל־פְּנֵ֥י הָאָ֖רֶץ לְטַהֲרָ֑הּ מִקְצֵ֥ה שִׁבְעָה־חֳדָשִׁ֖ים יַחְקֹֽרוּ׃


And permanent officers shall despatch men to range the land and with these rangers they shall bury those that remain above the earth, to cleanse it: after the end of seven months they will make their search.


###### Ezekiel 39:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/39:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/39:15)


וְעָבְר֤וּ הָעֹֽבְרִים֙ בָּאָ֔רֶץ וְרָאָה֙ עֶ֣צֶם אָדָ֔ם וּבָנָ֥ה אֶצְל֖וֹ צִיּ֑וּן עַ֣ד קָבְר֤וּ אֹתוֹ֙ הַֽמְקַבְּרִ֔ים אֶל־גֵּ֖יא הֲמ֥וֹן גּֽוֹג׃


And the rangers that pass through the land, when any sees a human bone, then shall he set up a sign by it, till the buriers have buried it in the valley of Hamon-gog.


###### Ezekiel 39:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/39:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/39:16)


וְגַ֥ם שֶׁם־עִ֛יר הֲמוֹנָ֖הֿ וְטִהֲר֥וּ הָאָֽרֶץ׃{פ}


And also the name of the city shall be Hamona. Thus shall they cleanse the land.


###### Ezekiel 39:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/39:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/39:17)


וְאַתָּ֨ה בֶן־אָדָ֜ם כֹּה־אָמַ֣ר**׀** אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֗ה אֱמֹר֩ לְצִפּ֨וֹר כׇּל־כָּנָ֜ף וּלְכֹ֣ל**׀** חַיַּ֣ת הַשָּׂדֶ֗ה הִקָּבְצ֤וּ וָבֹ֙אוּ֙ הֵאָסְפ֣וּ מִסָּבִ֔יב עַל־זִבְחִ֗י אֲשֶׁ֨ר אֲנִ֜י זֹבֵ֤חַ לָכֶם֙ זֶ֣בַח גָּד֔וֹל עַ֖ל הָרֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל וַאֲכַלְתֶּ֥ם בָּשָׂ֖ר וּשְׁתִ֥יתֶם דָּֽם׃


And, thou son of man, thus says the Lord God; Speak to every feathered bird, and to every beast of the field, Assemble yourselves, and come; gather yourselves on every side to my festive meal that I prepare for you, a great feast upon the mountains of Yisra᾽el, that you may eat meat, and drink blood.


###### Ezekiel 39:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/39:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/39:18)


בְּשַׂ֤ר גִּבּוֹרִים֙ תֹּאכֵ֔לוּ וְדַם־נְשִׂיאֵ֥י הָאָ֖רֶץ תִּשְׁתּ֑וּ אֵילִ֨ים כָּרִ֤ים וְעַתּוּדִים֙ פָּרִ֔ים מְרִיאֵ֥י בָשָׁ֖ן כֻּלָּֽם׃


You shall eat the flesh of the mighty, and drink the blood of the princes of the earth, of rams, of lambs, and of goats, of bullocks, all of them fatlings of Bashan.


###### Ezekiel 39:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/39:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/39:19)


וַאֲכַלְתֶּם־חֵ֣לֶב לְשׇׂבְעָ֔ה וּשְׁתִ֥יתֶם דָּ֖ם לְשִׁכָּר֑וֹן מִזִּבְחִ֖י אֲשֶׁר־זָבַ֥חְתִּי לָכֶֽם׃


And you shall eat fat till you are full, and drink blood till you are drunken, of my feast which I have prepared for you.


###### Ezekiel 39:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/39:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/39:20)


וּשְׂבַעְתֶּ֤ם עַל־שֻׁלְחָנִי֙ ס֣וּס וָרֶ֔כֶב גִּבּ֖וֹר וְכׇל־אִ֣ישׁ מִלְחָמָ֑ה נְאֻ֖ם אֲדֹנָ֥י יֱהֹוִֽה׃


Thus you shall be filled at my table with horses and chariots, with mighty men, and with all men of war, says the Lord God.


###### Ezekiel 39:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/39:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/39:21)


וְנָתַתִּ֥י אֶת־כְּבוֹדִ֖י בַּגּוֹיִ֑ם וְרָא֣וּ כׇל־הַגּוֹיִ֗ם אֶת־מִשְׁפָּטִי֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר עָשִׂ֔יתִי וְאֶת־יָדִ֖י אֲשֶׁר־שַׂ֥מְתִּי בָהֶֽם׃


And I will set my glory among the nations, and all the nations shall see my judgment that I have executed, and my hand that I have laid upon them.


###### Ezekiel 39:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/39:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/39:22)


וְיָֽדְעוּ֙ בֵּ֣ית יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל כִּ֛י אֲנִ֥י יְהֹוָ֖ה אֱלֹהֵיהֶ֑ם מִן־הַיּ֥וֹם הַה֖וּא וָהָֽלְאָה׃


So the house of Yisra᾽el shall know that I am the Lord their God from that day onwards.


###### Ezekiel 39:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/39:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/39:23)


וְיָדְע֣וּ הַ֠גּוֹיִ֠ם כִּ֣י בַעֲוֺנָ֞ם גָּל֣וּ בֵֽית־יִשְׂרָאֵ֗ל עַ֚ל אֲשֶׁ֣ר מָעֲלוּ־בִ֔י וָאַסְתִּ֥ר פָּנַ֖י מֵהֶ֑ם וָֽאֶתְּנֵם֙ בְּיַ֣ד צָרֵיהֶ֔ם וַיִּפְּל֥וּ בַחֶ֖רֶב כֻּלָּֽם׃


And the nations shall know that the house of Yisra᾽el went into exile for their iniquity: because they broke faith with me, therefore I hid my face from them, and gave them into the hand of their enemies: so they fell by the sword all of them.


###### Ezekiel 39:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/39:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/39:24)


כְּטֻמְאָתָ֥ם וּכְפִשְׁעֵיהֶ֖ם עָשִׂ֣יתִי אֹתָ֑ם וָאַסְתִּ֥ר פָּנַ֖י מֵהֶֽם׃{ס}


According to their uncleanness and according to their transgressions have I done to them, and I hid my face from them.


###### Ezekiel 39:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/39:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/39:25)


לָכֵ֗ן כֹּ֤ה אָמַר֙ אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֔ה עַתָּ֗ה אָשִׁיב֙ אֶת־(שבית) [שְׁב֣וּת] יַעֲקֹ֔ב וְרִחַמְתִּ֖י כׇּל־בֵּ֣ית יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל וְקִנֵּאתִ֖י לְשֵׁ֥ם קׇדְשִֽׁי׃


Therefore thus says the Lord God; Now will I bring back the captivity of Ya῾aqov, and have mercy upon the whole house of Yisra᾽el, and will be zealous for my holy name;


###### Ezekiel 39:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/39:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/39:26)


וְנָשׂוּ֙ אֶת־כְּלִמָּתָ֔ם וְאֶת־כׇּל־מַעֲלָ֖ם אֲשֶׁ֣ר מָעֲלוּ־בִ֑י בְּשִׁבְתָּ֧ם עַל־אַדְמָתָ֛ם לָבֶ֖טַח וְאֵ֥ין מַחֲרִֽיד׃


and they will be quit of their shame, and of all their faithlessness in which they have been faithless to me, when they dwell securely on their land, with none to make them afraid.


###### Ezekiel 39:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/39:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/39:27)


בְּשׁוֹבְבִ֤י אוֹתָם֙ מִן־הָ֣עַמִּ֔ים וְקִבַּצְתִּ֣י אֹתָ֔ם מֵאַרְצ֖וֹת אֹיְבֵיהֶ֑ם וְנִקְדַּ֣שְׁתִּי בָ֔ם לְעֵינֵ֖י הַגּוֹיִ֥ם רַבִּֽים׃


When I have brought them back from the peoples, and gathered them out of their enemies’ lands, and am sanctified in them in the sight of many nations;


###### Ezekiel 39:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/39:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/39:28)


וְיָדְע֗וּ כִּ֣י אֲנִ֤י יְהֹוָה֙ אֱלֹ֣הֵיהֶ֔ם בְּהַגְלוֹתִ֤י אֹתָם֙ אֶל־הַגּוֹיִ֔ם וְכִנַּסְתִּ֖ים עַל־אַדְמָתָ֑ם וְלֹא־אוֹתִ֥יר ע֛וֹד מֵהֶ֖ם שָֽׁם׃


then shall they know that I am the Lord their God, who caused them to be led into exile among the nations: but I have gathered them to their own land, and have left none of them there any more.


###### Ezekiel 39:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/39:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/39:29)


וְלֹא־אַסְתִּ֥יר ע֛וֹד פָּנַ֖י מֵהֶ֑ם אֲשֶׁ֨ר שָׁפַ֤כְתִּי אֶת־רוּחִי֙ עַל־בֵּ֣ית יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל נְאֻ֖ם אֲדֹנָ֥י יֱהֹוִֽה׃{פ}


Nor will I hide my face any more from them: for I have poured out my spirit upon the house of Yisra᾽el, says the Lord God.



## Chapter 40

###### Ezekiel 40:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/40:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/40:1)


בְּעֶשְׂרִ֣ים וְחָמֵ֣שׁ שָׁנָ֣ה לְ֠גָלוּתֵ֠נוּ בְּרֹ֨אשׁ הַשָּׁנָ֜ה בֶּעָשׂ֣וֹר לַחֹ֗דֶשׁ בְּאַרְבַּ֤ע עֶשְׂרֵה֙ שָׁנָ֔ה אַחַ֕ר אֲשֶׁ֥ר הֻכְּתָ֖ה הָעִ֑יר בְּעֶ֣צֶם**׀** הַיּ֣וֹם הַזֶּ֗ה הָיְתָ֤ה עָלַי֙ יַד־יְהֹוָ֔ה וַיָּבֵ֥א אֹתִ֖י שָֽׁמָּה׃


In the twenty-fifth year of our exile, in the beginning of the year, on the tenth day of the month, in the fourteenth year after the city was smitten, on that very day the hand of the Lord was upon me, and brought me there.


###### Ezekiel 40:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/40:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/40:2)


בְּמַרְא֣וֹת אֱלֹהִ֔ים הֱבִיאַ֖נִי אֶל־אֶ֣רֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל וַיְנִיחֵ֗נִי אֶל־הַ֤ר גָּבֹ֙הַּ֙ מְאֹ֔ד וְעָלָ֥יו כְּמִבְנֵה־עִ֖יר מִנֶּֽגֶב׃


In the visions of God he brought me into the land of Yisra᾽el, and set me upon a very high mountain, upon which was something like the structure of a city to the south.


###### Ezekiel 40:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/40:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/40:3)


וַיָּבֵ֨יא אוֹתִ֜י שָׁ֗מָּה וְהִנֵּה־אִישׁ֙ מַרְאֵ֙הוּ֙ כְּמַרְאֵ֣ה נְחֹ֔שֶׁת וּפְתִיל־פִּשְׁתִּ֥ים בְּיָד֖וֹ וּקְנֵ֣ה הַמִּדָּ֑ה וְה֥וּא עֹמֵ֖ד בַּשָּֽׁעַר׃


And he brought me there, and, behold, there was a man, whose appearance was like the appearance of brass, with a thread of flax in his hand, and a measuring reed; and he stood at the gate.


###### Ezekiel 40:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/40:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/40:4)


וַיְדַבֵּ֨ר אֵלַ֜י הָאִ֗ישׁ בֶּן־אָדָ֡ם רְאֵ֣ה בְעֵינֶ֩יךָ֩ וּבְאׇזְנֶ֨יךָ שְּׁמָ֜ע וְשִׂ֣ים לִבְּךָ֗ לְכֹ֤ל אֲשֶׁר־אֲנִי֙ מַרְאֶ֣ה אוֹתָ֔ךְ כִּ֛י לְמַ֥עַן הַרְאוֹתְכָ֖ה הֻבָ֣אתָה הֵ֑נָּה הַגֵּ֛ד אֶת־כׇּל־אֲשֶׁר־אַתָּ֥ה רֹאֶ֖ה לְבֵ֥ית יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


And the man said to me, Son of man, behold with thy eyes, and hear with thy ears, and put thy mind to all that I shall show thee; for thou wast brought here, in order that they might be shown to thee: declare all that thou seest to the house of Yisra᾽el.


###### Ezekiel 40:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/40:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/40:5)


וְהִנֵּ֥ה חוֹמָ֛ה מִח֥וּץ לַבַּ֖יִת סָבִ֣יב׀סָבִ֑יב וּבְיַ֨ד הָאִ֜ישׁ קְנֵ֣ה הַמִּדָּ֗ה שֵׁשׁ־אַמּ֤וֹת בָּֽאַמָּה֙ וָטֹ֔פַח וַיָּ֜מׇד אֶת־רֹ֤חַב הַבִּנְיָן֙ קָנֶ֣ה אֶחָ֔ד וְקוֹמָ֖ה קָנֶ֥ה אֶחָֽד׃


And behold a wall on the outside of the house round about, and in the man’s hand a measuring reed of six cubits long, of a cubit and a hand breadth each: so he measured the breadth of the building, one reed; and the height, one reed.


###### Ezekiel 40:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/40:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/40:6)


וַיָּב֗וֹא אֶל־שַׁ֙עַר֙ אֲשֶׁ֤ר פָּנָיו֙ דֶּ֣רֶךְ הַקָּדִ֔ימָה וַיַּ֖עַל בְּמַעֲלוֹתָ֑ו וַיָּ֣מׇד**׀** אֶת־סַ֣ף הַשַּׁ֗עַר קָנֶ֤ה אֶחָד֙ רֹ֔חַב וְאֵת֙ סַ֣ף אֶחָ֔ד קָנֶ֥ה אֶחָ֖ד רֹֽחַב׃


Then he came to the gate which looks toward the east, and went up by its stairs, and measured the threshold of the gate, which was one reed broad; and the other threshold of the gate, which was one reed broad.


###### Ezekiel 40:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/40:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/40:7)


וְהַתָּ֗א קָנֶ֨ה אֶחָ֥ד אֹ֙רֶךְ֙ וְקָנֶ֤ה אֶחָד֙ רֹ֔חַב וּבֵ֥ין הַתָּאִ֖ים חָמֵ֣שׁ אַמּ֑וֹת וְסַ֣ף הַ֠שַּׁ֠עַר מֵאֵ֨צֶל אֻלָ֥ם הַשַּׁ֛עַר מֵהַבַּ֖יִת קָנֶ֥ה אֶחָֽד׃


And every little chamber was one reed long, and one reed broad; and the space between the little chambers was five cubits; and the threshold of the gate by the porch of the gate within was one reed.


###### Ezekiel 40:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/40:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/40:8)


וַיָּ֜מׇד אֶת־אֻלָ֥ם הַשַּׁ֛עַר מֵהַבַּ֖יִת קָנֶ֥ה אֶחָֽד׃


He measured also the porch of the gate from within, one reed.


###### Ezekiel 40:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/40:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/40:9)


וַיָּ֜מׇד אֶת־אֻלָ֤ם הַשַּׁ֙עַר֙ שְׁמֹנֶ֣ה אַמּ֔וֹת וְאֵילָ֖ו שְׁתַּ֣יִם אַמּ֑וֹת וְאֻלָ֥ם הַשַּׁ֖עַר מֵהַבָּֽיִת׃


Then he measured the porch of the gate, eight cubits; and its posts two cubits; and the porch of the gate was on the inside.


###### Ezekiel 40:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/40:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/40:10)


וְתָאֵ֨י הַשַּׁ֜עַר דֶּ֣רֶךְ הַקָּדִ֗ים שְׁלֹשָׁ֤ה מִפֹּה֙ וּשְׁלֹשָׁ֣ה מִפֹּ֔ה מִדָּ֥ה אַחַ֖ת לִשְׁלׇשְׁתָּ֑ם וּמִדָּ֥ה אַחַ֛ת לָאֵילִ֖ם מִפֹּ֥ה וּמִפּֽוֹ׃


And the little chambers of the gate eastward were three on this side, and three on that side; they three were of one measure: and the posts had one measure on this side and on that side.


###### Ezekiel 40:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/40:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/40:11)


וַיָּ֛מׇד אֶת־רֹ֥חַב פֶּתַח־הַשַּׁ֖עַר עֶ֣שֶׂר אַמּ֑וֹת אֹ֣רֶךְ הַשַּׁ֔עַר שְׁל֥וֹשׁ עֶשְׂרֵ֖ה אַמּֽוֹת׃


And he measured the breadth of the entry of the gate, ten cubits; and the length of the gate, thirteen cubits.


###### Ezekiel 40:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/40:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/40:12)


וּגְב֞וּל לִפְנֵ֤י הַתָּאוֹת֙ אַמָּ֣ה אֶחָ֔ת וְאַמָּֽה־אַחַ֥ת גְּב֖וּל מִפֹּ֑ה וְהַתָּ֕א שֵׁשׁ־אַמּ֣וֹת מִפּ֔וֹ וְשֵׁ֥שׁ אַמּ֖וֹת מִפּֽוֹ׃


And there was a border of one cubit before the little chambers on this side, and a border, one cubit on that side: and the little chambers were six cubits on this side, and six cubits on that side.


###### Ezekiel 40:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/40:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/40:13)


וַיָּ֣מׇד אֶת־הַשַּׁ֗עַר מִגַּ֤ג הַתָּא֙ לְגַגּ֔וֹ רֹ֕חַב עֶשְׂרִ֥ים וְחָמֵ֖שׁ אַמּ֑וֹת פֶּ֖תַח נֶ֥גֶד פָּֽתַח׃


And he measured the gate from the roof of one little chamber to the roof of another: the breadth was twenty five cubits, from door to door.


###### Ezekiel 40:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/40:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/40:14)


וַיַּ֥עַשׂ אֶת־אֵילִ֖ים שִׁשִּׁ֣ים אַמָּ֑ה וְאֶל־אֵיל֙ הֶחָצֵ֔ר הַשַּׁ֖עַר סָבִ֥יב׀סָבִֽיב׃


And he made his measure of the posts, sixty cubits; also for the posts of the courts, round about the gate.


###### Ezekiel 40:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/40:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/40:15)


וְעַ֗ל פְּנֵי֙ הַשַּׁ֣עַר (היאתון) [הָאִית֔וֹן] עַל־לִפְנֵ֕י אֻלָ֥ם הַשַּׁ֖עַר הַפְּנִימִ֑י חֲמִשִּׁ֖ים אַמָּֽה׃


And from the forefront of the gate of the entrance to the front of the inner porch of the gate was a space of fifty cubits.


###### Ezekiel 40:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/40:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/40:16)


וְחַלּוֹנ֣וֹת אֲטֻמ֣וֹת אֶֽל־הַתָּאִ֡ים וְאֶל֩ אֵלֵיהֵ֨מָה לִפְנִ֤ימָה לַשַּׁ֙עַר֙ סָבִ֣יב׀סָבִ֔יב וְכֵ֖ן לָאֵֽלַמּ֑וֹת וְחַלּוֹנ֞וֹת סָבִ֤יב׀סָבִיב֙ לִפְנִ֔ימָה וְאֶל־אַ֖יִל תִּמֹרִֽים׃


And there were narrow windows to the little chambers, and to their posts within the gate round about, and likewise to the porches; and windows were round about inward: and upon each post were palm trees.


###### Ezekiel 40:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/40:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/40:17)


וַיְבִיאֵ֗נִי אֶל־הֶחָצֵר֙ הַחִ֣יצוֹנָ֔ה וְהִנֵּ֤ה לְשָׁכוֹת֙ וְרִֽצְפָ֔ה עָשׂ֥וּי לֶחָצֵ֖ר סָבִ֣יב׀סָבִ֑יב שְׁלֹשִׁ֥ים לְשָׁכ֖וֹת אֶל־הָרִֽצְפָֽה׃


Then he brought me into the outer court, and, lo, there were chambers, and a pavement made for the court round about: thirty chambers were upon the pavement.


###### Ezekiel 40:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/40:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/40:18)


וְהָרִֽצְפָה֙ אֶל־כֶּ֣תֶף הַשְּׁעָרִ֔ים לְעֻמַּ֖ת אֹ֣רֶךְ הַשְּׁעָרִ֑ים הָרִֽצְפָ֖ה הַתַּחְתּוֹנָֽה׃


And the pavement was by the side of the gates corresponding to the length of the gates; that was the lower pavement.


###### Ezekiel 40:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/40:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/40:19)


וַיָּ֣מׇד רֹ֡חַב מִלִּפְנֵי֩ הַשַּׁ֨עַר הַתַּחְתּ֜וֹנָה לִפְנֵ֨י הֶחָצֵ֧ר הַפְּנִימִ֛י מִח֖וּץ מֵאָ֣ה אַמָּ֑ה הַקָּדִ֖ים וְהַצָּפֽוֹן׃


Then he measured the breadth from the front of the lower gate to the front of the inner court without, a hundred cubits both eastward and northward.


###### Ezekiel 40:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/40:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/40:20)
