וַיְבִאֵ֘נִי֮ אֶל־אֻלָ֣ם הַבַּ֒יִת֒ וַיָּ֙מָד֙ אֵ֣ל אֻלָ֔ם חָמֵ֤שׁ אַמּוֹת֙ מִפֹּ֔ה וְחָמֵ֥שׁ אַמּ֖וֹת מִפֹּ֑ה וְרֹ֣חַב הַשַּׁ֔עַר שָׁלֹ֤שׁ אַמּוֹת֙ מִפּ֔וֹ וְשָׁלֹ֥שׁ אַמּ֖וֹת מִפּֽוֹ׃


And he brought me to the porch of the house, and measured each post of the porch, five cubits on this side, and five cubits on that side: and the breadth of the gate was three cubits on this side, and three cubits on that side.


###### Ezekiel 40:49

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/40:49](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/40:49)


אֹ֣רֶךְ הָאֻלָ֞ם עֶשְׂרִ֣ים אַמָּ֗ה וְרֹ֙חַב֙ עַשְׁתֵּ֣י עֶשְׂרֵ֣ה אַמָּ֔ה וּבַֽמַּעֲל֔וֹת אֲשֶׁ֥ר יַעֲל֖וּ אֵלָ֑יו וְעַמֻּדִים֙ אֶל־הָ֣אֵילִ֔ים אֶחָ֥ד מִפֹּ֖ה וְאֶחָ֥ד מִפֹּֽה׃


The length of the porch was twenty cubits, and the breadth eleven cubits; and it was ascended by steps: and there were pillars by the posts, one on this side, and another on that side.



## Chapter 41

###### Ezekiel 41:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/41:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/41:1)


וַיְבִיאֵ֖נִי אֶל־הַהֵיכָ֑ל וַיָּ֣מׇד אֶת־הָאֵילִ֗ים שֵׁשׁ־אַמּ֨וֹת רֹ֧חַב מִפּ֛וֹ וְשֵׁשׁ־אַמּֽוֹת־רֹ֥חַב מִפּ֖וֹ רֹ֥חַב הָאֹֽהֶל׃


Then he brought me to the temple, and measured the posts, six cubits broad on the one side, and six cubits broad on the other side, which was the breadth of the tent.


###### Ezekiel 41:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/41:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/41:2)


וְרֹ֣חַב הַפֶּ֘תַח֮ עֶ֣שֶׂר אַמּוֹת֒ וְכִתְפ֣וֹת הַפֶּ֔תַח חָמֵ֤שׁ אַמּוֹת֙ מִפּ֔וֹ וְחָמֵ֥שׁ אַמּ֖וֹת מִפּ֑וֹ וַיָּ֤מׇד אׇרְכּוֹ֙ אַרְבָּעִ֣ים אַמָּ֔ה וְרֹ֖חַב עֶשְׂרִ֥ים אַמָּֽה׃


And the breadth of the door was ten cubits; and the sides of the door were five cubits on the one side, and five cubits on the other side: and he measured its length, forty cubits: and the breadth, twenty cubits.


###### Ezekiel 41:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/41:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/41:3)


וּבָ֣א לִפְנִ֔ימָה וַיָּ֥מׇד אֵֽיל־הַפֶּ֖תַח שְׁתַּ֣יִם אַמּ֑וֹת וְהַפֶּ֙תַח֙ שֵׁ֣שׁ אַמּ֔וֹת וְרֹ֥חַב הַפֶּ֖תַח שֶׁ֥בַע אַמּֽוֹת׃


Then he came to the interior, and measured the post of the door, two cubits; and the door, six cubits; and the breadth of the door, seven cubits.


###### Ezekiel 41:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/41:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/41:4)


וַיָּ֨מׇד אֶת־אׇרְכּ֜וֹ עֶשְׂרִ֣ים אַמָּ֗ה וְרֹ֛חַב עֶשְׂרִ֥ים אַמָּ֖ה אֶל־פְּנֵ֣י הַהֵיכָ֑ל וַיֹּ֣אמֶר אֵלַ֔י זֶ֖ה קֹ֥דֶשׁ הַקֳּדָשִֽׁים׃


So he measured its length, twenty cubits; and the breadth, twenty cubits, according to the breadth of the temple: and he said to me, This is the most holy place.


###### Ezekiel 41:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/41:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/41:5)


וַיָּ֥מׇד קִֽיר־הַבַּ֖יִת שֵׁ֣שׁ אַמּ֑וֹת וְרֹ֣חַב הַצֵּלָע֩ אַרְבַּ֨ע אַמּ֜וֹת סָבִ֧יב׀סָבִ֛יב לַבַּ֖יִת סָבִֽיב׃


Then he measured the wall of the house, six cubits; and the breadth of every side chamber, four cubits, round about the house on every side.


###### Ezekiel 41:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/41:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/41:6)


וְהַצְּלָעוֹת֩ צֵלָ֨ע אֶל־צֵלָ֜ע שָׁל֧וֹשׁ וּשְׁלֹשִׁ֣ים פְּעָמִ֗ים וּ֠בָא֠וֹת בַּקִּ֨יר אֲשֶׁר־לַבַּ֧יִת לַצְּלָע֛וֹת סָבִ֥יב׀סָבִ֖יב לִהְי֣וֹת אֲחוּזִ֑ים וְלֹֽא־יִהְי֥וּ אֲחוּזִ֖ים בְּקִ֥יר הַבָּֽיִת׃


And the side chambers were one over another thirty three times; and there were recesses in the wall of the house for the side chambers round about, that they might have hold, so that they were not fastened into the wall of the house.


###### Ezekiel 41:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/41:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/41:7)


וְֽרָחֲבָ֡ה וְֽנָסְבָה֩ לְמַ֨עְלָה לְמַ֜עְלָה לַצְּלָע֗וֹת כִּ֣י מֽוּסַב־הַ֠בַּ֠יִת לְמַ֨עְלָה לְמַ֜עְלָה סָבִ֤יב׀סָבִיב֙ לַבַּ֔יִת עַל־כֵּ֥ן רֹחַב־לַבַּ֖יִת לְמָ֑עְלָה וְכֵ֧ן הַתַּחְתּוֹנָ֛ה יַעֲלֶ֥ה עַל־הָעֶלְיוֹנָ֖ה לַתִּיכוֹנָֽה׃


And the side chambers were broader as one circled higher and higher: for the winding about of the house went still upward round about the house: therefore the breadth let in to the house continued upward; and thus one ascended from the lowest level to the topmost level by the middle.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/41:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/41:8)


וְרָאִ֧יתִי לַבַּ֛יִת גֹּ֖בַהּ סָבִ֣יב׀סָבִ֑יב (מיסדות) [מוּסְד֤וֹת] הַצְּלָעוֹת֙ מְל֣וֹ הַקָּנֶ֔ה שֵׁ֥שׁ אַמּ֖וֹת אַצִּֽילָה׃

###### Ezekiel 41:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/41:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/41:11)


וּפֶ֤תַח הַצֵּלָע֙ לַמֻּנָּ֔ח פֶּ֤תַח אֶחָד֙ דֶּ֣רֶךְ הַצָּפ֔וֹן וּפֶ֥תַח אֶחָ֖ד לַדָּר֑וֹם וְרֹ֙חַב֙ מְק֣וֹם הַמֻּנָּ֔ח חָמֵ֥שׁ אַמּ֖וֹת סָבִ֥יב׀סָבִֽיב׃

וְהַבִּנְיָ֡ן אֲשֶׁר֩ אֶל־פְּנֵ֨י הַגִּזְרָ֜ה פְּאַ֣ת דֶּֽרֶךְ־הַיָּ֗ם רֹ֚חַב שִׁבְעִ֣ים אַמָּ֔ה וְקִ֧יר הַבִּנְיָ֛ן חָמֵשׁ־אַמּ֥וֹת רֹ֖חַב סָבִ֣יב׀סָבִ֑יב וְאׇרְכּ֖וֹ תִּשְׁעִ֥ים אַמָּֽה׃

וּמָדַ֣ד אֶת־הַבַּ֔יִת אֹ֖רֶךְ מֵאָ֣ה אַמָּ֑ה וְהַגִּזְרָ֤ה וְהַבִּנְיָה֙ וְקִ֣ירוֹתֶ֔יהָ אֹ֖רֶךְ מֵאָ֥ה אַמָּֽה׃

also the breadth of the front of the house and of the main wing towards the east, a hundred cubits.


###### Ezekiel 41:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/41:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/41:15)


וּמָדַ֣ד אֹֽרֶךְ־הַ֠בִּנְיָ֠ן אֶל־פְּנֵ֨י הַגִּזְרָ֜ה אֲשֶׁ֨ר עַל־אַחֲרֶ֧יהָ (ואתוקיהא) [וְאַתִּיקֶ֛יהָא] מִפּ֥וֹ וּמִפּ֖וֹ מֵאָ֣ה אַמָּ֑ה וְהַהֵיכָל֙ הַפְּנִימִ֔י וְאֻלַמֵּ֖י הֶחָצֵֽר׃

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/41:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/41:16)


הַסִּפִּ֡ים וְהַחַלּוֹנִ֣ים הָ֠אֲטֻמ֠וֹת וְהָאַתִּיקִ֤ים׀סָבִיב֙ לִשְׁלׇשְׁתָּ֔ם נֶ֧גֶד הַסַּ֛ף שְׂחִ֥יף עֵ֖ץ סָבִ֣יב׀סָבִ֑יב וְהָאָ֙רֶץ֙ עַד־הַֽחַלּוֹנ֔וֹת וְהַחַלֹּנ֖וֹת מְכֻסּֽוֹת׃


the thresholds, and the narrow windows, and the galleries were round about those three: opposite the threshold there was a wooden panelling all round, so the space from the ground up to the windows, and the windows were covered;

וְעָשׂ֥וּי כְּרוּבִ֖ים וְתִמֹרִ֑ים וְתִֽמֹרָה֙ בֵּין־כְּר֣וּב לִכְר֔וּב וּשְׁנַ֥יִם פָּנִ֖ים לַכְּרֽוּב׃


And it was made with keruvim and palm trees, so that a palm tree was between one keruv and another; and every keruv had two faces:


###### Ezekiel 41:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/41:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/41:19)


וּפְנֵ֨י אָדָ֤ם אֶל־הַתִּֽמֹרָה֙ מִפּ֔וֹ וּפְנֵי־כְפִ֥יר אֶל־הַתִּמֹרָ֖ה מִפּ֑וֹ עָשׂ֥וּי אֶל־כׇּל־הַבַּ֖יִת סָבִ֥יב׀סָבִֽיב׃


so that the face of a man was towards the palm tree on the one side, and the face of a young lion towards the palm tree on the other side: thus was it made through all the house round about.

הַמִּזְבֵּ֡חַ עֵ֣ץ שָׁלוֹשׁ֩ אַמּ֨וֹת גָּבֹ֜הַּ וְאׇרְכּ֣וֹ שְׁתַּֽיִם־אַמּ֗וֹת וּמִקְצֹֽעוֹתָיו֙ ל֔וֹ וְאׇרְכּ֥וֹ וְקִירֹתָ֖יו עֵ֑ץ וַיְדַבֵּ֣ר אֵלַ֔י זֶ֚ה הַשֻּׁלְחָ֔ן אֲשֶׁ֖ר לִפְנֵ֥י יְהֹוָֽה׃

###### Ezekiel 41:23

וּשְׁתַּ֧יִם דְּלָת֛וֹת לַהֵיכָ֖ל וְלַקֹּֽדֶשׁ׃


And the temple and the sanctuary had two doors.


###### Ezekiel 41:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/41:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/41:24)


וּשְׁתַּ֥יִם דְּלָת֖וֹת לַדְּלָת֑וֹת שְׁ֚תַּיִם מוּסַבּ֣וֹת דְּלָת֔וֹת שְׁ֚תַּיִם לְדֶ֣לֶת אֶחָ֔ת וּשְׁתֵּ֥י דְלָת֖וֹת לָאַחֶֽרֶת׃


And the doors had two leaves each, two turning leaves; two leaves for the one door, and two leaves for the other door.


###### Ezekiel 41:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/41:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/41:25)


וַעֲשׂוּיָ֨ה אֲלֵיהֶ֜ן אֶל־דַּלְת֤וֹת הַהֵיכָל֙ כְּרוּבִ֣ים וְתִמֹרִ֔ים כַּאֲשֶׁ֥ר עֲשׂוּיִ֖ם לַקִּיר֑וֹת וְעָ֥ב עֵ֛ץ אֶל־פְּנֵ֥י הָאוּלָ֖ם מֵהַחֽוּץ׃

###### Ezekiel 41:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/41:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/41:26)


וְחַלּוֹנִ֨ים אֲטֻמ֤וֹת וְתִֽמֹרִים֙ מִפּ֣וֹ וּמִפּ֔וֹ אֶל־כִּתְפ֖וֹת הָאוּלָ֑ם וְצַלְע֥וֹת הַבַּ֖יִת וְהָעֻבִּֽים׃


And there were narrow windows and palm trees on the one side and on the other side, on the sides of the porch, and upon the side chambers of the house, and on the thick planks.



## Chapter 42

###### Ezekiel 42:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/42:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/42:1)


וַיּוֹצִאֵ֗נִי אֶל־הֶחָצֵר֙ הַחִ֣יצוֹנָ֔ה הַדֶּ֖רֶךְ דֶּ֣רֶךְ הַצָּפ֑וֹן וַיְבִאֵ֣נִי אֶל־הַלִּשְׁכָּ֗ה אֲשֶׁ֨ר נֶ֧גֶד הַגִּזְרָ֛ה וַאֲשֶֽׁר־נֶ֥גֶד הַבִּנְיָ֖ן אֶל־הַצָּפֽוֹן׃


Then he brought me out into the outer court, the way towards the north: and brought me into the chamber that was over against the main wing, and which was before the building towards the north,


###### Ezekiel 42:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/42:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/42:2)


אֶל־פְּנֵי־אֹ֙רֶךְ֙ אַמּ֣וֹת הַמֵּאָ֔ה פֶּ֖תַח הַצָּפ֑וֹן וְהָרֹ֖חַב חֲמִשִּׁ֥ים אַמּֽוֹת׃


to the front of which the length was a hundred cubits with the door on the north, and the breadth was fifty cubits.


###### Ezekiel 42:3

נֶ֣גֶד הָעֶשְׂרִ֗ים אֲשֶׁר֙ לֶחָצֵ֣ר הַפְּנִימִ֔י וְנֶ֣גֶד רִֽצְפָ֔ה אֲשֶׁ֖ר לֶחָצֵ֣ר הַחִיצוֹנָ֑ה אַתִּ֥יק אֶל־פְּנֵֽי־אַתִּ֖יק בַּשְּׁלִשִֽׁים׃


Over against the twenty cubits which were for the inner court, and over against the pavement which was for the outer court, with gallery against gallery in three stories.

וְלִפְנֵ֨י הַלְּשָׁכ֜וֹת מַהֲלַךְ֩ עֶ֨שֶׂר אַמּ֥וֹת רֹ֙חַב֙ אֶל־הַפְּנִימִ֔ית דֶּ֖רֶךְ אַמָּ֣ה אֶחָ֑ת וּפִתְחֵיהֶ֖ם לַצָּפֽוֹן׃


And before the chambers was a walk of ten cubits breadth inward, a way of one cubit; and their doors were towards the north.

וְהַלְּשָׁכ֥וֹת הָעֶלְיוֹנֹ֖ת קְצֻר֑וֹת כִּֽי־יוֹכְל֨וּ אַתִּיקִ֜ים מֵהֵ֗נָּה מֵהַתַּחְתֹּנ֛וֹת וּמֵהַתִּכוֹנ֖וֹת בִּנְיָֽן׃


Now the upper chambers were shorter: for the galleries took away from these, more than from the lower, and the middlemost, in the building.

כִּ֤י מְשֻׁלָּשׁוֹת֙ הֵ֔נָּה וְאֵ֤ין לָהֶן֙ עַמּוּדִ֔ים כְּעַמּוּדֵ֖י הַחֲצֵר֑וֹת עַל־כֵּ֣ן נֶאֱצַ֗ל מֵהַתַּחְתּוֹנ֛וֹת וּמֵהַתִּיכֹנ֖וֹת מֵהָאָֽרֶץ׃


For they were in three stories, but had not pillars as the pillars of the courts: therefore it was set back from the ground more than the lowest and the middlemost.

וְגָדֵ֤ר אֲשֶׁר־לַחוּץ֙ לְעֻמַּ֣ת הַלְּשָׁכ֔וֹת דֶּ֛רֶךְ הֶחָצֵ֥ר הַחִצוֹנָ֖ה אֶל־פְּנֵ֣י הַלְּשָׁכ֑וֹת אׇרְכּ֖וֹ חֲמִשִּׁ֥ים אַמָּֽה׃


And the wall that was outside opposite the chambers, toward the outer court on the forefront of the chambers, the length of it was fifty cubits.

כִּֽי־אֹ֣רֶךְ הַלְּשָׁכ֗וֹת אֲשֶׁ֛ר לֶחָצֵ֥ר הַחִצוֹנָ֖ה חֲמִשִּׁ֣ים אַמָּ֑ה וְהִנֵּ֛ה עַל־פְּנֵ֥י הַהֵיכָ֖ל מֵאָ֥ה אַמָּֽה׃


For the length of the chambers that belonged to the outer court was fifty cubits: and, lo, in front of the temple was an extent of a hundred cubits.

(ומתחתה לשכות) [וּמִתַּ֖חַת הַלְּשָׁכ֣וֹת] הָאֵ֑לֶּה (המבוא) [הַמֵּבִיא֙] מֵֽהַקָּדִ֔ים בְּבֹא֣וֹ לָהֵ֔נָּה מֵהֶחָצֵ֖ר הַחִצֹנָֽה׃

###### Ezekiel 42:10

בְּרֹ֣חַב**׀** גֶּ֣דֶר הֶחָצֵ֗ר דֶּ֧רֶךְ הַקָּדִ֛ים אֶל־פְּנֵ֧י הַגִּזְרָ֛ה וְאֶל־פְּנֵ֥י הַבִּנְיָ֖ן לְשָׁכֽוֹת׃


Chambers were set in the thickness of the wall of the court towards the east, over against the main wing, and over against the building, with a way before them;


###### Ezekiel 42:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/42:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/42:11)


וְדֶ֙רֶךְ֙ לִפְנֵיהֶ֔ם כְּמַרְאֵ֣ה הַלְּשָׁכ֗וֹת אֲשֶׁר֙ דֶּ֣רֶךְ הַצָּפ֔וֹן כְּאׇרְכָּ֖ן כֵּ֣ן רׇחְבָּ֑ן וְכֹל֙ מוֹצָ֣אֵיהֶ֔ן וּכְמִשְׁפְּטֵיהֶ֖ן וּכְפִתְחֵיהֶֽן׃


like the appearance of the chambers which were towards the north, as long as they, and as broad as they, with all their exits, and according to their fashions;


###### Ezekiel 42:12

וּכְפִתְחֵ֣י הַלְּשָׁכ֗וֹת אֲשֶׁר֙ דֶּ֣רֶךְ הַדָּר֔וֹם פֶּ֖תַח בְּרֹ֣אשׁ דָּ֑רֶךְ דֶּ֗רֶךְ בִּפְנֵי֙ הַגְּדֶ֣רֶת הֲגִינָ֔ה דֶּ֥רֶךְ הַקָּדִ֖ים בְּבוֹאָֽן׃

###### Ezekiel 42:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/42:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/42:13)


וַיֹּ֣אמֶר אֵלַ֗י לִֽשְׁכ֨וֹת הַצָּפ֜וֹן לִֽשְׁכ֣וֹת הַדָּרוֹם֮ אֲשֶׁ֣ר אֶל־פְּנֵ֣י הַגִּזְרָה֒ הֵ֣נָּה**׀** לִֽשְׁכ֣וֹת הַקֹּ֗דֶשׁ אֲשֶׁ֨ר יֹאכְלוּ־שָׁ֧ם הַכֹּהֲנִ֛ים אֲשֶׁר־קְרוֹבִ֥ים לַיהֹוָ֖ה קׇדְשֵׁ֣י הַקֳּדָשִׁ֑ים שָׁ֞ם יַנִּ֣יחוּ**׀** קׇדְשֵׁ֣י הַקֳּדָשִׁ֗ים וְהַמִּנְחָה֙ וְהַחַטָּ֣את וְהָאָשָׁ֔ם כִּ֥י הַמָּק֖וֹם קָדֹֽשׁ׃


Then he said to me, The north chambers and the south chambers, which are before the main wing, they are holy chambers, where the priests that approach to the Lord shall eat the most holy things: there shall they lay the most holy things, and the meal offering, and the sin offering, and the guilt offering; for the place is holy.


###### Ezekiel 42:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/42:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/42:14)


בְּבֹאָ֣ם הַכֹּהֲנִ֗ים וְלֹֽא־יֵצְא֤וּ מֵהַקֹּ֙דֶשׁ֙ אֶל־הֶחָצֵ֣ר הַחִיצוֹנָ֔ה וְשָׁ֞ם יַנִּ֧יחוּ בִגְדֵיהֶ֛ם אֲשֶׁר־יְשָׁרְת֥וּ בָהֶ֖ן כִּֽי־קֹ֣דֶשׁ הֵ֑נָּה (ילבשו) [וְלָֽבְשׁוּ֙] בְּגָדִ֣ים אֲחֵרִ֔ים וְקָרְב֖וּ אֶל־אֲשֶׁ֥ר לָעָֽם׃


When the priests enter there, then shall they not go out of the holy place into the outer court, but there they shall lay their garments in which they minister; for they are holy; and shall put on other garments, and shall approach to those things which belong to the people.


###### Ezekiel 42:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/42:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/42:15)


וְכִלָּ֗ה אֶת־מִדּוֹת֙ הַבַּ֣יִת הַפְּנִימִ֔י וְהוֹצִיאַ֙נִי֙ דֶּ֣רֶךְ הַשַּׁ֔עַר אֲשֶׁ֥ר פָּנָ֖יו דֶּ֣רֶךְ הַקָּדִ֑ים וּמְדָד֖וֹ סָבִ֥יב׀סָבִֽיב׃


Now when he had made an end of measuring the inner house, he brought me out towards the gate whose prospect is towards the east, and measured it round about.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/42:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/42:16)

###### Ezekiel 42:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/42:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/42:18)


אֵ֛ת ר֥וּחַ הַדָּר֖וֹם מָדָ֑ד חֲמֵשׁ־מֵא֥וֹת קָנִ֖ים בִּקְנֵ֥ה הַמִּדָּֽה׃


He measured the south side, five hundred reeds, with the measuring reed.


###### Ezekiel 42:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/42:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/42:19)


סָבַ֖ב אֶל־ר֣וּחַ הַיָּ֑ם מָדַ֛ד חֲמֵשׁ־מֵא֥וֹת קָנִ֖ים בִּקְנֵ֥ה הַמִּדָּֽה׃

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/42:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/42:20)

## Chapter 43

###### Ezekiel 43:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/43:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/43:1)


וַיּוֹלִכֵ֖נִי אֶל־הַשָּׁ֑עַר שַׁ֕עַר אֲשֶׁ֥ר פֹּנֶ֖ה דֶּ֥רֶךְ הַקָּדִֽים׃


Then he brought me to the gate, the gate that looks towards the east:


###### Ezekiel 43:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/43:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/43:2)


וְהִנֵּ֗ה כְּבוֹד֙ אֱלֹהֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל בָּ֖א מִדֶּ֣רֶךְ הַקָּדִ֑ים וְקוֹל֗וֹ כְּקוֹל֙ מַ֣יִם רַבִּ֔ים וְהָאָ֖רֶץ הֵאִ֥ירָה מִכְּבֹדֽוֹ׃


and, behold, the glory of the God of Yisra᾽el came from the way of the east: and his voice was like the sound of many waters: and the earth shone with his glory.


###### Ezekiel 43:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/43:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/43:3)


וּכְמַרְאֵ֨ה הַמַּרְאֶ֜ה אֲשֶׁ֣ר רָאִ֗יתִי כַּמַּרְאֶ֤ה אֲשֶׁר־רָאִ֙יתִי֙ בְּבֹאִי֙ לְשַׁחֵ֣ת אֶת־הָעִ֔יר וּמַרְא֕וֹת כַּמַּרְאֶ֕ה אֲשֶׁ֥ר רָאִ֖יתִי אֶל־נְהַר־כְּבָ֑ר וָאֶפֹּ֖ל אֶל־פָּנָֽי׃


And the appearance of the vision which I saw, was like the vision that I saw when I came to destroy the city: and the visions were like the vision that I saw by the river Kevar, and I fell upon my face.


###### Ezekiel 43:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/43:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/43:4)


וּכְב֥וֹד יְהֹוָ֖ה בָּ֣א אֶל־הַבָּ֑יִת דֶּ֣רֶךְ שַׁ֔עַר אֲשֶׁ֥ר פָּנָ֖יו דֶּ֥רֶךְ הַקָּדִֽים׃


And the glory of the Lord came into the house by the way of the gate whose prospect is towards the east.


###### Ezekiel 43:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/43:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/43:5)


וַתִּשָּׂאֵ֣נִי ר֔וּחַ וַתְּבִאֵ֕נִי אֶל־הֶחָצֵ֖ר הַפְּנִימִ֑י וְהִנֵּ֛ה מָלֵ֥א כְבוֹד־יְהֹוָ֖ה הַבָּֽיִת׃


And a spirit took me up, and brought me into the inner court; and, behold, the glory of the Lord filled the house.


###### Ezekiel 43:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/43:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/43:6)


וָאֶשְׁמַ֛ע מִדַּבֵּ֥ר אֵלַ֖י מֵהַבָּ֑יִת וְאִ֕ישׁ הָיָ֥ה עֹמֵ֖ד אֶצְלִֽי׃


And I heard one speaking to me out of the house; and a man stood by me.


###### Ezekiel 43:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/43:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/43:7)


וַיֹּ֣אמֶר אֵלַ֗י בֶּן־אָדָם֙ אֶת־מְק֣וֹם כִּסְאִ֗י וְאֶת־מְקוֹם֙ כַּפּ֣וֹת רַגְלַ֔י אֲשֶׁ֧ר אֶשְׁכׇּן־שָׁ֛ם בְּת֥וֹךְ בְּנֵֽי־יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל לְעוֹלָ֑ם וְלֹ֣א יְטַמְּא֣וּ ע֣וֹד בֵּֽית־יִ֠שְׂרָאֵ֠ל שֵׁ֣ם קׇדְשִׁ֞י הֵ֤מָּה וּמַלְכֵיהֶם֙ בִּזְנוּתָ֔ם וּבְפִגְרֵ֥י מַלְכֵיהֶ֖ם בָּמוֹתָֽם׃


And he said to me, Son of man, behold the place of my throne, and the place of the soles of my feet, where I will dwell in the midst of the children of Yisra᾽el for ever; and the house of Yisra᾽el shall no more profane my holy name, neither they, nor their kings, by their harlotry, nor by the carcasses of their kings in their high places.


###### Ezekiel 43:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/43:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/43:8)


בְּתִתָּ֨ם סִפָּ֜ם אֶת־סִפִּ֗י וּמְזֽוּזָתָם֙ אֵ֣צֶל מְזוּזָתִ֔י וְהַקִּ֖יר בֵּינִ֣י וּבֵינֵיהֶ֑ם וְטִמְּא֣וּ**׀** אֶת־שֵׁ֣ם קׇדְשִׁ֗י בְּתֽוֹעֲבוֹתָם֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר עָשׂ֔וּ וָאֲכַ֥ל אֹתָ֖ם בְּאַפִּֽי׃


In their setting of their threshold by my thresholds, and their doorpost by my posts, and only the wall between me and them, they have defiled my holy name by their disgusting deeds which they have committed: and so I have consumed them in my anger.


###### Ezekiel 43:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/43:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/43:9)


עַתָּ֞ה יְרַחֲק֧וּ אֶת־זְנוּתָ֛ם וּפִגְרֵ֥י מַלְכֵיהֶ֖ם מִמֶּ֑נִּי וְשָׁכַנְתִּ֥י בְתוֹכָ֖ם לְעוֹלָֽם׃{ס}

אַתָּ֣ה בֶן־אָדָ֗ם הַגֵּ֤ד אֶת־בֵּֽית־יִשְׂרָאֵל֙ אֶת־הַבַּ֔יִת וְיִכָּלְמ֖וּ מֵעֲוֺנֽוֹתֵיהֶ֑ם וּמָדְד֖וּ אֶת־תׇּכְנִֽית׃


Thou son of man, describe the house to the house of Yisra᾽el, that they may be ashamed of their iniquities: and let them measure the pattern.


###### Ezekiel 43:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/43:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/43:11)


וְאִֽם־נִכְלְמ֞וּ מִכֹּ֣ל אֲשֶׁר־עָשׂ֗וּ צוּרַ֣ת הַבַּ֡יִת וּתְכוּנָת֡וֹ וּמוֹצָאָ֡יו וּמוֹבָאָ֣יו וְֽכׇל־צוּרֹתָ֡ו וְאֵ֣ת כׇּל־חֻקֹּתָיו֩ וְכׇל־צ֨וּרֹתָ֤ו וְכׇל־תּֽוֹרֹתָו֙ הוֹדַ֣ע אוֹתָ֔ם וּכְתֹ֖ב לְעֵינֵיהֶ֑ם וְיִשְׁמְר֞וּ אֶת־כׇּל־צוּרָת֛וֹ וְאֶת־כׇּל־חֻקֹּתָ֖יו וְעָשׂ֥וּ אוֹתָֽם׃


And if they are ashamed of all that they have done, make known to them the form of the house, and its fashion, and its exits, and its entrances, and all its forms, and its ordinances, and all its shapes, and all its Torot: and write it in their sight, that they may keep its whole form, and all its ordinances, and do them.

And these are the measures of the altar by cubits: the cubit is a cubit and a hand breadth; the bottom shall be a cubit, and the breadth a cubit, and its border by its edge round about shall be a span: and this shall be the higher part of the altar.

וּמֵחֵ֨יק הָאָ֜רֶץ עַד־הָעֲזָרָ֤ה הַתַּחְתּוֹנָה֙ שְׁתַּ֣יִם אַמּ֔וֹת וְרֹ֖חַב אַמָּ֣ה אֶחָ֑ת וּמֵהָעֲזָרָ֨ה הַקְּטַנָּ֜ה עַד־הָעֲזָרָ֤ה הַגְּדוֹלָה֙ אַרְבַּ֣ע אַמּ֔וֹת וְרֹ֖חַב הָאַמָּֽה׃

וְהָעֲזָרָ֞ה אַרְבַּ֧ע עֶשְׂרֵ֣ה אֹ֗רֶךְ בְּאַרְבַּ֤ע עֶשְׂרֵה֙ רֹ֔חַב אֶ֖ל אַרְבַּ֣עַת רְבָעֶ֑יהָ וְהַגְּבוּל֩ סָבִ֨יב אוֹתָ֜הּ חֲצִ֣י הָאַמָּ֗ה וְהַחֵֽיק־לָ֤הּ אַמָּה֙ סָבִ֔יב וּמַעֲלֹתֵ֖הוּ פְּנ֥וֹת קָדִֽים׃

And when these days are expired, it shall be, that upon the eighth day, and onwards, the priests shall make your burnt offerings upon the altar, and your peace offerings; and I will accept you, says the Lord God.



## Chapter 44

###### Ezekiel 44:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/44:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/44:1)


וַיָּ֣שֶׁב אֹתִ֗י דֶּ֣רֶךְ שַׁ֤עַר הַמִּקְדָּשׁ֙ הַחִיצ֔וֹן הַפֹּנֶ֖ה קָדִ֑ים וְה֖וּא סָגֽוּר׃


Then he brought me back the way of the outer gate of the sanctuary which looks towards the east; and it was shut.


###### Ezekiel 44:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/44:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/44:2)


וַיֹּ֨אמֶר אֵלַ֜י יְהֹוָ֗ה הַשַּׁ֣עַר הַזֶּה֩ סָג֨וּר יִֽהְיֶ֜ה לֹ֣א יִפָּתֵ֗חַ וְאִישׁ֙ לֹא־יָ֣בֹא ב֔וֹ כִּ֛י יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֵֽי־יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל בָּ֣א ב֑וֹ וְהָיָ֖ה סָגֽוּר׃


Then the Lord said to me; This gate shall be shut, it shall not be opened, and no man shall enter in by it; because the Lord, the God of Yisra᾽el, has entered in by it, therefore it shall be shut.


###### Ezekiel 44:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/44:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/44:3)


אֶֽת־הַנָּשִׂ֗יא נָ֥שִׂיא ה֛וּא יֵשֶׁב־בּ֥וֹ (לאכול) [לֶאֱכׇל־]לֶ֖חֶם לִפְנֵ֣י יְהֹוָ֑ה מִדֶּ֨רֶךְ אוּלָ֤ם הַשַּׁ֙עַר֙ יָב֔וֹא וּמִדַּרְכּ֖וֹ יֵצֵֽא׃


As for the prince, he, being a prince, shall sit in it to eat bread before the Lord; he shall enter by the way of the porch of that gate, and shall go out by the way of the same.


###### Ezekiel 44:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/44:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/44:4)


וַיְבִיאֵ֜נִי דֶּרֶךְ־שַׁ֣עַר הַצָּפוֹן֮ אֶל־פְּנֵ֣י הַבַּ֒יִת֒ וָאֵ֕רֶא וְהִנֵּ֛ה מָלֵ֥א כְבוֹד־יְהֹוָ֖ה אֶת־בֵּ֣ית יְהֹוָ֑ה וָאֶפֹּ֖ל אֶל־פָּנָֽי׃


Then he brought me the way of the north gate before the house: and I looked, and, behold, the glory of the Lord filled the house of the Lord: and I fell upon my face.


###### Ezekiel 44:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/44:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/44:5)


וַיֹּ֨אמֶר אֵלַ֜י יְהֹוָ֗ה בֶּן־אָדָ֡ם שִׂ֣ים לִבְּךָ֩ וּרְאֵ֨ה בְעֵינֶ֜יךָ וּבְאׇזְנֶ֣יךָ שְּׁמָ֗ע אֵ֣ת כׇּל־אֲשֶׁ֤ר אֲנִי֙ מְדַבֵּ֣ר אֹתָ֔ךְ לְכׇל־חֻקּ֥וֹת בֵּית־יְהֹוָ֖ה וּלְכׇל־תּוֹרֹתָ֑ו וְשַׂמְתָּ֤ לִבְּךָ֙ לִמְב֣וֹא הַבַּ֔יִת בְּכֹ֖ל מוֹצָאֵ֥י הַמִּקְדָּֽשׁ׃


And the Lord said to me, Son of man, mark well, and behold with thy eyes, and hear with thy ears all that I say to thee concerning all the ordinances of the house of the Lord, and all its Torot; and mark well the entrance of the house, with all the exits of the sanctuary.

And thou shalt say to the rebellious ones, to the house of Yisra᾽el, Thus says the Lord God; O house of Yisra᾽el, enough now of all your disgusting deeds,

בַּהֲבִיאֲכֶ֣ם בְּנֵי־נֵכָ֗ר עַרְלֵי־לֵב֙ וְעַרְלֵ֣י בָשָׂ֔ר לִהְי֥וֹת בְּמִקְדָּשִׁ֖י לְחַלְּל֣וֹ אֶת־בֵּיתִ֑י בְּהַקְרִֽיבְכֶ֤ם אֶת־לַחְמִי֙ חֵ֣לֶב וָדָ֔ם וַיָּפֵ֙רוּ֙ אֶת־בְּרִיתִ֔י אֶ֖ל כׇּל־תּוֹעֲבוֹתֵיכֶֽם׃


in that you have brought into my sanctuary strangers, uncircumcised in heart, and uncircumcised in flesh, to be in my sanctuary, to pollute it, even my house, when you offer my bread, the fat and the blood, and they have broken my covenant because of all your disgusting deeds;

וְלֹ֥א שְׁמַרְתֶּ֖ם מִשְׁמֶ֣רֶת קׇדָשָׁ֑י וַתְּשִׂימ֗וּן לְשֹׁמְרֵ֧י מִשְׁמַרְתִּ֛י בְּמִקְדָּשִׁ֖י לָכֶֽם׃{ס}

###### Ezekiel 44:30

וְרֵאשִׁית֩ כׇּל־בִּכּ֨וּרֵי כֹ֜ל וְכׇל־תְּר֣וּמַת כֹּ֗ל מִכֹּל֙ תְּרוּמ֣וֹתֵיכֶ֔ם לַכֹּהֲנִ֖ים יִהְיֶ֑ה וְרֵאשִׁ֤ית עֲרִסֽוֹתֵיכֶם֙ תִּתְּנ֣וּ לַכֹּהֵ֔ן לְהָנִ֥יחַ בְּרָכָ֖ה אֶל־בֵּיתֶֽךָ׃


And the first of all the firstfruits of all things, and every heave offering of all your heave offerings, shall belong to the priests: you shall also give to the priest the first of your dough, that he may cause a blessing to rest on thy house.


###### Ezekiel 44:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/44:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/44:31)


כׇּל־נְבֵלָה֙ וּטְרֵפָ֔ה מִן־הָע֖וֹף וּמִן־הַבְּהֵמָ֑ה לֹ֥א יֹאכְל֖וּ הַכֹּהֲנִֽים׃{פ}


The priests shall not eat of anything that dies of itself, or is torn, whether it be bird or beast.



## Chapter 45

###### Ezekiel 45:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/45:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/45:1)


וּבְהַפִּֽילְכֶ֨ם אֶת־הָאָ֜רֶץ בְּנַחֲלָ֗ה תָּרִ֩ימוּ֩ תְרוּמָ֨ה לַיהֹוָ֥ה׀קֹ֘דֶשׁ֮ מִן־הָאָ֒רֶץ֒ אֹ֗רֶךְ חֲמִשָּׁ֨ה וְעֶשְׂרִ֥ים אֶ֙לֶף֙ אֹ֔רֶךְ וְרֹ֖חַב עֲשָׂ֣רָה אָ֑לֶף קֹדֶשׁ־ה֥וּא בְכׇל־גְּבוּלָ֖הּ סָבִֽיב׃


And when you divide by lot the land for inheritance, you shall designate a portion to the Lord, a holy area of the land: the length shall be the length of twenty five thousand reeds, and the breadth shall be ten thousand. This shall be holy in all its border round about.


###### Ezekiel 45:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/45:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/45:2)


יִֽהְיֶ֤ה מִזֶּה֙ אֶל־הַקֹּ֔דֶשׁ חֲמֵ֥שׁ מֵא֛וֹת בַּחֲמֵ֥שׁ מֵא֖וֹת מְרֻבָּ֣ע סָבִ֑יב וַחֲמִשִּׁ֣ים אַמָּ֔ה מִגְרָ֥שׁ ל֖וֹ סָבִֽיב׃


Of this there shall be a square plot for the sanctuary, five hundred by five hundred round about; and there shall be an open space for it of fifty cubits round about.


###### Ezekiel 45:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/45:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/45:3)


וּמִן־הַמִּדָּ֤ה הַזֹּאת֙ תָּמ֔וֹד אֹ֗רֶךְ (חמש) [חֲמִשָּׁ֤ה] וְעֶשְׂרִים֙ אֶ֔לֶף וְרֹ֖חַב עֲשֶׂ֣רֶת אֲלָפִ֑ים וּבֽוֹ־יִהְיֶ֥ה הַמִּקְדָּ֖שׁ קֹ֥דֶשׁ קׇדָשִֽׁים׃


And of this measure shalt thou measure a length of twenty five thousand, and a breadth of ten thousand: and in it shall be the sanctuary, which is most holy.


###### Ezekiel 45:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/45:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/45:4)


קֹ֣דֶשׁ מִן־הָאָ֜רֶץ ה֗וּא לַכֹּ֨הֲנִ֜ים מְשָׁרְתֵ֤י הַמִּקְדָּשׁ֙ יִֽהְיֶ֔ה הַקְּרֵבִ֖ים לְשָׁרֵ֣ת אֶת־יְהֹוָ֑ה וְהָיָ֨ה לָהֶ֤ם מָקוֹם֙ לְבָ֣תִּ֔ים וּמִקְדָּ֖שׁ לַמִּקְדָּֽשׁ׃


The holy portion of the land shall be for the priests the ministers of the sanctuary, who come near to minister to the Lord: and it shall be a place for their houses, and a holy place for the sanctuary.


###### Ezekiel 45:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/45:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/45:5)


וַחֲמִשָּׁ֨ה וְעֶשְׂרִ֥ים אֶ֙לֶף֙ אֹ֔רֶךְ וַעֲשֶׂ֥רֶת אֲלָפִ֖ים רֹ֑חַב (יהיה) [וְֽהָיָ֡ה] לַלְוִיִּם֩ מְשָׁרְתֵ֨י הַבַּ֧יִת לָהֶ֛ם לַאֲחֻזָּ֖ה עֶשְׂרִ֥ים לְשָׁכֹֽת׃


And an area of twenty five thousand in length, and ten thousand in breadth, shall also the Levites, the ministers of the house, have for themselves, for a possession, twenty districts.


###### Ezekiel 45:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/45:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/45:6)


וַאֲחֻזַּ֨ת הָעִ֜יר תִּתְּנ֗וּ חֲמֵ֤שֶׁת אֲלָפִים֙ רֹ֔חַב וְאֹ֗רֶךְ חֲמִשָּׁ֤ה וְעֶשְׂרִים֙ אֶ֔לֶף לְעֻמַּ֖ת תְּרוּמַ֣ת הַקֹּ֑דֶשׁ לְכׇל־בֵּ֥ית יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל יִהְיֶֽה׃


And you shall appoint the possession of the city five thousand broad, and twenty five thousand long, adjoining the designated portion of the holy area: it shall be for the whole house of Yisra᾽el.


###### Ezekiel 45:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/45:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/45:7)


וְלַנָּשִׂ֡יא מִזֶּ֣ה וּמִזֶּה֩ לִתְרוּמַ֨ת הַקֹּ֜דֶשׁ וְלַאֲחֻזַּ֣ת הָעִ֗יר אֶל־פְּנֵ֤י תְרֽוּמַת־הַקֹּ֙דֶשׁ֙ וְאֶל־פְּנֵי֙ אֲחֻזַּ֣ת הָעִ֔יר מִפְּאַת־יָ֣ם יָ֔מָּה וּמִפְּאַת־קֵ֖דְמָה קָדִ֑ימָה וְאֹ֗רֶךְ לְעֻמּוֹת֙ אַחַ֣ד הַחֲלָקִ֔ים מִגְּב֥וּל יָ֖ם אֶל־גְּב֥וּל קָדִֽימָה׃


And the prince’s portion shall be on either side of the designated holy area, and of the possession of the city, in front of the designated holy area, and in front of the possession of the city, on the west side westward, and on the east side eastward: and the length shall correspond to one of the portions, from the western boundary to the eastern boundary.


###### Ezekiel 45:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/45:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/45:8)


לָאָ֛רֶץ יִֽהְיֶה־לּ֥וֹ לַאֲחֻזָּ֖ה בְּיִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל וְלֹא־יוֹנ֨וּ ע֤וֹד נְשִׂיאַי֙ אֶת־עַמִּ֔י וְהָאָ֛רֶץ יִתְּנ֥וּ לְבֵֽית־יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל לְשִׁבְטֵיהֶֽם׃{פ}


It shall be his possession of the land in Yisra᾽el: and my princes shall no more oppress my people; but they shall give to the house of Yisra᾽el the rest of the land according to their tribes.


###### Ezekiel 45:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/45:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/45:9)


כֹּה־אָמַ֞ר אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֗ה רַב־לָכֶם֙ נְשִׂיאֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל חָמָ֤ס וָשֹׁד֙ הָסִ֔ירוּ וּמִשְׁפָּ֥ט וּצְדָקָ֖ה עֲשׂ֑וּ הָרִ֤ימוּ גְרֻשֹֽׁתֵיכֶם֙ מֵעַ֣ל עַמִּ֔י נְאֻ֖ם אֲדֹנָ֥י יֱהֹוִֽה׃


Thus said the Lord God; Enough now, you princes of Yisra᾽el: remove violence and spoil, and execute judgment and justice, take away your exactions from my people, says the Lord God.


###### Ezekiel 45:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/45:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/45:10)


מֹֽאזְנֵי־צֶ֧דֶק וְאֵֽיפַת־צֶ֛דֶק וּבַת־צֶ֖דֶק יְהִ֥י לָכֶֽם׃


You shall have just balances, and a just efa, and a just bat.


###### Ezekiel 45:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/45:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/45:11)


הָאֵיפָ֣ה וְהַבַּ֗ת תֹּ֤כֶן אֶחָד֙ יִֽהְיֶ֔ה לָשֵׂ֕את מַעְשַׂ֥ר הַחֹ֖מֶר הַבָּ֑ת וַעֲשִׂירִ֤ת הַחֹ֙מֶר֙ הָאֵיפָ֔ה אֶל־הַחֹ֖מֶר יִהְיֶ֥ה מַתְכֻּנְתּֽוֹ׃


The efa and the bat shall be of one measure, that the bat may contain the tenth part of a ĥomer, and the efa the tenth part of a ĥomer: its measure shall be after the ĥomer.


###### Ezekiel 45:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/45:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/45:12)


וְהַשֶּׁ֖קֶל עֶשְׂרִ֣ים גֵּרָ֑ה עֶשְׂרִ֨ים שְׁקָלִ֜ים חֲמִשָּׁ֧ה וְעֶשְׂרִ֣ים שְׁקָלִ֗ים עֲשָׂרָ֤ה וַחֲמִשָּׁה֙ שֶׁ֔קֶל הַמָּנֶ֖ה יִהְיֶ֥ה לָכֶֽם׃


And the shekel shall be twenty gera: twenty shekels, five and twenty shekels, fifteen shekels, shall be your mane.


###### Ezekiel 45:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/45:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/45:13)


זֹ֥את הַתְּרוּמָ֖ה אֲשֶׁ֣ר תָּרִ֑ימוּ שִׁשִּׁ֤ית הָאֵיפָה֙ מֵחֹ֣מֶר הַחִטִּ֔ים וְשִׁשִּׁיתֶם֙ הָאֵיפָ֔ה מֵחֹ֖מֶר הַשְּׂעֹרִֽים׃


This is the offering that you shall set apart; the sixth part of an efa of a ĥomer of wheat, and you shall give the sixth part of an efa of a ĥomer of barley:


###### Ezekiel 45:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/45:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/45:14)


וְחֹ֨ק הַשֶּׁ֜מֶן הַבַּ֣ת הַשֶּׁ֗מֶן מַעְשַׂ֤ר הַבַּת֙ מִן־הַכֹּ֔ר עֲשֶׂ֥רֶת הַבַּתִּ֖ים חֹ֑מֶר כִּֽי־עֲשֶׂ֥רֶת הַבַּתִּ֖ים חֹֽמֶר׃


concerning the ordinance of oil, the bat of oil, you shall offer the tenth part of a bat out of the kor, which is a ĥomer of ten bats; for ten bats are a ĥomer:


###### Ezekiel 45:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/45:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/45:15)


וְשֶׂה־אַחַ֨ת מִן־הַצֹּ֤אן מִן־הַמָּאתַ֙יִם֙ מִמַּשְׁקֵ֣ה יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל לְמִנְחָ֖ה וּלְעוֹלָ֣ה וְלִשְׁלָמִ֑ים לְכַפֵּ֣ר עֲלֵיהֶ֔ם נְאֻ֖ם אֲדֹנָ֥י יֱהֹוִֽה׃{פ}


and one lamb out of the flock, out of two hundred, out of the well watered pastures of Yisra᾽el; for a meal offering, and for a burnt offering, and for peace offerings, to atone for them, says the Lord God.


###### Ezekiel 45:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/45:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/45:16)


כֹּ֚ל הָעָ֣ם הָאָ֔רֶץ יִהְי֖וּ אֶל־הַתְּרוּמָ֣ה הַזֹּ֑את לַנָּשִׂ֖יא בְּיִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


All the people of the land shall give this offering for the prince in Yisra᾽el.


###### Ezekiel 45:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/45:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/45:17)


וְעַֽל־הַנָּשִׂ֣יא יִֽהְיֶ֗ה הָעוֹל֣וֹת וְהַמִּנְחָה֮ וְהַנֵּ֒סֶךְ֒ בַּחַגִּ֤ים וּבֶחֳדָשִׁים֙ וּבַשַּׁבָּת֔וֹת בְּכׇֽל־מוֹעֲדֵ֖י בֵּ֣ית יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל הֽוּא־יַעֲשֶׂ֞ה אֶת־הַחַטָּ֣את וְאֶת־הַמִּנְחָ֗ה וְאֶת־הָעוֹלָה֙ וְאֶת־הַשְּׁלָמִ֔ים לְכַפֵּ֖ר בְּעַ֥ד בֵּֽית־יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃{ס}


And it shall be the prince’s part to give burnt offerings, and meal offerings, and drink offerings, on the feasts, and on the new moons, and on the sabbaths, at all appointed times of the house of Yisra᾽el: he shall prepare the sin offering, and the meal offering, and the burnt offering, and the peace offerings, to make atonement for the house of Yisra᾽el.


###### Ezekiel 45:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/45:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/45:18)


כֹּה־אָמַר֮ אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִה֒ בָּֽרִאשׁוֹן֙ בְּאֶחָ֣ד לַחֹ֔דֶשׁ תִּקַּ֥ח פַּר־בֶּן־בָּקָ֖ר תָּמִ֑ים וְחִטֵּאתָ֖ אֶת־הַמִּקְדָּֽשׁ׃


Thus says the Lord God; In the first month, on the first day of the month, thou shalt take a young bullock without blemish, and purify the sanctuary:


###### Ezekiel 45:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/45:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/45:19)


וְלָקַ֨ח הַכֹּהֵ֜ן מִדַּ֣ם הַחַטָּ֗את וְנָתַן֙ אֶל־מְזוּזַ֣ת הַבַּ֔יִת וְאֶל־אַרְבַּ֛ע פִּנּ֥וֹת הָעֲזָרָ֖ה לַמִּזְבֵּ֑חַ וְעַ֨ל־מְזוּזַ֔ת שַׁ֖עַר הֶחָצֵ֥ר הַפְּנִימִֽית׃


and the priest shall take of the blood of the sin offering, and put it upon the doorposts of the house, and upon the four corners of the ledge of the altar, and upon the doorpost of the gate of the inner court.


###### Ezekiel 45:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/45:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/45:20)


וְכֵ֤ן תַּֽעֲשֶׂה֙ בְּשִׁבְעָ֣ה בַחֹ֔דֶשׁ מֵאִ֥ישׁ שֹׁגֶ֖ה וּמִפֶּ֑תִי וְכִפַּרְתֶּ֖ם אֶת־הַבָּֽיִת׃


And so thou shalt do on the seventh day of the month for everyone that sins in error or in ignorance: so shall you make atonement for the house.


###### Ezekiel 45:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/45:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/45:21)


בָּ֠רִאשׁ֠וֹן בְּאַרְבָּעָ֨ה עָשָׂ֥ר יוֹם֙ לַחֹ֔דֶשׁ יִהְיֶ֥ה לָכֶ֖ם הַפָּ֑סַח חָ֕ג שְׁבֻע֣וֹת יָמִ֔ים מַצּ֖וֹת יֵאָכֵֽל׃


In the first month, on the fourteenth day of the month, you shall have the Passover, a feast of seven days; unleavened bread shall be eaten.


###### Ezekiel 45:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/45:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/45:22)


וְעָשָׂ֤ה הַנָּשִׂיא֙ בַּיּ֣וֹם הַה֔וּא בַּעֲד֕וֹ וּבְעַ֖ד כׇּל־עַ֣ם הָאָ֑רֶץ פַּ֖ר חַטָּֽאת׃


And upon that day shall the prince prepare for himself and for all the people of the land a bullock for a sin offering.


###### Ezekiel 45:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/45:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/45:23)


וְשִׁבְעַ֨ת יְמֵי־הֶחָ֜ג יַעֲשֶׂ֧ה עוֹלָ֣ה לַיהֹוָ֗ה שִׁבְעַ֣ת פָּ֠רִ֠ים וְשִׁבְעַ֨ת אֵילִ֤ים תְּמִימִם֙ לַיּ֔וֹם שִׁבְעַ֖ת הַיָּמִ֑ים וְחַטָּ֕את שְׂעִ֥יר עִזִּ֖ים לַיּֽוֹם׃


And seven days of the feast he shall prepare a burnt offering to the Lord, seven bullocks and seven rams without blemish daily for the seven days; and a kid of the goats daily for a sin offering.


###### Ezekiel 45:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/45:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/45:24)


וּמִנְחָ֗ה אֵיפָ֥ה לַפָּ֛ר וְאֵיפָ֥ה לָאַ֖יִל יַעֲשֶׂ֑ה וְשֶׁ֖מֶן הִ֥ין לָאֵיפָֽה׃


And he shall prepare a meal offering of an efa for a bullock, and an efa for a ram, and a hin of oil for an efa.


###### Ezekiel 45:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/45:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/45:25)


בַּשְּׁבִיעִ֡י בַּחֲמִשָּׁה֩ עָשָׂ֨ר י֤וֹם לַחֹ֙דֶשׁ֙ בֶּחָ֔ג יַעֲשֶׂ֥ה כָאֵ֖לֶּה שִׁבְעַ֣ת הַיָּמִ֑ים כַּֽחַטָּאת֙ כָּעֹלָ֔ה וְכַמִּנְחָ֖ה וְכַשָּֽׁמֶן׃{ס}


In the seventh month, on the fifteenth day of the month, in the feast, shall he do the like the seven days; both as to the sin offering, as to the burnt offering, and as to the meal offering, and as to the oil.



## Chapter 46

###### Ezekiel 46:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/46:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/46:1)


כֹּה־אָמַר֮ אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִה֒ שַׁ֜עַר הֶחָצֵ֤ר הַפְּנִימִית֙ הַפֹּנֶ֣ה קָדִ֔ים יִהְיֶ֣ה סָג֔וּר שֵׁ֖שֶׁת יְמֵ֣י הַֽמַּעֲשֶׂ֑ה וּבְי֤וֹם הַשַּׁבָּת֙ יִפָּתֵ֔חַ וּבְי֥וֹם הַחֹ֖דֶשׁ יִפָּתֵֽחַ׃


Thus says the Lord God; The gate of the inner court that looks towards the east shall be shut for the six working days; but on the sabbath it shall be opened, and on the day of the new moon it shall be opened.


###### Ezekiel 46:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/46:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/46:2)


וּבָ֣א הַנָּשִׂ֡יא דֶּ֩רֶךְ֩ אוּלָ֨ם הַשַּׁ֜עַר מִח֗וּץ וְעָמַד֙ עַל־מְזוּזַ֣ת הַשַּׁ֔עַר וְעָשׂ֣וּ הַכֹּהֲנִ֗ים אֶת־עֽוֹלָתוֹ֙ וְאֶת־שְׁלָמָ֔יו וְהִֽשְׁתַּחֲוָ֛ה עַל־מִפְתַּ֥ן הַשַּׁ֖עַר וְיָצָ֑א וְהַשַּׁ֥עַר לֹא־יִסָּגֵ֖ר עַד־הָעָֽרֶב׃


And the prince shall enter by the way of the porch of that gate from outside, and shall stand by the post of the gate, and the priests shall prepare his burnt offering, and his peace offerings, and he shall bow down at the threshold of the gate: then he shall go out; but the gate shall not be shut until the evening.


###### Ezekiel 46:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/46:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/46:3)


וְהִשְׁתַּחֲו֣וּ עַם־הָאָ֗רֶץ פֶּ֚תַח הַשַּׁ֣עַר הַה֔וּא בַּשַּׁבָּת֖וֹת וּבֶחֳדָשִׁ֑ים לִפְנֵ֖י יְהֹוָֽה׃


Likewise the people of the land shall bow down at the door of this gate before the Lord on the sabbaths and on the new moons.


###### Ezekiel 46:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/46:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/46:4)


וְהָ֣עֹלָ֔ה אֲשֶׁר־יַקְרִ֥ב הַנָּשִׂ֖יא לַיהֹוָ֑ה בְּי֣וֹם הַשַּׁבָּ֗ת שִׁשָּׁ֧ה כְבָשִׂ֛ים תְּמִימִ֖ם וְאַ֥יִל תָּמִֽים׃


And the burnt offering that the prince shall offer to the Lord on the sabbath day shall be six lambs without blemish, and a ram without blemish.


###### Ezekiel 46:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/46:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/46:5)


וּמִנְחָה֙ אֵיפָ֣ה לָאַ֔יִל וְלַכְּבָשִׂ֥ים מִנְחָ֖ה מַתַּ֣ת יָד֑וֹ וְשֶׁ֖מֶן הִ֥ין לָאֵיפָֽה׃{ס}


And the meal offering shall be an efa for a ram, and the meal offering for the lambs as he shall be able to give, and a hin of oil to every efa.


###### Ezekiel 46:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/46:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/46:6)


וּבְי֣וֹם הַחֹ֔דֶשׁ פַּ֥ר בֶּן־בָּקָ֖ר תְּמִימִ֑ם וְשֵׁ֧שֶׁת כְּבָשִׂ֛ים וָאַ֖יִל תְּמִימִ֥ם יִהְיֽוּ׃


And on the day of the new moon it shall be a young bullock without blemish, and six lambs, and a ram: they shall be without blemish.


###### Ezekiel 46:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/46:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/46:7)


וְאֵיפָ֨ה לַפָּ֜ר וְאֵיפָ֤ה לָאַ֙יִל֙ יַעֲשֶׂ֣ה מִנְחָ֔ה וְלַ֨כְּבָשִׂ֔ים כַּאֲשֶׁ֥ר תַּשִּׂ֖יג יָד֑וֹ וְשֶׁ֖מֶן הִ֥ין לָאֵיפָֽה׃


And he shall prepare a meal offering, an efa for a bullock, and an efa for a ram, and for the lambs according as his means extend, and a hin of oil to an efa.


###### Ezekiel 46:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/46:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/46:8)


וּבְב֖וֹא הַנָּשִׂ֑יא דֶּ֣רֶךְ אוּלָ֤ם הַשַּׁ֙עַר֙ יָב֔וֹא וּבְדַרְכּ֖וֹ יֵצֵֽא׃


And when the prince shall enter, he shall go in by the way of the porch of that gate, and he shall go out by the same way.


###### Ezekiel 46:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/46:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/46:9)


וּבְב֨וֹא עַם־הָאָ֜רֶץ לִפְנֵ֣י יְהֹוָה֮ בַּמּוֹעֲדִים֒ הַבָּ֡א דֶּרֶךְ־שַׁ֨עַר צָפ֜וֹן לְהִֽשְׁתַּחֲוֺ֗ת יֵצֵא֙ דֶּרֶךְ־שַׁ֣עַר נֶ֔גֶב וְהַבָּא֙ דֶּרֶךְ־שַׁ֣עַר נֶ֔גֶב יֵצֵ֖א דֶּרֶךְ־שַׁ֣עַר צָפ֑וֹנָה לֹ֣א יָשׁ֗וּב דֶּ֤רֶךְ הַשַּׁ֙עַר֙ אֲשֶׁר־בָּ֣א ב֔וֹ כִּ֥י נִכְח֖וֹ (יצאו) [יֵצֵֽא]׃


But when the people of the land shall come before the Lord on the appointed seasons, he that enters in by the way of the north gate to bow down shall go out by the way of the south gate; and he that enters by the way of the south gate shall go out by the way of the north gate: he shall not return by the way of the same gate through which he came in, but shall go out straight ahead.


###### Ezekiel 46:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/46:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/46:10)


וְֽהַנָּשִׂ֑יא בְּתוֹכָ֤ם בְּבוֹאָם֙ יָב֔וֹא וּבְצֵאתָ֖ם יֵצֵֽאוּ׃


And the prince shall be in the midst of them; when they go in, he shall go in; and when they go out, they shall go out together.


###### Ezekiel 46:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/46:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/46:11)


וּבַחַגִּ֣ים וּבַמּוֹעֲדִ֗ים תִּהְיֶ֤ה הַמִּנְחָה֙ אֵיפָ֤ה לַפָּר֙ וְאֵיפָ֣ה לָאַ֔יִל וְלַכְּבָשִׂ֖ים מַתַּ֣ת יָד֑וֹ וְשֶׁ֖מֶן הִ֥ין לָאֵיפָֽה׃{פ}


And on the feasts and on the appointed seasons the meal offering shall be an efa for a bullock, and an efa for a ram, and for the lambs as he is able to give, and a hin of oil with every efa.


###### Ezekiel 46:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/46:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/46:12)


וְכִֽי־יַעֲשֶׂה֩ הַנָּשִׂ֨יא נְדָבָ֜ה עוֹלָ֣ה אוֹ־שְׁלָמִים֮ נְדָבָ֣ה לַיהֹוָה֒ וּפָ֣תַֽח ל֗וֹ אֶת־הַשַּׁ֙עַר֙ הַפֹּנֶ֣ה קָדִ֔ים וְעָשָׂ֤ה אֶת־עֹֽלָתוֹ֙ וְאֶת־שְׁלָמָ֔יו כַּאֲשֶׁ֥ר יַעֲשֶׂ֖ה בְּי֣וֹם הַשַּׁבָּ֑ת וְיָצָ֛א וְסָגַ֥ר אֶת־הַשַּׁ֖עַר אַחֲרֵ֥י צֵאתֽוֹ׃


Now when the prince shall prepare a voluntary burnt offering or peace offerings voluntarily to the Lord, the gate that looks towards the east shall be opened for him, and he shall prepare his burnt offering and his peace offerings, as he shall do on the sabbath day: then he shall go out; and after his going out the gate shall be shut.


###### Ezekiel 46:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/46:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/46:13)


וְכֶ֨בֶשׂ בֶּן־שְׁנָת֜וֹ תָּמִ֗ים תַּעֲשֶׂ֥ה עוֹלָ֛ה לַיּ֖וֹם לַיהֹוָ֑ה בַּבֹּ֥קֶר בַּבֹּ֖קֶר תַּעֲשֶׂ֥ה אֹתֽוֹ׃


And thou shalt daily prepare a burnt offering to the Lord of a lamb of the first year without blemish: thou shalt prepare it every morning.


###### Ezekiel 46:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/46:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/46:14)


וּמִנְחָה֩ תַעֲשֶׂ֨ה עָלָ֜יו בַּבֹּ֤קֶר בַּבֹּ֙קֶר֙ שִׁשִּׁ֣ית הָאֵיפָ֔ה וְשֶׁ֛מֶן שְׁלִישִׁ֥ית הַהִ֖ין לָרֹ֣ס אֶת־הַסֹּ֑לֶת מִנְחָה֙ לַיהֹוָ֔ה חֻקּ֥וֹת עוֹלָ֖ם תָּמִֽיד׃


And thou shalt prepare a meal offering for it every morning, the sixth part of an efa, and the third part of a hin of oil, to moisten the fine flour; a meal offering continually by perpetual ordinances to the Lord.


###### Ezekiel 46:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/46:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/46:15)


(ועשו) [יַעֲשׂ֨וּ] אֶת־הַכֶּ֧בֶשׂ וְאֶת־הַמִּנְחָ֛ה וְאֶת־הַשֶּׁ֖מֶן בַּבֹּ֣קֶר בַּבֹּ֑קֶר עוֹלַ֖ת תָּמִֽיד׃{ס}


Thus shall they prepare the lamb, and the meal offering, and the oil, every morning for a continual burnt offering.


###### Ezekiel 46:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/46:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/46:16)


כֹּה־אָמַ֞ר אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֗ה כִּֽי־יִתֵּ֨ן הַנָּשִׂ֤יא מַתָּנָה֙ לְאִ֣ישׁ מִבָּנָ֔יו נַחֲלָת֥וֹ הִ֖יא לְבָנָ֣יו תִּֽהְיֶ֑ה אֲחֻזָּתָ֥ם הִ֖יא בְּנַחֲלָֽה׃{ס}


Thus says the Lord God; if the prince give a gift to any of his sons, it shall be his inheritance, it shall be his sons’; it shall be their possession by inheritance.


###### Ezekiel 46:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/46:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/46:17)


וְכִֽי־יִתֵּ֨ן מַתָּנָ֜ה מִנַּחֲלָת֗וֹ לְאַחַד֙ מֵֽעֲבָדָ֔יו וְהָ֤יְתָה לּוֹ֙ עַד־שְׁנַ֣ת הַדְּר֔וֹר וְשָׁבַ֖ת לַנָּשִׂ֑יא אַ֚ךְ נַחֲלָת֔וֹ בָּנָ֖יו לָהֶ֥ם תִּהְיֶֽה׃


But if he give a gift of his inheritance to one of his servants, then it shall be his to the year of liberty; then it shall return to the prince: but his inheritance shall be his sons’ for them.


###### Ezekiel 46:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/46:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/46:18)


וְלֹא־יִקַּ֨ח הַנָּשִׂ֜יא מִנַּחֲלַ֣ת הָעָ֗ם לְהֽוֹנֹתָם֙ מֵאֲחֻזָּתָ֔ם מֵאֲחֻזָּת֖וֹ יַנְחִ֣ל אֶת־בָּנָ֑יו לְמַ֙עַן֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר לֹא־יָפֻ֣צוּ עַמִּ֔י אִ֖ישׁ מֵאֲחֻזָּתֽוֹ׃


Moreover the prince shall not take of the people’s inheritance to thrust them wrongfully out of their possession; but he shall give his sons inheritance out of his own possession: that my people be not scattered every man from his possession.


###### Ezekiel 46:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/46:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/46:19)


וַיְבִיאֵ֣נִי בַמָּבוֹא֮ אֲשֶׁ֣ר עַל־כֶּ֣תֶף הַשַּׁ֒עַר֒ אֶל־הַלִּשְׁכ֤וֹת הַקֹּ֙דֶשׁ֙ אֶל־הַכֹּ֣הֲנִ֔ים הַפֹּנ֖וֹת צָפ֑וֹנָה וְהִנֵּה־שָׁ֣ם מָק֔וֹם (בירכתם) [בַּיַּרְכָתַ֖יִם] יָֽמָּה׃


Then he brought me through the entry, which was at the side of the gate, into the holy chambers of the priests, which looked towards the north: and, behold, there was a place on the far side westward.


###### Ezekiel 46:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/46:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/46:20)


וַיֹּ֣אמֶר אֵלַ֔י זֶ֣ה הַמָּק֗וֹם אֲשֶׁ֤ר יְבַשְּׁלוּ־שָׁם֙ הַכֹּ֣הֲנִ֔ים אֶת־הָאָשָׁ֖ם וְאֶת־הַחַטָּ֑את אֲשֶׁ֤ר יֹאפוּ֙ אֶת־הַמִּנְחָ֔ה לְבִלְתִּ֥י הוֹצִ֛יא אֶל־הֶחָצֵ֥ר הַחִיצוֹנָ֖ה לְקַדֵּ֥שׁ אֶת־הָעָֽם׃


Then he said to me, This is the place where the priests shall cook the guilt offering and the sin offering, where they shall bake the meal offering; so that they do not carry them out into the outer court, to hallow the people.


###### Ezekiel 46:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/46:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/46:21)


וַיּוֹצִיאֵ֗נִי אֶל־הֶחָצֵר֙ הַחִ֣יצוֹנָ֔ה וַיַּ֣עֲבִרֵ֔נִי אֶל־אַרְבַּ֖עַת מִקְצוֹעֵ֣י הֶחָצֵ֑ר וְהִנֵּ֤ה חָצֵר֙ בְּמִקְצֹ֣עַ הֶחָצֵ֔ר חָצֵ֖ר בְּמִקְצֹ֥עַ הֶחָצֵֽר׃


Then he brought me out into the outer court, and caused me to pass by the four corners of the court; and, behold, in every corner of the court there was a court.


###### Ezekiel 46:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/46:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/46:22)


בְּאַרְבַּ֜עַת מִקְצֹע֤וֹת הֶחָצֵר֙ חֲצֵר֣וֹת קְטֻר֔וֹת אַרְבָּעִ֣ים אֹ֔רֶךְ וּשְׁלֹשִׁ֖ים רֹ֑חַב מִדָּ֣ה אַחַ֔ת לְאַרְבַּעְתָּ֖ם מְׄהֻׄקְׄצָׄעֽׄוֹׄתׄ׃


In the four corners of the court there were roofless courts, forty cubits long and thirty broad: these four corners were of one measure.


###### Ezekiel 46:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/46:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/46:23)


וְט֨וּר סָבִ֥יב בָּהֶ֛ם סָבִ֖יב לְאַרְבַּעְתָּ֑ם וּמְבַשְּׁל֣וֹת עָשׂ֔וּי מִתַּ֥חַת הַטִּיר֖וֹת סָבִֽיב׃


And there was a row of masonry round about in them, round about those four, and it was made with cooking hearths under the rows round about.


###### Ezekiel 46:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/46:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/46:24)


וַיֹּ֖אמֶר אֵלָ֑י אֵ֚לֶּה בֵּ֣ית הַֽמְבַשְּׁלִ֔ים אֲשֶׁ֧ר יְבַשְּׁלוּ־שָׁ֛ם מְשָׁרְתֵ֥י הַבַּ֖יִת אֶת־זֶ֥בַח הָעָֽם׃


Then he said to me, These are the places of them that cook, where the ministers of the house shall boil the sacrifice of the people.



## Chapter 47

###### Ezekiel 47:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/47:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/47:1)


וַיְשִׁבֵ֘נִי֮ אֶל־פֶּ֣תַח הַבַּ֒יִת֒ וְהִנֵּה־מַ֣יִם יֹצְאִ֗ים מִתַּ֨חַת מִפְתַּ֤ן הַבַּ֙יִת֙ קָדִ֔ימָה כִּֽי־פְנֵ֥י הַבַּ֖יִת קָדִ֑ים וְהַמַּ֣יִם יֹרְדִ֗ים מִתַּ֜חַת מִכֶּ֤תֶף הַבַּ֙יִת֙ הַיְמָנִ֔ית מִנֶּ֖גֶב לַמִּזְבֵּֽחַ׃


Then he brought me back to the door of the house; and, behold, water issued out from under the threshold of the house eastward: for the forefront of the house looked towards the east, and the water came down from beneath, from the right side of the house, at the south side of the altar.


###### Ezekiel 47:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/47:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/47:2)


וַיּוֹצִאֵ֘נִי֮ דֶּרֶךְ־שַׁ֣עַר צָפ֒וֹנָה֒ וַיְסִבֵּ֙נִי֙ דֶּ֣רֶךְ ח֔וּץ אֶל־שַׁ֣עַר הַח֔וּץ דֶּ֖רֶךְ הַפּוֹנֶ֣ה קָדִ֑ים וְהִנֵּה־מַ֣יִם מְפַכִּ֔ים מִן־הַכָּתֵ֖ף הַיְמָנִֽית׃


Then he brought me out by the way of the gate northward, and led me round the way outside to the outer gate by the way what looks eastward; and, behold, there ran out water on the right side.


###### Ezekiel 47:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/47:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/47:3)


בְּצֵאת־הָאִ֥ישׁ קָדִ֖ים וְקָ֣ו בְּיָד֑וֹ וַיָּ֤מׇד אֶ֙לֶף֙ בָּֽאַמָּ֔ה וַיַּעֲבִרֵ֥נִי בַמַּ֖יִם מֵ֥י אׇפְסָֽיִם׃


And when the man that had the line in his hand went out eastward, he measured a thousand cubits, and he made me pass through the water; the water was up to the ankles.


###### Ezekiel 47:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/47:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/47:4)


וַיָּ֣מׇד אֶ֔לֶף וַיַּעֲבִרֵ֥נִי בַמַּ֖יִם מַ֣יִם בִּרְכָּ֑יִם וַיָּ֣מׇד אֶ֔לֶף וַיַּעֲבִרֵ֖נִי מֵ֥י מׇתְנָֽיִם׃


Again he measured a thousand, and made me pass through the water; the water was up to the knees. Again he measured a thousand, and made me pass through water up to the loins.


###### Ezekiel 47:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/47:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/47:5)


וַיָּ֣מׇד אֶ֔לֶף נַ֕חַל אֲשֶׁ֥ר לֹא־אוּכַ֖ל לַעֲבֹ֑ר כִּֽי־גָא֤וּ הַמַּ֙יִם֙ מֵ֣י שָׂ֔חוּ נַ֖חַל אֲשֶׁ֥ר לֹא־יֵעָבֵֽר׃


And he measured a further thousand; and it was a stream that I could not pass over: for the waters were risen, waters to swim in, a stream that could not be passed over.


###### Ezekiel 47:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/47:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/47:6)


וַיֹּ֥אמֶר אֵלַ֖י הֲרָאִ֣יתָ בֶן־אָדָ֑ם וַיּוֹלִכֵ֥נִי וַיְשִׁבֵ֖נִי שְׂפַ֥ת הַנָּֽחַל׃


And he said to me, Son of man, hast thou seen this? Then he brought me, and caused me to return to the edge of the stream.


###### Ezekiel 47:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/47:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/47:7)


בְּשׁוּבֵ֕נִי וְהִנֵּה֙ אֶל־שְׂפַ֣ת הַנַּ֔חַל עֵ֖ץ רַ֣ב מְאֹ֑ד מִזֶּ֖ה וּמִזֶּֽה׃


Now when I had returned, behold, at the bank of the stream were very many trees on the one side and on the other.


###### Ezekiel 47:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/47:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/47:8)


וַיֹּ֣אמֶר אֵלַ֗י הַמַּ֤יִם הָאֵ֙לֶּה֙ יוֹצְאִ֗ים אֶל־הַגְּלִילָה֙ הַקַּדְמוֹנָ֔ה וְיָרְד֖וּ עַל־הָעֲרָבָ֑ה וּבָ֣אוּ הַיָּ֔מָּה אֶל־הַיָּ֥מָּה הַמּוּצָאִ֖ים וְנִרְפּ֥אוּ הַמָּֽיִם׃


Then he said to me, These waters issue out towards the eastern region, and go down into the ῾Arava; and on their entering the sea, the sea of issuing waters, the waters shall be healed.


###### Ezekiel 47:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/47:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/47:9)


וְהָיָ֣ה כׇל־נֶ֣פֶשׁ חַיָּ֣ה׀אֲֽשֶׁר־יִשְׁרֹ֡ץ אֶ֣ל כׇּל־אֲשֶׁר֩ יָב֨וֹא שָׁ֤ם נַחֲלַ֙יִם֙ יִֽחְיֶ֔ה וְהָיָ֥ה הַדָּגָ֖ה רַבָּ֣ה מְאֹ֑ד כִּי֩ בָ֨אוּ שָׁ֜מָּה הַמַּ֣יִם הָאֵ֗לֶּה וְיֵרָֽפְאוּ֙ וָחָ֔י כֹּ֛ל אֲשֶׁר־יָ֥בוֹא שָׁ֖מָּה הַנָּֽחַל׃


And it shall come to pass, that every living thing, with which it swarms, wherever the streams shall come, shall live: and there shall be a very great multitude of fish, because these waters shall come thither: so that everything shall be healed, and shall live where that stream comes.


###### Ezekiel 47:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/47:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/47:10)


וְהָיָה֩ (יעמדו) [עָמְד֨וּ] עָלָ֜יו דַּוָּגִ֗ים מֵעֵ֥ין גֶּ֙דִי֙ וְעַד־עֵ֣ין עֶגְלַ֔יִם מִשְׁט֥וֹחַ לַחֲרָמִ֖ים יִֽהְי֑וּ לְמִינָהֿ֙ תִּֽהְיֶ֣ה דְגָתָ֔ם כִּדְגַ֛ת הַיָּ֥ם הַגָּד֖וֹל רַבָּ֥ה מְאֹֽד׃


And it shall come to pass, that the fishers shall stand upon it from ῾En-gedi as far as ῾En-῾eglayim; there shall be a place for the spreading of nets; their fish shall be of various kinds, like the fish of the great sea, exceeding many.


###### Ezekiel 47:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/47:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/47:11)


בִּצֹּאתָ֧ו וּגְבָאָ֛יו וְלֹ֥א יֵרָפְא֖וּ לְמֶ֥לַח נִתָּֽנוּ׃


But its miry places and its marshes shall not be healed; they shall be given for salt.


###### Ezekiel 47:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/47:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/47:12)


וְעַל־הַנַּ֣חַל יַעֲלֶ֣ה עַל־שְׂפָת֣וֹ מִזֶּ֣ה׀וּמִזֶּ֣ה׀כׇּל־עֵֽץ־מַ֠אֲכָ֠ל לֹא־יִבּ֨וֹל עָלֵ֜הוּ וְלֹֽא־יִתֹּ֣ם פִּרְי֗וֹ לׇחֳדָשָׁיו֙ יְבַכֵּ֔ר כִּ֣י מֵימָ֔יו מִן־הַמִּקְדָּ֖שׁ הֵ֣מָּה יוֹצְאִ֑ים (והיו) [וְהָיָ֤ה] פִרְיוֹ֙ לְמַאֲכָ֔ל וְעָלֵ֖הוּ לִתְרוּפָֽה׃{פ}


And by the stream upon its bank, on this side and on that side, shall grow all trees for food, whose leaf shall not wither, neither shall its fruit fail: it shall bring forth new fruit every month, because their waters have issued out of the sanctuary: and its fruit shall be for food, and its leaf for medicine.


###### Ezekiel 47:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/47:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/47:13)


כֹּ֤ה אָמַר֙ אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֔ה גֵּ֤ה גְבוּל֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר תִּתְנַחֲל֣וּ אֶת־הָאָ֔רֶץ לִשְׁנֵ֥י עָשָׂ֖ר שִׁבְטֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל יוֹסֵ֖ף חֲבָלִֽים׃


Thus says the Lord God; This shall be the border, according to which you shall divide the land for inheritance to the twelve tribes of Yisra᾽el: Yosef shall have two portions.


###### Ezekiel 47:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/47:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/47:14)


וּנְחַלְתֶּ֤ם אוֹתָהּ֙ אִ֣ישׁ כְּאָחִ֔יו אֲשֶׁ֤ר נָשָׂ֙אתִי֙ אֶת־יָדִ֔י לְתִתָּ֖הּ לַאֲבֹֽתֵיכֶ֑ם וְנָ֨פְלָ֜ה הָאָ֧רֶץ הַזֹּ֛את לָכֶ֖ם בְּנַחֲלָֽה׃


And you shall inherit it, one as well as another: that concerning which I lifted up my hand to give it to your fathers: and this land shall fall to you for inheritance.


###### Ezekiel 47:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/47:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/47:15)


וְזֶ֖ה גְּב֣וּל הָאָ֑רֶץ לִפְאַ֨ת צָפ֜וֹנָה מִן־הַיָּ֧ם הַגָּד֛וֹל הַדֶּ֥רֶךְ חֶתְלֹ֖ן לְב֥וֹא צְדָֽדָה׃


And this shall be the border of the land towards the north side, from the great sea, by the way of Ḥetlon, as you go to Żedad;


###### Ezekiel 47:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/47:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/47:16)


חֲמָ֤ת׀בֵּר֙וֹתָה֙ סִבְרַ֔יִם אֲשֶׁר֙ בֵּין־גְּב֣וּל דַּמֶּ֔שֶׂק וּבֵ֖ין גְּב֣וּל חֲמָ֑ת חָצֵר֙ הַתִּיכ֔וֹן אֲשֶׁ֖ר אֶל־גְּב֥וּל חַוְרָֽן׃


Ḥamat, Berota, Sivrayim, which is between the border of Dammeseq and the border of Ḥamat; the middle Ḥażer, which is by the border of Ḥavran.


###### Ezekiel 47:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/47:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/47:17)


וְהָיָ֨ה גְב֜וּל מִן־הַיָּ֗ם חֲצַ֤ר עֵינוֹן֙ גְּב֣וּל דַּמֶּ֔שֶׂק וְצָפ֥וֹן׀צָפ֖וֹנָה וּגְב֣וּל חֲמָ֑ת וְאֵ֖ת פְּאַ֥ת צָפֽוֹן׃


And the border from the sea shall be Ḥażar-῾enon, the border of Dammeseq, and the north northward, and the border of Ḥamat. That is the north side.


###### Ezekiel 47:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/47:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/47:18)


וּפְאַ֣ת קָדִ֡ים מִבֵּ֣ין חַוְרָ֣ן וּמִבֵּין־דַּמֶּ֩שֶׂק֩ וּמִבֵּ֨ין הַגִּלְעָ֜ד וּמִבֵּ֨ין אֶ֤רֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל֙ הַיַּרְדֵּ֔ן מִגְּב֛וּל עַל־הַיָּ֥ם הַקַּדְמוֹנִ֖י תָּמֹ֑דּוּ וְאֵ֖ת פְּאַ֥ת קָדִֽימָה׃


And the east side, between Ḥavran, and Dammeseq and Gil῾ad, and between the land of Yisra᾽el and the Yarden, from the border to the eastern sea shall you measure. That is the east side.


###### Ezekiel 47:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/47:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/47:19)


וּפְאַת֙ נֶ֣גֶב תֵּימָ֔נָה מִתָּמָ֗ר עַד־מֵי֙ מְרִיב֣וֹת קָדֵ֔שׁ נַחֲלָ֖ה אֶל־הַיָּ֣ם הַגָּד֑וֹל וְאֵ֥ת פְּאַת־תֵּימָ֖נָה נֶֽגְבָּה׃


And the Negev side southward from Tamar to the waters of Merivot-qadesh to the wadi, as far as the great sea. That is the south side towards the Negev.


###### Ezekiel 47:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/47:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/47:20)


וּפְאַת־יָם֙ הַיָּ֣ם הַגָּד֔וֹל מִגְּב֕וּל עַד־נֹ֖כַח לְב֣וֹא חֲמָ֑ת זֹ֖את פְּאַת־יָֽם׃


The west side also shall be the great sea from the border, as far as over against the entrance of Ḥamat. That is the west side.


###### Ezekiel 47:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/47:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/47:21)


וְחִלַּקְתֶּ֞ם אֶת־הָאָ֧רֶץ הַזֹּ֛את לָכֶ֖ם לְשִׁבְטֵ֥י יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


So shall you divide this land for yourselves according to the tribes of Yisra᾽el.


###### Ezekiel 47:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/47:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/47:22)


וְהָיָ֗ה תַּפִּ֣לוּ אוֹתָהּ֮ בְּנַחֲלָה֒ לָכֶ֗ם וּלְהַגֵּרִים֙ הַגָּרִ֣ים בְּתוֹכְכֶ֔ם אֲשֶׁר־הוֹלִ֥דוּ בָנִ֖ים בְּתוֹכְכֶ֑ם וְהָי֣וּ לָכֶ֗ם כְּאֶזְרָח֙ בִּבְנֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל אִתְּכֶם֙ יִפְּל֣וּ בְנַֽחֲלָ֔ה בְּת֖וֹךְ שִׁבְטֵ֥י יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


And it shall come to pass, that you shall divide it by lot for an inheritance to you, and to the strangers that sojourn among you, who shall beget children among you: and they shall be to you as those born in the country among the children of Yisra᾽el; they shall have inheritance with you among the tribes of Yisra᾽el.


###### Ezekiel 47:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/47:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/47:23)


וְהָיָ֣ה בַשֵּׁ֔בֶט אֲשֶׁר־גָּ֥ר הַגֵּ֖ר אִתּ֑וֹ שָׁ֚ם תִּתְּנ֣וּ נַחֲלָת֔וֹ נְאֻ֖ם אֲדֹנָ֥י יֱהֹוִֽה׃{פ}


And it shall come to pass, that in whatever tribe the stranger may sojourn, there shall you give him his inheritance, says the Lord God.



## Chapter 48

###### Ezekiel 48:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/48:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/48:1)


וְאֵ֖לֶּה שְׁמ֣וֹת הַשְּׁבָטִ֑ים מִקְצֵ֣ה צָפ֡וֹנָה אֶל־יַ֣ד דֶּֽרֶךְ־חֶתְלֹ֣ן׀לְֽבוֹא־חֲמָ֡ת חֲצַ֣ר עֵינָן֩ גְּב֨וּל דַּמֶּ֤שֶׂק צָפ֙וֹנָה֙ אֶל־יַ֣ד חֲמָ֔ת וְהָיוּ־ל֧וֹ פְאַת־קָדִ֛ים הַיָּ֖ם דָּ֥ן אֶחָֽד׃


Now these are the names of the tribes. From the north end beside the way to Ḥetlon, as one goes to Ḥamat, Ḥażar-῾enan, the border of Dammeseq northward beside Ḥamat; for these are his sides east and west; a portion for Dan.


###### Ezekiel 48:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/48:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/48:2)


וְעַ֣ל**׀** גְּב֣וּל דָּ֗ן מִפְּאַ֥ת קָדִ֛ים עַד־פְּאַת־יָ֖מָּה אָשֵׁ֥ר אֶחָֽד׃


And by the border of Dan, from the east side to the west side a portion for Asher.

###### Ezekiel 48:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/48:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/48:30)


וְאֵ֖לֶּה תּוֹצְאֹ֣ת הָעִ֑יר מִפְּאַ֣ת צָפ֔וֹן חֲמֵ֥שׁ מֵא֛וֹת וְאַרְבַּ֥עַת אֲלָפִ֖ים מִדָּֽה׃


And these are the exits of the city. On the north side, there is measure of four thousand five hundred.


###### Ezekiel 48:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/48:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/48:31)


וְשַׁעֲרֵ֣י הָעִ֗יר עַל־שְׁמוֹת֙ שִׁבְטֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל שְׁעָרִ֥ים שְׁלוֹשָׁ֖ה צָפ֑וֹנָה שַׁ֣עַר רְאוּבֵ֞ן אֶחָ֗ד שַׁ֤עַר יְהוּדָה֙ אֶחָ֔ד שַׁ֥עַר לֵוִ֖י אֶחָֽד׃


And the gates of the city shall be after the names of the tribes of Yisra᾽el: three gates northward; one gate of Re᾽uven, one gate of Yehuda, one gate of Levi.


###### Ezekiel 48:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/48:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/48:32)


וְאֶל־פְּאַ֣ת קָדִ֗ימָה חֲמֵ֤שׁ מֵאוֹת֙ וְאַרְבַּ֣עַת אֲלָפִ֔ים וּשְׁעָרִ֖ים שְׁלֹשָׁ֑ה וְשַׁ֨עַר יוֹסֵ֜ף אֶחָ֗ד שַׁ֤עַר בִּנְיָמִן֙ אֶחָ֔ד שַׁ֥עַר דָּ֖ן אֶחָֽד׃


And at the east side there is a measure of four thousand five hundred: and three gates; one gate of Yosef, one gate of Binyamin, one gate of Dan.


###### Ezekiel 48:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/48:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/48:33)


וּפְאַת־נֶ֗גְבָּה חֲמֵ֨שׁ מֵא֜וֹת וְאַרְבַּ֤עַת אֲלָפִים֙ מִדָּ֔ה וּשְׁעָרִ֖ים שְׁלֹשָׁ֑ה שַׁ֣עַר שִׁמְע֞וֹן אֶחָ֗ד שַׁ֤עַר יִשָּׂשכָר֙ אֶחָ֔ד שַׁ֥עַר זְבוּלֻ֖ן אֶחָֽד׃


And at the south side there is a measure of four thousand five hundred; and three gates; one gate of Shim῾on, one gate of Yissakhar, one gate of Zevulun.


###### Ezekiel 48:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/48:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/48:34)


פְּאַת־יָ֗מָּה חֲמֵ֤שׁ מֵאוֹת֙ וְאַרְבַּ֣עַת אֲלָפִ֔ים שַׁעֲרֵיהֶ֖ם שְׁלֹשָׁ֑ה שַׁ֣עַר גָּ֞ד אֶחָ֗ד שַׁ֤עַר אָשֵׁר֙ אֶחָ֔ד שַׁ֥עַר נַפְתָּלִ֖י אֶחָֽד׃


At the west side there is a measure of four thousand five hundred, with their three gates; one gate of Gad, one gate of Asher, one gate of Naftali.


###### Ezekiel 48:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/48:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ezekiel/r/48:35)


סָבִ֕יב שְׁמֹנָ֥ה עָשָׂ֖ר אָ֑לֶף וְשֵׁם־הָעִ֥יר מִיּ֖וֹם יְהֹוָ֥ה׀שָֽׁמָּה׃


It shall be round about eighteen thousand measures: and the name of the city from that day shall be, The Lord is there.
